    // === src/__tests__/adversarial/eventosEscrowSync.adversarial.test.ts === //
import fc from 'fast-check';
import {
  buildCleanGalleryUrls,
  buildCleanKineticEventSharePayload,
  buildCleanSquadInviteMessage,
  computeEscrow4060Split,
  reconcileStudioOccupiedSpots,
  stepGalleryIndex,
} from '../../components/eventos/eventDetailsPureHelpers';
import { assertIntegridadCupos, assertIntegridadMonetaria } from './invariants.helper';

const g = globalThis as Record<string, unknown>;
const describeSuite =
  typeof g.describe === 'function'
    ? (g.describe as (name: string, fn: () => void) => void)
    : (_name: string, fn: () => void) => fn();
const itCase =
  typeof g.it === 'function'
    ? (g.it as (name: string, fn: () => void) => void)
    : (_name: string, fn: () => void) => fn();

describeSuite('KINETIC OS v2.6.3 — Adversarial PBT Suite (Eventos + Escrow + Sync)', () => {
  itCase('Propiedad 1: Reconciliación 3D de cupos jamás rebasa cupoMaximo ni genera cupos negativos (500 runs)', () => {
    assertIntegridadCupos((inscritosActuales, cupoMaximo, solicitados) => {
      if (solicitados <= 0 || cupoMaximo <= 0) return false;
      const { freeSpots } = reconcileStudioOccupiedSpots([], cupoMaximo, inscritosActuales, null);
      return solicitados <= freeSpots && inscritosActuales + solicitados <= cupoMaximo;
    });

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.array(fc.integer({ min: -50, max: 250 }), { maxLength: 80 }),
        fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 150 }),
        fc.integer({ min: -20, max: 300 }),
        fc.option(fc.integer({ min: -5, max: 200 }), { nil: null }),
        (liveSpots, cupoMaximo, occupiedCount, userPicked) => {
          const res = reconcileStudioOccupiedSpots(liveSpots, cupoMaximo, occupiedCount, userPicked);
          const validLiveUnique = new Set(
            liveSpots.filter((s) => Number.isFinite(s) && Math.floor(s) >= 1 && Math.floor(s) <= cupoMaximo).map((s) => Math.floor(s))
          ).size;
          const expectedCount = Math.min(cupoMaximo, Math.max(validLiveUnique, Math.max(0, occupiedCount)));

          return (
            res.reconciledCount >= 0 &&
            res.reconciledCount <= cupoMaximo &&
            res.freeSpots >= 0 &&
            res.reconciledCount + res.freeSpots === cupoMaximo &&
            res.occupiedSpots.length === res.reconciledCount &&
            res.occupiedSet.size === res.reconciledCount &&
            res.reconciledCount === expectedCount &&
            res.occupiedSpots.every((s) => s >= 1 && s <= cupoMaximo)
          );
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  itCase('Propiedad 2: Sanitizador Share & Squad elimina Maps URLs, paréntesis vacíos y deja exactamente 1 URL oficial (500 runs)', () => {
    const poisonedUrlArb = fc.constantFrom(
      'https://maps.app.goo.gl/XyZ123AbC',
      '(https://maps.app.goo.gl/998877)',
      '(((https://www.google.com/maps/place/Cabo+San+Lucas)))',
      'http://goo.gl/maps/test123 ()',
      'maps.google.com/?q=22.89,-109.91 [] {}'
    );

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.string({ maxLength: 40 }),
        fc.string({ maxLength: 40 }),
        fc.string({ maxLength: 40 }),
        fc.string({ maxLength: 30 }),
        poisonedUrlArb,
        fc.integer({ min: 0, max: 50000 }),
        fc.integer({ min: 2, max: 12 }),
        fc.constantFrom('es', 'en'),
        (rawTitle, rawClub, rawLoc, rawGuest, injectedMaps, totalKc, splitCount, lang) => {
          const dirtyLocation = `${rawLoc} ${injectedMaps} () (   )`;
          const dirtyTitle = `${rawTitle} (${injectedMaps}) ()`;
          const dirtyClub = `${rawClub} () ${injectedMaps}`;
          const dirtyGuest = `${rawGuest} (${injectedMaps})`;

          const eventShare = buildCleanKineticEventSharePayload({
            eventId: `evt-123 ${injectedMaps}`,
            title: dirtyTitle,
            clubName: dirtyClub,
            discipline: `Indoor Cycling () ${injectedMaps}`,
            location: dirtyLocation,
            detectedZone: `El Tezal ${injectedMaps}`,
            dateFormatted: `Thu, Nov 12 • 08:00 HRS ${injectedMaps}`,
            totalKc,
            escrow40: Math.round(totalKc * 0.4),
            language: lang,
          });

          const squadShare = buildCleanSquadInviteMessage({
            guestName: dirtyGuest,
            eventId: `evt-123 ${injectedMaps}`,
            title: dirtyTitle,
            discipline: `Ciclismo Indoor () ${injectedMaps}`,
            clubName: dirtyClub,
            location: dirtyLocation,
            readableDate: `Jue, 12 nov • 08:00 HRS ${injectedMaps}`,
            splitCount,
            totalKc,
            hostId: `usr-1 ${injectedMaps}`,
            language: lang,
          });

          const checkCleanMessage = (msg: string): boolean => {
            const lower = msg.toLowerCase();
            if (
              lower.includes('maps.app.goo.gl') ||
              lower.includes('goo.gl/maps') ||
              lower.includes('google.com/maps') ||
              lower.includes('maps.google.')
            ) {
              return false;
            }
            if (/\(\s*\)/.test(msg)) return false;
            const urls = msg.match(/https?:\/\/\S+/g) || [];
            return urls.length === 1 && urls[0].includes('?evento=');
          };

          return checkCleanMessage(eventShare.message) && checkCleanMessage(squadShare.message);
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  itCase('Propiedad 3: Integridad monetaria Split-Escrow 40/60 y Master Pass Admin 0 KC sin pérdida de unidades (500 runs)', () => {
    assertIntegridadMonetaria((montoCentavos, tasaPct) => {
      const comisionKineticCentavos = Math.round((montoCentavos * tasaPct) / 100);
      const netoClubCentavos = montoCentavos - comisionKineticCentavos;
      return {
        netoClubCentavos,
        comisionKineticCentavos,
        totalCentavos: netoClubCentavos + comisionKineticCentavos,
      };
    });

    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.double({ min: -50000, max: 1000000, noNaN: false }),
        fc.boolean(),
        (rawPrice, isAdmin) => {
          const split = computeEscrow4060Split(rawPrice, isAdmin);
          if (isAdmin) {
            return split.totalKc === 0 && split.escrow40 === 0 && split.puerta60 === 0;
          }
          return (
            Number.isInteger(split.totalKc) &&
            Number.isInteger(split.escrow40) &&
            Number.isInteger(split.puerta60) &&
            split.totalKc >= 0 &&
            split.escrow40 >= 0 &&
            split.puerta60 >= 0 &&
            split.escrow40 + split.puerta60 === split.totalKc
          );
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });

  itCase('Propiedad 4: Navegación circular de galería fullscreen mantiene índices en rango [0, N-1] y deduplica URLs (500 runs)', () => {
    fc.assert(
      fc.property(
        fc.webUrl(),
        fc.array(fc.webUrl(), { minLength: 0, maxLength: 25 }),
        fc.integer({ min: -1000, max: 1000 }),
        fc.array(fc.integer({ min: -25, max: 25 }), { minLength: 1, maxLength: 40 }),
        (coverUrl, extraUrls, startIdx, deltas) => {
          const gallery = buildCleanGalleryUrls(coverUrl, [coverUrl, ...extraUrls, '', '   ', null]);
          if (gallery.length < 1) return false;
          if (new Set(gallery).size !== gallery.length) return false;

          let idx = stepGalleryIndex(startIdx, 0, gallery.length);
          if (idx < 0 || idx >= gallery.length) return false;

          for (const d of deltas) {
            idx = stepGalleryIndex(idx, d, gallery.length);
            if (!Number.isInteger(idx) || idx < 0 || idx >= gallery.length) {
              return false;
            }
          }
          return typeof gallery[idx] === 'string' && gallery[idx].length >= 5;
        }
      ),
      { numRuns: 500 }
    );
  });
});

    FILE: src/app/(tabs)/eventos.tsx

    // === src/app/(tabs)/eventos.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useFocusEffect } from 'expo-router';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Platform, RefreshControl, ScrollView, StatusBar, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { MembershipModal, TacticalAlertModal } from '../../components/BovedaModals';
import { EventDetailsModal } from '../../components/EventDetailsModal';
import {
  EventUnifiedRatingAuditModal,
  registerGlobalEventRatingAuditOpener,
} from '../../components/EventDetailsSubModals';
import { ScreenSkeletonScanner, triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import {
  AgendaEventBanner,
  CATEGORY_CARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH,
} from '../../components/eventos/EventosFlashAndAgendaViews';
import { EventosAgendaCalendarSection } from '../../components/eventos/EventosAgendaCalendarSection';
import {
  EventosClockwiseBoltBanner,
  EventosFilterSortModals,
  EventosFlashCarouselSection,
} from '../../components/eventos/EventosOverlaysAndCarousel';
import { dismissOpenFilterSortMenus, registerGlobalFilterBarSelectListener } from '../../components/shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { formatDisciplineBilingual } from '../../constants/kineticDisciplineMap';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { useEventosScreen } from '../../hooks/useEventosScreen';
import { bovedaService } from '../../services/bovedaService';
import { getLocalTodayIso, isKineticOfficialClubName, parseIsoLocal } from '../../services/eventosScreenHelpers';
import { filterFlashSalesUpcoming } from '../../services/tacticalAlertsService';
import { COLORS, SPACING, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { s as baseStyles } from '../../styles/tabs/eventos.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { KineticPhysicsPressable } from '../../utils/kineticPhysicsEngine';

export default function EventosScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
  const vm = useEventosScreen();
  const isLight = Boolean(vm.themeColors.isLight);
  const eventosScrollRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);
  const [auditRatingEvent, setAuditRatingEvent] = useState<any>(null);
  const [, setPassSnapshotRev] = useState(0);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      if (user?.id) {
        bovedaService.fetchReservasAtleta(user.id).catch(() => {});
      }
    }, [user?.id])
  );

  useEffect(() => bovedaService.subscribePassesSnapshot(() => setPassSnapshotRev((v) => v + 1)), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) return;
    bovedaService.fetchReservasAtleta(user.id).catch(() => {});
    return bovedaService.subscribeUserReservasRealtimeAndFocus(user.id, () => {
      bovedaService.fetchReservasAtleta(user.id).catch(() => {});
      vm.fetchCatalog(true, true).catch(() => {});
    });
  }, [user?.id, vm.fetchCatalog]);

  useEffect(() => registerGlobalEventRatingAuditOpener((ev) => setAuditRatingEvent(ev)), []);

  useEffect(
    () =>
      registerGlobalFilterBarSelectListener(() => {
        if (vm.catPickerOpen) vm.setQuickChip('todas');
      }),
    [vm.catPickerOpen, vm.setQuickChip]
  );

  const handlePullToRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    eventosScrollRef.current?.scrollTo?.({ y: 0, animated: true });
    const freshToday = getLocalTodayIso();
    if (vm.selectedIsoDate < freshToday) {
      const d = parseIsoLocal(freshToday);
      vm.setSelectedIsoDate(freshToday);
      vm.setActiveMonth(new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1));
    }
    if (vm.viewMode === 'lista') {
      vm.topCarouselIdxRef.current = 0;
      vm.setActiveCarouselIdx(0);
      vm.topCarouselRef.current?.scrollTo?.({ x: 0, animated: false });
    }
    vm.setRefreshing(true);
    await vm.fetchCatalog(true, true);
    eventosScrollRef.current?.scrollTo?.({ y: 0, animated: true });
  }, [vm]);

  const flashSales = useMemo(() => filterFlashSalesUpcoming(vm.catalog || []), [vm.catalog]);
  const displayRecommended = useMemo(() => {
    const isStrictlyActive = (ev: any) =>
      Boolean(
        ev &&
          !ev.isInactive &&
          !ev.isExpired &&
          (isKineticOfficialClubName(ev.negocio_nombre) || (ev.esClubActivo !== false && !ev.isGhost)) &&
          (ev.es_venta_flash || ev.isFlashSale || isKineticOfficialClubName(ev.negocio_nombre))
      );
    const activeRecommended = (vm.recommendedEvents || []).filter(isStrictlyActive);
    if (!flashSales.length) return activeRecommended;
    const rawFlash = flashSales.map((f) => f.rawServicio).filter(isStrictlyActive);
    const seen = new Set(rawFlash.map((f) => String(f.id)));
    return [...rawFlash, ...activeRecommended.filter((e: any) => !seen.has(String(e.id)))];
  }, [flashSales, vm.recommendedEvents]);

  const s = useMemo(
    () =>
      adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, [
        'filterBtnDark', 'filterBtnLight', 'sortBtnDark', 'sortBtnLight',
        'quickRow3DDark', 'quickRow3DLight', 'quickRowCyanActiveDark', 'quickRowCyanActiveLight',
        'quickRowGreenActiveDark', 'quickRowGreenActiveLight', 'quickRowPurpleActiveDark', 'quickRowPurpleActiveLight',
        'resetListFilterBtn', 'resetListFilterBtnTxt', 'createOfficialEventBtn', 'createOfficialEventBtnTxt',
      ]),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );

  if (vm.isLoading && !vm.hasLoadedInitial && vm.catalog.length === 0) {
    return <ScreenSkeletonScanner variant="agenda" topInset={insets.top} />;
  }

  const noSel: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;
  const cyanInk = isLight ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan;
  const purpleInk = isLight ? '#6D28D9' : '#D8B4FE';
  const activeFilterLabel =
    vm.selectedCategory !== 'Todas'
      ? formatDisciplineBilingual(vm.selectedCategory, vm.language, vm.tDisc)
      : vm.quickChip === 'favoritos'
        ? `⚡ ${vm.tx('Favoritos', 'Favorites')}`
        : vm.quickChip === 'mas_buscados'
          ? `🔥 ${vm.tx('Tendencia', 'Trending')}`
          : vm.quickChip === 'wellness'
            ? `✨ ${vm.tx('Bienestar & Spa', 'Wellness & Spa')}`
            : vm.tx('Todas', 'All');
  const sortLabelStr = vm.language === 'en' ? vm.activeSortOption.shortEn : vm.activeSortOption.shortEs;
  const hasFiltersApplied =
    vm.selectedCategory !== 'Todas' || vm.quickChip !== 'todas' || vm.searchQuery.trim().length > 0;
  const isEmptyFavoritesView = vm.quickChip === 'favoritos' && vm.groupedByCategory.length === 0;

  return (
    <View style={[globalStyles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: vm.themeColors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={vm.themeColors.statusBarStyle} backgroundColor={vm.themeColors.background} />

      <View style={[s.headerBar, { paddingTop: insets.top + 10 }]}>
        <View>
          <Text style={[s.headerMicro, { color: vm.themeColors.cyanText }]}>{vm.t('events.micro')}</Text>
          <Text style={[s.headerMain, { color: vm.themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{vm.t('events.title')}</Text>
        </View>
        <KineticPhysicsPressable
          preset="tactileChip"
          style={[
            s.modeBtn,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#06B6D4', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4', shadowColor: '#00B8D4', shadowOpacity: 0.24 },
            noSel,
          ]}
          onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); vm.setViewMode((p) => (p === 'agenda' ? 'lista' : 'agenda')); }}
        >
          <Feather name={vm.viewMode === 'agenda' ? 'list' : 'calendar'} size={14} color={cyanInk} />
          <Text style={[s.modeBtnTxt, { color: cyanInk }]}>
            {vm.viewMode === 'agenda' ? vm.t('events.list') : vm.t('events.agenda')}
          </Text>
        </KineticPhysicsPressable>
      </View>

      <View style={s.controlsWrap}>
        <View
          style={[
            s.searchBox,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#38BDF8', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#00B8D4', shadowColor: '#00B8D4', shadowOpacity: 0.16 },
          ]}
        >
          <Feather name="search" size={15} color={cyanInk} />
          <TextInput
            style={[s.searchInput, { fontSize: 16, color: vm.themeColors.textPrimary }]}
            placeholder={vm.tx('Buscar evento, disciplina, coach o club...', 'Search event, discipline, coach, or club...')}
            placeholderTextColor={vm.themeColors.textMuted}
            value={vm.searchQuery}
            onChangeText={vm.setSearchQuery}
            returnKeyType="search"
          />
          {vm.searchQuery.length > 0 && (
            <TouchableOpacity hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); vm.setSearchQuery(''); }}>
              <Feather name="x" size={15} color={vm.themeColors.textMuted} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={s.selectsRow}>
          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="tactileChip"
            style={[s.halfBtn3D, isLight ? s.filterBtnLight : s.filterBtnDark, noSel]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); vm.setCatPickerOpen(true); }}
          >
            <Feather name="filter" size={12} color={cyanInk} />
            <Text style={[s.halfBtnText, { color: cyanInk }]} numberOfLines={1}>
              {vm.tx('Filtrar', 'Filter')}: {activeFilterLabel} ▼
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>

          <KineticPhysicsPressable
            preset="tactileChip"
            style={[s.halfBtn3D, isLight ? s.sortBtnLight : s.sortBtnDark, noSel]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); vm.setSortPickerOpen(true); }}
          >
            <Feather name="sliders" size={12} color={purpleInk} />
            <Text style={[s.halfBtnText, { color: purpleInk }]} numberOfLines={1}>
              {vm.tx('Ordenar', 'Sort')}: {sortLabelStr} ▼
            </Text>
          </KineticPhysicsPressable>
        </View>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        ref={eventosScrollRef}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
        onScrollBeginDrag={() => {
          dismissOpenFilterSortMenus();
          vm.setSortPickerOpen(false);
          vm.setCatPickerOpen(false);
        }}
        onScroll={() => {
          dismissOpenFilterSortMenus();
          vm.setSortPickerOpen(false);
          vm.setCatPickerOpen(false);
        }}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 115 }}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl
            refreshing={vm.refreshing}
            onRefresh={handlePullToRefresh}
            tintColor={isLight ? '#0284C7' : '#00F0FF'}
            colors={[isLight ? '#0284C7' : '#00F0FF']}
          />
        }
      >
        <EventosClockwiseBoltBanner visible={vm.refreshing} isLight={isLight} />
        {vm.viewMode === 'agenda' ? (
          <EventosAgendaCalendarSection
            monthOpen={vm.monthOpen}
            setMonthOpen={vm.setMonthOpen}
            activeMonth={vm.activeMonth}
            stepMonth={vm.stepMonth}
            stepWeek={vm.stepWeek}
            calendarSwipeResponder={vm.calendarSwipeResponder}
            slideAnimX={vm.slideAnimX}
            monthGridCells={vm.monthGridCells}
            weekCells={vm.weekCells}
            selectedIsoDate={vm.searchQuery.trim() ? '' : vm.selectedIsoDate}
            setSelectedIsoDate={(iso: string) => vm.selectAgendaDate(iso, true)}
            swipedRecentlyRef={vm.swipedRecentlyRef}
            daySessions={vm.daySessions}
            onSelectEvent={vm.setSelectedEvent}
            canCreateEvents={vm.canCreateEvents}
            onPressCreateEvent={() => vm.setIsCreateEventOpen(true)}
            hasActiveSearch={Boolean(vm.searchQuery.trim())}
          />
        ) : (
          <View>
            {!isEmptyFavoritesView && (
              <EventosFlashCarouselSection
                recommendedEvents={displayRecommended}
                activeCarouselIdx={vm.activeCarouselIdx}
                setActiveCarouselIdx={vm.setActiveCarouselIdx}
                topCarouselRef={vm.topCarouselRef}
                topCarouselIdxRef={vm.topCarouselIdxRef}
                isUserTouchingCarouselRef={vm.isUserTouchingCarouselRef}
                todayIso={vm.todayIso}
                onSelectEvent={vm.setSelectedEvent}
              />
            )}
            {vm.groupedByCategory.length === 0 ? (
              <View
                style={[
                  s.emptyListFilterBox,
                  isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
                ]}
              >
                <Feather name="calendar" size={28} color={vm.themeColors.cyanText} />
                <Text style={[s.emptyListFilterTitle, { color: vm.themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                  {vm.quickChip === 'favoritos'
                    ? vm.tx('SIN EVENTOS EN TUS FAVORITOS', 'NO EVENTS IN YOUR FAVORITES')
                    : vm.catalog.length === 0 && !hasFiltersApplied
                      ? vm.tx('SIN EVENTOS ACTIVOS EN LA CARTELERA', 'NO ACTIVE EVENTS ON THE SCHEDULE')
                      : vm.tx('SIN EVENTOS ACTIVOS EN ESTE FILTRO', 'NO ACTIVE EVENTS IN THIS FILTER')}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[s.emptyListFilterSub, { color: vm.themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                  {vm.quickChip === 'favoritos'
                    ? vm.tx('Marca tus eventos favoritos con el Rayo Cian (⚡) para verlos aquí.', 'Bookmark your favorite events with the Cyan Bolt (⚡) to see them here.')
                    : vm.catalog.length === 0 && !hasFiltersApplied
                      ? vm.tx('Pronto se publicarán nuevos entrenamientos, torneos y sesiones oficiales verificadas.', 'New verified workouts, tournaments, and official sessions will be published soon.')
                      : vm.tx('Explora otra disciplina deportiva o restablece los filtros de la cartelera.', 'Explore another discipline or reset the schedule filters.')}
                </Text>
                {(isEmptyFavoritesView || hasFiltersApplied || vm.catalog.length > 0) && (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={s.resetListFilterBtn}
                    onPress={() => {
                      triggerHaptic('Selection');
                      (vm as any).setOnlyFavorites?.(false);
                      vm.setQuickChip('todas');
                      vm.setSelectedCategory('Todas');
                      vm.setSearchQuery('');
                      vm.setSortBy('relevancia');
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text style={s.resetListFilterBtnTxt}>{vm.tx('VER TODOS LOS EVENTOS', 'VIEW ALL EVENTS')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}

                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={s.createOfficialEventBtn}
                    onPress={() => {
                      triggerHaptic('Medium');
                      vm.setIsCreateEventOpen(true);
                    }}
                  >
                    <Feather name="plus-circle" size={14} color="#050505" />
                    <Text style={s.createOfficialEventBtnTxt}>
                      {vm.tx('+ CREAR EVENTO OFICIAL', '+ CREATE OFFICIAL EVENT')}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </View>
            ) : (
              vm.groupedByCategory.map((grp) => (
                <View key={grp.title} style={{ marginBottom: 18 }}>
                  <View style={[s.grpHeader, { paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg }]}>
                    <Text style={[s.grpTitle, { color: vm.themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                      {formatDisciplineBilingual(grp.title, vm.language, vm.tDisc)}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[s.grpCount, { color: vm.themeColors.cyanText, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>
                      {grp.items.length} {grp.items.length === 1 ? vm.tx('EVENTO', 'EVENT') : vm.tx('EVENTOS', 'EVENTS')}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  {grp.items.length > 1 ? (
                    <FlatList
                      data={grp.items}
                      horizontal
                      keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
                      initialNumToRender={6}
                      maxToRenderPerBatch={8}
                      windowSize={5}
                      removeClippedSubviews={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
                      showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
                      decelerationRate="fast"
                      snapToInterval={CATEGORY_CARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH + 14}
                      snapToAlignment="start"
                      contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg, gap: 14 }}
                      keyExtractor={(ev: any, idx: number) => String(ev?.id || `${grp.title}_${idx}`)}
                      renderItem={({ item: ev }) => {
                        const effIso = String(ev?.fecha_proyectada || ev?.fecha || vm.todayIso || '').split('T')[0];
                        return (
                          <AgendaEventBanner
                            item={ev}
                            dateIso={effIso}
                            cardWidth={CATEGORY_CARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH}
                            onSelect={vm.setSelectedEvent}
                            hasActivePassProp={bovedaService.hasActivePassForEventSync(user?.id, ev, effIso)}
                            onOpenRatingAudit={setAuditRatingEvent}
                          />
                        );
                      }}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg }}>
                      {grp.items.map((ev: any) => {
                        const effIso = String(ev?.fecha_proyectada || ev?.fecha || vm.todayIso || '').split('T')[0];
                        return (
                          <AgendaEventBanner
                            key={ev.id}
                            item={ev}
                            dateIso={effIso}
                            onSelect={vm.setSelectedEvent}
                            hasActivePassProp={bovedaService.hasActivePassForEventSync(user?.id, ev, effIso)}
                            onOpenRatingAudit={setAuditRatingEvent}
                          />
                        );
                      })}
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
              ))
            )}
          </View>
        )}
      </ScrollView>

      <EventosFilterSortModals
        catPickerOpen={vm.catPickerOpen}
        setCatPickerOpen={vm.setCatPickerOpen}
        sortPickerOpen={vm.sortPickerOpen}
        setSortPickerOpen={vm.setSortPickerOpen}
        selectedCategory={vm.selectedCategory}
        setSelectedCategory={(cat: any) => {
          const nextCat = typeof cat === 'function' ? cat(vm.selectedCategory) : cat;
          vm.setSelectedCategory(nextCat);
          vm.setQuickChip('todas');
        }}
        quickChip={vm.quickChip}
        setQuickChip={vm.setQuickChip}
        sortBy={vm.sortBy}
        setSortBy={vm.setSortBy}
      />

      <EventUnifiedRatingAuditModal
        visible={Boolean(auditRatingEvent)}
        eventItem={auditRatingEvent}
        onClose={() => setAuditRatingEvent(null)}
      />

      <EventDetailsModal
        visible={Boolean(vm.selectedEvent)}
        event={vm.selectedEvent}
        onClose={() => vm.setSelectedEvent(null)}
        onReserve={vm.handleReserve}
        onRefreshEvents={() => vm.fetchCatalog(true, true)}
      />
      <EventDetailsModal
        visible={vm.isCreateEventOpen}
        isCreateMode={true}
        defaultClubName={vm.defaultCreatorClubName}
        onClose={() => vm.setIsCreateEventOpen(false)}
        onRefreshEvents={() => vm.fetchCatalog(true, true)}
      />
      <MembershipModal
        isVisible={vm.isMembershipOpen}
        onClose={() => vm.setIsMembershipOpen(false)}
        onPurchase={vm.handleMembershipPurchase}
        isProcessingPayment={vm.isCheckoutProcessing}
      />
      <TacticalAlertModal alertConfig={vm.alertConfig} setAlertConfig={vm.setAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />
    </View>
  );
}

import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { RefreshControl, ScrollView, StatusBar, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { MembershipModal } from '../components/MembershipModal';
import { KineticDualConfirmModal } from '../components/shared/KineticDualConfirmModal';
import { SkeletonPulse, triggerHaptic } from '../components/SharedUI';
import {
  ReservationDetailBreakdownCard,
  ReservationTacticalAlertModal,
  SplitEscrowGuestInviteCard,
} from '../components/eventos/ReservationDetailBreakdownCard';
import { useReservationDetailScreen } from '../hooks/useReservationDetailScreen';
import { baseStyles } from '../styles/reservation-detail.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';

export default function ReservationDetailsScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const [pendingReserveTarget, setPendingReserveTarget] = useState<{ srv: Parameters<typeof procesarReserva>[0]; cnt: number } | null>(null);
  const {
    router, profile, tx, themeColors, resolvedTheme, isLight, splitCount,
    isSplitGuestMode, hostName, preferredDateIso, servicios, isFetching, refreshing, onRefresh,
    bookingId, reservedServiceIds, isMembershipModalOpen, setIsMembershipModalOpen,
    isProcessingPayment, processingPlanId, alertConfig, setAlertConfig,
    procesarReserva, handlePurchase, saldoTotal, resolvedHeaderTitle,
  } = useReservationDetailScreen();

  const styles = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, ['tacticalOverlayDark']),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );

  return (
    <View style={[styles.mainContainer, { backgroundColor: themeColors.background }]}>
      <StatusBar barStyle={themeColors.statusBarStyle} backgroundColor="transparent" translucent />
      <View
        style={[
          styles.header,
          { paddingTop: insets.top + 15 },
          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={styles.headerTopRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Light');
              if (isSplitGuestMode) router.replace('/(tabs)/eventos');
              else if (router.canGoBack()) router.back();
              else router.replace('/(tabs)');
            }}
            style={[styles.backBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' }]}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <Feather name="arrow-left" size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[styles.backBtnText, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {isSplitGuestMode ? tx('VOLVER A EVENTOS', 'BACK TO EVENTS') : tx('VOLVER AL RADAR', 'BACK TO RADAR')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.walletHeaderPill, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7' }]}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); setIsMembershipModalOpen(true); }}
          >
            <Feather name="zap" size={12} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[styles.walletHeaderText, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>
              {saldoTotal} KC
            </Text>
            <Feather name="plus-circle" size={12} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
        <Text style={[styles.title, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={2}>
          {resolvedHeaderTitle}
        </Text>
        <Text style={[styles.subtitle, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
          {isSplitGuestMode
            ? tx(`INVITACIÓN EQUIPO • DIVISIÓN 1/${splitCount} PROTEGIDA`, `TEAM INVITE • 1/${splitCount} PROTECTED SPLIT`)
            : tx('CATÁLOGO DE EXPERIENCIAS • ANTICIPO PROTEGIDO 40/60', 'EXPERIENCE CATALOG • 40/60 PROTECTED BOOKING')}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={[styles.container, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 40 }]}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} tintColor={themeColors.cyanText} />}
      >
        {isFetching && servicios.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={{ gap: 16 }}>
            <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '100%', height: 165, borderRadius: 20 }} />
            <SkeletonPulse style={{ width: '100%', height: 165, borderRadius: 20 }} />
          </View>
        ) : isSplitGuestMode && servicios.length > 0 ? (
          servicios.map((servicio) => (
            <SplitEscrowGuestInviteCard
              key={servicio.id}
              servicio={servicio}
              splitCount={splitCount}
              hostName={hostName || tx('Anfitrión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Host')}
              preferredDateIso={preferredDateIso}
              isAlreadyReserved={reservedServiceIds.includes(servicio.id)}
              bookingId={bookingId}
              onAcceptSplit={(srv, cnt) => setPendingReserveTarget({ srv, cnt })}
              onDeclineSplit={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); router.replace('/(tabs)/eventos'); }}
              onOpenVault={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); router.replace('/(tabs)/boveda'); }}
            />
          ))
        ) : (
          servicios.map((servicio) => (
            <ReservationDetailBreakdownCard
              key={servicio.id}
              servicio={servicio}
              isAlreadyReserved={reservedServiceIds.includes(servicio.id)}
              bookingId={bookingId}
              onReserve={(srv) => setPendingReserveTarget({ srv, cnt: 1 })}
              onOpenVault={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); router.replace('/(tabs)/boveda'); }}
            />
          ))
        )}
        {!isFetching && servicios.length === 0 && (
          <View style={[styles.emptyBox, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' }]}>
            <Feather name="clock" size={32} color={themeColors.textMuted} style={{ marginBottom: 10 }} />
            <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('No hay servicios públicos activos en este club todavía.', 'No active public sessions at this venue yet.')}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}
      </ScrollView>

      <KineticDualConfirmModal
        visible={Boolean(pendingReserveTarget)}
        title={tx('Confirmar Anticipo Protegido 40/60', 'Confirm 40/60 Protected Booking')}
        message={
          pendingReserveTarget
            ? tx(
                `¿Apartar lugar en "${pendingReserveTarget.srv.titulo || pendingReserveTarget.srv.nombre_servicio || 'Sesión Kinetic'}" con Anticipo Protegido (40%) y Liquidación en Recepción (60%)?`,
                `Confirm booking "${pendingReserveTarget.srv.titulo || pendingReserveTarget.srv.nombre_servicio || 'Kinetic Session'}" with Protected Deposit (40%) and Front Desk Settlement (60%)?`
              )
            : ''
        }
        confirmLabel={tx('Reservar Cupo', 'Book Spot')}
        cancelLabel={tx('Cancelar', 'Cancel')}
        tone="cyan"
        onConfirm={() => {
          const t = pendingReserveTarget;
          setPendingReserveTarget(null);
          if (t) procesarReserva(t.srv, t.cnt);
        }}
        onCancel={() => setPendingReserveTarget(null)}
      />

      <MembershipModal
        isVisible={isMembershipModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsMembershipModalOpen(false)}
        onPurchase={handlePurchase}
        isProcessingPayment={isProcessingPayment}
        processingPlanId={processingPlanId}
        currentLevel={profile?.nivel}
      />

      <ReservationTacticalAlertModal alertConfig={alertConfig} setAlertConfig={setAlertConfig} />
    </View>
  );
}

export { default } from './reservation-detail';

    FILE: src/components/boveda/BovedaRateEventModal.tsx

    import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator, KeyboardAvoidingView, Modal, Platform, ScrollView, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  RATED_RESERVATIONS_STORAGE_KEY,
  calificarReservaAtleta,
  hydrateLocalRatingsMap,
  resolveReservaRating,
  subscribeRatingUpdates,
} from '../../services/bovedaRatingService';
import { triggerArcadeHaptic, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export { RATED_RESERVATIONS_STORAGE_KEY };
const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as any) : null;

const getStarLabelText = (val: number, tx: (es: string, en: string) => string) => {
  if (val === 5) return tx('5/5 ★ Excelente', '5/5 ★ Excellent');
  if (val === 4) return tx('4/5 ★ Muy Bueno', '4/5 ★ Very Good');
  if (val === 3) return tx('3/5 ★ Bueno', '3/5 ★ Good');
  if (val === 2) return tx('2/5 ★ Regular', '2/5 ★ Fair');
  return tx('1/5 ★ Mejorable', '1/5 ★ Poor');
};

export const ReceiptInlineRatingBlock = ({
  activeReceipt,
  onRated,
}: {
  activeReceipt: any;
  onRated?: (reservaId: string, stars: number, comment?: string) => void;
}) => {
  const { themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  const stLower = String(activeReceipt?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const initial = resolveReservaRating(activeReceipt);
  const [savedStars, setSavedStars] = useState<number | null>(initial.stars);
  const [savedComment, setSavedComment] = useState<string>(initial.comment);
  const [draftStars, setDraftStars] = useState<number>(initial.stars || 5);
  const [draftComment, setDraftComment] = useState<string>(initial.comment);
  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState<boolean>(!initial.stars);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [justSavedMsg, setJustSavedMsg] = useState<string>('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const rid = String(activeReceipt?.id || '').trim();
    if (!rid) return;
    const syncFromData = (map?: any) => {
      const cur = resolveReservaRating(activeReceipt, map);
      setSavedStars(cur.stars);
      setSavedComment(cur.comment);
      setDraftStars(cur.stars || 5);
      setDraftComment(cur.comment);
      setIsEditing(!cur.stars);
      setJustSavedMsg('');
    };
    syncFromData();
    hydrateLocalRatingsMap().then(syncFromData).catch(() => {});
    return subscribeRatingUpdates((updatedId, st, cm) => {
      if (updatedId === rid) {
        setSavedStars(st);
        setSavedComment(cm);
        setDraftStars(st);
        setDraftComment(cm);
        setIsEditing(false);
      }
    });
  }, [activeReceipt?.id, activeReceipt?.calificacion_atleta, activeReceipt?.comentario_atleta]);

  if (stLower !== 'completada' || !activeReceipt?.id) return null;

  const submitInline = async () => {
    if (isSaving) return;
    setIsSaving(true);
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    try {
      const res = await calificarReservaAtleta(activeReceipt, draftStars, draftComment);
      triggerArcadeHaptic('levelUp');
      setSavedStars(res.stars);
      setSavedComment(res.comment);
      setIsEditing(false);
      setJustSavedMsg(
        res.clubRating
          ? tx(`✓ Calificación guardada • Promedio sede: ★ ${res.clubRating}/5`, `✓ Review saved • Venue rating: ★ ${res.clubRating}/5`)
          : tx('✓ Evaluación registrada en tu Bóveda', '✓ Rating saved in your Vault'),
      );
      onRated?.(res.reservaId, res.stars, res.comment);
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  if (savedStars && !isEditing) {
    return (
      <View
        style={[
          {
            marginTop: 12, borderRadius: 14, padding: 12, borderWidth: 1.2, elevation: 0,
            backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFBEB' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1)',
            borderColor: isLight ? '#F59E0B' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45)',
          },
          NO_SEL,
        ]}
      >
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
            <Text style={{ color: '#F59E0B', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900' }}>
              {'★'.repeat(savedStars)}{'☆'.repeat(Math.max(0, 5 - savedStars))}
            </Text>
            <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FDE68A', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900' }}>
              {tx(`TU EVALUACIÓN: ${savedStars}/5`, `YOUR RATING: ${savedStars}/5`)}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setIsEditing(true); setJustSavedMsg(''); }}
            style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 4, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FEF3C7' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)', elevation: 0 }}
          >
            <Feather name="edit-2" size={11} color={isLight ? '#92400E' : '#FBBF24'} />
            <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#92400E' : '#FBBF24', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('EDITAR', 'EDIT')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
        {savedComment ? (
          <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#475569' : '#CBD5E1', fontSize: 11.5, fontStyle: 'italic', marginTop: 6 }}>
            “{savedComment}”
          </Text>
        ) : null}
        {justSavedMsg ? (
          <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#047857' : '#34D399', fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '800', marginTop: 6 }}>
            {justSavedMsg}
          </Text>
        ) : null}
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        {
          marginTop: 12, borderRadius: 14, padding: 12, borderWidth: 1.2, elevation: 0,
          backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#0B101B',
          borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.4)',
        },
        NO_SEL,
      ]}
    >
      <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FBBF24', fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', textAlign: 'center', letterSpacing: 0.5 }}>
        {tx('⭐ CALIFICA TU EXPERIENCIA (1 A 5 ★)', '⭐ RATE YOUR EXPERIENCE (1 TO 5 ★)')}
      </Text>
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 10, marginVertical: 8 }}>
        {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((val) => (
          <TouchableOpacity key={val} activeOpacity={0.75} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setDraftStars(val); }} style={{ padding: 2 }}>
            <Text style={{ fontSize: 26, color: val <= draftStars ? '#F59E0B' : isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#334155' }}>★</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ))}
      </View>
      <TextInput
        value={draftComment}
        onChangeText={setDraftComment}
        placeholder={tx('Comentario opcional sobre el coach o club...', 'Optional review about coach or venue...')}
        placeholderTextColor={isLight ? '#94A3B8' : '#64748B'}
        maxLength={240}
        style={{
          fontSize: 16, elevation: 0, color: themeColors.textPrimary,
          backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#111827', borderWidth: 1,
          borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#1E293B', borderRadius: 10,
          paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 8, marginBottom: 8,
        }}
      />
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
        {savedStars ? (
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => { setIsEditing(false); setDraftStars(savedStars); setDraftComment(savedComment); }}
            style={{ paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 9, borderRadius: 10, backgroundColor: isLight ? '#E2E8F0' : '#1E293B', justifyContent: 'center', elevation: 0 }}
          >
            <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ) : null}
        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={submitInline}
          disabled={isSaving}
          style={{
            flex: 1, paddingVertical: 9, borderRadius: 10, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
            backgroundColor: '#F59E0B', borderWidth: 1, borderTopColor: '#FDE68A', borderBottomWidth: 3, borderBottomColor: '#B45309',
          }}
        >
          {isSaving ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#050505" />
          ) : (
            <Text style={{ color: '#050505', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.4 }}>
              {tx(`GUARDAR ${draftStars}/5 ★`, `SAVE ${draftStars}/5 ★`)}
            </Text>
          )}
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

interface BovedaRateEventModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  reserva: any;
  onClose: () => void;
  onRated?: (reservaId: string, stars: number, comment?: string) => void;
  showTacticalAlert?: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void;
}

export const BovedaRateEventModal = ({ visible, reserva, onClose, onRated, showTacticalAlert }: BovedaRateEventModalProps) => {
  const { themeColors, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = themeColors.isLight;
  const [stars, setStars] = useState<number>(5);
  const [comment, setComment] = useState<string>('');
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const savingRef = useRef(false);
  const alertTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (alertTimerRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(alertTimerRef.current);
        alertTimerRef.current = null;
      }
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!reserva?.id || !visible) return;
    const init = resolveReservaRating(reserva);
    setStars(init.stars || 5);
    setComment(init.comment);
    hydrateLocalRatingsMap().then((map) => {
      const hydrated = resolveReservaRating(reserva, map);
      if (hydrated.stars) setStars(hydrated.stars);
      if (hydrated.comment) setComment(hydrated.comment);
    }).catch(() => {});
  }, [reserva, visible]);

  if (!visible || !reserva) return null;

  const handleSaveRating = async () => {
    if (savingRef.current || isSaving) return;
    savingRef.current = true;
    setIsSaving(true);
    triggerHaptic('Medium');
    const clubLabel = reserva.negocio_nombre || tx('Kinetic Oficial', 'Kinetic Official');
    try {
      const res = await calificarReservaAtleta(reserva, stars, comment);
      triggerArcadeHaptic('levelUp');
      onRated?.(res.reservaId, res.stars, res.comment);
      onClose();
      if (showTacticalAlert) {
        if (alertTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(alertTimerRef.current);
        alertTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          alertTimerRef.current = null;
          showTacticalAlert(
            tx(`¡Evaluación Registrada! (★ ${res.stars}/5)`, `Review Saved! (★ ${res.stars}/5)`),
            res.clubRating
              ? tx(
                  `Gracias por evaluar tu sesión en ${clubLabel}. El promedio actualizado del club es ★ ${res.clubRating}/5.`,
                  `Thank you for rating your session at ${clubLabel}. The updated venue rating is ★ ${res.clubRating}/5.`,
                )
              : tx(
                  `Tu calificación de ${res.stars}/5 estrellas para ${clubLabel} se guardó en tu Bóveda.`,
                  `Your ${res.stars}/5 star review for ${clubLabel} has been saved in your Vault.`,
                ),
            'success',
          );
        }, 190);
      }
    } catch {
      onClose();
    } finally {
      savingRef.current = false;
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={onClose}>
      <KeyboardAvoidingView
        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
        style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.78)', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', padding: 20, elevation: 0 }}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            {
              width: '100%', maxWidth: 420, borderRadius: 22, padding: 22,
              backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0B111A', borderWidth: 1.5,
              borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#00F0FF',
              borderTopColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#38BDF8', borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#94A3B8' : '#0369A1',
              elevation: 0,
            },
            NO_SEL,
          ]}
        >
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 14 }}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.8 }}>
                {tx('⭐ EVALUACIÓN DE SESIÓN (1 A 5 ★)', '⭐ SESSION REVIEW (1 TO 5 ★)')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: '900', marginTop: 2 }} numberOfLines={1}>
                {reserva.servicio || reserva.nombre_servicio || tx('Sesión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Session')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '600', marginTop: 1 }}>
                📍 {reserva.negocio_nombre || tx('Kinetic Oficial', 'Kinetic Official')}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); onClose(); }} style={{ padding: 6, borderRadius: 12, backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F1F5F9' : '#1E293B', elevation: 0 }}>
              <Feather name="x" size={18} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} bounces={false} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginVertical: 14 }}>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
                {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((val) => (
                  <TouchableOpacity key={val} activeOpacity={0.7} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setStars(val); }} style={{ padding: 4 }}>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 34, color: val <= stars ? '#F59E0B' : isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#334155' }}>★</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                ))}
              </View>
              <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#B45309' : '#F59E0B', fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: '800', marginTop: 8 }}>{getStarLabelText(stars, tx)}</Text>
            </View>

            <View style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700', marginBottom: 6 }}>
                {tx('Comentario breve (opcional):', 'Short review (optional):')}
              </Text>
              <TextInput
                value={comment}
                onChangeText={setComment}
                placeholder={tx('¿Qué te pareció el coach, el espacio y la clase?', 'How was the coach, space, and workout?')}
                placeholderTextColor={isLight ? '#94A3B8' : '#64748B'}
                multiline
                maxLength={240}
                style={{
                  fontSize: 16, elevation: 0, color: themeColors.textPrimary,
                  backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#111827', borderWidth: 1.5,
                  borderColor: isLight ? '#E2E8F0' : '#1E293B', borderRadius: 12,
                  padding: 12, minHeight: 74, textAlignVertical: 'top',
                }}
              />
            </View>

            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10, marginTop: 4 }}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Light'); onClose(); }}
                disabled={isSaving}
                style={{ flex: 1, paddingVertical: 12, borderRadius: 12, alignItems: 'center', backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F1F5F9' : '#1E293B', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#334155', elevation: 0 }}
              >
                <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={handleSaveRating}
                disabled={isSaving}
                style={{
                  flex: 1.6, paddingVertical: 12, borderRadius: 12, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                  backgroundColor: isLight ? '#0284C7' : '#00F0FF', borderWidth: 1.5,
                  borderTopColor: isLight ? '#38BDF8' : '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#0369A1' : '#0284C7',
                }}
              >
                {isSaving ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#050505'} />
                ) : (
                  <Text style={{ color: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#050505', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.3 }}>
                    {tx('GUARDAR RESEÑA', 'SAVE REVIEW')}
                  </Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
    </Modal>
  );
};

    // === src/components/EventDetailsModal.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { useEventDetailsModal } from '../hooks/useEventDetailsModal';
import { bovedaService } from '../services/bovedaService';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { createSwipeDownToCloseHandlers } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { TacticalAlertModal } from './BovedaModals';
import { ed as baseEd } from './EventDetailsModal.styles';
import { EventInternalSheetModal } from './EventDetailsSubModals';
import { KineticLoader, SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';
import { EventDetailsAthleteBody } from './eventos/EventDetailsAthleteBody';
import { EventDetailsBookingFooter } from './eventos/EventDetailsBookingFooter';
import { EventDetailsOverlayModals } from './eventos/EventDetailsOverlayModals';
import { EventEditorB2BView, isEventEditorFormValid } from './eventos/EventEditorB2BView';
import { executeOfficialEventBooking, shareCleanKineticEvent } from './eventos/eventDetailsActions';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';

const KINETIC_LOGO = require('../../assets/logo.png');
const webNoSelect: ViewStyle | null = Platform.OS === 'web'
  ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle)
  : null;

export type EventDetailsModalProps = Parameters<typeof useEventDetailsModal>[0];

export const EventDetailsModal = (props: EventDetailsModalProps) => {
  const { isCreateMode = false, onClose, onReserve, onRefreshEvents } = props;
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { refreshProfile } = useAuth();
  const { resolvedTheme, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const coverSwipeHandlers = useMemo(() => createSwipeDownToCloseHandlers(onClose), [onClose]);
  const vm = useEventDetailsModal(props);
  const frozenCreateModeRef = React.useRef(Boolean(isCreateMode));
  if (vm.isOpen) frozenCreateModeRef.current = Boolean(isCreateMode);
  const effectiveCreateMode = vm.isOpen ? Boolean(isCreateMode) : frozenCreateModeRef.current;
  const isLight = Boolean(vm.themeColors.isLight);
  const ed = useMemo(
    () =>
      adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseEd, resolvedTheme, [
        'heroBox', 'heroDim', 'swipeBubble3D', 'dragPillBar', 'dragHint',
        'iconRoundBtn', 'editBadge', 'editBadgeTxt', 'checkCircleBtn',
        'clubLogoBox', 'discBadge', 'discBadgeTxt', 'starBadge', 'starBadgeTxt',
        'holdBtn', 'holdBtnTxt',
      ]),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );
  const onMediaAttr: Record<string, unknown> = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } : {};
  const openAthleteStudioMap = () => { vm.setStudioInitialConfigMode(false); vm.setIsStudioMapOpen(true); };
  const openB2BStudioConfig = () => { vm.setStudioInitialConfigMode(true); vm.setIsStudioMapOpen(true); };
  const [isSplitInviteOpen, setIsSplitInviteOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isOfficialBookingBusy, setIsOfficialBookingBusy] = useState(false);
  const [localBookedPass, setLocalBookedPass] = useState(false);
  const [modalScrollY, setModalScrollY] = useState(0);
  const [, setPassSnapshotRev] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    setLocalBookedPass(false);
    setModalScrollY(0);
  }, [vm.isOpen, vm.currentEv?.id, vm.localBookingDateIso]);

  useEffect(() => bovedaService.subscribePassesSnapshot(() => setPassSnapshotRev((v) => v + 1)), []);

  const hasActivePass = Boolean(
    bovedaService.hasActivePassForEventSync(vm.userId, vm.currentEv, vm.localBookingDateIso) ||
    vm.hasActiveBookingForThis ||
    localBookedPass
  );
  const isBookingBusy = Boolean(vm.isBookingBusy || isOfficialBookingBusy);
  const isSoldOutLocked = !hasActivePass && !vm.isAdmin && vm.freeSpots <= 0;
  const hasChildOverlayOpen = Boolean(
    vm.activeSheet || vm.isStudioMapOpen || isSplitInviteOpen || vm.isPhotoLightboxOpen ||
    vm.isStoryOpen || vm.isMembershipModalOpen || vm.alertConfig?.visible
  );

  const navigateToVault = useCallback(() => {
    if (vm.selectedSpot != null) vm.persistAndSelectSpot(vm.selectedSpot);
    vm.router.push('/(tabs)/boveda');
    props.onNavigateVault?.();
    onClose?.();
  }, [onClose, props, vm]);

  const handleCleanShare = useCallback(() => {
    void shareCleanKineticEvent({
      eventId: vm.currentEv?.id, titleVal: vm.titleVal, clubName: vm.clubName, discVal: vm.discVal,
      locVal: vm.locVal, detectedZone: vm.currentEv?.ciudad || null, localBookingDateIso: vm.localBookingDateIso,
      timeVal: vm.timeVal || vm.currentEv?.hora_inicio || '', totalKc: vm.totalKc, escrow40: vm.escrow40, language, tDisc: vm.tDisc,
    });
  }, [vm, language]);

  const bookingLockRef = useRef(false);
  const handleConfirmOfficialBooking = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isBookingBusy || bookingLockRef.current || !vm.currentEv) return;
    bookingLockRef.current = true;
    setIsOfficialBookingBusy(true);
    try {
      await executeOfficialEventBooking({
        userId: vm.userId, isAdmin: vm.isAdmin, currentEv: vm.currentEv, titleVal: vm.titleVal, clubName: vm.clubName,
        localBookingDateIso: vm.localBookingDateIso, timeVal: vm.timeVal || vm.currentEv?.hora_inicio || '', totalKc: vm.totalKc,
        vipLevel: vm.userLevel, kcMembresia: vm.userKcMembresia, availableKc: vm.availableKc,
        splitCount: vm.effectiveSplitCount, splitGuests: vm.pendingSplitConfig?.selectedGuests || [],
        studioLayout: vm.studioLayout,
        selectedSpot: vm.selectedSpot, setSelectedSpot: vm.setSelectedSpot, language,
        setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen: vm.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen,
        onBookedSuccessState: () => setLocalBookedPass(true),
        onSplitDispatched: () => {
          vm.resetPreBookingSplit();
          vm.persistAndSelectSpot(null);
        },
        onGhostPurged: (msg) => {
          vm.resetPreBookingSplit();
          vm.persistAndSelectSpot(null);
          onClose?.();
          if (vm.navTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(vm.navTimeoutRef.current);
          vm.navTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
            vm.triggerAlert(vm.tx('EXPERIENCIA RETIRADA', 'EXPERIENCE RETIRED'), msg, 'warning');
          }, 140);
        },
        onInsufficientKc: (missingKc, availKc, requiredEscrow) => {
          vm.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(false);
          vm.setInsufficientKcPrompt({ missingKc, availableKc: availKc, requiredEscrow });
        },
        onRefreshEvents, refreshProfile, triggerAlert: vm.triggerAlert,
        onNavigateToVault: navigateToVault,
        onNavigateToLogin: () => {
          onClose?.();
          if (vm.navTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(vm.navTimeoutRef.current);
          vm.navTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => vm.router.push('/login'), 120);
        },
      });
    } finally {
      bookingLockRef.current = false;
      setIsOfficialBookingBusy(false);
    }
  }, [isBookingBusy, vm, language, onRefreshEvents, refreshProfile, navigateToVault, onClose]);

  const handleConfirmBookingUnified = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isBookingBusy || bookingLockRef.current) return;
    if (!hasActivePass && vm.bookingLockState?.isLocked) {
      vm.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(false);
      vm.triggerAlert(vm.tx('SESIÓN NO DISPONIBLE', 'SESSION UNAVAILABLE'), vm.bookingLockState.label, 'warning');
      return;
    }
    if (vm.isStrictB2B || vm.isTerminalOperational) {
      vm.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(false);
      vm.triggerAlert(vm.tx('SOLO CUENTAS DE ATLETA', 'ATHLETE ACCOUNTS ONLY'), vm.tx('Las cuentas operativas B2B no pueden reservar como atleta.', 'B2B operator accounts cannot book as an athlete.'), 'error');
      return;
    }
    if (!vm.isAdmin && vm.effectiveEscrowKc > 0 && vm.availableKc < vm.effectiveEscrowKc) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      vm.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(false);
      vm.setInsufficientKcPrompt({
        missingKc: Math.max(1, vm.effectiveEscrowKc - vm.availableKc),
        availableKc: vm.availableKc,
        requiredEscrow: vm.effectiveEscrowKc,
      });
      return;
    }
    if (onReserve) {
      bookingLockRef.current = true;
      setIsOfficialBookingBusy(true);
      try {
        await vm.handleConfirmWithAntiCollision();
      } finally {
        bookingLockRef.current = false;
        setIsOfficialBookingBusy(false);
      }
      return;
    }
    await handleConfirmOfficialBooking();
  }, [isBookingBusy, vm, onReserve, handleConfirmOfficialBooking]);

  const handleDismissModalOverlay = useCallback((allowEditCancel: boolean) => {
    if (vm.alertConfig?.visible) vm.setAlertConfig({ ...vm.alertConfig, visible: false });
    else if (vm.isStoryOpen) vm.setIsStoryOpen(false);
    else if (vm.isMembershipModalOpen) vm.setIsMembershipModalOpen(false);
    else if (vm.isPhotoLightboxOpen) vm.setIsPhotoLightboxOpen(false);
    else if (vm.isStudioMapOpen) vm.setIsStudioMapOpen(false);
    else if (isSplitInviteOpen) setIsSplitInviteOpen(false);
    else if (vm.activeSheet) vm.setActiveSheet(null);
    else if (allowEditCancel && vm.isEditMode && !isCreateMode && !vm.isSaving) vm.cancelEditMode();
    else if (!vm.isSaving) onClose?.();
  }, [vm, isSplitInviteOpen, isCreateMode, onClose]);

  const isEditorValid = isEventEditorFormValid({
    titleVal: vm.titleVal, discVal: vm.discVal, dateVal: vm.dateVal,
    timeVal: vm.timeVal, priceVal: vm.priceVal, cupoVal: vm.cupoVal,
  });

  const modeBadgeLabel = vm.isSaving
    ? vm.tx('PUBLICANDO...', 'PUBLISHING...')
    : effectiveCreateMode
      ? vm.tx('CREACIÓN', 'CREATE MODE')
      : vm.tx('EDICIÓN', 'EDIT MODE');

  return (
    <>
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={vm.isOpen}
        onBackButtonPress={() => handleDismissModalOverlay(true)}
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        backdropColor="#050505"
        backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.58 : 0.92}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
        backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        animationInTiming={200}
        animationOutTiming={180}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        onBackdropPress={() => handleDismissModalOverlay(false)}
        avoidKeyboard={true}
      >
        <KeyboardAvoidingView
          behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
          style={{ width: '100%', flex: 1, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        >
          <View
            style={[
              globalStyles.bottomSheet,
              KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
              {
                backgroundColor: vm.themeColors.background,
                paddingHorizontal: 0,
                paddingTop: 6,
                paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 10 : 14,
                overflow: 'hidden',
              },
              webNoSelect,
            ]}
          >
            <View
              style={{
                backgroundColor: vm.themeColors.background,
                overflow: 'hidden',
                zIndex: 25,
                borderBottomWidth: modalScrollY > 4 ? 1 : 0,
                borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.16)',
              }}
            >
              {!hasChildOverlayOpen && <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} style={{ marginBottom: 6 }} />}
              {vm.isEditMode && (
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingBottom: 8 }}>
                  <View style={ed.editBadge} {...onMediaAttr}>
                    <Feather name="edit-2" size={11} color={COLORS.cyan} />
                    <Text selectable={false} style={ed.editBadgeTxt}>{modeBadgeLabel}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={ed.viewTopActions}>
                    {!effectiveCreateMode && !vm.isSaving && (
                      <TouchableOpacity style={ed.iconRoundBtn} onPress={vm.cancelEditMode} {...onMediaAttr}>
                        <Feather name="x" size={16} color="#FFFFFF" />
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={[ed.checkCircleBtn, { backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan }, !isEditorValid && !vm.isSaving && { opacity: 0.38 }]}
                      onPress={vm.saveExperience}
                      disabled={vm.isSaving}
                    >
                      {vm.isSaving ? <KineticLoader size={16} color="#050505" /> : <Feather name="check" size={18} color="#050505" />}
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  </View>
                </View>
              )}
            </View>

            <ScrollView
              ref={vm.scrollRef}
              style={{ flex: 1 }}
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={true}
              nestedScrollEnabled={true}
              bounces={true}
              keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
              scrollEventThrottle={16}
              onScroll={(e) => setModalScrollY(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y)}
              contentContainerStyle={{
                paddingHorizontal: 0,
                paddingTop: 0,
                paddingBottom: vm.isEditMode ? 48 : Math.max(insets.bottom + 148, 168),
              }}
            >
              <View style={[ed.heroBox, { height: undefined, width: '100%', aspectRatio: 16 / 9 }, webNoSelect]} {...coverSwipeHandlers}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={ed.heroAbsoluteFill}
                  activeOpacity={0.95}
                  onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); vm.setIsPhotoLightboxOpen(true); }}
                >
                  <SmartImage source={{ uri: vm.coverUri }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" contentPosition="center" fallbackCategory={vm.discVal} />
                  <View style={[ed.heroDim, isLight && { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)' }]} />
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <View style={[ed.heroTopBar, { justifyContent: 'flex-end' }]} pointerEvents="box-none">
                  {!vm.isEditMode && (
                    <View style={ed.viewTopActions}>
                      <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.iconRoundBtn, vm.isFavEvent && { borderColor: COLORS.cyan, borderBottomColor: COLORS.cyan }]} onPress={vm.handleToggleFavorite} {...onMediaAttr}>
                        <Ionicons name={vm.isFavEvent ? 'flash' : 'flash-outline'} size={15} color={vm.isFavEvent ? COLORS.cyan : '#FFFFFF'} />
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                      <TouchableOpacity style={ed.iconRoundBtn} onPress={handleCleanShare} {...onMediaAttr}>
                        <Feather name="share-2" size={15} color="#FFFFFF" />
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                      {vm.canManage && (
                        <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.iconRoundBtn, { borderColor: COLORS.cyan }]} onPress={() => vm.setIsEditMode(true)} {...onMediaAttr}>
                          <Feather name="edit-2" size={14} color={COLORS.cyan} />
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                      )}
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>

                <View style={ed.heroBottomRow} pointerEvents="box-none">
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
                    <TouchableOpacity style={ed.clubLogoBox} onPress={() => vm.setIsStoryOpen(true)}>
                      {vm.clubLogo ? (
                        <SmartImage source={{ uri: vm.clubLogo }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" fallbackCategory={vm.discVal} />
                      ) : (
                        <Image source={KINETIC_LOGO} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
                      )}
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                    <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.discBadge, { backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan }]} onPress={() => vm.isEditMode && vm.openInternalSheet('disciplina')}>
                      <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.discBadgeTxt, { color: '#050505' }]}>{vm.tDisc(vm.discVal || 'FUERZA').toUpperCase()}</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  </View>
                  <TouchableOpacity style={ed.starBadge} onPress={() => !vm.isEditMode && vm.openInternalSheet('auditoria_rating')} {...onMediaAttr}>
                    <Feather name="star" size={11} color={COLORS.yellow} />
                    <Text selectable={false} style={ed.starBadgeTxt}>{Number(vm.currentEv?.rating || 5.0).toFixed(1)}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: 18, paddingTop: 14 }}>
                {vm.isEditMode ? (
                  <EventEditorB2BView
                    titleVal={vm.titleVal} setTitleVal={vm.setTitleVal} discVal={vm.discVal} freqVal={vm.freqVal}
                    dateVal={vm.dateVal} timeVal={vm.timeVal} durVal={vm.durVal} coachVal={vm.coachVal} setCoachVal={vm.setCoachVal}
                    locVal={vm.locVal} handleLocInput={vm.handleLocInput} locCoords={vm.locCoords} detectedZone={vm.detectedZone}
                    cupoVal={vm.cupoVal} reqsVal={vm.reqsVal} ageVal={vm.ageVal} priceVal={vm.priceVal} escrow40={vm.escrow40} puerta60={vm.puerta60}
                    descVal={vm.descVal} setDescVal={vm.setDescVal} attrInt={vm.attrInt} attrEnf={vm.attrEnf} attrImp={vm.attrImp} attrNiv={vm.attrNiv} attrEnt={vm.attrEnt}
                    galleryUris={vm.galleryUris} activePhotoIdx={vm.activePhotoIdx} setActivePhotoIdx={vm.setActivePhotoIdx}
                    standardDisciplineImage={vm.standardDisciplineImage}
                    customCoverUri={vm.customCoverUri} pickCoverImage={vm.pickCoverImage} removeCustomCover={vm.removeCustomCover}
                    pickGalleryImages={vm.pickGalleryImages} removeGalleryPhotoAt={vm.removeGalleryPhotoAt}
                    openInternalSheet={vm.openInternalSheet} isSaving={vm.isSaving} saveExperience={vm.saveExperience}
                    isCreateMode={isCreateMode} currentEvId={vm.currentEv?.id != null ? String(vm.currentEv.id) : undefined} onClose={onClose} setIsEditMode={vm.setIsEditMode}
                    cancelEditMode={vm.cancelEditMode} triggerAlert={vm.triggerAlert} onRefreshEvents={onRefreshEvents}
                    studioLayout={vm.studioLayout} onOpenStudioConfig={openB2BStudioConfig}
                    permiteDividirCuenta={vm.splitEscrowEnabled} setPermiteDividirCuenta={vm.setSplitEscrowEnabled}
                    maxDivisionPersonas={vm.splitMaxPersonas} setMaxDivisionPersonas={vm.setSplitMaxPersonas}
                  />
                ) : (
                  <EventDetailsAthleteBody
                    titleVal={vm.titleVal} clubName={vm.clubName} discVal={vm.discVal} allDisplayPhotos={vm.allDisplayPhotos}
                    activePhotoIdx={vm.activePhotoIdx} setActivePhotoIdx={vm.setActivePhotoIdx} reqsVal={vm.reqsVal}
                    durVal={vm.durVal} coachVal={vm.coachVal} readableDate={vm.readableDate} locVal={vm.locVal}
                    openMapsNavigation={vm.openMapsNavigation} confirmedCount={vm.confirmedCount} maxCupo={vm.maxCupo} freeSpots={vm.freeSpots}
                    ageVal={vm.ageVal} totalKc={vm.totalKc} basePriceKc={vm.basePriceKc} hasFlashDiscount={vm.hasFlashDiscount} flashBadgeText={vm.flashBadgeText}
                    escrow40={vm.escrow40} effectiveEscrowKc={vm.effectiveEscrowKc} effectiveSplitCount={vm.effectiveSplitCount} isAdmin={vm.isAdmin} descVal={vm.descVal}
                    attrNiv={vm.attrNiv} attrEnf={vm.attrEnf} attrEnt={vm.attrEnt} attrImp={vm.attrImp} attrInt={vm.attrInt}
                    openInternalSheet={vm.openInternalSheet} onOpenStudioMap={openAthleteStudioMap}
                    selectedSpot={vm.selectedSpot} studioLayout={vm.studioLayout}
                  />
                )}
              </View>
            </ScrollView>

            <EventDetailsBookingFooter
              ed={ed} themeColors={vm.themeColors} isLight={isLight} tx={vm.tx}
              isEditMode={vm.isEditMode} isSaving={vm.isSaving}
              isPreBookingConfirmOpen={vm.isPreBookingConfirmOpen} setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen={vm.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen}
              isBookingBusy={isBookingBusy} hasActivePass={hasActivePass} isSoldOutLocked={isSoldOutLocked}
              bookingLockLabel={!hasActivePass && vm.bookingLockState?.isLocked ? vm.bookingLockState.label : ''}
              isAdmin={vm.isAdmin} isStrictB2B={vm.isStrictB2B} isTerminalOperational={vm.isTerminalOperational} canManage={vm.canManage}
              onEnterEditMode={() => vm.setIsEditMode(true)}
              onOpenStudioAdmin={() => { vm.setStudioInitialConfigMode(vm.canManage); vm.setIsStudioMapOpen(true); }}
              titleVal={vm.titleVal} clubName={vm.clubName} escrow40={vm.escrow40} puerta60={vm.puerta60}
              effectiveEscrowKc={vm.effectiveEscrowKc} effectiveDoorKc={vm.effectiveDoorKc} effectiveSplitCount={vm.effectiveSplitCount}
              selectedSplitGuests={vm.pendingSplitConfig?.selectedGuests || []} onResetIndividualSplit={vm.resetPreBookingSplit}
              totalKc={vm.totalKc} basePriceKc={vm.basePriceKc} hasFlashDiscount={vm.hasFlashDiscount} flashBadgeText={vm.flashBadgeText}
              selectedSpot={vm.selectedSpot} equipmentType={vm.studioLayout?.equipmentType || null} maxCupo={vm.maxCupo} onOpenStudioMap={openAthleteStudioMap}
              splitEscrowEnabled={vm.splitEscrowEnabled} splitMaxPersonas={vm.splitMaxPersonas} eventId={vm.currentEv?.id != null ? String(vm.currentEv.id) : undefined}
              discVal={vm.discVal} locVal={vm.locVal} coachVal={vm.coachVal} durVal={vm.durVal} timeVal={vm.timeVal} localBookingDateIso={vm.localBookingDateIso} readableDate={vm.readableDate}
              insufficientKcPrompt={vm.insufficientKcPrompt} onDismissInsufficientKc={() => vm.setInsufficientKcPrompt(null)}
              onOpenTopUpModal={() => vm.setIsMembershipModalOpen(true)} onOpenSplitInvite={() => setIsSplitInviteOpen(true)}
              onOpenVault={navigateToVault} onClose={onClose || (() => {})} coverSwipeHandlers={coverSwipeHandlers}
              onConfirmBooking={handleConfirmBookingUnified}
            />

            <EventInternalSheetModal
              activeSheet={vm.activeSheet} setActiveSheet={vm.setActiveSheet}
              confirmedCount={vm.confirmedCount} maxCupo={vm.maxCupo} freeSpots={vm.freeSpots}
              totalKc={vm.totalKc} escrow40={vm.escrow40} puerta60={vm.puerta60}
              ratingValue={vm.currentEv?.rating} calMonth={vm.calMonth} changeCalMonth={vm.changeCalMonth}
              minTomorrowIso={vm.minTomorrowIso} maxFutureIso={vm.maxFutureIso}
              dateVal={vm.dateVal} setDateVal={vm.setDateVal} reqsVal={vm.reqsVal} setReqsVal={vm.setReqsVal}
              selectOptionValue={vm.selectOptionValue} onOpenStudioMap={openAthleteStudioMap}
              splitEscrowEnabled={vm.splitEscrowEnabled} splitMaxPersonas={vm.splitMaxPersonas}
            />
          </View>
          <EventDetailsOverlayModals
            vm={vm}
            isSplitInviteOpen={isSplitInviteOpen}
            setIsSplitInviteOpen={setIsSplitInviteOpen}
            hasActivePass={hasActivePass}
          />
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </ReactNativeModal>
      {!vm.isOpen && <TacticalAlertModal alertConfig={vm.alertConfig} setAlertConfig={vm.setAlertConfig} triggerHaptic={triggerHaptic} />}
    </>
  );
};

export default EventDetailsModal;

    FILE: src/components/EventDetailsSubModals.styles.ts

    flexDirection: 'row',
    height: 50,
    borderRadius: 14,
    backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan,
    borderWidth: 1.2,
    borderColor: '#5CEEFF',
    borderTopWidth: 1.8,
    borderTopColor: '#D6FFFF',
    borderBottomWidth: 4.5,
