    FILE: src/components/EventDetailsSubModals.tsx

    // === src/components/EventDetailsSubModals.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { isWellnessCategory, SORTED_CATEGORIES } from '../constants/exploreData';
import {
  KINETIC_20_ENVIRONMENTS,
  KINETIC_7_DURATIONS,
  KINETIC_AGE_POLICIES,
} from '../constants/kineticEventCatalogs';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { toMondayFirstDayIndex } from '../services/eventosScreenHelpers';
import { COLORS } from '../styles/globalStyles';
import {
  ActiveSheetType,
  DAYS_HEADER,
  DAYS_HEADER_EN,
  formatIsoLocal,
  getThreeMonthsIsoStr,
  getTomorrowIsoStr,
  MONTHS_HEADER,
  MONTHS_HEADER_EN,
  normalizeCityLabel,
  OPTIONS_CATALOG,
  translateEventCatalogTerm,
} from '../utils/eventEditorHelpers';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { setKineticInlineOverlayOpen } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { translateDiscipline } from '../utils/kineticSmartTranslator';
import { baseEsStyles } from './EventDetailsSubModals.styles';
import { triggerHaptic } from './SharedUI';
import {
  EventFullscreenGalleryModal,
  EventUnifiedRatingAuditContent,
  EventUnifiedRatingAuditModal,
  openGlobalEventRatingAudit,
  registerGlobalEventRatingAuditOpener,
} from './eventos/EventGalleryAndRatingModals';
import { EventSubSheetCatalogBodyLeaf } from './eventos/EventSubSheetCatalogBodyLeaf';
import { KineticSwipeableSubSheet, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from './shared/KineticModalHeader3D';

export {
  DAYS_HEADER, DAYS_HEADER_EN, EventFullscreenGalleryModal, EventUnifiedRatingAuditContent,
  EventUnifiedRatingAuditModal, MONTHS_HEADER, MONTHS_HEADER_EN, OPTIONS_CATALOG,
  formatIsoLocal, getThreeMonthsIsoStr, getTomorrowIsoStr, normalizeCityLabel,
  openGlobalEventRatingAudit, registerGlobalEventRatingAuditOpener, translateEventCatalogTerm,
};
export type { ActiveSheetType };

const stripAccents = (s: string) => s.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase();

export interface EventInternalSheetModalProps {
  activeSheet: ActiveSheetType;
  setActiveSheet: (s: ActiveSheetType) => void;
  confirmedCount: number;
  maxCupo: number;
  freeSpots: number;
  totalKc?: number;
  escrow40: number;
  puerta60: number;
  ratingValue?: number | null;
  calMonth: Date;
  changeCalMonth: (delta: number) => void;
  minTomorrowIso: string;
  maxFutureIso: string;
  dateVal: string;
  setDateVal: (iso: string) => void;
  reqsVal: string[];
  setReqsVal: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string[]>>;
  selectOptionValue: (sheet: ActiveSheetType, val: string) => void;
  onOpenStudioMap?: () => void;
  splitEscrowEnabled?: boolean;
  splitMaxPersonas?: number;
}

export const EventInternalSheetModal = memo(
  ({
    activeSheet,
    setActiveSheet,
    confirmedCount,
    maxCupo,
    freeSpots,
    escrow40,
    puerta60,
    ratingValue,
    calMonth,
    changeCalMonth,
    minTomorrowIso,
    maxFutureIso,
    dateVal,
    setDateVal,
    reqsVal,
    setReqsVal,
    selectOptionValue,
    onOpenStudioMap,
    splitEscrowEnabled = false,
    splitMaxPersonas = 4,
  }: EventInternalSheetModalProps) => {
    const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const isEn = language === 'en';
    const esStyles = useMemo(
      () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseEsStyles, resolvedTheme, ['reqBtn3D', 'chkBoxOn', 'primaryCyanBtn', 'primaryCyanTxt']),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );

    const [discSearch, setDiscSearch] = useState('');
    const [discFilter, setDiscFilter] = useState<'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness'>('all');
    const [discSort, setDiscSort] = useState<'az' | 'za' | 'wellness_first'>('az');
    const [splitEscrowPeople, setSplitEscrowPeople] = useState<number>(1);
    const calTouchXRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!activeSheet) {
        setDiscSearch('');
        setDiscFilter('all');
        setDiscSort('az');
        return;
      }
      setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(true);
      return () => setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(false);
    }, [activeSheet]);

    const calendarDays = useMemo(() => {
      const y = calMonth.getFullYear();
      const m = calMonth.getMonth();
      const firstW = toMondayFirstDayIndex(new Date(y, m, 1).getDay());
      const totalD = new Date(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();
      const arr: Array<{ key: string; iso?: string; day?: number; blocked?: boolean }> = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < firstW; i++) arr.push({ key: `b_${i}` });
      for (let day = 1; day <= totalD; day++) {
        const iso = formatIsoLocal(new Date(y, m, day));
        const blocked = iso < minTomorrowIso || iso > maxFutureIso;
        arr.push({ key: iso, iso, day, blocked });
      }
      return arr;
    }, [calMonth, minTomorrowIso, maxFutureIso]);

    const minCalMonth = useMemo(() => {
      const n = new Date();
      return new Date(n.getFullYear(), n.getMonth(), 1);
    }, []);
    const maxCalMonth = useMemo(() => new Date(minCalMonth.getFullYear(), minCalMonth.getMonth() + 3, 1), [minCalMonth]);
    const isAtMinCalMonth =
      calMonth.getFullYear() < minCalMonth.getFullYear() ||
      (calMonth.getFullYear() === minCalMonth.getFullYear() && calMonth.getMonth() <= minCalMonth.getMonth());
    const isAtMaxCalMonth =
      calMonth.getFullYear() > maxCalMonth.getFullYear() ||
      (calMonth.getFullYear() === maxCalMonth.getFullYear() && calMonth.getMonth() >= maxCalMonth.getMonth());

    const discCounts = useMemo(() => {
      let well = 0;
      SORTED_CATEGORIES.forEach((c) => { if (isWellnessCategory(c)) well += 1; });
      return { all: SORTED_CATEGORIES.length, fitness: SORTED_CATEGORIES.length - well, wellness: well };
    }, []);

    const filteredDisciplines = useMemo(() => {
      if (activeSheet !== 'disciplina') return [];
      const q = stripAccents(discSearch.trim());
      const base = SORTED_CATEGORIES.filter((cat) => {
        const well = isWellnessCategory(cat);
        if (discFilter === 'fitness' && well) return false;
        if (discFilter === 'wellness' && !well) return false;
        if (!q) return true;
        const disp = translateDiscipline(cat, language);
        return stripAccents(`${cat} ${disp}`).includes(q);
      });
      return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
        const la = translateDiscipline(a, language);
        const lb = translateDiscipline(b, language);
        if (discSort === 'za') return lb.localeCompare(la, isEn ? 'en' : 'es');
        if (discSort === 'wellness_first') {
          const wa = isWellnessCategory(a) ? 0 : 1;
          const wb = isWellnessCategory(b) ? 0 : 1;
          if (wa !== wb) return wa - wb;
        }
        return la.localeCompare(lb, isEn ? 'en' : 'es');
      });
    }, [activeSheet, discSearch, discFilter, discSort, language, isEn]);

    if (!activeSheet) return null;

    const closeSheet = () => setActiveSheet(null);
    const cleanEscrow40 = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(escrow40) || 0));
    const cleanPuerta60 = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(puerta60) || 0));
    const cleanTotal100 = cleanEscrow40 + cleanPuerta60;
    const monthsArr = isEn ? MONTHS_HEADER_EN : MONTHS_HEADER;
    const daysArr = isEn ? DAYS_HEADER_EN : DAYS_HEADER;

    const sheetTitleText =
      activeSheet === 'hora' ? tx('Seleccionar Horario (06:00 a 22:00)', 'Select Schedule (06:00 to 22:00)')
      : activeSheet === 'cupo' ? tx('Aforo Máximo (1 a 50 Atletas)', 'Max Capacity (1 to 50 Athletes)')
      : activeSheet === 'precio' ? tx('Inversión (0 a 3,000 KC)', 'Investment (0 to 3,000 KC)')
      : activeSheet === 'requisitos' ? tx('Qué Llevar / Requisitos (16 Opciones)', 'What to Bring / Requirements (16 Options)')
      : activeSheet === 'disciplina' ? tx(`Seleccionar Disciplina (${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} Categorías)`, `Select Discipline (${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} Categories)`)
      : activeSheet === 'frecuencia' ? tx('Seleccionar Frecuencia', 'Select Frequency')
      : activeSheet === 'duracion' ? tx('Seleccionar Duración', 'Select Duration')
      : activeSheet === 'edad' ? tx('Política de Edad', 'Age Policy')
      : activeSheet === 'intensidad' ? tx('Seleccionar Intensidad', 'Select Intensity')
      : activeSheet === 'enfoque' ? tx('Seleccionar Enfoque', 'Select Focus')
      : activeSheet === 'impacto' ? tx('Impacto Articular', 'Joint Impact')
      : activeSheet === 'nivel' ? tx('Nivel Recomendado', 'Recommended Level')
      : activeSheet === 'entorno' ? tx('Entorno de Entrenamiento (20 Entornos)', 'Training Environment (20 Environments)')
      : tx('Seleccionar Opción', 'Select Option');

    const webSheetAttrs = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } : undefined;
    const isCompactSheet = ['frecuencia', 'duracion', 'edad', 'intensidad', 'enfoque', 'impacto', 'nivel', 'lista_confirmados', 'desglose_escrow', 'fecha'].includes(String(activeSheet));

    const resolvedCatalogOptions =
      activeSheet === 'duracion'
        ? KINETIC_7_DURATIONS.map((d) => ({ value: String(d.val), label: isEn ? d.labelEn : d.label }))
        : activeSheet === 'edad'
          ? KINETIC_AGE_POLICIES.map((a) => ({ value: a.es, label: isEn ? a.en : a.es }))
          : activeSheet === 'entorno'
            ? KINETIC_20_ENVIRONMENTS.map((e) => ({ value: e.es, label: isEn ? e.en : e.es }))
            : activeSheet === 'disciplina'
              ? filteredDisciplines.map((c) => ({ value: c, label: translateDiscipline(c, language) }))
              : (OPTIONS_CATALOG[activeSheet] || []).map((o) => ({
                  value: o.value,
                  label: isEn && o.labelEn ? o.labelEn : translateEventCatalogTerm(o.label, language),
                }));

    return (
      <KineticSwipeableSubSheet
        visible={Boolean(activeSheet)}
        onClose={closeSheet}
        backdropStyle={[esStyles.sheetBackdrop, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC' }]}
        sheetStyle={[
          esStyles.sheetBox,
          activeSheet === 'auditoria_rating' && { height: '82%', maxHeight: '84%' },
          isCompactSheet && { height: 'auto', maxHeight: '88%', borderTopLeftRadius: 24, borderTopRightRadius: 24 },
          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
        ]}
        webAttrs={webSheetAttrs}
      >
          <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={closeSheet} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }} />

          {activeSheet === 'lista_confirmados' ? (
            <View style={isCompactSheet ? { flexShrink: 1 } : { flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[esStyles.sheetTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                {tx('Aforo y Privacidad KINETIC', 'KINETIC Capacity & Privacy')}
              </Text>
              <View style={[esStyles.breakdownBox, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]}>
                <Text style={[esStyles.infoVal, { textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 16, color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                  🔒 {confirmedCount}/{maxCupo} {tx('Confirmados', 'Confirmed')} • {freeSpots} {tx('libres', 'open')}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[esStyles.descTxt, { textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 8, color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
                  {tx(
                    'Por protocolo de privacidad KINETIC, la identidad de los atletas asistentes es 100% anónima y confidencial.',
                    'Under KINETIC privacy protocol, attending athletes’ identities remain 100% anonymous and confidential.'
                  )}
                </Text>
                {onOpenStudioMap && (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[esStyles.primaryCyanBtn, { marginTop: 12, height: 44 }]}
                    onPress={() => { closeSheet(); onOpenStudioMap(); }}
                  >
                    <Text style={esStyles.primaryCyanTxt}>{tx('ABRIR MAPA 3D DE LUGARES', 'OPEN 3D STUDIO MAP')}</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : activeSheet === 'desglose_escrow' ? (
            <View style={{ flexShrink: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[esStyles.sheetTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                {tx('Desglose de Apartado 40% Hoy • 60% al Llegar', '40% Deposit Today • 60% on Arrival Breakdown')}
              </Text>
              <View style={[esStyles.breakdownBox, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]}>
                <View style={esStyles.breakdownLine}>
                  <Text style={[esStyles.descTxt, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
                    {tx('Valor Total de la Sesión (100%):', 'Total Session Value (100%):')}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[esStyles.infoVal, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>{cleanTotal100} KC</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginVertical: 4 }}>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 54, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', borderRadius: 10, padding: 8, backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)', elevation: 0 }}>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textMuted, textAlign: 'center' }} numberOfLines={2}>
                      {tx('Anticipo Protegido (40%)', 'Protected Deposit (40%)')}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.purpleText, marginTop: 3, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }}>
                      {cleanEscrow40} KC
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 54, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', borderRadius: 10, padding: 8, backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)', elevation: 0 }}>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.textMuted, textAlign: 'center' }} numberOfLines={2}>
                      {tx('Liquidación en Recepción (60%)', 'Front Desk Settlement (60%)')}
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 13.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.cyanText, marginTop: 3, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }}>
                      {cleanPuerta60} KC
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
                {splitEscrowEnabled && (
                  <>
                    <View style={[esStyles.divider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.textMuted, marginBottom: 6, letterSpacing: 0.6 }}>
                      {tx('DIVISIÓN PROPORCIONAL EQUIPO (40%):', 'PROPORTIONAL TEAM SPLIT (40%):')}
                    </Text>
                    <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, marginBottom: 8 }}>
                      {[1, 2, 3, 4].filter((cnt) => cnt <= Math.max(2, Math.min(4, Number(splitMaxPersonas) || 4))).map((cnt) => {
                        const sel = splitEscrowPeople === cnt;
                        return (
                          <TouchableOpacity
                            key={cnt}
                            onPress={() => setSplitEscrowPeople(cnt)}
                            style={[
                              { flex: 1, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 9, alignItems: 'center', borderWidth: 1.2, elevation: 0 },
                              sel
                                ? { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.15)', borderColor: COLORS.cyan }
                                : { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F1F5F9' : '#0B1320', borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#1E293B' },
                            ]}
                          >
                            <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: sel ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted }}>
                              {cnt === 1 ? tx('1 Solo', '1 Solo') : `${cnt} ${tx('Personas', 'People')}`}
                            </Text>
                            <Text style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: sel ? themeColors.textPrimary : themeColors.textSecondary, marginTop: 1 }}>
                              {Math.ceil(cleanEscrow40 / cnt)} KC
                            </Text>
                          </TouchableOpacity>
                        );
                      })}
                    </View>
                  </>
                )}
                <View style={[esStyles.divider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />
                <Text style={[esStyles.infoLbl, { color: themeColors.green, lineHeight: 16 }]}>
                  {tx(
                    `✓ PROTECCIÓN AL ATLETA: Si cancelas dentro de la ventana permitida, tu Anticipo Protegido del 40% (${cleanEscrow40} KC) se reembolsa íntegro a tu Bóveda.`,
                    `✓ ATHLETE PROTECTION: If you cancel within the allowed window, your 40% Protected Deposit (${cleanEscrow40} KC) is refunded in full to your Vault.`
                  )}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : activeSheet === 'auditoria_rating' ? (
            <EventUnifiedRatingAuditContent ratingValue={ratingValue ?? undefined} />
          ) : activeSheet === 'fecha' ? (
            <View style={{ flexShrink: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[esStyles.sheetTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                {tx('Seleccionar Fecha (Mañana a +3 Meses)', 'Select Date (Tomorrow to +3 Months)')}
              </Text>
              <View style={esStyles.calNav}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  disabled={isAtMinCalMonth}
                  style={[esStyles.calBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }, isAtMinCalMonth && { opacity: 0.28 }]}
                  onPress={() => changeCalMonth(-1)}
                >
                  <Feather name="chevron-left" size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <Text style={[esStyles.calTitle, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                  {monthsArr[calMonth.getMonth()]} {calMonth.getFullYear()}
                </Text>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  disabled={isAtMaxCalMonth}
                  style={[esStyles.calBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }, isAtMaxCalMonth && { opacity: 0.28 }]}
                  onPress={() => changeCalMonth(1)}
                >
                  <Feather name="chevron-right" size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
              <View
                onTouchStart={(e) => { calTouchXRef.current = e.nativeEvent.pageX; }}
                onTouchEnd={(e) => {
                  if (calTouchXRef.current === null) return;
                  const dx = e.nativeEvent.pageX - calTouchXRef.current;
                  calTouchXRef.current = null;
                  if (Math.abs(dx) > 42) changeCalMonth(dx < 0 ? 1 : -1);
                }}
              >
                <View style={esStyles.calWeek}>
                  {daysArr.map((w, i) => (
                    <Text key={i} style={[esStyles.calWeekTxt, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{w}</Text>
                  ))}
                </View>
                <View style={esStyles.calGrid}>
                  {calendarDays.map((c) => {
                    if (!c.iso) return <View key={c.key} style={esStyles.calCellWrap} />;
                    const pickedIso = c.iso;
                    const isSel = pickedIso === dateVal;
                    return (
                      <View key={c.key} style={esStyles.calCellWrap}>
                        <TouchableOpacity
                          disabled={c.blocked}
                          style={[
                            esStyles.calCell,
                            isLight && !isSel && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
                            isSel && esStyles.calCellSel,
                            c.blocked && esStyles.calCellBlock,
                          ]}
                          onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setDateVal(pickedIso); closeSheet(); }}
                        >
                          <Text style={[esStyles.calDayTxt, { color: isSel ? '#050505' : themeColors.textPrimary }, isSel && { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                            {c.day}
                          </Text>
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                      </View>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : (
            <EventSubSheetCatalogBodyLeaf
              activeSheet={String(activeSheet)}
              isCompactSheet={isCompactSheet}
              isLight={isLight}
              isEn={isEn}
              sheetTitleText={sheetTitleText}
              discSearch={discSearch}
              setDiscSearch={setDiscSearch}
              discFilter={discFilter}
              setDiscFilter={setDiscFilter}
              discSort={discSort}
              setDiscSort={setDiscSort}
              discCounts={discCounts}
              reqsVal={reqsVal}
              setReqsVal={setReqsVal}
              resolvedCatalogOptions={resolvedCatalogOptions}
              selectOptionValue={(sheetKey, val) => selectOptionValue(sheetKey as ActiveSheetType, val)}
              esStyles={esStyles as unknown as Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>}
              baseEsStyles={baseEsStyles as unknown as Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>}
              themeColors={themeColors}
              tx={tx}
            />
          )}
          <TouchableOpacity style={esStyles.primaryCyanBtn} onPress={closeSheet}>
            <Text style={esStyles.primaryCyanTxt}>
              {activeSheet === 'requisitos'
                ? tx(`CONFIRMAR (${reqsVal.length} SELECCIONADOS)`, `CONFIRM (${reqsVal.length} SELECTED)`)
                : tx('ENTENDIDO / CERRAR', 'GOT IT / CLOSE')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
      </KineticSwipeableSubSheet>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/eventos/eventDetailsActions.ts

    // === src/components/eventos/eventDetailsActions.ts === //

import { injectConfirmedBovedaPass } from '../../services/kineticOptimisticStore';
import { isKineticMasterAdminEmail } from '../../services/kineticSessionPersistence';
import { reconcileRealSupabaseJwtSession } from '../../services/kineticSessionReconciler';
import { computeSplitEscrow1NMath, persistSelectedSpotSession } from '../../services/kineticSplitPreBookingStore';

import { buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel, formatDigitalPassAlertBody } from '../../utils/kineticDigitalPassViewModelLeaf';

import type { IStudioLayoutConfig } from './KineticStudioLayoutModal';
import { triggerArcadeHaptic, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { hydrateRemoteEventDetails, syncEventModalDateOccupancy, type EventAttrsRecord } from './eventDetailsHydrationHelper';

  buildCleanEventDeepLink, buildCleanGalleryUrls, buildCleanKineticEventSharePayload,

const UUID_V4_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const isUuid = (v: unknown): v is string => typeof v === 'string' && UUID_V4_RE.test(v.trim());

export interface IShareCleanEventParams {
  eventId?: unknown;
  titleVal: string;
  clubName: string;
  discVal?: string;
  locVal?: string;
  detectedZone?: string | null;
  localBookingDateIso: string;
  timeVal?: string;
  totalKc: number;
  escrow40: number;
  language: string;
  tDisc?: (d: string) => string;
}

export const shareCleanKineticEvent = async (params: IShareCleanEventParams): Promise<void> => {
  triggerHaptic('Light');
  const dateFormatted = formatBookingConfirmedDate(
    params.localBookingDateIso,
    params.timeVal || '08:00',
    params.language
  );
  const translatedDisc = params.discVal
    ? params.tDisc
      ? params.tDisc(params.discVal)
      : params.discVal
    : '';
  const payload = buildCleanKineticEventSharePayload({
    eventId: params.eventId,
    title: params.titleVal,
    clubName: params.clubName,
    discipline: translatedDisc,
    location: params.locVal,
    detectedZone: params.detectedZone,
    dateFormatted,
    totalKc: params.totalKc,
    escrow40: params.escrow40,
    language: params.language,
  });
  await shareTacticalMessageSafe(payload.title, payload.message, payload.url);
};

export interface IExecuteOfficialBookingParams {
  userId?: string | null;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  currentEv: EventAttrsRecord & { id?: string | number | null; servicio_id?: string | number | null; hora_inicio?: string | null; fecha?: string | null; cupo_maximo?: number | string | null; precio_kc?: number | string | null };
  titleVal: string;
  clubName: string;
  localBookingDateIso: string;
  timeVal?: string;
  totalKc: number;
  vipLevel?: string | null;
  kcMembresia?: number | null;
  availableKc?: number;
  splitCount?: number;
  splitGuests?: ISplitInviteAthlete[];
  onSplitDispatched?: () => void;
  onGhostPurged?: (msg: string) => void;
  studioLayout?: IStudioLayoutConfig | null;
  selectedSpot?: number | null;
  setSelectedSpot?: (s: number | null) => void;
  language: string;
  setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  onBookedSuccessState: () => void;
  onInsufficientKc?: (missingKc: number, availableKc: number, requiredEscrow: number) => void;
  onRefreshEvents?: () => void;
  refreshProfile?: () => Promise<void> | void;
  triggerAlert: (
    title: string,
    msg: string,
    type?: string,
    onConfirm?: (() => void) | null,
    confirmText?: string
  ) => void;
  onNavigateToVault: () => void;
  onNavigateToLogin: () => void;
}

const _inFlightBookingKeys = new Set<string>();

const handleRetiredGhostService = (
  serviceId: string,
  isEn: boolean,
  params: Pick<IExecuteOfficialBookingParams, 'onRefreshEvents' | 'onGhostPurged' | 'triggerAlert'>
) => {
  purgeGhostServiceEverywhere(serviceId);
  params.onRefreshEvents?.();
  const ghostMsg = isEn
    ? 'This experience is no longer available and has been removed from the schedule.'
    : 'Esta experiencia ya no está disponible y fue retirada de la cartelera.';
  if (params.onGhostPurged) params.onGhostPurged(ghostMsg);
  else params.triggerAlert(isEn ? 'EXPERIENCE RETIRED' : 'EXPERIENCIA RETIRADA', ghostMsg, 'warning');
};

export const executeOfficialEventBooking = async (
  params: IExecuteOfficialBookingParams
): Promise<boolean> => {
  const isEn = params.language === 'en';
  const cleanUserId = String(params.userId || '').trim();

  if (!cleanUserId) {
    params.triggerAlert(
      isEn ? 'SIGN IN REQUIRED' : 'SESIÓN REQUERIDA',
      isEn
        ? 'Please sign in to your Kinetic account to book this session.'
        : 'Inicia sesión en tu cuenta Kinetic para reservar esta sesión.',
      'warning',
      params.onNavigateToLogin,
      isEn ? 'SIGN IN' : 'INICIAR SESIÓN'
    );
    return false;
  }

  const rawServiceId = String(params.currentEv?.servicio_id || params.currentEv?.id || '').trim();
  const validServiceId = isUuid(rawServiceId) ? rawServiceId : null;
  const cleanDateIso = String(params.localBookingDateIso || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  const preLockKey = `${cleanUserId}::${validServiceId || rawServiceId || params.titleVal}::${cleanDateIso}`;
  if (_inFlightBookingKeys.has(preLockKey)) return false;
  _inFlightBookingKeys.add(preLockKey);

  try {
    const jwtCheck = await reconcileRealSupabaseJwtSession(supabase, 3500);
    if (jwtCheck.status === 'transient') {
      params.triggerAlert(
        isEn ? 'UNSTABLE CONNECTION' : 'CONEXIÓN INESTABLE',
        translateSupabaseBookingError('network timeout', params.language),
        'warning'
      );
      return false;
    }
    const authUid = String(jwtCheck.user?.id || '').trim();
    if (jwtCheck.status !== 'valid' || !authUid || authUid !== cleanUserId) {
      params.triggerAlert(
        isEn ? 'CLOUD SESSION EXPIRED' : 'SESIÓN EN NUBE EXPIRADA',
        translateSupabaseBookingError('jwt', params.language),
        'warning',
        params.onNavigateToLogin,
        isEn ? 'SIGN IN' : 'INICIAR SESIÓN'
      );
      return false;
    }

    if (!validServiceId) {
      params.triggerAlert(
        isEn ? 'BOOKING NOTICE' : 'AVISO DE RESERVA',
        translateSupabaseBookingError('servicio no disponible', params.language),
        'error'
      );
      return false;
    }

    if (bovedaService.hasActivePassForEventSync(authUid, params.currentEv, cleanDateIso)) {
      params.onBookedSuccessState();
      return false;
    }

    const [dupCheck, svcLookup] = await Promise.all([
      cleanDateIso
        ? supabase
            .from('reservas')
            .select('id')
            .eq('usuario_id', authUid)
            .eq('servicio_id', validServiceId)
            .eq('fecha_clase', cleanDateIso)
            .in('estado', ['pendiente', 'confirmada', 'activa'])
            .limit(1)
        : Promise.resolve({ data: null }),
      supabase.from('servicios').select('id, estado, activo, precio_kc, fecha, hora_inicio, es_venta_flash, atributos').eq('id', validServiceId).maybeSingle(),
    ]);

    if (Array.isArray(dupCheck.data) && dupCheck.data.length > 0) {
      bovedaService.registerOptimisticActivePass(authUid, {
        id: dupCheck.data[0].id,
        servicio_id: validServiceId,
        nombre_servicio: params.titleVal,
        fecha_clase: cleanDateIso || undefined,
      });
      params.onBookedSuccessState();
      return false;
    }

    if (params.selectedSpot && cleanDateIso) {
      try {
        const spotCheck = await verifyLiveSpotAvailability({
          serviceId: validServiceId,
          eventKey: validServiceId,
          eventTitle: params.titleVal,
          fechaClase: cleanDateIso,
          spotNumber: params.selectedSpot,
          userId: authUid,
          lang: params.language,
        });
        if (!spotCheck.available) {
          params.setSelectedSpot?.(null);
          persistSelectedSpotSession(validServiceId, null, params.titleVal, cleanDateIso);
          params.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'COLLISION_RESET' }) === 2);
          params.triggerAlert(
            isEn ? 'STATION TAKEN' : 'ESTACIÓN OCUPADA',
            spotCheck.message || getSpotCollisionAlertMessage(params.language),
            'warning'
          );
          return false;
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
    }

    if (svcLookup.error) {
      params.triggerAlert(
        isEn ? 'BOOKING NOTICE' : 'AVISO DE RESERVA',
        translateSupabaseBookingError(svcLookup.error, params.language),
        'error'
      );
      return false;
    }
    if (!svcLookup.data || isConfirmedRetiredServiceRecord(svcLookup.data)) {
      handleRetiredGhostService(validServiceId, isEn, params);
      return false;
    }

    const verifiedIsAdmin = Boolean(params.isAdmin && jwtCheck.user?.email_confirmed_at && isKineticMasterAdminEmail(jwtCheck.user?.email));
    const safeSplit = Math.min(4, Math.max(1, Number(params.splitCount) || 1));
    const srvData: EventAttrsRecord & { fecha?: string | null; hora_inicio?: string | null } = (svcLookup.data as Record<string, unknown> | null) || params.currentEv || {};
    const rawCanonical = svcLookup.data?.precio_kc != null ? Number(svcLookup.data.precio_kc) : params.totalKc;
    const fp = resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing({
      event: { ...srvData, precio_kc: rawCanonical, fecha: cleanDateIso || srvData.fecha, hora_inicio: params.timeVal || srvData.hora_inicio },
      vipLevel: params.vipLevel,
      kcMembresia: params.kcMembresia,
      isAdmin: verifiedIsAdmin,
      isEn,
    });
    const split = computeEscrow4060Split(verifiedIsAdmin ? 0 : fp.discountedTotalKc, verifiedIsAdmin);
    const effectivePriceKc = verifiedIsAdmin ? 0 : split.totalKc;
    const effectiveEscrowKc = computeSplitEscrow1NMath(effectivePriceKc, safeSplit, split.escrow40, verifiedIsAdmin).effectiveEscrowKc;

    if (!verifiedIsAdmin && effectiveEscrowKc > 0 && typeof params.availableKc === 'number' && params.availableKc < effectiveEscrowKc) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      params.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'INSUFFICIENT_KC' }) === 2);
      if (params.onInsufficientKc) {
        params.onInsufficientKc(Math.max(1, effectiveEscrowKc - params.availableKc), params.availableKc, effectiveEscrowKc);
      } else {
        params.triggerAlert(
          isEn ? 'INSUFFICIENT BALANCE' : 'SALDO INSUFICIENTE',
          translateSupabaseBookingError('saldo insuficiente', params.language),
          'warning'
        );
      }
      return false;
    }

    if (params.selectedSpot != null) {
      setSpotLock90s(String(validServiceId || params.titleVal || ''), params.selectedSpot);
    }

    const { data: rpcData, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('procesar_reserva_escrow', {
      p_usuario_id: authUid,
      p_negocio_nombre: params.clubName || 'Kinetic Oficial',
      p_servicio_nombre: params.titleVal || (isEn ? 'Kinetic Session' : 'Sesión Kinetic'),
      p_precio_kc: effectivePriceKc,
      p_servicio_id: validServiceId,
      p_fecha_clase: cleanDateIso || null,
      p_spot_number: params.selectedSpot ?? null,
      p_split_count: safeSplit,
      p_es_pase_maestro: verifiedIsAdmin,
    });

    if (rpcErr) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      const errRec = rpcErr as { message?: unknown; details?: unknown; code?: unknown };
      const errRaw = String(errRec?.message || errRec?.details || '');
      const errCode = String(errRec?.code || '').trim();
      if (
        errCode === '23505' ||
        /spot_ocupado|estaci[oó]n.*ocupada|idx_reservas_unique_active_spot|duplicate key/i.test(errRaw)
      ) {
        params.setSelectedSpot?.(null);
        persistSelectedSpotSession(validServiceId, null, params.titleVal, cleanDateIso);
        params.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'COLLISION_RESET' }) === 2);
        params.triggerAlert(
          isEn ? 'STATION TAKEN' : 'ESTACIÓN OCUPADA',
          getSpotCollisionAlertMessage(params.language),
          'warning'
        );
        return false;
      }
      if (!verifiedIsAdmin && params.onInsufficientKc && /saldo|fondos|insufficient|kineticoins/i.test(errRaw)) {
        const avail = Math.max(0, Number(params.availableKc) || 0);
        params.setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'INSUFFICIENT_KC' }) === 2);
        params.onInsufficientKc(Math.max(1, effectiveEscrowKc - avail), avail, effectiveEscrowKc);
        return false;
      }
      if (await verifyAndPurgeConfirmedGhostService(supabase, validServiceId)) {
        handleRetiredGhostService(validServiceId, isEn, params);
        return false;
      }
      params.triggerAlert(
        isEn ? 'BOOKING NOTICE' : 'AVISO DE RESERVA',
        translateSupabaseBookingError(rpcErr, params.language),
        'error'
      );
      return false;
    }

    const rpcDataRec = typeof rpcData === 'object' && rpcData !== null ? (rpcData as { reserva_id?: unknown; id?: unknown }) : null;
    const returnedId =
      typeof rpcData === 'string'
        ? rpcData
        : String(rpcDataRec?.reserva_id || rpcDataRec?.id || '').trim();

    persistSelectedSpotSession(validServiceId, null, params.titleVal, cleanDateIso);
    if (safeSplit > 1 && Array.isArray(params.splitGuests) && params.splitGuests.length > 0) {
      await createSplitEscrowInvites({
        reservaId: returnedId || undefined,
        eventId: validServiceId,
        title: params.titleVal,
        anfitrionId: authUid,
        invitados: params.splitGuests,
        montoKcParte: effectiveEscrowKc,
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
    params.onSplitDispatched?.();

    bovedaService.registerOptimisticActivePass(authUid, {
      id: returnedId || undefined,
      servicio_id: validServiceId,
      nombre_servicio: params.titleVal,
      servicio: params.titleVal,
      fecha_clase: cleanDateIso || undefined,
    });
    if (returnedId) {
      rememberCreatedReservaId(validServiceId, cleanDateIso, returnedId);
      shouldAutoSyncCalendarReminders(authUid)
        .then((autoOk) => {
          if (!autoOk) return;
          return syncReservationToNativeCalendar({
            silent: true,
            input: {
              reservaId: returnedId,
              title: params.titleVal || 'Sesión Kinetic',
              venueName: params.clubName || 'Kinetic Oficial',
              location: params.currentEv?.ubicacion ? String(params.currentEv.ubicacion) : null,
              fechaIso: cleanDateIso,
              horaInicio: params.timeVal || params.currentEv?.hora_inicio || '08:00',
              spotNumber: params.selectedSpot ?? null,
              escrowKc: effectiveEscrowKc,
              puertaKc: Math.max(0, effectivePriceKc - effectiveEscrowKc),
              status: 'CONFIRMED',
              language: isEn ? 'en' : 'es',
            },
          });
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
    await bovedaService.invalidateBovedaPassesCache(authUid).catch(() => {});
    const effectivePuertaKc = verifiedIsAdmin ? 0 : Math.max(0, effectivePriceKc - effectiveEscrowKc);
    injectConfirmedBovedaPass(authUid, {
      id: returnedId, servicioId: validServiceId, title: params.titleVal, venueName: params.clubName,
      fechaIso: cleanDateIso, horaInicio: params.timeVal || params.currentEv?.hora_inicio || '08:00',
      spotNumber: params.selectedSpot ?? null, totalKc: effectivePriceKc, escrowKc: effectiveEscrowKc,
      puertaKc: effectivePuertaKc, ubicacion: params.currentEv?.ubicacion ? String(params.currentEv.ubicacion) : null,
      categoria: params.currentEv?.categoria ? String(params.currentEv.categoria) : null,
      instructorNombre: params.currentEv?.instructor_nombre ? String(params.currentEv.instructor_nombre) : null,
      duracionMinutos: Number(params.currentEv?.duracion_minutos) || 60, isAdmin: verifiedIsAdmin,
    });
    params.onBookedSuccessState();
    notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed();
    Promise.resolve(params.refreshProfile?.()).catch(() => {});
    params.onRefreshEvents?.();
    triggerArcadeHaptic('unlock');
    const dateFormatted = formatBookingConfirmedDate(cleanDateIso, params.timeVal || params.currentEv?.hora_inicio || '08:00', params.language);
    const passVm = buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel(
      {
        negocio_nombre: params.clubName, servicio: params.titleVal, categoria: String(params.currentEv?.categoria || 'Fuerza'),
        fecha_clase: cleanDateIso, hora_inicio: params.timeVal || params.currentEv?.hora_inicio || '08:00',
        duracion_minutos: Number(params.currentEv?.duracion_minutos) || 60,
        ubicacion: params.currentEv?.ubicacion ? String(params.currentEv.ubicacion) : '',
        instructor_nombre: params.currentEv?.instructor_nombre ? String(params.currentEv.instructor_nombre) : '',
        spot_number: params.selectedSpot ?? null, es_pase_maestro: verifiedIsAdmin,
        precio_kc: effectivePriceKc, monto_escrow: effectiveEscrowKc, monto_puerta: effectivePuertaKc,
      },
      { isEn, equipmentTypeOverride: params.studioLayout?.equipmentType || null, isAdminOverride: verifiedIsAdmin }
    );
    params.triggerAlert(passVm.headerTitle, formatDigitalPassAlertBody(passVm, dateFormatted), 'success', params.onNavigateToVault, isEn ? 'MY VAULT' : 'MI BÓVEDA');
    return true;
  } finally {
    _inFlightBookingKeys.delete(preLockKey);
  }
};

    FILE: src/components/eventos/EventDetailsAthleteBody.tsx

import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { formatDisciplineBilingual } from '../../constants/kineticDisciplineMap';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { ActiveSheetType } from '../../utils/eventEditorHelpers';
import { extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { ed } from '../EventDetailsModal.styles';
import { SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { EventDetailsPolicyGearStrip, EventDetailsTechAttributesStrip } from './EventDetailsAttributesStrips';
import { sanitizeShareLocation } from './eventDetailsPureHelpers';
import { formatStudioStationBadge, inferEquipmentByDiscipline, IStudioLayoutConfig } from './KineticStudioLayoutModal';

const webNoSelect: any =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

export interface IEventDetailsAthleteBodyProps {
  titleVal: string;
  clubName: string;
  discVal: string;
  allDisplayPhotos: string[];
  activePhotoIdx: number;
  setActivePhotoIdx: (idx: number) => void;
  reqsVal: string[];
  durVal: string;
  coachVal: string;
  readableDate: string;
  locVal: string;
  openMapsNavigation: () => void;
  confirmedCount: number;
  maxCupo: number;
  freeSpots: number;
  ageVal: string;
  totalKc: number;
  basePriceKc?: number;
  hasFlashDiscount?: boolean;
  flashBadgeText?: string | null;
  escrow40: number;
  effectiveEscrowKc?: number;
  effectiveSplitCount?: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  descVal: string;
  attrNiv: string;
  attrEnf: string;
  attrEnt: string;
  attrImp: string;
  attrInt: string;
  openInternalSheet: (sheet: ActiveSheetType) => void;
  onOpenStudioMap: () => void;
  selectedSpot: number | null;
  studioLayout: IStudioLayoutConfig | null;
}

export const EventDetailsAthleteBody = memo((props: IEventDetailsAthleteBodyProps) => {
  const {
    titleVal, clubName, discVal, allDisplayPhotos, activePhotoIdx, setActivePhotoIdx,
    reqsVal, coachVal, readableDate, locVal, openMapsNavigation,
    confirmedCount, maxCupo, freeSpots, ageVal, totalKc, basePriceKc = totalKc,
    hasFlashDiscount = false, flashBadgeText = null, escrow40,
    effectiveEscrowKc, effectiveSplitCount = 1, isAdmin,
    descVal, attrNiv, attrEnf, attrEnt, attrImp, attrInt, openInternalSheet,
    onOpenStudioMap, selectedSpot, studioLayout,
  } = props;

  const { tx, td, tDisc, language, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const effectiveRawReqs = reqsVal.length > 0 ? reqsVal : ['Ropa Deportiva', 'Hidratación / Termo'];
  const parsedGeo = extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords(locVal);
  const locDisplayLabel = sanitizeShareLocation(locVal, parsedGeo.detectedZone, language === 'en');

  const cleanTitleStr = String(titleVal || '').trim();
  const cleanDescStr = String(descVal || '').trim();
  const shouldShowDescription = Boolean(
    cleanDescStr && cleanDescStr.toLowerCase() !== cleanTitleStr.toLowerCase()
  );

  return (
    <View style={webNoSelect}>
      <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.mainTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }, webNoSelect]}>
        {td(cleanTitleStr || tx('Evento Kinetic', 'Kinetic Event'))}
      </Text>
      <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.clubSub, { color: themeColors.cyanText }, webNoSelect]}>
        {clubName} • {formatDisciplineBilingual(discVal, language, tDisc)}
      </Text>

      {allDisplayPhotos.length > 1 && (
        <ScrollView horizontal nestedScrollEnabled={true} removeClippedSubviews={false} showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 8, paddingLeft: 0, marginBottom: 12 }}>
          {allDisplayPhotos.map((gUri, idx) => (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={`view_gal_${gUri}_${idx}`}
              style={[ed.viewMiniThumb, isLight && { borderColor: '#CBD5E1', backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9' }, activePhotoIdx === idx && { borderColor: COLORS.cyan, borderWidth: 2 }, webNoSelect]}
              onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setActivePhotoIdx(idx); }}
            >
              <SmartImage source={{ uri: gUri }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" contentPosition="center" fallbackCategory={discVal} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
        </ScrollView>
      )}

      <View
        style={[
          ed.cardBox,
          isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
          webNoSelect,
        ]}
      >
        <View style={ed.infoRow}>
          <Feather name="calendar" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 12 }}>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.infoLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('FECHA, HORARIO Y DURACIÓN', 'DATE, SCHEDULE & DURATION')}
            </Text>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.infoVal, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{readableDate}</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[ed.divider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />

        <View style={ed.infoRow}>
          <Feather name="user" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 12 }}>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.infoLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('COACH / ESPECIALISTA A CARGO', 'LEAD COACH / SPECIALIST')}
            </Text>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.infoVal, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {coachVal && coachVal !== 'Coach Oficial Kinetic'
                ? td(coachVal)
                : tx('Coach Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Coach')}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={[ed.divider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            ed.maps3DBox,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#38BDF8', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
            webNoSelect,
          ]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={openMapsNavigation}
        >
          <Feather name="map-pin" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 10 }}>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.infoLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>

              {tx('AFORO ANÓNIMO Y ESTACIONES 3D', 'ANONYMOUS CAPACITY & 3D STATIONS')}
            </Text>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.infoVal, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>
              {confirmedCount}/{maxCupo} {tx('Confirmados', 'Confirmed')} • {freeSpots} {tx('libres', 'open')}
            </Text>
            {selectedSpot ? (
              <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: themeColors.green, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', marginTop: 2 }}>
                {(() => {
                  const resolvedEq = studioLayout?.equipmentType || inferEquipmentByDiscipline(discVal, titleVal);
                  return `✓ ${tx(`Estación seleccionada: ${formatStudioStationBadge(resolvedEq, selectedSpot, false)}`, `Selected station: ${formatStudioStationBadge(resolvedEq, selectedSpot, true)}`)}`;
                })()}
              </Text>
            ) : null}
          </View>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              ed.studioPillBtn,
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' },
              webNoSelect,
            ]}
            activeOpacity={0.82}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onOpenStudioMap(); }}
          >
            <Feather name="grid" size={11} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.studioPillTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {tx('MAPA 3D', '3D MAP')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={[ed.divider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />

        <EventDetailsPolicyGearStrip ageVal={ageVal} effectiveRawReqs={effectiveRawReqs} />

        <View style={[ed.divider, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#E2E8F0' }]} />

        <View style={ed.infoRow}>
          <Ionicons name="flash" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 12 }}>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.infoLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('INVERSIÓN Y APARTADO 40% HOY • 60% AL LLEGAR', 'INVESTMENT & 40% DEPOSIT TODAY • 60% ON ARRIVAL')}
            </Text>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.infoVal, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>
              {hasFlashDiscount && basePriceKc > totalKc && !isAdmin && (
                <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, textDecorationLine: 'line-through', fontSize: 12 }}>
                  {basePriceKc} KC{'  '}
                </Text>
              )}
              {totalKc} KC{' '}
              <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: isAdmin ? themeColors.green : themeColors.purpleText, fontSize: 12 }}>
                {!isAdmin && effectiveSplitCount > 1
                  ? `(${tx(`División 1/${effectiveSplitCount} • Hoy ${effectiveEscrowKc ?? escrow40} KC`, `1/${effectiveSplitCount} Split • Lock ${effectiveEscrowKc ?? escrow40} KC today`)})`
                  : `(${tx(`Hoy congelas ${isAdmin ? 0 : (effectiveEscrowKc ?? escrow40)} KC`, `Lock ${isAdmin ? 0 : (effectiveEscrowKc ?? escrow40)} KC today`)})`}
              </Text>
            </Text>
            {flashBadgeText && (
              <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: isLight ? '#BE123C' : '#FB7185', fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', marginTop: 2 }}>
                ⚡ {flashBadgeText}
              </Text>
            )}
          </View>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[
              ed.cyanPillBtn,
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' },
              webNoSelect,
            ]}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
            onPress={() => openInternalSheet('desglose_escrow')}
          >
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.cyanPillTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tx('DESGLOSE', 'BREAKDOWN')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {shouldShowDescription && (
        <>
          <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.secHeader, { color: themeColors.textMuted }, webNoSelect]}>
            {tx('SOBRE EL EVENTO', 'ABOUT THIS EVENT')}
          </Text>
          <View
            style={[
              ed.descBox,
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
              webNoSelect,
            ]}
          >
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.descTxt, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
              {td(cleanDescStr)}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </>
      )}

      <EventDetailsTechAttributesStrip
        attrInt={attrInt}
        attrEnf={attrEnf}
        attrImp={attrImp}
        attrNiv={attrNiv}
        attrEnt={attrEnt}
      />
    </View>
  );
});

import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo } from 'react';

export const EventDetailsPolicyGearStrip = memo(
  ({ ageVal, effectiveRawReqs }: { ageVal: string; effectiveRawReqs: string[] }) => {
    const { tx, language, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const safeAge = normalizeAgePolicy(ageVal);
    const policyAndGearCards = [
      { key: 'age_policy', isPurple: true, ...describeAgePolicyCard(safeAge, language) },
      ...effectiveRawReqs.map((r, i) => ({
        key: `gear_${i}_${r}`,
        isPurple: i % 2 === 1,
        ...describeWhatToBringCard(r, language),
      })),
    ];

    return (
      <View>
        <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.infoLbl, { marginBottom: 6, color: themeColors.cyanText, letterSpacing: 0.5 }]}>
          {tx('QUÉ TRAER A TU SESIÓN • POLÍTICAS DE EDAD', 'WHAT TO BRING • AGE POLICIES')}
        </Text>
        <ScrollView
          horizontal
          nestedScrollEnabled={true}
          removeClippedSubviews={false}
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          snapToInterval={168}
          snapToAlignment="start"
          decelerationRate="fast"
          style={{ marginHorizontal: -14 }}
          contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 10, paddingVertical: 6, paddingLeft: 14, paddingRight: 36 }}
        >
          {policyAndGearCards.map((card) => {
            const accent = card.isPurple ? COLORS.purple : COLORS.cyan;
            const accentText = card.isPurple ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText;
            return (
              <View
                key={card.key}
                style={[
                  {
                    width: 158, backgroundColor: '#090C12', borderRadius: 14, borderWidth: 1.2,
                    borderColor: `${accent}66`, borderTopWidth: 1.7,
                    borderTopColor: card.isPurple ? 'rgba(224, 170, 255, 0.55)' : 'rgba(184, 255, 255, 0.55)',
                    borderBottomWidth: 3.8, borderBottomColor: card.isPurple ? '#4A1478' : '#005A66',
                    paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 11, elevation: 0,
                  },
                  isLight && {
                    backgroundColor: card.isPurple ? '#FAF5FF' : '#F8FAFC',
                    borderColor: card.isPurple ? '#C4B5FD' : '#7DD3FC',
                    borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderBottomColor: card.isPurple ? '#7C3AED' : '#0284C7',
                  },
                  webNoSelect,
                ]}
              >
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 7, marginBottom: 5 }}>
                  <View
                    style={{
                      width: 26, height: 26, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: `${accent}18`,
                      borderWidth: 1, borderColor: accent, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', elevation: 0,
                    }}
                  >
                    <Feather name={card.icon as any} size={13} color={accentText} />
                  </View>
                  <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} style={{ flex: 1, color: accentText, fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 }}>
                    {card.cat}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={2} style={{ color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 12.5, fontWeight: '900', marginBottom: 3 }}>
                  {card.title}
                </Text>
                <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={3} style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 10, lineHeight: 13, fontWeight: '600' }}>
                  {card.desc}
                </Text>
              </View>
            );
          })}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>
    );
  }
);

export const EventDetailsTechAttributesStrip = memo(
  ({
    attrInt, attrEnf, attrImp, attrNiv, attrEnt,
  }: {
    attrInt: string; attrEnf: string; attrImp: string; attrNiv: string; attrEnt: string;
  }) => {
    const { tx, language, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const cleanEnv = normalizeEnvironmentLabel(attrEnt);

    const chips = [
      {
        key: 'intensidad', icon: 'zap', cat: tx('INTENSIDAD', 'INTENSITY'),
        val: translateEventCatalogTerm(attrInt || 'Intensidad Alta', language),
        desc: tx('Exigencia metabólica y cardiovascular de la sesión.', 'Metabolic and cardiovascular demand of this session.'),
        isPurple: false,
      },
      {
        key: 'enfoque', icon: 'target', cat: tx('ENFOQUE', 'FOCUS'),
        val: translateEventCatalogTerm(attrEnf || 'Cardio / Quema', language),
        desc: tx('Objetivo fisiológico y muscular prioritario.', 'Primary physiological and muscular training focus.'),
        isPurple: true,
      },
      {
        key: 'impacto', icon: 'activity', cat: tx('IMPACTO ARTICULAR', 'JOINT IMPACT'),
        val: translateEventCatalogTerm(attrImp || 'Impacto Moderado', language),
        desc: tx('Carga biomecánica sobre rodillas, tobillos y espalda.', 'Biomechanical load on knees, ankles, and spine.'),
        isPurple: false,
      },
      {
        key: 'nivel', icon: 'award', cat: tx('NIVEL', 'LEVEL'),
        val: translateEventCatalogTerm(attrNiv || 'Multi-Nivel (Todos)', language),
        desc: tx('Grado de técnica previa recomendado para fluir.', 'Recommended prior technique level to flow safely.'),
        isPurple: true,
      },
      {
        key: 'entorno', icon: 'home', cat: tx('ENTORNO', 'ENVIRONMENT'),
        val: translateEventCatalogTerm(cleanEnv || KINETIC_20_ENVIRONMENTS[0].es, language),
        desc: tx('Condiciones físicas y climatización del estudio.', 'Studio physical layout and climate setting.'),
        isPurple: false,
      },
    ];

    return (
      <View>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 6 }}>

          </Text>
        </View>
        <ScrollView
          horizontal
          nestedScrollEnabled={true}
          removeClippedSubviews={false}
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 10, paddingVertical: 6, paddingLeft: 0, paddingRight: 16 }}
        >
          {chips.map((item) => {
            const accent = item.isPurple ? COLORS.purple : COLORS.cyan;
            const accentText = item.isPurple ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText;
            return (
              <View
                key={item.key}
                style={[
                  {
                    width: 165, backgroundColor: '#090C12', borderRadius: 14, borderWidth: 1.2,
                    borderColor: `${accent}55`, borderTopWidth: 1.6,
                    borderTopColor: item.isPurple ? 'rgba(224, 170, 255, 0.5)' : 'rgba(184, 255, 255, 0.5)',
                    borderBottomWidth: 3.8, borderBottomColor: item.isPurple ? '#4A1478' : '#005A66',
                    padding: 11, elevation: 0,
                  },
                  isLight && {
                    backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderColor: item.isPurple ? '#C4B5FD' : '#CBD5E1',
                    borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
                    borderBottomColor: item.isPurple ? '#7C3AED' : '#0284C7',
                  },
                  webNoSelect,
                ]}
              >
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 7, marginBottom: 6 }}>
                  <View
                    style={{
                      width: 28, height: 28, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: `${accent}18`,

                  >
                    <Feather name={item.icon as any} size={13} color={accentText} />

                  <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={2} style={{ flex: 1, color: accentText, fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 }}>
                    {item.cat}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={2} style={{ color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900', textTransform: 'capitalize', marginBottom: 3 }}>
                  {item.val}
                </Text>

                </Text>
