  }
);

    FILE: src/components/eventos/EventDetailsBookingFooter.tsx

    // === src/components/eventos/EventDetailsBookingFooter.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Platform, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewProps, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences, type KineticThemeColors } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel } from '../../utils/kineticDigitalPassViewModelLeaf';
import { stepStudioBookingStepMachine } from '../../utils/kineticVolumetric3dPureLeaf';
import type { ed as eventEdStyles } from '../EventDetailsModal.styles';
import { KineticLoader, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { SquadSplitEscrowButton } from '../shared/KineticDisruptiveWidgets';
import { EventDetailsPreBookDrawer } from './EventDetailsPreBookDrawer';

const webNoSelect: ViewStyle | null =
  Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle) : null;

export interface IInsufficientKcPrompt {
  missingKc: number;
  availableKc: number;
  requiredEscrow: number;
}

export interface IEventDetailsBookingFooterProps {
  ed: typeof eventEdStyles;
  themeColors: KineticThemeColors;
  isLight: boolean;
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
  isEditMode: boolean;
  isSaving: boolean;
  isPreBookingConfirmOpen: boolean;
  setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen: (v: boolean) => void;
  isBookingBusy: boolean;
  hasActivePass: boolean;
  isSoldOutLocked: boolean;
  bookingLockLabel?: string;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  isStrictB2B?: boolean;
  isTerminalOperational?: boolean;
  canManage?: boolean;
  onEnterEditMode?: () => void;
  onOpenStudioAdmin?: () => void;
  titleVal: string;
  clubName: string;
  escrow40: number;
  puerta60: number;
  effectiveEscrowKc?: number;
  effectiveDoorKc?: number;
  effectiveSplitCount?: number;
  selectedSplitGuests?: { id: string; nombre: string }[];
  onResetIndividualSplit?: () => void;
  totalKc: number;
  basePriceKc?: number;
  hasFlashDiscount?: boolean;
  flashBadgeText?: string | null;
  selectedSpot?: number | null;
  equipmentType?: string | null;
  maxCupo?: number;
  onOpenStudioMap?: () => void;
  splitEscrowEnabled: boolean;
  splitMaxPersonas: number;
  eventId?: string;
  discVal: string;
  locVal: string;
  coachVal?: string;
  durVal?: string;
  timeVal?: string;
  localBookingDateIso?: string;
  readableDate: string;
  insufficientKcPrompt: IInsufficientKcPrompt | null;
  onDismissInsufficientKc: () => void;
  onOpenTopUpModal: () => void;
  onOpenSplitInvite: () => void;
  onOpenVault: () => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  coverSwipeHandlers: Pick<ViewProps, 'onTouchStart' | 'onTouchMove' | 'onTouchEnd'>;
  onConfirmBooking: () => void;
}

export const EventDetailsBookingFooter = memo((props: IEventDetailsBookingFooterProps) => {
  const {
    ed, themeColors, isLight, tx, isEditMode, isSaving, isPreBookingConfirmOpen,
    setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen, isBookingBusy, hasActivePass, isSoldOutLocked,
    isAdmin, isStrictB2B = false, isTerminalOperational = false, canManage = false,
    onEnterEditMode, onOpenStudioAdmin, titleVal, clubName, escrow40, puerta60,
    effectiveEscrowKc, effectiveDoorKc, effectiveSplitCount = 1,
    selectedSplitGuests = [], onResetIndividualSplit, totalKc,
    basePriceKc = totalKc, hasFlashDiscount = false, flashBadgeText = null,
    selectedSpot = null, equipmentType = null, maxCupo = 30, onOpenStudioMap,
    splitEscrowEnabled, splitMaxPersonas, eventId, discVal, locVal, coachVal,
    durVal, timeVal, localBookingDateIso, readableDate,
    insufficientKcPrompt, onDismissInsufficientKc, onOpenTopUpModal,
    onOpenSplitInvite, onOpenVault, onClose, coverSwipeHandlers, onConfirmBooking,
  } = props;
  const { language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const [optionsOpen, setOptionsOpen] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => { setOptionsOpen(false); }, [eventId]);

  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const displayEscrow = isAdmin ? 0 : (effectiveEscrowKc ?? escrow40);
  const displayDoor = isAdmin ? 0 : (effectiveDoorKc ?? puerta60);
  const isSplitActive = !isAdmin && effectiveSplitCount > 1;
  const guestNamesTxt = selectedSplitGuests.map((g) => g.nombre).filter(Boolean).join(', ');
  const passVm = useMemo(
    () =>
      buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel(
        {
          negocio_nombre: clubName,
          servicio: titleVal,
          categoria: discVal,
          fecha_clase: localBookingDateIso || '',
          hora_inicio: timeVal || '08:00',
          duracion_minutos: Number(durVal) || 60,
          ubicacion: locVal,
          instructor_nombre: coachVal,
          spot_number: selectedSpot,
          es_pase_maestro: isAdmin,
          precio_kc: isAdmin ? 0 : totalKc,
          monto_escrow: displayEscrow,
          monto_puerta: displayDoor,
        },
        { isEn, equipmentTypeOverride: equipmentType, isAdminOverride: isAdmin }
      ),
    [clubName, titleVal, discVal, localBookingDateIso, timeVal, durVal, locVal, coachVal, selectedSpot, isAdmin, totalKc, displayEscrow, displayDoor, isEn, equipmentType]
  );

  if (isEditMode && isSaving) {
    return (
      <View style={[ed.footerBar, { paddingTop: 8 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderTopColor: '#CBD5E1' }, webNoSelect]}>
        <View style={[ed.holdBtn, { flex: 0, width: '100%', minHeight: 46 }]}>
          <KineticLoader size={16} color="#050505" />
          <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} style={[ed.holdBtnTxt, { color: '#050505' }]}>
            {tx('⚡ PUBLICANDO EVENTO...', '⚡ PUBLISHING EVENT...')}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }
  if (isEditMode) return null;

  if (isStrictB2B || isTerminalOperational) {
    return (
      <View style={[ed.footerBar, { paddingTop: 10 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderTopColor: '#CBD5E1' }, webNoSelect]}>
        <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.6, marginBottom: 8 }}>
          {canManage ? tx('GESTIÓN OPERATIVA DE TERMINAL B2B', 'B2B TERMINAL OPERATIONAL MANAGEMENT') : tx('CUENTA OPERATIVA B2B • SOLO LECTURA', 'B2B OPERATOR ACCOUNT • READ ONLY')}
        </Text>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.backFooterBtn, { minHeight: 46, paddingVertical: 6, flex: 0.55 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]} onPress={onClose}>
            <Feather name="arrow-left" size={14} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.backFooterTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('ATRÁS', 'BACK')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          {canManage && onEnterEditMode && (
            <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.holdBtn, { minHeight: 46, paddingVertical: 6, flex: 1, backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan }]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onEnterEditMode(); }}>
              <Feather name="edit-2" size={13} color="#050505" />
              <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.8} style={[ed.holdBtnTxt, { color: '#050505' }]}>{tx('EDITAR EVENTO', 'EDIT EVENT')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
          {canManage && onOpenStudioAdmin && (
            <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.holdBtn, { minHeight: 46, paddingVertical: 6, flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#A855F7', borderColor: '#C084FC', borderTopColor: '#F3E8FF', borderBottomColor: '#6B21A8' }]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onOpenStudioAdmin(); }}>
              <Feather name="grid" size={13} color="#FFFFFF" />
              <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.8} style={[ed.holdBtnTxt, { color: '#FFFFFF' }]}>{tx('SALA 3D / AFORO', '3D STUDIO')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (insufficientKcPrompt) {
    return (
      <View style={[ed.preBookDrawer, { borderTopColor: '#F59E0B', paddingVertical: 12 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFBEB', borderTopColor: '#D97706' }, webNoSelect]}>
        <View style={localStyles.drawerHeadRow}>
          <Text selectable={false} style={[localStyles.warnTitle, { color: isLight ? '#B45309' : '#FBBF24' }]}>{tx('⚠️ FALTAN KINETICOINS PARA APARTAR', '⚠️ MORE KINETICOINS NEEDED')}</Text>
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={onDismissInsufficientKc} hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}>
            <Feather name="x" size={16} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
        <Text selectable={false} style={[localStyles.kcMissingHeadline, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
          {tx(`Te faltan ${insufficientKcPrompt.missingKc} KC • Disponible: ${insufficientKcPrompt.availableKc} KC`, `You need ${insufficientKcPrompt.missingKc} more KC • Available: ${insufficientKcPrompt.availableKc} KC`)}
        </Text>
        <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: themeColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11, marginTop: 2, marginBottom: 10 }}>
          {tx(`Tu evento sigue abierto. Este apartado requiere ${insufficientKcPrompt.requiredEscrow} KC (40% Anticipo Protegido).`, `Your event remains open. Booking requires ${insufficientKcPrompt.requiredEscrow} KC (40% Protected Deposit).`)}
        </Text>
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.backFooterBtn, { minHeight: 44, paddingVertical: 6, flex: 0.56 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]} onPress={onDismissInsufficientKc}>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.backFooterTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('CERRAR', 'DISMISS')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[ed.holdBtn, { minHeight: 44, paddingVertical: 6, flex: 1.84, backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan }]}
            activeOpacity={0.86}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Medium'); onOpenTopUpModal(); }}
          >
            <Feather name="zap" size={14} color="#050505" />
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.holdBtnTxt, { color: '#050505' }]} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.78}>
              {tx('RECARGAR / SUBIR NIVEL', 'TOP UP / LEVEL UP')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (isPreBookingConfirmOpen) {
    return (
      <EventDetailsPreBookDrawer
        ed={ed} themeColors={themeColors} isLight={isLight} tx={tx} isAdmin={isAdmin}
        isBookingBusy={isBookingBusy} hasActivePass={hasActivePass} hasFlashDiscount={hasFlashDiscount}
        basePriceKc={basePriceKc} totalKc={totalKc} flashBadgeText={flashBadgeText} maxCupo={maxCupo}
        discVal={discVal} isSplitActive={isSplitActive} effectiveSplitCount={effectiveSplitCount}
        guestNamesTxt={guestNamesTxt} passVm={passVm} onResetIndividualSplit={onResetIndividualSplit}
        onOpenStudioMap={onOpenStudioMap} onOpenVault={onOpenVault}
        onBackStep={() => {
          if (stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'BACK_TAP', isBusy: isBookingBusy, isSealed: hasActivePass }) === 1) {
            setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(false);
          }
        }} onConfirmBooking={onConfirmBooking}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={[ed.footerBar, { paddingTop: 8 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderTopColor: '#CBD5E1' }, webNoSelect]}>
      <View style={[ed.footerStrip, { marginBottom: 7, gap: 8 }]}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, paddingRight: 4 }}>
          <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.stripLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {isAdmin
              ? tx('PASE MAESTRO ADMIN', 'ADMIN MASTER PASS')
              : isSplitActive
                ? tx(`PASO 1/2 • DIVISIÓN 1/${effectiveSplitCount}`, `STEP 1/2 • 1/${effectiveSplitCount} SPLIT`)
                : tx('PASO 1/2 • ANTICIPO 40% / 60%', 'STEP 1/2 • 40% / 60% DEPOSIT')}
          </Text>
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            {!isAdmin && hasFlashDiscount && basePriceKc > totalKc && (
              <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800', textDecorationLine: 'line-through' }}>
                {basePriceKc} KC
              </Text>
            )}
            <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.stripVal, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
              {isAdmin ? tx('0 KC (0 KC en recepción)', '0 KC (0 KC at desk)') : tx(`${displayEscrow} KC (+${displayDoor} KC en QR)`, `${displayEscrow} KC (+${displayDoor} KC at QR)`)}
            </Text>
            {flashBadgeText && (
              <Text selectable={false} style={{ color: isLight ? '#BE123C' : '#FB7185', fontSize: 9.5, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                ⚡ {flashBadgeText}
              </Text>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
        <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end', flexShrink: 0, gap: 3 }}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[localStyles.vaultMiniPill, isLight && localStyles.vaultMiniPillLight]}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onOpenVault(); }}
          >
            <Feather name="shield" size={10} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} style={[localStyles.vaultMiniTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {tx('MI BÓVEDA', 'MY VAULT')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.stripVal, { color: isAdmin ? themeColors.green : themeColors.cyanText, marginTop: 0 }]}>
            {isAdmin ? tx('0 KC CORTESÍA', '0 KC COURTESY') : `${totalKc} KC TOTAL`}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      {!(isSoldOutLocked || Boolean(props.bookingLockLabel)) && ((!hasActivePass && maxCupo <= 50 && Boolean(onOpenStudioMap)) || splitEscrowEnabled) && (
        <>
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setOptionsOpen((p) => !p); }}
            style={[localStyles.toggleOptsBtn, { borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.28)', backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : 'rgba(15,23,42,0.72)' }]}
          >
            <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.cyanText }}>
              {optionsOpen ? tx('▼ Ocultar opciones', '▼ Hide options') : passVm.stationBadge ? `▲ ${passVm.stationBadge}` : tx('▲ Mapa de Posiciones 3D y Equipo', '▲ 3D Studio Map & Team')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          {optionsOpen && (
            <>
              {!hasActivePass && maxCupo <= 50 && onOpenStudioMap && (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onOpenStudioMap(); }}
                  style={[localStyles.toggleOptsBtn, { borderColor: selectedSpot ? (isLight ? '#10B981' : COLORS.green) : (isLight ? '#0284C7' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.42)'), backgroundColor: selectedSpot ? (isLight ? '#ECFDF5' : 'rgba(0,255,102,0.12)') : (isLight ? '#F0F9FF' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.10)') }]}
                >
                  <Text selectable={false} style={{ fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', color: selectedSpot ? (isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : themeColors.cyanText, flex: 1 }} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {passVm.stationBadge ? `🎯 ${passVm.stationBadge}` : tx('🎯 Elegir estación 3D en sala (opcional)', '🎯 Pick 3D station in studio (optional)')}
                  </Text>
                  <Text selectable={false} style={{ fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.cyanText, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                    {selectedSpot ? tx('Cambiar ›', 'Change ›') : tx('Abrir Mapa ›', 'Open Map ›')}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
              {splitEscrowEnabled && (
                <SquadSplitEscrowButton
                  enabled={splitEscrowEnabled} inlineOverlay={true} onOpenInviteOverride={onOpenSplitInvite}
                  maxPersonas={splitMaxPersonas === 2 ? 2 : splitMaxPersonas === 3 ? 3 : 4} eventId={eventId}
                  title={titleVal || tx('Sesión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Session')} discipline={discVal || 'Fuerza'} clubName={clubName}
                  location={locVal || tx('Ubicación Verificada', 'Verified Location')} readableDate={readableDate} totalKc={totalKc}
                  escrow40={escrow40} activeSplitCount={effectiveSplitCount} activeGuestCount={selectedSplitGuests.length} activePerPersonEscrow={displayEscrow}
                />
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 9 }}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[ed.backFooterBtn, { minHeight: 46, paddingVertical: 6, flex: 0.56 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]}
          onPress={onClose} {...coverSwipeHandlers}
        >
          <Feather name="arrow-left" size={14} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
          <Text selectable={false} style={[ed.backFooterTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('ATRÁS', 'BACK')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            ed.holdBtn, { minHeight: 46, paddingVertical: 6, flex: 1.94 },
            hasActivePass && { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#DCFCE7' : 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.18)', borderColor: isLight ? '#10B981' : COLORS.green, borderTopColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#B8FFD8', borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#047857' : '#008033', elevation: 0 },
            (isSoldOutLocked || Boolean(props.bookingLockLabel)) && { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#E2E8F0' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.16)', borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.35)', borderTopColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.18)', borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#94A3B8' : 'rgba(71, 85, 105, 0.65)', elevation: 0 },
          ]}
          activeOpacity={(isSoldOutLocked || Boolean(props.bookingLockLabel)) ? 1 : 0.86}
          disabled={isBookingBusy || isSoldOutLocked || Boolean(props.bookingLockLabel)}
          onPress={() => {
            if (isSoldOutLocked || Boolean(props.bookingLockLabel)) return;
            triggerHaptic('Medium');
            if (hasActivePass) { onOpenVault(); return; }
            setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(true);
          }}
        >
          {isBookingBusy ? <KineticLoader size={16} color="#050505" /> : (
            <>
              <Feather name={hasActivePass ? 'check-circle' : (isSoldOutLocked || Boolean(props.bookingLockLabel)) ? 'lock' : isAdmin ? 'zap' : 'calendar'} size={14} color={hasActivePass ? (isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : (isSoldOutLocked || Boolean(props.bookingLockLabel)) ? (isLight ? '#475569' : '#94A3B8') : '#050505'} />
              <Text selectable={false} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.76} style={[ed.holdBtnTxt, { color: hasActivePass ? (isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : (isSoldOutLocked || Boolean(props.bookingLockLabel)) ? (isLight ? '#475569' : '#94A3B8') : '#050505' }]}>
                {hasActivePass ? tx('✓ PASE ACTIVO • MI BÓVEDA', '✓ ACTIVE PASS • MY VAULT') : props.bookingLockLabel ? props.bookingLockLabel : isSoldOutLocked ? tx('CUPO AGOTADO', 'SOLD OUT') : isAdmin ? tx('⚡ PASE MAESTRO (0 KC)', '⚡ MASTER PASS (0 KC)') : tx('RESERVAR • PASO 1/2', 'BOOK • STEP 1/2')}
              </Text>
            </>
          )}
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
});

    // === src/components/eventos/eventDetailsHydrationHelper.ts === //
import { normalizeAgePolicy, normalizeEnvironmentLabel } from '../../constants/kineticEventCatalogs';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing } from '../../services/kineticFlashSaleHelper';
import { extractExtraEventPhotos, loadEventAttrsDualCache, parseRequirementsInput, resolveSplitEscrowConfig } from '../../utils/eventEditorHelpers';
import { extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { resolveEventModalEscrowPreview } from './eventDetailsPureHelpers';
import type { IStudioLayoutConfig } from './KineticStudioLayoutModal';
import { resolveDefaultStudioLayout } from './KineticStudioLayoutModal';

const UUID_V4_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const isUuid = (v: unknown): v is string => typeof v === 'string' && UUID_V4_RE.test(v.trim());

export interface EventAttrsRecord {
  id?: string | number | null;
  ciudad?: string | null;
  frecuencia?: string | null;
  duracion_minutos?: number | string | null;
  requisitos_traer?: unknown;
  edad_minima?: string | null;
  precio_kc?: number | string | null;
  intensidad?: string | null;
  enfoque?: string | null;
  impacto?: string | null;
  nivel?: string | null;
  entorno?: string | null;
  split_escrow_enabled?: boolean;
  studio_layout?: unknown;
  studio_layout_json?: unknown;
  galeria?: string[] | null;
  cupo_maximo?: number | string | null;
  hora_inicio?: string | null;
  ubicacion?: string | null;
  negocio_nombre?: string | null;
  nombre_servicio?: string | null;
  titulo?: string | null;
  propietario_id?: string | null;
  afiliado_id?: string | null;
  latitud?: number | null;
  longitud?: number | null;
  rating?: number | null;
  es_sede_externa?: boolean;
  openInEditMode?: boolean;
  openStudioConfigOnMount?: boolean;
  isTerminalOperational?: boolean;
  [k: string]: unknown;
}

export function buildBookingConfirmPayload(p: {
  currentEv: EventAttrsRecord;
  realTitle: string;
  localBookingDateIso: string;
  timeVal: string;
  selectedSpot: number | null;
  effectiveSplitCount: number;
  selectedGuests: unknown[];
  isAdmin: boolean;
  totalKc: number;
  onInsufficientKc: (missingKc: number, avail: number, req: number) => void;
  onBookingError: (msg: string) => void;
  onGhostPurged: (msg: string) => void;
}): EventAttrsRecord {
  return {
    ...p.currentEv,
    nombre_servicio: p.realTitle,
    titulo: p.realTitle,
    fecha: p.localBookingDateIso,
    fecha_clase: p.localBookingDateIso,
    fecha_proyectada: p.localBookingDateIso,
    fecha_seleccionada: p.localBookingDateIso,
    hora_inicio: p.timeVal,
    estacion: p.selectedSpot,
    spot: p.selectedSpot,
    spot_number: p.selectedSpot,
    split_count: p.effectiveSplitCount,
    splitCount: p.effectiveSplitCount,
    splitGuests: p.selectedGuests,
    precio_kc: p.isAdmin ? 0 : p.totalKc,
    isMasterPass: p.isAdmin,
    _fromDetailsModal: true,
    _onInsufficientKc: p.onInsufficientKc,
    _onBookingError: p.onBookingError,
    _onGhostPurged: p.onGhostPurged,
  };
}

export function resolveEventModalFlashAndEscrowPreview(p: {
  currentEv: EventAttrsRecord | null | undefined;
  priceVal: string;
  dateVal: string;
  timeVal: string;
  vipLevel: string | null | undefined;
  kcMembresia: number | null | undefined;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  isCreateMode: boolean;
  isEditMode: boolean;
  language: string;
}) {
  const rawPriceInt = Math.max(0, parseInt(p.priceVal, 10) || 0);
  const preIsEditorMode = Boolean(p.isCreateMode || p.isEditMode);
  const effectiveFlashDate = p.dateVal || (p.currentEv?.fecha_proyectada as string | undefined) || (p.currentEv?.fecha as string | undefined);
  const baseEvDateIso = String(p.currentEv?.fecha_proyectada || p.currentEv?.fecha || '').slice(0, 10);
  const isCustomFlashDate = Boolean(p.dateVal && baseEvDateIso && p.dateVal !== baseEvDateIso);
  const fp = resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing({
    event: {
      ...(p.currentEv || {}),
      precio_kc: rawPriceInt,
      fecha: effectiveFlashDate,
      fecha_proyectada: effectiveFlashDate,
      fecha_clase: effectiveFlashDate,
      hora_inicio: p.timeVal || (p.currentEv?.hora_inicio as string | undefined) || (p.currentEv?.hora as string | undefined),
      ...(p.dateVal ? { nextTimestamp: undefined } : {}),
      ...(isCustomFlashDate
        ? {
            es_venta_flash: false,
            isFlashSale: false,
            isFlashUrgent3h: false,
            descuento_flash_pct: 0,
            atributos: { ...(p.currentEv?.atributos || {}), es_venta_flash: false, descuento_flash_pct: 0 },
          }
        : {}),
    },
    vipLevel: p.vipLevel,
    kcMembresia: p.kcMembresia,
    isAdmin: Boolean(p.isAdmin && !preIsEditorMode),
    isEn: p.language === 'en',
  });
  const escrowPreview = resolveEventModalEscrowPreview({
    rawPriceInt,
    discountedTotalKc: fp.discountedTotalKc,
    discountedEscrow40: fp.escrow40,
    discountedPuerta60: fp.puerta60,
    isAdmin: p.isAdmin,
    isCreateMode: p.isCreateMode,
    isEditMode: p.isEditMode,
  });
  return { fp, escrowPreview };
}

export async function hydrateRemoteEventDetails(p: {
  evIdStr: string;
  titleVal: string;
  currentEv: EventAttrsRecord | null | undefined;
  dbAttrs: EventAttrsRecord | null | undefined;
  rawLoc: string;
  discVal: string;
  maxCupo: number;
  stdCatImg: string;
  coverUri: string;
  existingGalleryLen: number;
  maxExtraPhotos: number;
  userId?: string;
  isStale: () => boolean;
  setters: {
    setFreqVal: (v: string) => void;
    setDurVal: (v: string) => void;
    setReqsVal: (v: string[]) => void;
    setAgeVal: (v: string) => void;
    setPriceVal: (v: string) => void;
    setLocVal: (v: string) => void;
    setMapsDeepUrl: (v: string) => void;
    setDetectedZone: (v: string | null) => void;
    setAttrInt: (v: string) => void;
    setAttrEnf: (v: string) => void;
    setAttrImp: (v: string) => void;
    setAttrNiv: (v: string) => void;
    setAttrEnt: (v: string) => void;
    setSplitEscrowEnabled: (v: boolean) => void;
    setSplitMaxPersonas: (v: number) => void;
    setStudioLayout: (v: IStudioLayoutConfig) => void;
    setGalleryUris: (v: string[]) => void;
    setConfirmedReservationsCount: (v: number) => void;
    setUserBookedInDb: (v: boolean) => void;
  };
}) {
  try {
    const cached = await loadEventAttrsDualCache(p.evIdStr, p.titleVal);
    if (cached && !p.isStale()) {
      const s = p.setters;
      if (!p.currentEv?.frecuencia && !p.dbAttrs?.frecuencia && cached.frecuencia) s.setFreqVal(String(cached.frecuencia));
      if (p.currentEv?.duracion_minutos == null && p.dbAttrs?.duracion_minutos == null && cached.duracion_minutos != null) {
        s.setDurVal(String(cached.duracion_minutos));
      }
      if (!p.currentEv?.requisitos_traer && !p.dbAttrs?.requisitos_traer && cached.requisitos_traer) {
        const cReqs = parseRequirementsInput(cached.requisitos_traer);
        if (cReqs.length > 0) s.setReqsVal(cReqs);
      }
      if (!p.currentEv?.edad_minima && !p.dbAttrs?.edad_minima && cached.edad_minima) s.setAgeVal(normalizeAgePolicy(cached.edad_minima));
      if (p.currentEv?.precio_kc == null && p.dbAttrs?.precio_kc == null && cached.precio_kc != null) s.setPriceVal(String(cached.precio_kc));
      if (!p.rawLoc && cached.ubicacion) {
        const pLoc = extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords(String(cached.ubicacion));
        s.setLocVal(String(cached.ubicacion));
        s.setMapsDeepUrl(pLoc.url || '');
        s.setDetectedZone(pLoc.detectedZone);
      }
      if (!p.dbAttrs?.intensidad && cached.intensidad) s.setAttrInt(cached.intensidad);
      if (!p.dbAttrs?.enfoque && cached.enfoque) s.setAttrEnf(cached.enfoque);
      if (!p.dbAttrs?.impacto && cached.impacto) s.setAttrImp(cached.impacto);
      if (!p.dbAttrs?.nivel && cached.nivel) s.setAttrNiv(cached.nivel);
      if (!p.dbAttrs?.entorno && cached.entorno) s.setAttrEnt(normalizeEnvironmentLabel(cached.entorno));
      if (p.dbAttrs?.split_escrow_enabled === undefined && cached.split_escrow_enabled !== undefined) {
        const sp = resolveSplitEscrowConfig({ ...cached, ...p.currentEv });
        s.setSplitEscrowEnabled(sp.enabled);
        s.setSplitMaxPersonas(sp.maxPersonas);
      }
      if (!p.currentEv?.studio_layout_json && !p.dbAttrs?.studio_layout && cached.studio_layout) {
        s.setStudioLayout(resolveDefaultStudioLayout(p.discVal, p.maxCupo, cached.studio_layout, p.titleVal));
      }
      if (p.existingGalleryLen === 0 && Array.isArray(cached.galeria) && cached.galeria.length > 0) {
        s.setGalleryUris(extractExtraEventPhotos(cached.galeria, p.stdCatImg, p.coverUri || '', p.maxExtraPhotos));
      }
    }
  } catch {}
}

export async function syncEventModalDateOccupancy(params: {
  evIdStr: string;
  titleVal: string;
  targetDateIso: string;
  userId?: string | null;
  isStale: () => boolean;
  setConfirmedReservationsCount: (n: number) => void;
  setUserBookedInDb: (v: boolean) => void;
}): Promise<void> {
  if (!params.titleVal && !isUuid(params.evIdStr)) return;
  try {
    const cleanDate = String(params.targetDateIso || '').split('T')[0].trim();
    let resQ = supabase.from('reservas').select('id, usuario_id, estado').in('estado', ['pendiente', 'confirmada', 'activa']);
    if (isUuid(params.evIdStr)) resQ = resQ.eq('servicio_id', params.evIdStr);
    else resQ = resQ.eq('servicio', params.titleVal);
    if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(cleanDate)) resQ = resQ.eq('fecha_clase', cleanDate);
    const { data: resRows } = await resQ.limit(60);
    if (params.isStale()) return;
    const rows = resRows || [];
    params.setConfirmedReservationsCount(rows.length);
    const uid = params.userId ? String(params.userId) : '';
    params.setUserBookedInDb(Boolean(uid && rows.some((r: Record<string, unknown>) => String(r?.usuario_id || '') === uid)));
  } catch {}
}

export function buildEventModalMediaAndBrandMeta(p: {
  clubName: string;
  customCoverUri: string;
  standardDisciplineImage: string;
  galleryUris: string[];
  maxExtraPhotos: number;
  activePhotoIdx: number;
}) {
  const low = String(p.clubName).trim().toLowerCase();
  const isOfficial = low === 'kinetic oficial' || low === 'kinetic sports club';
  const primaryCoverUri = p.customCoverUri || p.standardDisciplineImage;
  const extras = p.galleryUris.filter((u) => u && u !== p.standardDisciplineImage && u !== primaryCoverUri);
  const allDisplayPhotos = [primaryCoverUri, ...extras.slice(0, p.maxExtraPhotos)];
  const coverUri = allDisplayPhotos[p.activePhotoIdx] || primaryCoverUri;
  return { isOfficial, primaryCoverUri, allDisplayPhotos, coverUri };
}

export function deriveEventModalBookingStats(p: {
  isEditMode: boolean;
  fp: { basePriceKc: number; hasFlashDiscount: boolean; flashBadgeText: string | null };
  cupoVal: string;
  maxStudioSpots: number;
  localBookingDateIso: string;
  baseProjectedIso: string;
  confirmedReservationsCount: number;
  currentEv: EventAttrsRecord | null | undefined;
}) {
  const maxCupo = Math.min(p.maxStudioSpots, Math.max(1, parseInt(p.cupoVal, 10) || 10));
  const confirmedCount = p.localBookingDateIso === p.baseProjectedIso
    ? Math.max(p.confirmedReservationsCount, Number(p.currentEv?.cupo_actual ?? p.currentEv?.reservadosCount ?? 0))
    : p.confirmedReservationsCount;
  return {
    basePriceKc: p.fp.basePriceKc,
    hasFlashDiscount: !p.isEditMode && p.fp.hasFlashDiscount,
    flashBadgeText: p.isEditMode ? null : p.fp.flashBadgeText,
    maxCupo,
    confirmedCount,
    freeSpots: Math.max(0, maxCupo - confirmedCount),
  };
}

export function computeNextModalCalMonth(calMonth: Date, delta: number): Date | null {
  const now = new Date();
  const minMonth = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);
  const maxMonth = new Date(minMonth.getFullYear(), minMonth.getMonth() + 3, 1);
  const nextMonth = new Date(calMonth.getFullYear(), calMonth.getMonth() + delta, 1);
  return nextMonth < minMonth || nextMonth > maxMonth ? null : nextMonth;
}

    FILE: src/components/eventos/eventDetailsPureHelpers.ts

    // === src/components/eventos/eventDetailsPureHelpers.ts === //
import { computeSplitEscrow1NMath } from '../../services/kineticSplitPreBookingStore';
import { getMazatlanNowParts } from '../../services/kineticVenueCanonicalGeoAndHours';

const URL_AND_MAPS_RE =
  /(?:https?:\/\/|(?:www\.)?(?:maps\.app\.goo\.gl|goo\.gl\/maps|google\.[a-z.]+\/maps)|maps\.google\.[a-z.]+)[^\s()[\]{}<>"']*/gi;

const encodeUriRfc3986 = (val: string): string =>
  encodeURIComponent(val).replace(/[!'()*]/g, (c) => `%${c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase()}`);

export const stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens = (raw: unknown): string => {
  const str = String(raw ?? '').trim();
  if (!str) return '';
  let cleaned = str.replace(URL_AND_MAPS_RE, ' ');
  let prev = '';
  let guard = 0;
  while (prev !== cleaned && guard < 12) {
    prev = cleaned;
    cleaned = cleaned.replace(/\(\s*\)|\[\s*\]|\{\s*\}/g, ' ');
    guard += 1;
  }
  return cleaned
    .replace(/^[\s•·\-–—|,:/()[\]{}]+|[\s•·\-–—|,:/()[\]{}]+$/g, '')
    .replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ')
    .trim();
};

export const sanitizeShareLocation = (
  rawLoc: unknown,
  detectedZone?: string | null,
  isEn = false
): string => {
  const cleanedLoc = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(rawLoc);
  if (cleanedLoc) return cleanedLoc;
  const cleanedZone = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(detectedZone);
  if (cleanedZone) return cleanedZone;
  return isEn ? 'GPS Verified Venue' : 'Sede con Ubicación GPS';
};

export const buildCleanEventDeepLink = (eventId?: unknown, fallbackTitle?: unknown): string => {
  const cleanId = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(eventId) || stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(fallbackTitle) || 'kinetic';
  return `https://kineticapp.com.mx/eventos?evento=${encodeUriRfc3986(cleanId)}`;
};

export const buildCleanSquadDeepLink = (
  eventId?: unknown,
  fallbackTitle?: unknown,
  splitCount = 2,
  hostId?: unknown
): string => {
  const cleanId = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(eventId) || stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(fallbackTitle) || 'kinetic';
  const safeSplit = Math.max(2, Math.min(20, Math.floor(Number(splitCount) || 2)));
  const cleanHost = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(hostId) || 'host';
  return `https://kineticapp.com.mx/reservation-detail?evento=${encodeUriRfc3986(cleanId)}&split=${safeSplit}&host=${encodeUriRfc3986(cleanHost)}`;
};

export interface ICleanEventShareInput {
  eventId?: unknown;
  title: unknown;
  clubName: unknown;
  discipline?: unknown;
  location?: unknown;
  detectedZone?: string | null;
  dateFormatted: unknown;
  totalKc: number;
  escrow40: number;
  language?: string;
}

export const buildCleanKineticEventSharePayload = (input: ICleanEventShareInput) => {
  const isEn = input.language === 'en';
  const cleanTitle = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(input.title) || (isEn ? 'Kinetic Session' : 'Sesión Kinetic');
  const cleanClub = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(input.clubName) || 'Kinetic Oficial';
  const cleanDisc = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(input.discipline);
  const locDisplay = sanitizeShareLocation(input.location, input.detectedZone, isEn);
  const cleanDate = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(input.dateFormatted);
  const split = computeEscrow4060Split(input.totalKc, false);
  const shareUrl = buildCleanEventDeepLink(input.eventId, cleanTitle);
  const discSuffix = cleanDisc ? ` (${cleanDisc})` : '';

  const message = isEn
    ? `Join me at KINETIC!\n\nSession: ${cleanTitle}${discSuffix}\nVenue: ${cleanClub} (${locDisplay})\nSchedule: ${cleanDate}\nInvestment: ${split.totalKc} KC (40% deposit today: ${split.escrow40} KC)\n\nBook your spot here:\n${shareUrl}`
    : `¡Entrena conmigo en KINETIC!\n\nSesión: ${cleanTitle}${discSuffix}\nSede: ${cleanClub} (${locDisplay})\nHorario: ${cleanDate}\nInversión: ${split.totalKc} KC (40% apartado hoy: ${split.escrow40} KC)\n\nReserva tu lugar aquí:\n${shareUrl}`;

  return {
    title: isEn ? `Kinetic — ${cleanTitle}` : `Kinetic — ${cleanTitle}`,
    message,
    url: shareUrl,
    locDisplay,
  };
};

export interface ICleanSquadShareInput {
  guestName?: unknown;
  eventId?: unknown;
  title: unknown;
  discipline?: unknown;
  clubName: unknown;
  location?: unknown;
  readableDate: unknown;
  splitCount: number;
  totalKc: number;
  hostId?: unknown;
  language?: string;
}

export const buildCleanSquadInviteMessage = (input: ICleanSquadShareInput) => {
  const isEn = input.language === 'en';
  const safeCount = Math.max(2, Math.min(20, Math.floor(Number(input.splitCount) || 2)));
  const cleanGuest = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(input.guestName);
  const cleanTitle = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(input.title) || (isEn ? 'Kinetic Session' : 'Sesión Kinetic');
  const cleanDisc = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(input.discipline) || 'Fitness';
  const cleanClub = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(input.clubName) || 'Kinetic Oficial';
  const locDisplay = sanitizeShareLocation(input.location, null, isEn);
  const cleanDate = stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(input.readableDate);
  const split = computeEscrow4060Split(input.totalKc, false);
  const split1N = safeCount <= 4 ? computeSplitEscrow1NMath(split.totalKc, safeCount, split.escrow40, false) : null;
  const perPersonEscrow = split1N ? split1N.effectiveEscrowKc : Math.ceil(split.escrow40 / safeCount);
  const perPersonDoor = split1N ? split1N.effectiveDoorKc : Math.ceil(split.puerta60 / safeCount);
  const deepLinkUrl = buildCleanSquadDeepLink(input.eventId, cleanTitle, safeCount, input.hostId);

  const greetingEs = cleanGuest ? `¡Hola ${cleanGuest}! ` : '';
  const greetingEn = cleanGuest ? `Hey ${cleanGuest}! ` : '';
  const message = isEn
    ? `${greetingEn}Join my KINETIC Team (${safeCount} members)!\n\nSession: ${cleanTitle} (${cleanDisc})\nVenue: ${cleanClub} (${locDisplay})\nSchedule: ${cleanDate}\nYour Protected Deposit (40%) share today: ${perPersonEscrow} KC (Total session: ${split.totalKc} KC • Front Desk Settlement (60%): ${perPersonDoor} KC)\n\nAccept or decline directly here:\n${deepLinkUrl}`
    : `${greetingEs}¡Únete a mi Equipo KINETIC (${safeCount} personas)!\n\nSesión: ${cleanTitle} (${cleanDisc})\nSede: ${cleanClub} (${locDisplay})\nHorario: ${cleanDate}\nTu parte del Anticipo Protegido (40%) hoy: ${perPersonEscrow} KC (Total sesión: ${split.totalKc} KC • Liquidación en Recepción (60%): ${perPersonDoor} KC)\n\nAcepta o rechaza directo aquí:\n${deepLinkUrl}`;

  return { message, deepLinkUrl, locDisplay, perPersonEscrow, perPersonDoor };
};

export interface IEventModalEscrowPreviewInput {
  rawPriceInt: number;
  discountedTotalKc: number;
  discountedEscrow40: number;
  discountedPuerta60: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  isCreateMode: boolean;
  isEditMode: boolean;
}

export const resolveEventModalEscrowPreview = (input: IEventModalEscrowPreviewInput) => {
  const isEditorFormMode = Boolean(input.isCreateMode || input.isEditMode);
  const applyAdminZeroPreview = Boolean(input.isAdmin && !isEditorFormMode);
  const safePrice = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(input.rawPriceInt) || 0));
  if (isEditorFormMode) {
    const escrow40 = Math.round(safePrice * 0.4);
    return {
      isEditorFormMode: true,
      applyAdminZeroPreview: false,
      totalKc: safePrice,
      escrow40,
      puerta60: Math.max(0, safePrice - escrow40),
    };
  }
  if (applyAdminZeroPreview) {
    return { isEditorFormMode: false, applyAdminZeroPreview: true, totalKc: safePrice, escrow40: 0, puerta60: 0 };
  }
  return {
    isEditorFormMode: false,
    applyAdminZeroPreview: false,
    totalKc: input.discountedTotalKc,
    escrow40: input.discountedEscrow40,
    puerta60: input.discountedPuerta60,
  };
};

export const computeEscrow4060Split = (rawTotalKc: number, isAdmin = false) => {
  const safeTotal = Number.isFinite(Number(rawTotalKc)) ? Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(rawTotalKc))) : 0;
  if (isAdmin || safeTotal === 0) {
    return { totalKc: 0, escrow40: 0, puerta60: 0 };
  }
  const escrow40 = Math.round(safeTotal * 0.4);
  const puerta60 = Math.max(0, safeTotal - escrow40);
  return { totalKc: escrow40 + puerta60, escrow40, puerta60 };
};

export const reconcileStudioOccupiedSpots = (
  liveOccupiedSpots: readonly number[] | null | undefined,
  maxCupo: number,
  occupiedCount: number,
  userPickedSpot?: number | null
) => {
  const safeMax = Number.isFinite(Number(maxCupo)) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(maxCupo))) : 0;
  if (safeMax <= 0) {
    return { occupiedSet: new Set<number>(), occupiedSpots: [] as number[], reconciledCount: 0, freeSpots: 0 };
  }
  const realSet = new Set<number>();
  if (Array.isArray(liveOccupiedSpots)) {
    for (const raw of liveOccupiedSpots) {
      const n = Math.floor(Number(raw));
      if (Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= safeMax) realSet.add(n);
    }
  }
  const safeEventCount = Number.isFinite(Number(occupiedCount)) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(occupiedCount))) : 0;
  const targetOccupied = Math.min(safeMax, Math.max(realSet.size, safeEventCount));
  const occupiedSet = new Set<number>(realSet);
  const picked = Number.isFinite(Number(userPickedSpot)) ? Math.floor(Number(userPickedSpot)) : null;

  for (let s = 1; s <= safeMax && occupiedSet.size < targetOccupied; s += 1) {
    if (!occupiedSet.has(s) && s !== picked) occupiedSet.add(s);
  }
  for (let s = 1; s <= safeMax && occupiedSet.size < targetOccupied; s += 1) {
    if (!occupiedSet.has(s)) occupiedSet.add(s);
  }

  const occupiedSpots = Array.from(occupiedSet).sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const reconciledCount = occupiedSpots.length;
  const freeSpots = Math.max(0, safeMax - reconciledCount);
  return { occupiedSet, occupiedSpots, reconciledCount, freeSpots };
};

export const buildCleanGalleryUrls = (
  coverUri?: string | null,
  galleryUris?: readonly (string | null | undefined)[] | null,
  fallbackPhotos?: readonly (string | null | undefined)[] | null
): string[] => {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const out: string[] = [];
  const pushClean = (u?: string | null) => {
    const clean = typeof u === 'string' ? u.trim() : '';
    if (!clean || clean.length < 5) return;
    if (seen.has(clean)) return;
    seen.add(clean);
    out.push(clean);
  };
  pushClean(coverUri);
  if (Array.isArray(galleryUris)) galleryUris.forEach(pushClean);
  if (Array.isArray(fallbackPhotos)) fallbackPhotos.forEach(pushClean);
  return out;
};

export const stepGalleryIndex = (currentIdx: number, delta: number, totalPhotos: number): number => {
  const len = Number.isFinite(Number(totalPhotos)) ? Math.floor(Number(totalPhotos)) : 0;
  if (len <= 1) return 0;
  const cur = Number.isFinite(Number(currentIdx)) ? Math.trunc(Number(currentIdx)) : 0;
  const step = Number.isFinite(Number(delta)) ? Math.trunc(Number(delta)) : 0;
  return (((cur + step) % len) + len) % len;
};

export const formatBookingConfirmedDate = (isoDate: string, timeStr: string, language = 'es'): string => {
  const cleanIso = String(isoDate || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  const cleanTime = String(timeStr || '08:00').trim().slice(0, 5) || '08:00';
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(cleanIso);
  if (!m) return `${cleanIso || '--'} • ${cleanTime} HRS`;
  const dt = new Date(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3]), 12, 0, 0);
  if (Number.isNaN(dt.getTime())) return `${cleanIso} • ${cleanTime} HRS`;
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const daysEn = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];
  const monthsEn = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];
  const daysEs = ['Dom', 'Lun', 'Mar', 'Mié', 'Jue', 'Vie', 'Sáb'];
  const monthsEs = ['ene', 'feb', 'mar', 'abr', 'may', 'jun', 'jul', 'ago', 'sep', 'oct', 'nov', 'dic'];
  const dayNum = dt.getDate();
  if (isEn) {
    return `${daysEn[dt.getDay()]}, ${monthsEn[dt.getMonth()]} ${dayNum} • ${cleanTime} HRS`;
  }
  return `${daysEs[dt.getDay()]}, ${dayNum} ${monthsEs[dt.getMonth()]} • ${cleanTime} HRS`;
};

export const translateAthleteTierLabel = (rawTier: unknown, language = 'es'): string => {
  const raw = String(rawTier || '').trim();
  const low = raw.toLowerCase();
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  if (!raw || low === 'acceso base' || low === 'base access' || low === 'base') {
    return isEn ? 'Base Access' : 'Acceso Base';
  }
  if (low === 'bronze' || low === 'bronce') return isEn ? 'Bronze' : 'Bronce';
  if (low === 'silver' || low === 'plata') return isEn ? 'Silver' : 'Plata';
  if (low === 'gold' || low === 'oro') return isEn ? 'Gold' : 'Oro';
  if (low === 'platinum' || low === 'platino') return isEn ? 'Platinum' : 'Platino';
  if (low === 'black') return 'Black';
  return raw;
};

export const translateSupabaseBookingError = (rawErr: unknown, language = 'es'): string => {
  const errObj = rawErr && typeof rawErr === 'object' ? (rawErr as { message?: unknown }) : null;
  const raw = String(errObj?.message ?? rawErr ?? '').trim();
  const low = raw.toLowerCase();
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  if (!raw) {
    return isEn
      ? 'Could not complete your booking right now. Please try again.'
      : 'No se pudo completar tu reserva en este momento. Intenta de nuevo.';
  }
  if (low.includes('horario_no_disponible') || low.includes('transcurrio') || low.includes('fecha limite') || low.includes('semanal solo') || low.includes('mensual solo') || low.includes('fecha unica')) {
    return isEn
      ? 'This session date or time is no longer open for booking in Los Cabos.'
      : raw.replace(/^HORARIO_NO_DISPONIBLE:\s*/i, '');
  }
  if (low.includes('saldo') || low.includes('insufficient') || low.includes('fondos') || low.includes('kineticoins')) {
    return isEn
      ? 'Insufficient KinetiCoins in your Vault to cover the 40% Protected Deposit.'
      : 'Saldo insuficiente de KinetiCoins en tu Bóveda para cubrir el Anticipo Protegido (40%).';
  }
  if (low.includes('cupo') || low.includes('agotad') || low.includes('full') || low.includes('lleno') || low.includes('spot')) {
    return isEn
      ? 'All spots for this session were just taken. Please choose another date or station.'
      : 'Los lugares de esta sesión se acaban de ocupar. Elige otro horario o estación.';
  }
  if (low.includes('duplicad') || low.includes('ya tienes') || low.includes('already')) {
    return isEn
      ? 'You already have an active pass for this session in your Vault.'
      : 'Ya cuentas con un pase activo para esta sesión en tu Bóveda.';
  }
  if (low.includes('jwt') || low.includes('auth.uid') || low.includes('sesion requerida') || low.includes('sesión requerida') || low.includes('session expired') || low.includes('token')) {
    return isEn
      ? 'Your cloud session expired. Please sign in again to generate your verified QR pass.'
      : 'Tu sesión en la nube expiró. Inicia sesión nuevamente para generar tu pase QR verificado.';
  }
  if (low.includes('custodia') || low.includes('escrow') || low.includes('bypass')) {
    return isEn
      ? 'Security verification required: please refresh your session to process this pass with Kinetic protection.'
      : 'Verificación de custodia requerida: actualiza tu sesión para procesar este pase bajo protección Kinetic.';
  }
  if (isEn) {
    return 'Could not process your booking on the server. Please verify your connection and try again.';
  }
  return raw;
};

export const shouldShowLastSpotsBadge = (ocupados: unknown, cupoMaximo: unknown): boolean => {
  if (ocupados === null || ocupados === undefined || ocupados === '' || typeof ocupados === 'boolean') return false;
  if (cupoMaximo === null || cupoMaximo === undefined || cupoMaximo === '' || typeof cupoMaximo === 'boolean') return false;
  const occ = Number(ocupados);
  const max = Number(cupoMaximo);
  if (!Number.isFinite(occ) || !Number.isFinite(max)) return false;
  const rem = max - occ;
  return occ > 0 && occ < max && rem > 0 && rem <= 3;
};

export const isEventEditorFormValid = (input: {
  titleVal?: unknown;
  discVal?: unknown;
  dateVal?: unknown;
  timeVal?: unknown;
  priceVal?: unknown;
  cupoVal?: unknown;
}): boolean => {
  if (!input || typeof input !== 'object') return false;
  if (typeof input.titleVal !== 'string' || input.titleVal.trim().length === 0) return false;
  if (typeof input.discVal !== 'string' || input.discVal.trim().length === 0) return false;
  const dateStr = typeof input.dateVal === 'string' ? input.dateVal.trim() : '';
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateStr)) return false;
  const timeStr = typeof input.timeVal === 'string' ? input.timeVal.trim() : '';
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}/.test(timeStr)) return false;
  const rawPrice =
    typeof input.priceVal === 'number'
      ? String(input.priceVal)
      : typeof input.priceVal === 'string'
        ? input.priceVal.trim().replace(/\s*kc$/i, '').replace(/,/g, '')
        : '';
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(rawPrice)) return false;
  const priceNum = Number(rawPrice);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(priceNum) || priceNum < 0 || priceNum > 3000 || priceNum % 10 !== 0) return false;
  const rawCupo =
    typeof input.cupoVal === 'number'
      ? String(input.cupoVal)
      : typeof input.cupoVal === 'string'
        ? input.cupoVal.trim()
        : '';
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(rawCupo)) return false;
  const cupoNum = Number(rawCupo);
  return Number.isSafeInteger(cupoNum) && cupoNum >= 1 && cupoNum <= 200;
};

const INACTIVE_LOCK_STATES = new Set([
  'inactivo', 'inactive', 'pausado', 'paused', 'borrador', 'draft',
  'archivado', 'archived', 'cancelado', 'cancelada', 'cancelled', 'canceled',
  'suspendido', 'suspended', 'eliminado', 'eliminada', 'rechazado', 'pendiente', 'pending',
]);

const normalizeHHMM = (rawHora: unknown): string | null => {
  const m = String(rawHora ?? '').trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\s*(a\.?m\.?|p\.?m\.?)?/i);
  if (!m) return null;
  let hh = parseInt(m[1], 10);
  const mm = Math.max(0, Math.min(59, parseInt(m[2], 10) || 0));
  const ampm = (m[3] || '').toLowerCase();
  if (ampm.startsWith('p') && hh < 12) hh += 12;
  if (ampm.startsWith('a') && hh === 12) hh = 0;
  if (!Number.isFinite(hh)) return null;
  return `${String(Math.max(0, Math.min(23, hh))).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(mm).padStart(2, '0')}`;
};

type EventLockRecord = { activo?: boolean; esActivo?: boolean; esClubActivo?: boolean; isServiceApproved?: boolean; isInactive?: boolean; isExpired?: boolean; estado?: unknown; clubEstado?: unknown; fecha_fin?: unknown; fecha_proyectada?: unknown; fecha?: unknown; hora_inicio?: unknown; hora?: unknown; frecuencia?: unknown; descripcion?: unknown; atributos?: { activo?: boolean; fecha_fin?: unknown; frecuencia?: unknown } | null } | null | undefined;

export const resolveEventBookingLockState = (
  ev: EventLockRecord,
  freqVal?: unknown,
  dateVal?: unknown,
  timeVal?: unknown,
  language?: string,
  nowRef?: Date
): { isLocked: boolean; isInactive: boolean; isExpiredOneTime: boolean; label: string } => {
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const st = String(ev?.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const clubSt = String(ev?.clubEstado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const isInactive =
    ev?.activo === false ||
    ev?.esActivo === false ||
    ev?.esClubActivo === false ||
    ev?.atributos?.activo === false ||
    ev?.isServiceApproved === false ||
    (ev?.isInactive === true && ev?.isExpired !== true) ||
    INACTIVE_LOCK_STATES.has(st) ||
    INACTIVE_LOCK_STATES.has(clubSt);
  if (isInactive) {
    return { isLocked: true, isInactive: true, isExpiredOneTime: false, label: isEn ? 'UNAVAILABLE' : 'NO DISPONIBLE' };
  }
  const mz = getMazatlanNowParts(nowRef);
  const endDateIso = String(ev?.fecha_fin || ev?.atributos?.fecha_fin || '').slice(0, 10);
  const pastEndDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(endDateIso) && endDateIso < mz.isoDate;
  if (pastEndDate) {
    return { isLocked: true, isInactive: false, isExpiredOneTime: true, label: isEn ? 'SESSION ENDED' : 'SESIÓN FINALIZADA' };
  }
  const freqRaw = String(freqVal ?? ev?.frecuencia ?? ev?.atributos?.frecuencia ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const descFreq = String(ev?.descripcion || '').match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*(.*?)\]/i)?.[1]?.trim().toLowerCase() || '';
  const isRecurring = /diario|diaria|semanal|mensual|daily|weekly|monthly/.test(`${freqRaw} ${descFreq}`);
  if (!isRecurring) {
    const isoDate = String(dateVal ?? ev?.fecha_proyectada ?? ev?.fecha ?? '').slice(0, 10);
    const rawHora = timeVal ?? ev?.hora_inicio ?? ev?.hora;
    const hhmm = normalizeHHMM(rawHora);
    const expiredDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(isoDate) && (isoDate < mz.isoDate || (isoDate === mz.isoDate && Boolean(hhmm) && hhmm! < mz.hhmm));
    if ((dateVal == null && ev?.isExpired === true) || expiredDate) {
      return { isLocked: true, isInactive: false, isExpiredOneTime: true, label: isEn ? 'SESSION ENDED' : 'SESIÓN FINALIZADA' };
    }
  }
  return { isLocked: false, isInactive: false, isExpiredOneTime: false, label: '' };
};
