    // === src/components/eventos/EventEditorB2BView.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo } from 'react';
import { ScrollView, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { normalizeAgePolicy, normalizeEnvironmentLabel } from '../../constants/kineticEventCatalogs';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { eventsService } from '../../services/eventsService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { ActiveSheetType, translateEventCatalogTerm } from '../../utils/eventEditorHelpers';
import { openExternalLinkSafe } from '../../utils/externalLinks';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { KineticLoader, SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { baseEd } from './EventEditorB2BView.styles';
export { isEventEditorFormValid } from './eventDetailsPureHelpers';
import {
  IStudioLayoutConfig,
  formatStudioCapacityLabel,
  formatStudioLayoutButtonLabel,
  getDisciplineEventTitlePlaceholder,
} from './KineticStudioLayoutModal';

export const MAX_EXTRA_EVENT_PHOTOS = 10;

export interface IEventEditorB2BViewProps {
  titleVal: string;
  setTitleVal: (v: string) => void;
  discVal: string;
  freqVal: string;
  dateVal: string;
  timeVal: string;
  durVal: string;
  coachVal: string;
  setCoachVal: (v: string) => void;
  locVal: string;
  handleLocInput: (v: string) => void;
  locCoords?: { lat: number | null; lng: number | null };
  detectedZone?: string | null;
  cupoVal: string;
  reqsVal: string[];
  ageVal: string;
  priceVal: string;
  escrow40: number;
  puerta60: number;
  descVal: string;
  setDescVal: (v: string) => void;
  attrInt: string;
  attrEnf: string;
  attrImp: string;
  attrNiv: string;
  attrEnt: string;
  galleryUris: string[];
  activePhotoIdx: number;
  setActivePhotoIdx: (idx: number) => void;
  standardDisciplineImage: string;
  customCoverUri?: string;
  pickCoverImage?: () => void;
  removeCustomCover?: () => void;
  pickGalleryImages: () => void;
  removeGalleryPhotoAt: (idx: number) => void;
  openInternalSheet: (sheet: ActiveSheetType) => void;
  isSaving: boolean;
  saveExperience: () => void;
  isCreateMode: boolean;
  currentEvId?: string;
  onClose?: () => void;
  setIsEditMode: (v: boolean) => void;
  cancelEditMode?: () => void;
  triggerAlert: (title: string, message: string, type?: string, onConfirm?: (() => void) | null) => void;
  onRefreshEvents?: () => Promise<void> | void;
  studioLayout?: IStudioLayoutConfig | null;
  onOpenStudioConfig?: () => void;
  permiteDividirCuenta?: boolean;
  setPermiteDividirCuenta?: (v: boolean) => void;
  maxDivisionPersonas?: number;
  setMaxDivisionPersonas?: (v: number) => void;
}

export const EventEditorB2BView = memo((props: IEventEditorB2BViewProps) => {
  const {
    titleVal, setTitleVal, discVal, freqVal, dateVal, timeVal, durVal, coachVal, setCoachVal,
    locVal, handleLocInput, locCoords, detectedZone, cupoVal, reqsVal, ageVal, escrow40, puerta60,
    descVal, setDescVal, attrInt, attrEnf, attrImp, attrNiv, attrEnt,
    galleryUris, activePhotoIdx, setActivePhotoIdx, standardDisciplineImage,
    customCoverUri, pickCoverImage, removeCustomCover,
    pickGalleryImages, removeGalleryPhotoAt, openInternalSheet, isSaving, saveExperience,
    isCreateMode, currentEvId, onClose, setIsEditMode, triggerAlert, onRefreshEvents,
    studioLayout, onOpenStudioConfig,
  } = props;

  const { resolvedTheme, themeColors, language, tx, tDisc } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const ed = useMemo(
    () => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseEd, resolvedTheme, ['attrBtn3D', 'attrValPill3D', 'primaryCyanBtn', 'primaryCyanTxt']),
    [resolvedTheme]
  );
  const fieldLight = isLight ? { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' } : null;
  const cardLight = isLight ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' } : null;
  const cleanEnv = normalizeEnvironmentLabel(attrEnt);

  return (
    <>
      <TextInput
        style={[ed.titleInput, { fontSize: 16 }, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', color: '#0F172A', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }]}
        value={titleVal}
        onChangeText={setTitleVal}
        placeholder={getDisciplineEventTitlePlaceholder(discVal, language === 'en')}
        placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
      />
      <View style={[ed.cardBox, cardLight]}>
        <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('DISCIPLINA Y FRECUENCIA', 'DISCIPLINE & FREQUENCY')}</Text>
        <View style={ed.row8}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.fieldBtn, fieldLight]} onPress={() => openInternalSheet('disciplina')}>
            <Feather name="tag" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{tDisc(discVal)}</Text>
            <Feather name="chevron-down" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.fieldBtn, fieldLight]} onPress={() => openInternalSheet('frecuencia')}>
            <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{translateEventCatalogTerm(freqVal, language)}</Text>
            <Feather name="repeat" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
          {tx('FECHA (MAÑANA A +3 MESES), HORARIO Y DURACIÓN', 'DATE (TOMORROW TO +3 MONTHS), TIME & DURATION')}
        </Text>
        <View style={ed.row8}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.fieldBtn, { flex: 1.15 }, fieldLight]} onPress={() => openInternalSheet('fecha')}>
            <Feather name="calendar" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{dateVal}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.fieldBtn, { flex: 0.85 }, fieldLight]} onPress={() => openInternalSheet('hora')}>
            <Feather name="clock" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{timeVal}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.fieldBtn, { flex: 0.8 }, fieldLight]} onPress={() => openInternalSheet('duracion')}>
            <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{durVal} min</Text>
            <Feather name="chevron-down" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('COACH / ESPECIALISTA A CARGO', 'LEAD COACH / SPECIALIST')}</Text>
        <View style={[ed.fieldBtn, { marginBottom: 10 }, fieldLight]}>
          <Feather name="user" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <TextInput
            style={[ed.inlineInput, { flex: 1, marginLeft: 8, fontSize: 16, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
            value={coachVal}
            onChangeText={setCoachVal}
            placeholder={tx('Nombre del Coach', 'Coach Name')}
            placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
          />
        </View>

        <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('UBICACIÓN / LINK DE GOOGLE MAPS', 'LOCATION / GOOGLE MAPS LINK')}</Text>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[
            ed.mapsGetLinkBtn3D,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' },
          ]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={() => {
            triggerHaptic('Light');
            openExternalLinkSafe('https://www.google.com/maps');
          }}
        >
          <Text style={[ed.mapsGetLinkTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {tx('📍 Toca para obtener link en Google Maps', '📍 Tap to get link on Google Maps')}
          </Text>
          <Feather name="external-link" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <View style={[ed.fieldBtn, { marginBottom: detectedZone || locCoords?.lat != null ? 6 : 10 }, fieldLight]}>
          <Feather name="map-pin" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <TextInput
            style={[ed.inlineInput, { flex: 1, marginLeft: 8, fontSize: 16, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
            value={locVal}
            onChangeText={handleLocInput}
            placeholder={tx('Pega aquí el link de Google Maps...', 'Paste the Google Maps link here...')}
            placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
            autoCapitalize="none"
            autoCorrect={false}
          />
        </View>

        {(detectedZone || (locCoords?.lat != null && locCoords?.lng != null)) && (
          <View style={[ed.detectedGeoBadge, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFDF5', borderColor: '#10B981' }]}>
            <Feather name="check-circle" size={12} color={themeColors.green} />
            <Text style={[ed.detectedGeoTxt, { color: themeColors.green }]} numberOfLines={1}>
              {detectedZone
                ? `${tx('Lugar detectado:', 'Detected place:')} ${detectedZone}`
                : `PIN GPS: ${Number(locCoords?.lat).toFixed(4)}, ${Number(locCoords?.lng).toFixed(4)}`}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}

        <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('AFORO MÁXIMO Y MAPA 3D DEL ESTUDIO', 'MAX CAPACITY & 3D STUDIO MAP')}</Text>
        <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.fieldBtn, { marginBottom: 8 }, fieldLight]} onPress={() => openInternalSheet('cupo')}>
          <Feather name="users" size={14} color={themeColors.purpleText} />
          <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
            {formatStudioCapacityLabel(cupoVal, studioLayout, discVal, titleVal, tx)}
          </Text>
          <Feather name="chevron-down" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
        {onOpenStudioConfig && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[ed.studioCfgBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }]}
            onPress={onOpenStudioConfig}
          >
            <Feather name="grid" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
              {tx(
                `DISEÑAR MAPA 3D (${formatStudioLayoutButtonLabel(studioLayout, cupoVal, discVal, titleVal, false)})`,
                `DESIGN 3D STUDIO MAP (${formatStudioLayoutButtonLabel(studioLayout, cupoVal, discVal, titleVal, true)})`
              )}
            </Text>
            <Feather name="sliders" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}

        <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('QUÉ TRAER Y POLÍTICA DE EDAD', 'WHAT TO BRING & AGE POLICY')}</Text>
        <View style={ed.row8}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.fieldBtn, { flex: 1.3, height: 'auto', minHeight: 46, paddingVertical: 8 }, fieldLight]} onPress={() => openInternalSheet('requisitos')}>
            <Feather name="briefcase" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { fontSize: 12, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={2}>
              {reqsVal.map((r) => translateEventCatalogTerm(r, language)).join(', ')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.fieldBtn, { flex: 0.9, height: 'auto', minHeight: 46, paddingVertical: 8 }, fieldLight]} onPress={() => openInternalSheet('edad')}>
            <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { fontSize: 12, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={2}>
              {translateEventCatalogTerm(normalizeAgePolicy(ageVal), language)}
            </Text>
            <Feather name="chevron-down" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit minimumFontScale={0.84}>
          {tx('INVERSIÓN Y APARTADO 40% HOY • 60% AL LLEGAR (0\u00A0A\u00A03,000\u00A0KC)', 'INVESTMENT & 40% DEPOSIT TODAY • 60% ON ARRIVAL (0\u00A0TO\u00A03,000\u00A0KC)')}
        </Text>
        <TouchableOpacity style={[ed.fieldBtn, { height: 'auto', minHeight: 46, paddingVertical: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }, fieldLight]} onPress={() => openInternalSheet('precio')}>
          <Ionicons name="flash" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text style={[ed.fieldBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, flexShrink: 1, flexWrap: 'wrap' }]} numberOfLines={2}>
            {escrow40 + puerta60} KC ({tx('Custodia 40%', 'Escrow 40%')}: {escrow40} KC • {tx('Puerta 60%', 'Door 60%')}: {puerta60} KC)
          </Text>
          <Feather name="chevron-down" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <View
          style={[
            ed.splitBox,
            !props.permiteDividirCuenta && ed.splitBoxOff,
            isLight && (props.permiteDividirCuenta
              ? { backgroundColor: '#FAF5FF', borderColor: '#C4B5FD', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED' }
              : { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }),
          ]}
        >
          <TouchableOpacity
            activeOpacity={0.85}
            style={ed.splitTopRow}
            onPress={() => {
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              const next = !props.permiteDividirCuenta;
              props.setPermiteDividirCuenta?.(next);
              if (next && (!props.maxDivisionPersonas || props.maxDivisionPersonas < 2)) {
                props.setMaxDivisionPersonas?.(4);
              }
            }}
          >
            <View style={{ flex: 1, paddingRight: 6 }}>
              <Text style={[ed.splitTitle, { color: props.permiteDividirCuenta ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                {tx('Dividir Cuenta (Split-Escrow 2 a 4 personas)', 'Split Bill (Split-Escrow 2 to 4 people)')}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[ed.splitSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {props.permiteDividirCuenta
                  ? tx(`Equipo habilitado hasta ${props.maxDivisionPersonas || 4} personas en el 40% Escrow.`, `Team enabled up to ${props.maxDivisionPersonas || 4} people on the 40% Escrow.`)
                  : tx('Oculto en la tarjeta de evento a menos que lo actives.', 'Hidden on event view unless enabled here.')}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View
              style={[
                ed.splitSwitchPill,
                props.permiteDividirCuenta
                  ? { backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.16)', borderColor: COLORS.cyan, borderTopColor: '#B8FFFF', borderBottomColor: '#006B78' }
                  : { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#E2E8F0' : '#0B1320', borderColor: isLight ? '#94A3B8' : '#334155', borderTopColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#475569', borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#64748B' : '#020617' },
              ]}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: props.permiteDividirCuenta ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted }}>
                {props.permiteDividirCuenta ? tx('ACTIVO', 'ENABLED') : tx('DESACTIVADO', 'DISABLED')}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          {Boolean(props.permiteDividirCuenta) && (
            <View style={ed.splitCountRow}>
              {[2, 3, 4].map((n) => {
                const active = (props.maxDivisionPersonas || 4) === n;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={n}
                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                    onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); props.setMaxDivisionPersonas?.(n); }}
                    style={[
                      ed.splitCountPill,
                      active
                        ? { backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan, borderColor: '#B8FFFF', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#006B78' }
                        : { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0B111E', borderColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : '#1E293B', borderTopColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#334155', borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#94A3B8' : '#020617' },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text style={[ed.splitCountPillTxt, { color: active ? '#050505' : themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                      {tx(`Máx. ${n} Pers.`, `Max ${n} Pax`)}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={[ed.cardBox, cardLight]}>
        <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{tx('SOBRE EL EVENTO', 'ABOUT THIS EVENT')}</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={[ed.areaInput, fieldLight, { fontSize: 16, color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}
          multiline
          value={descVal}
          onChangeText={setDescVal}
          placeholder={tx('Describe la sesión...', 'Describe this session...')}
          placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
        />
      </View>

      <View style={[ed.cardBox, cardLight]}>
        <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted, marginBottom: 8 }]}>
          {tx('ATRIBUTOS TÉCNICOS', 'TECHNICAL ATTRIBUTES')}
        </Text>
        {[
          { label: tx('INTENSIDAD', 'INTENSITY'), val: attrInt, key: 'intensidad' as const },
          { label: tx('ENFOQUE', 'FOCUS'), val: attrEnf, key: 'enfoque' as const },
          { label: tx('IMPACTO', 'IMPACT'), val: attrImp, key: 'impacto' as const },
          { label: tx('NIVEL', 'LEVEL'), val: attrNiv, key: 'nivel' as const },
          { label: tx('ENTORNO', 'ENVIRONMENT'), val: cleanEnv, key: 'entorno' as const },
        ].map((at) => (
          <TouchableOpacity
            key={at.key}
            activeOpacity={0.84}
            style={[ed.attrBtn3D, isLight && baseEd.attrBtn3DLight]}
            onPress={() => openInternalSheet(at.key)}
          >
            <Text style={[ed.attrLbl, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{at.label}</Text>
            <View style={[ed.attrValPill3D, isLight && baseEd.attrValPill3DLight]}>
              <Text style={[ed.attrVal, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {translateEventCatalogTerm(at.val, language)}
              </Text>
              <Feather name="chevron-right" size={13} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            </View>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ))}
      </View>

      <Text style={[ed.lbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
        {tx(`FOTOGRAFÍAS DEL EVENTO (${galleryUris.length}/${MAX_EXTRA_EVENT_PHOTOS} ADICIONALES)`, `EVENT PHOTOS (${galleryUris.length}/${MAX_EXTRA_EVENT_PHOTOS} EXTRA)`)}
      </Text>
      <ScrollView horizontal nestedScrollEnabled={true} removeClippedSubviews={false} showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 10, marginBottom: 18 }}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[ed.photoThumb, activePhotoIdx === 0 && { borderColor: COLORS.cyan, borderWidth: 2 }]}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          onPress={() => {
            setActivePhotoIdx(0);
            pickCoverImage?.();
          }}
        >
          <SmartImage source={{ uri: customCoverUri || standardDisciplineImage }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" fallbackCategory={discVal} />
          <View style={ed.fixedCoverBadge}>
            <Feather name="camera" size={9} color="#050505" />
            <Text style={ed.fixedCoverBadgeTxt}>{customCoverUri ? tx('PORTADA #1 CDN', 'COVER #1 CDN') : tx('SUBIR PORTADA #1', 'UPLOAD COVER #1')}</Text>
          </View>
          {Boolean(customCoverUri && removeCustomCover) && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={ed.removePhotoMiniBtn}
              onPress={(e: any) => {
                e?.stopPropagation?.();
                removeCustomCover?.();
              }}
            >
              <Feather name="x" size={12} color="#FFFFFF" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </TouchableOpacity>
        {galleryUris.length < MAX_EXTRA_EVENT_PHOTOS && (
          <TouchableOpacity style={ed.photoBtn} onPress={pickGalleryImages}>
            <Feather name="camera" size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[ed.photoBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {galleryUris.length > 0 ? tx('+ AGREGAR FOTOS', '+ ADD PHOTOS') : tx('+ SUBIR FOTOS', '+ UPLOAD PHOTOS')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
        {galleryUris.map((uriItem, idx) => (
          <TouchableOpacity key={`gal_${uriItem}_${idx}`} style={[ed.photoThumb, activePhotoIdx === idx + 1 && { borderColor: COLORS.green, borderWidth: 2 }]} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => setActivePhotoIdx(idx + 1)}>
            <SmartImage source={{ uri: uriItem }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
            <TouchableOpacity style={ed.removePhotoMiniBtn} onPress={() => removeGalleryPhotoAt(idx)}>
              <Feather name="x" size={12} color="#FFFFFF" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>

      <TouchableOpacity style={ed.primaryCyanBtn} disabled={isSaving} onPress={saveExperience}>
        {isSaving ? (
          <>
            <KineticLoader size={18} color="#050505" />
            <Text style={ed.primaryCyanTxt}>
              {tx('⚡ PUBLICANDO EVENTO...', '⚡ PUBLISHING EVENT...')}
            </Text>
          </>
        ) : (
          <Text style={ed.primaryCyanTxt}>
            {isCreateMode ? tx('PUBLICAR EVENTO EN RADAR', 'PUBLISH EVENT TO RADAR') : tx('GUARDAR Y ACTIVAR EVENTO', 'SAVE & ACTIVATE EVENT')}
          </Text>
        )}
      </TouchableOpacity>

      {!isSaving && (
        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10 }}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[ed.cancelEditBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]}
            onPress={() => {
              if (isCreateMode) {
                onClose?.();
              } else if (props.cancelEditMode) {
                props.cancelEditMode();
              } else {
                setIsEditMode(false);
              }
            }}
          >
            <Feather name="arrow-left" size={14} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
            <Text style={[ed.cancelEditTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {isCreateMode ? tx('SALIR DE CREACIÓN', 'EXIT CREATION') : tx('CANCELAR EDICIÓN', 'CANCEL EDIT')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
          {!isCreateMode && currentEvId && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={ed.deleteRedBtn}
              onPress={() =>
                triggerAlert(
                  tx('ELIMINAR EVENTO', 'DELETE EVENT'),
                  tx(`¿Confirmas eliminar "${titleVal}"?`, `Confirm deleting "${titleVal}"?`),
                  'confirm',
                  async () => {
                    try {
                      await eventsService.eliminarEventoConReembolso(String(currentEvId));
                      emitKineticRadarUpdated();
                      await onRefreshEvents?.();
                      onClose?.();
                    } catch (err: any) {
                      triggerAlert(
                        tx('ERROR AL ELIMINAR', 'DELETE ERROR'),
                        err?.message ||
                          tx(
                            'No se pudo eliminar el evento ni procesar los reembolsos Escrow.',
                            'Could not delete event or process Escrow refunds.'
                          ),
                        'error'
                      );
                    }
                  }
                )
              }
            >
              <Feather name="trash-2" size={14} color={COLORS.red} />
              <Text style={ed.deleteRedTxt}>{tx('ELIMINAR EVENTO', 'DELETE EVENT')}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </View>
      )}
    </>
  );
});

    FILE: src/components/eventos/EventFullscreenGalleryOverlay.tsx

    // === src/components/eventos/EventFullscreenGalleryOverlay.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { PanResponder, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { setKineticInlineOverlayOpen } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { buildCleanGalleryUrls, stepGalleryIndex } from './eventDetailsPureHelpers';

export interface IEventFullscreenGalleryModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  coverUri?: string | null;
  galleryUris?: readonly (string | null | undefined)[] | null;
  images?: readonly (string | null | undefined)[] | null;
  initialIndex?: number;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const EventFullscreenGalleryModal = memo(
  ({ visible, coverUri, galleryUris, images, initialIndex = 0, onClose }: IEventFullscreenGalleryModalProps) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { tx } = useKineticPreferences();
    const photos = useMemo(
      () => buildCleanGalleryUrls(images?.length ? null : coverUri, images, galleryUris),
      [coverUri, galleryUris, images]
    );
    const [activeIdx, setActiveIdx] = useState<number>(() =>
      stepGalleryIndex(initialIndex, 0, Math.max(1, photos.length))
    );
    const swipedByPanRef = useRef<number>(0);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (!visible) return;
      setActiveIdx(stepGalleryIndex(initialIndex, 0, Math.max(1, photos.length)));
      setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(true);
      return () => setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(false);
    }, [visible, initialIndex, photos.length]);

    const handleStep = useCallback(
      (delta: -1 | 1) => {
        if (photos.length <= 1) return;
        triggerHaptic('Selection');
        setActiveIdx((prev) => stepGalleryIndex(prev, delta, photos.length));
      },
      [photos.length]
    );

    const panResponder = useMemo(
      () =>
        PanResponder.create({
          onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: (_, g) =>
            photos.length > 1 && Math.abs(g.dx) > 12 && Math.abs(g.dx) > Math.abs(g.dy) * 1.1,
          onPanResponderRelease: (_, g) => {
            if (photos.length > 1 && Math.abs(g.dx) > 30) {
              swipedByPanRef.current = Date.now();
              handleStep(g.dx < 0 ? 1 : -1);
            }
          },
        }),
      [photos.length, handleStep]
    );

    if (!visible || photos.length === 0) return null;

    const safeIdx = stepGalleryIndex(activeIdx, 0, photos.length);
    const activePhotoUri = photos[safeIdx] || photos[0];

    return (
      <View
        style={[
          st.galleryRoot,
          { paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 14) + 6, paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 18) + 12 },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={st.galleryTopBar}>
          <View style={st.counterPill}>
            <Text selectable={false} style={st.counterTxt}>
              {tx(`FOTO ${safeIdx + 1} DE ${photos.length}`, `PHOTO ${safeIdx + 1} OF ${photos.length}`)}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <TouchableOpacity style={st.closeRoundBtn} onPress={onClose} activeOpacity={0.85} hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}>
            <Feather name="x" size={19} color="#FFFFFF" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={st.stageWrap} {...panResponder.panHandlers}>
          <Image
            key={`gallery-slide-${safeIdx}-${activePhotoUri}`}
            source={{ uri: activePhotoUri }}
            style={st.stageImg}
            contentFit="contain"
            transition={120}
          />
          {photos.length > 1 && (
            <>
              <TouchableOpacity style={[st.arrowBtn, { left: 12 }]} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => handleStep(-1)}>
                <Feather name="chevron-left" size={22} color={COLORS.cyan} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity style={[st.arrowBtn, { right: 12 }]} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => handleStep(1)}>
                <Feather name="chevron-right" size={22} color={COLORS.cyan} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </>
          )}
        </View>

        {photos.length > 1 && (
          <View style={st.dotsRow}>
            {photos.map((uri, idx) => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={`dot_${idx}_${uri}`}
                onPress={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('Selection');
                  setActiveIdx(idx);
                }}
                hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 6, right: 6 }}
                style={[st.dot, idx === safeIdx && st.dotActive]}
              />
            ))}
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

const st = StyleSheet.create({
  galleryRoot: {
    position: 'absolute',
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    zIndex: 999,
    backgroundColor: '#030509',
    paddingHorizontal: 14,
  },
  galleryTopBar: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  counterPill: {
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderRadius: 999,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)',
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.5)',
    elevation: 0,
  },
  counterTxt: { color: COLORS.cyan, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.7 },
  closeRoundBtn: {
    width: 40,
    height: 40,
    borderRadius: 20,
    backgroundColor: '#0F172A',
    borderWidth: 1.2,
    borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.32)',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    elevation: 0,
  },
  stageWrap: { flex: 1, justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', position: 'relative' },
  stageImg: { width: '100%', height: '100%', borderRadius: 16 },
  arrowBtn: {
    position: 'absolute',
    top: '50%',
    marginTop: -22,
    width: 44,
    height: 44,
    borderRadius: 22,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(8, 12, 20, 0.88)',
    borderWidth: 1.3,
    borderColor: COLORS.cyan,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    elevation: 0,
  },
  dotsRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, paddingVertical: 12 },
  dot: { width: 8, height: 8, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.4)', elevation: 0 },
  dotActive: { width: 22, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan },
});

    FILE: src/components/eventos/EventGalleryAndRatingModals.tsx

import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Platform,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { IRealClubReview, fetchRealClubReviewsFromSupabase } from '../../services/clubSheetPersistenceHelper';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { setKineticInlineOverlayOpen } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { KineticErgonomicFooter } from '../shared/KineticErgonomicFooter';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { EventFullscreenGalleryModal, IEventFullscreenGalleryModalProps } from './EventFullscreenGalleryOverlay';

export { EventFullscreenGalleryModal };
export type { IEventFullscreenGalleryModalProps };

export interface EventRatingAuditTarget {
  rating?: number;
  nombre_servicio?: string;
  titulo?: string;
  title?: string;
  afiliado_id?: string | null;
  club_id?: string | null;
  negocio_nombre?: string | null;
  club?: string | null;
}

type RatingAuditListener = (eventItem: EventRatingAuditTarget) => void;
let _globalRatingOpener: RatingAuditListener | null = null;

export const registerGlobalEventRatingAuditOpener = (fn: RatingAuditListener | null) => {
  _globalRatingOpener = fn;
  return () => {
    if (_globalRatingOpener === fn) _globalRatingOpener = null;
  };
};

export const openGlobalEventRatingAudit = (eventItem: EventRatingAuditTarget): void => {
  _globalRatingOpener?.(eventItem);
};

export const EventUnifiedRatingAuditContent = memo(
  ({
    ratingValue,
    eventTitle,
    clubId,
    clubName,
    onScrollYChange,
  }: {
    ratingValue?: number;
    eventTitle?: string;
    clubId?: string | null;
    clubName?: string | null;
    onScrollYChange?: (y: number) => void;
  }) => {
    const { tx, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const score = Number(ratingValue || 5.0).toFixed(1);
    const [realReviews, setRealReviews] = useState<IRealClubReview[]>([]);

    useEffect(() => {
      let active = true;
      fetchRealClubReviewsFromSupabase(clubId || null, clubName || null)
        .then((rows) => {
          if (active) setRealReviews(Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []);
        })
        .catch(() => {
          if (active) setRealReviews([]);
        });
      return () => { active = false; };
    }, [clubId, clubName]);

    const pillars = useMemo(
      () => [
        { label: tx('1. Equipamiento e Infraestructura', '1. Equipment & Infrastructure'), val: '5.0' },
        { label: tx('2. Certificación Técnica del Coach', '2. Technical Coach Certification'), val: '5.0' },
        { label: tx('3. Puntualidad y Control de Aforo', '3. Punctuality & Capacity Control'), val: '5.0' },
        { label: tx('4. Higiene, Seguridad y Clima', '4. Hygiene, Safety & Climate'), val: '5.0' },
        { label: tx('5. Protección Anticipo 40/60 en Puerta', '5. Front-Desk 40/60 Booking Protection'), val: '5.0' },
      ],
      [tx]
    );

    return (
      <ScrollView
        style={{ flex: 1 }}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 14, paddingTop: 8 }}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={true}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
        onScroll={(e) => onScrollYChange?.(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y)}
      >
        <Text style={[st.auditSheetTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
          {tx('Auditoría Técnica Oficial y Reseñas', 'Official Technical Audit & Reviews')}
        </Text>
        {eventTitle ? (
          <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '700', textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 8 }} numberOfLines={2}>
            {eventTitle}
          </Text>
        ) : null}

        <View
          style={[
            st.scoreHeroCard,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 8, paddingTop: 6 }}>
            <Feather name="star" size={26} color={COLORS.yellow} />
            <Text
              style={{
                color: themeColors.textPrimary,
                fontSize: 34,
                lineHeight: 44,
                fontWeight: '900',
                fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'],
              }}
            >
              {score}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.7, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 2 }}>
            {tx('ESTÁNDAR OFICIAL VERIFICADO 5.0 ★ KINETIC', 'KINETIC 5.0 ★ VERIFIED OFFICIAL STANDARD')}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <View
          style={[
            st.sectionCard,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
          ]}
        >
          <Text style={[st.secHeader, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
            {tx('DESGLOSE DE AUDITORÍA EN 5 PILARES', '5-PILLAR TECHNICAL AUDIT BREAKDOWN')}
          </Text>
          {pillars.map((p) => (
            <View key={p.label} style={st.pillarRow}>
              <Text style={{ flex: 1, color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '700' }}>{p.label}</Text>
              <View style={st.pillarScorePill}>
                <Feather name="star" size={10} color={COLORS.yellow} />
                <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900' }}>{p.val}</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>

        <View
          style={[
            st.sectionCard,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
          ]}
        >
          <Text style={[st.secHeader, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
            {tx('RESEÑAS VERIFICADAS CON CHECK-IN QR', 'QR CHECK-IN VERIFIED REVIEWS')}
          </Text>
          {realReviews.length === 0 ? (
            <View style={{ paddingVertical: 8, gap: 4 }}>
              <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, lineHeight: 17, fontWeight: '700' }}>
                {tx(
                  'Sin reseñas de atletas aún — Las calificaciones se habilitan únicamente tras validar Pase QR en recepción.',
                  'No athlete reviews yet — Ratings unlock exclusively after front-desk QR check-in.'
                )}
              </Text>
            </View>
          ) : (
            realReviews.slice(0, 8).map((rev) => (
              <View key={rev.id} style={[st.reviewItem, isLight && { borderTopColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 3 }}>
                  <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900' }}>{rev.author}</Text>
                  <Text style={{ color: themeColors.amberText, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900' }}>
                    {'★'.repeat(Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(rev.stars))))}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {Boolean(rev.comment) && (
                  <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textSecondary, fontSize: 11.5, lineHeight: 16, fontWeight: '600' }}>
                    {rev.comment}
                  </Text>
                )}
              </View>
            ))
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    );
  }
);

export const EventUnifiedRatingAuditModal = memo(
  ({ visible, eventItem, onClose }: { visible: boolean; eventItem: EventRatingAuditTarget | null; onClose: () => void }) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { tx, td, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const [scrollY, setScrollY] = useState(0);
    useEffect(() => {
      if (visible) setScrollY(0);
    }, [visible]);
    if (!visible) return null;
    const rawTitle = eventItem?.nombre_servicio || eventItem?.titulo || '';
    const titleStr = rawTitle ? td(rawTitle) : '';

    return (
      <ReactNativeModal
        isVisible={visible}
        onBackButtonPress={onClose}
        onBackdropPress={onClose}
        swipeDirection={scrollY <= 0 ? ['down'] : undefined}
        onSwipeComplete={onClose}
        propagateSwipe
        style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
        backdropColor="#050505"
        backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.55 : 0.88}
        animationInTiming={180}
        animationOutTiming={160}
        backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
        backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
        hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
        useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
        useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            globalStyles.bottomSheet,
            KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
            st.auditModalSheet,
            isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
            { paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 14 : 20, overflow: 'hidden' },
          ]}
        >
          <View
            style={{
              backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#080C14',
              zIndex: 20,
              paddingBottom: 6,
              borderBottomWidth: scrollY > 4 ? 1 : 0,
              borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#CBD5E1' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.16)',
            }}
          >
            <KineticSwipeCloseHeader visible={Boolean(visible)} onClose={onClose} style={{ marginBottom: 0 }} />
          </View>
          <EventUnifiedRatingAuditContent
            ratingValue={eventItem?.rating || 5.0}
            eventTitle={titleStr}
            clubId={eventItem?.afiliado_id || eventItem?.club_id || null}
            clubName={eventItem?.negocio_nombre || eventItem?.club || null}
            onScrollYChange={setScrollY}
          />
          <KineticErgonomicFooter onBack={onClose} style={{ marginHorizontal: -16 }} />
        </View>
      </ReactNativeModal>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/eventos/EventosAgendaCalendarSection.tsx

    // === src/components/eventos/EventosAgendaCalendarSection.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Animated, PanResponder, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { COLORS, SPACING } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { calendarStyles } from './EventosAgendaCalendar.styles';
import {
  AGENDA_MEDIA_LOCK_KEYS,
  AgendaEventBanner,
  MONTHS_LABELS,
  WEEK_INITIALS,
} from './EventosFlashAndAgendaViews';
import { s as bannerBaseStyles } from './EventosFlashAndAgendaViews.styles';

type SwipeGestureResponder = ReturnType<typeof PanResponder.create>;
interface CalendarGridCell {
  key?: string;
  iso?: string;
  dayNum?: number;
  count?: number;
  isPast?: boolean;
}
interface CalendarEventRow extends Record<string, unknown> {
  id?: string | number;
  fecha_proyectada?: string;
  fecha?: string;
}

const baseStyles = { ...bannerBaseStyles, ...calendarStyles };
const NO_SEL = Platform.OS === 'web'
  ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as Record<string, string>)
  : null;

export const EventosAgendaCalendarSection = memo(
  ({
    monthOpen, setMonthOpen, activeMonth, stepMonth, stepWeek, calendarSwipeResponder: externalSwipeResponder,
    slideAnimX, monthGridCells, weekCells,
    selectedIsoDate, setSelectedIsoDate, swipedRecentlyRef, daySessions, onSelectEvent,
    canCreateEvents, onPressCreateEvent, hasActiveSearch = false,
  }: {
    monthOpen: boolean; setMonthOpen: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>; activeMonth: Date;
    stepMonth: (dir: -1 | 1) => void; stepWeek: (dir: -1 | 1) => void; calendarSwipeResponder?: SwipeGestureResponder;
    slideAnimX: Animated.Value; monthGridCells: CalendarGridCell[]; weekCells: CalendarGridCell[]; selectedIsoDate: string;
    setSelectedIsoDate: (iso: string) => void; swipedRecentlyRef: React.MutableRefObject<number>;
    daySessions: CalendarEventRow[]; onSelectEvent: (ev: CalendarEventRow) => void; canCreateEvents?: boolean;
    onPressCreateEvent?: () => void; hasActiveSearch?: boolean;
  }) => {
    const { tx, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme, isLightMode } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const s = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, AGENDA_MEDIA_LOCK_KEYS), [resolvedTheme]);
    const fallbackSwipeResponder = useMemo(
      () =>
        PanResponder.create({
          onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: (_, g) => !externalSwipeResponder && Math.abs(g.dx) > 8 && Math.abs(g.dx) > Math.abs(g.dy) * 1.15,
          onPanResponderMove: (_, g) => { slideAnimX.setValue(Math.max(-105, Math.min(105, g.dx))); },
          onPanResponderRelease: (_, g) => {
            Animated.spring(slideAnimX, { toValue: 0, useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web', friction: 8, tension: 75 }).start();
            if (Math.abs(g.dx) > 32) {
              swipedRecentlyRef.current = Date.now();
              const dir: -1 | 1 = g.dx < 0 ? 1 : -1;
              triggerHaptic('Selection');
              if (monthOpen) stepMonth(dir); else stepWeek(dir);
            }
          },
          onPanResponderTerminate: () => {
            Animated.spring(slideAnimX, { toValue: 0, useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web' }).start();
          },
        }),
      [externalSwipeResponder, monthOpen, stepMonth, stepWeek, slideAnimX, swipedRecentlyRef]
    );
    const activeSwipeResponder = externalSwipeResponder || fallbackSwipeResponder;
    const monthsArr = language === 'en'
      ? ['JANUARY', 'FEBRUARY', 'MARCH', 'APRIL', 'MAY', 'JUNE', 'JULY', 'AUGUST', 'SEPTEMBER', 'OCTOBER', 'NOVEMBER', 'DECEMBER']
      : MONTHS_LABELS;
    const weekArr = language === 'en' ? ['M', 'T', 'W', 'T', 'F', 'S', 'S'] : WEEK_INITIALS;

    const lightDayCell = isLight
      ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#7DD3FC', borderTopColor: '#BAE6FD', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7', shadowColor: '#0284C7', shadowOpacity: 0.14 }
      : null;
    const lightNavBtn = isLight
      ? { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#7DD3FC', borderTopColor: '#BAE6FD', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7', shadowColor: '#0284C7', shadowOpacity: 0.14 }
      : null;

    return (
      <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg }}>
        <View style={s.calNavRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[s.calArrow, NO_SEL, lightNavBtn]} onPress={() => (monthOpen ? stepMonth(-1) : stepWeek(-1))}>
            <Feather name="chevron-left" size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[s.calMonthBtn, NO_SEL, lightNavBtn]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setMonthOpen((p) => !p); }}>
            <Text style={[s.calMonthTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{monthsArr[activeMonth.getMonth()]} {activeMonth.getFullYear()}</Text>
            <Feather name={monthOpen ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down'} size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <TouchableOpacity style={[s.calArrow, NO_SEL, lightNavBtn]} onPress={() => (monthOpen ? stepMonth(1) : stepWeek(1))}>
            <Feather name="chevron-right" size={18} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={s.weekHeaderRow}>
          {weekArr.map((w, idx) => (
            <Text key={idx} style={[s.weekHeaderTxt, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>{w}</Text>
          ))}
        </View>

        <View
          {...activeSwipeResponder.panHandlers}
          style={Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ touchAction: 'pan-y', userSelect: 'none', overflow: 'hidden' } as unknown as ViewStyle) : { overflow: 'hidden' }}
        >
          <Animated.View style={{ transform: [{ translateX: slideAnimX }] }}>
            {monthOpen ? (
              <View style={s.monthGrid}>
                {monthGridCells.map((cell) => {
                  if (!cell.iso) return <View key={cell.key} style={s.monthCellWrap} />;
                  const isSel = cell.iso === selectedIsoDate;
                  return (
                    <View key={cell.key} style={s.monthCellWrap}>
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        disabled={cell.isPast}
                        style={[
                          s.monthCell,
                          NO_SEL,
                          !isSel && lightDayCell,
                          isSel && s.cellActive,
                          isSel && isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#00D0E6' },
                          cell.isPast && s.cellDisabled,
                        ]}
                        onPress={() => {
                          if (Date.now() - swipedRecentlyRef.current < 300) return;
                          triggerHaptic('Selection');
                          if (cell.iso) setSelectedIsoDate(cell.iso);
                          setMonthOpen(false);
                        }}
                      >
                        <Text style={[s.cellNum, { color: isSel ? '#050505' : themeColors.textPrimary }, isSel && { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                          {cell.dayNum}
                        </Text>
                        {!cell.isPast && (cell.count ?? 0) > 0 && (
                          <View
                            {...(Platform.OS === 'web' && (isSel || isLightMode) ? ({ dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } as Record<string, unknown>) : {})}
                            style={[
                              s.countDot,
                              { backgroundColor: isSel ? '#050505' : isLightMode ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan },
                            ]}
                          >
                            <Text style={[s.countDotTxt, { color: isSel ? '#FFFFFF' : isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#050505' }]}>
                              {cell.count}
                            </Text>
                          </View>
                        )}
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                  );
                })}
              </View>
            ) : (
              <View style={s.weekRow}>
                {weekCells.map((d, slotIdx) => {
                  const isSel = d.iso === selectedIsoDate;
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      key={`week_col_${d.iso || slotIdx}`}
                      disabled={d.isPast}
                      style={[
                        s.weekCell,
                        NO_SEL,
                        !isSel && lightDayCell,
                        isSel && s.cellActive,
                        isSel && isLightMode && { backgroundColor: '#00D0E6' },
                        d.isPast && s.cellDisabled,
                      ]}
                      onPress={() => {
                        if (Date.now() - swipedRecentlyRef.current < 300) return;
                        triggerHaptic('Selection');
                        if (d.iso) setSelectedIsoDate(d.iso);
                      }}
                    >
                      <Text style={[s.cellNum, { color: isSel ? '#050505' : themeColors.textPrimary }, isSel && { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                        {d.dayNum}
                      </Text>
                      {!d.isPast && (d.count ?? 0) > 0 && (
                        <View
                          {...(Platform.OS === 'web' && (isSel || isLightMode) ? ({ dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } as Record<string, unknown>) : {})}
                          style={[
                            s.countDot,
                            { backgroundColor: isSel ? '#050505' : isLightMode ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan },
                          ]}
                        >
                          <Text style={[s.countDotTxt, { color: isSel ? '#FFFFFF' : isLightMode ? '#FFFFFF' : '#050505' }]}>
                            {d.count}
                          </Text>
                        </View>
                      )}
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </View>
            )}
          </Animated.View>
        </View>

        {daySessions.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={[s.emptyBox, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]}>
            <Feather name="calendar" size={28} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text style={[s.emptyTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('SIN EVENTOS EN ESTA FECHA', 'NO EVENTS ON THIS DATE')}</Text>
            <Text style={[s.emptySub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
              {tx('Desliza la barra de días a la izquierda o derecha, o selecciona otra fecha con indicador azul.', 'Swipe the day bar left or right, or select another date with a cyan dot.')}
            </Text>
            {Boolean(canCreateEvents && onPressCreateEvent) && (
              <TouchableOpacity style={[s.createOfficialBtn, NO_SEL]} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); onPressCreateEvent?.(); }}>
                <Text style={s.createOfficialBtnTxt}>{tx('+ CREAR EVENTO OFICIAL', '+ CREATE OFFICIAL EVENT')}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>
        ) : (
          <>
            {hasActiveSearch && daySessions.length > 0 && (
              <Text selectable={false} style={{ fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.8, color: themeColors.cyanText, marginBottom: 8, marginTop: 2 }}>
                {tx('RESULTADOS EN PRÓXIMAS FECHAS (' + daySessions.length + ')', 'UPCOMING MATCHING SESSIONS (' + daySessions.length + ')')}
              </Text>
            )}
            {daySessions.map((ev, idx) => (
              <AgendaEventBanner key={`agenda_ev_${String(ev.id || idx)}`} item={ev} dateIso={selectedIsoDate || ev.fecha_proyectada || ev.fecha || ''} onSelect={onSelectEvent} />
            ))}
          </>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/eventos/EventosFilterSortModals.tsx

    // === src/components/eventos/EventosFilterSortModals.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { FlatList, Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { isWellnessCategory } from '../../constants/categoriesCatalog';
import { SORTED_CATEGORIES } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { EVENT_SORT_OPTIONS, EventSortKey } from '../../hooks/useEventosScreen';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { s as baseEventosStyles } from '../../styles/tabs/eventos.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { setKineticInlineOverlayOpen } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';

const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

export const EventosFilterSortModals = memo(
  ({
    catPickerOpen, setCatPickerOpen, sortPickerOpen, setSortPickerOpen,
    selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory, quickChip, setQuickChip, sortBy, setSortBy,
  }: {
    catPickerOpen: boolean;
    setCatPickerOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
    sortPickerOpen: boolean;
    setSortPickerOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
    selectedCategory: string;
    setSelectedCategory: (cat: string) => void;
    quickChip: 'todas' | 'favoritos' | 'mas_buscados' | 'wellness';
    setQuickChip: (chip: 'todas' | 'favoritos' | 'mas_buscados' | 'wellness') => void;
    sortBy: EventSortKey;
    setSortBy: (sort: EventSortKey) => void;
  }) => {
    const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
    const { tx, tDisc, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const [discQuery, setDiscQuery] = useState('');
    const [discFilter, setDiscFilter] = useState<'all' | 'sport' | 'wellness'>('all');
    const [discSort, setDiscSort] = useState<'az' | 'za'>('az');

    const s = useMemo(
      () =>
        adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseEventosStyles, resolvedTheme, [
          'quickRow3DDark', 'quickRow3DLight', 'quickRowCyanActiveDark', 'quickRowCyanActiveLight',
          'quickRowGreenActiveDark', 'quickRowGreenActiveLight', 'quickRowPurpleActiveDark', 'quickRowPurpleActiveLight',
        ]),
      [resolvedTheme]
    );

    useEffect(() => {
      if (catPickerOpen || sortPickerOpen) {
        setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(true);
        return () => setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(false);
      }
    }, [catPickerOpen, sortPickerOpen]);

    useEffect(() => {
      if (catPickerOpen) {
        setDiscQuery('');
        setDiscFilter('all');
        setDiscSort('az');
      }
    }, [catPickerOpen]);

    const filteredDisciplineCats = useMemo(() => {
      const q = discQuery.trim().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase();
      const matched = SORTED_CATEGORIES.filter((cat) => {
        const well = isWellnessCategory(cat);
        if (discFilter === 'sport' && well) return false;
        if (discFilter === 'wellness' && !well) return false;
        if (!q) return true;
        const hay = `${cat} ${tDisc(cat)}`.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase();
        return hay.includes(q);
      });
      matched.sort((a, b) => {
        const la = tDisc(a);
        const lb = tDisc(b);
        return discSort === 'za' ? lb.localeCompare(la, language) : la.localeCompare(lb, language);
      });
      return matched;
    }, [discQuery, discFilter, discSort, tDisc, language]);

    const cyanInk = isLight ? '#006B82' : COLORS.cyan;
    const purpleInk = isLight ? '#6D28D9' : '#D8B4FE';
    const quickFilterOpts: { key: 'todas' | 'favoritos' | 'mas_buscados' | 'wellness'; label: string; tone: 'cyan' | 'green' | 'purple' }[] = [
      { key: 'todas', label: tx('Todas', 'All'), tone: 'cyan' },
      { key: 'favoritos', label: `⚡ ${tx('Favoritos', 'Favorites')}`, tone: 'cyan' },
      { key: 'mas_buscados', label: `🔥 ${tx('Tendencia', 'Trending')}`, tone: 'green' },
      { key: 'wellness', label: `✨ ${tx('Bienestar & Spa', 'Wellness & Spa')}`, tone: 'purple' },
    ];

    return (
      <>
        <ReactNativeModal
          isVisible={catPickerOpen}
          onBackdropPress={() => setCatPickerOpen(false)}
          onBackButtonPress={() => setCatPickerOpen(false)}
          style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
          backdropColor="#04060B"
          backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.55 : 0.88}
          backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
          backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
          animationInTiming={200}
          animationOutTiming={180}
          hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
          useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
          useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
          avoidKeyboard={true}
        >
          <View
            {...(Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } as any) : {})}
            style={[
              globalStyles.bottomSheet,
              KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
              { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 16 },
            ]}
          >
            <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={() => setCatPickerOpen(false)} />
            <Text style={[s.modalTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx(`Filtrar Disciplina (${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length})`, `Filter Discipline (${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length})`)}
            </Text>
            <KineticListFilterSortBar
              searchQuery={discQuery}
              onChangeSearch={setDiscQuery}
              searchPlaceholder={tx(`Buscar entre ${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} disciplinas...`, `Search ${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} disciplines...`)}
              filterChips={[
                { key: 'all', label: tx('Todas', 'All'), count: SORTED_CATEGORIES.length },
                { key: 'sport', label: tx('Deporte / Fitness', 'Sports / Fitness') },
                { key: 'wellness', label: tx('Bienestar / Recuperación', 'Wellness / Recovery') },
              ]}
              activeFilter={discFilter}
              onSelectFilter={(k) => setDiscFilter(k as any)}
              sortOptions={[
                { key: 'az', label: 'A → Z' },
                { key: 'za', label: 'Z → A' },
              ]}
              activeSort={discSort}
              onSelectSort={(k) => setDiscSort(k as any)}
            />
            <FlatList
              data={filteredDisciplineCats}
              keyExtractor={(item) => item}
              initialNumToRender={18}
              maxToRenderPerBatch={24}
              windowSize={7}
              removeClippedSubviews={false}
              keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
              style={{ flex: 1 }}
              ListHeaderComponent={
                <>
                  <View style={{ gap: 7, marginBottom: 10 }}>
                    {quickFilterOpts.map((qf) => {
                      const isActive = selectedCategory === 'Todas' && quickChip === qf.key;
                      const ink = qf.tone === 'purple' ? purpleInk : qf.tone === 'green' ? themeColors.green : cyanInk;
                      const activeStyle =
                        qf.tone === 'purple'
                          ? isLight ? s.quickRowPurpleActiveLight : s.quickRowPurpleActiveDark
                          : qf.tone === 'green'
                            ? isLight ? s.quickRowGreenActiveLight : s.quickRowGreenActiveDark
                            : isLight ? s.quickRowCyanActiveLight : s.quickRowCyanActiveDark;
                      return (
                        <TouchableOpacity
                          key={qf.key}
                          activeOpacity={0.85}
                          style={[s.quickRow3DBase, isLight ? s.quickRow3DLight : s.quickRow3DDark, isActive && activeStyle, NO_SEL]}
                          onPress={() => {
                            triggerHaptic('Selection');
                            setQuickChip(qf.key);
                            setSelectedCategory('Todas');
                            setCatPickerOpen(false);
                          }}
                        >
                          <Text style={[s.quickRow3DText, { color: isActive ? ink : themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{qf.label}</Text>
                          <Feather name={isActive ? 'check-circle' : 'chevron-right'} size={15} color={isActive ? ink : themeColors.textMuted} />
                        </TouchableOpacity>
                      );
                    })}
                  </View>
                  <Text style={[s.catListMicroHeader, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                    {tx(`DISCIPLINAS Y TERAPIAS (${filteredDisciplineCats.length})`, `DISCIPLINES & THERAPIES (${filteredDisciplineCats.length})`)}
                  </Text>
                </>
              }
              renderItem={({ item: cat }) => {
                const isWell = isWellnessCategory(cat);
                const isSelected = selectedCategory === cat;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[s.modalRow, isLight && { borderBottomColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}
                    onPress={() => {
                      triggerHaptic('Selection');
                      setQuickChip('todas');
                      setSelectedCategory(cat);
                      setCatPickerOpen(false);
                    }}
                  >
                    <View style={s.modalRowLeft}>
                      <View
                        style={[
                          s.medallion3d,
                          isWell
                            ? { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F3E8FF' : 'rgba(157,78,221,0.16)', borderColor: isLight ? '#C4B5FD' : COLORS.purple, borderTopColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#E0AAFF', borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#7C3AED' : '#3B0F63', elevation: 0 }
                            : { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#ECFEFF' : 'rgba(0,240,255,0.14)', borderColor: isLight ? '#38BDF8' : COLORS.cyan, borderTopColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#B8FFFF', borderBottomColor: isLight ? '#0284C7' : '#006B78', elevation: 0 },
                        ]}
                      >
                        <Feather name={isWell ? 'sun' : 'zap'} size={12} color={isWell ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText} />
                      </View>
                      <Text style={[s.modalRowTxt, { color: isSelected ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textPrimary }, isSelected && { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                        {tDisc(cat)}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                    {isSelected && <Feather name="check" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />}
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              }}
            />
          </View>
        </ReactNativeModal>

        <ReactNativeModal
          isVisible={sortPickerOpen}
          onBackdropPress={() => setSortPickerOpen(false)}
          onBackButtonPress={() => setSortPickerOpen(false)}
          style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
          backdropColor="#04060B"
          backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.55 : 0.88}
          backdropTransitionOutTiming={0}
          backdropTransitionInTiming={0}
          animationInTiming={200}
          animationOutTiming={180}
          hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
          useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
          useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}
          avoidKeyboard={true}
        >
          <View
            {...(Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } as any) : {})}
            style={[
              globalStyles.bottomSheet,
              KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
              { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 16 },
            ]}
          >
            <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={() => setSortPickerOpen(false)} />
            <Text style={[s.modalTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
              {tx('Ordenar Eventos', 'Sort Events')}
            </Text>
            <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
              {EVENT_SORT_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
                const isSel = sortBy === opt.key;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={opt.key}
                    style={[s.modalRow, isLight && { borderBottomColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}
                    onPress={() => {
                      triggerHaptic('Selection');
                      setSortBy(opt.key);
                      setSortPickerOpen(false);
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        s.modalRowTxt,
                        { color: isSel ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textPrimary },
                        isSel && { fontWeight: '900' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {language === 'en' ? opt.en : opt.es}
                    </Text>
                    {isSel && <Feather name="check" size={16} color={themeColors.cyanText} />}
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        </ReactNativeModal>
      </>
    );
  }
);
