    // === src/components/eventos/EventosFlashAndAgendaViews.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Dimensions, Platform, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES, resolveCategoryImage } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { formatDisciplineBilingual } from '../../constants/kineticDisciplineMap';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { bovedaService } from '../../services/bovedaService';
import {
  KINETIC_OFFICIAL_DEFAULT_LOCATION, KINETIC_OFFICIAL_LOGO, addDaysToIso, getFreqFromDesc,
  isEventosAdminView, isKineticOfficialClubName, matchesDaySchedule, parseIsoLocal, toIsoLocal,
} from '../../services/eventosScreenHelpers';
import { resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing } from '../../services/kineticFlashSaleHelper';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import {
  isEventFavoriteSync,
  subscribeFavoriteEventsMemory,
  toggleFavoriteEventOptimistic,
} from '../../utils/kineticFavoriteEventsMemoryLeaf';
import { getAvatarUrl } from '../../utils/supabaseImage';
import { SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens } from './eventDetailsPureHelpers';
import { openGlobalEventRatingAudit } from './EventGalleryAndRatingModals';
import { s as baseStyles } from './EventosFlashAndAgendaViews.styles';

export { addDaysToIso, getFreqFromDesc, matchesDaySchedule, parseIsoLocal, toIsoLocal };

export const cleanLocationDisplay = (rawUbicacion: unknown, isEn: boolean): string => {
  const raw = String(rawUbicacion ?? '').trim();
  if (!raw) return '';
  return stripInjectedUrlsAndEmptyParens(raw) || (isEn ? 'GPS Map Location' : 'Ubicación en Mapa GPS');
};

const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

export const AGENDA_MEDIA_LOCK_KEYS = [
  'bannerHero', 'pillCyan',
  'pillCyanTxt', 'pillFlashMini', 'pillFlashMiniTxt', 'pillFlashUrgentMini', 'pillFlashUrgentMiniTxt',
  'starBadgeMini', 'starBadgeMiniTxt', 'cardCtaBtnCyan', 'cardCtaBtnCyanTxt',
  'pillPremiumMini', 'pillPremiumMiniTxt', 'pillInactiveMini', 'pillInactiveMiniTxt',
  'clubLogoMiniBox', 'pillDark', 'pillDarkTxt', 'cellActive', 'countDot', 'countDotTxt',
  'recommendedBoltBadge', 'createOfficialBtn', 'createOfficialBtnTxt',
];

const { width: SCREEN_W } = Dimensions.get('window');
export const MINICARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH = Math.min(244, Math.round(SCREEN_W * 0.65));
export const CATEGORY_CARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH = Math.min(350, Math.round(SCREEN_W * 0.85));

export const WEEK_INITIALS = ['L', 'M', 'M', 'J', 'V', 'S', 'D'];
export const MONTHS_LABELS = [
  'ENERO', 'FEBRERO', 'MARZO', 'ABRIL', 'MAYO', 'JUNIO',
  'JULIO', 'AGOSTO', 'SEPTIEMBRE', 'OCTUBRE', 'NOVIEMBRE', 'DICIEMBRE',
];

export const AgendaEventBanner = memo(
  ({
    item, dateIso, onSelect, cardWidth, badgeMode, hasActivePassProp, onOpenRatingAudit,
  }: {
    item: any; dateIso: string; onSelect: (ev: any) => void; cardWidth?: number;
    badgeMode?: 'flash' | 'premium' | null; hasActivePassProp?: boolean; onOpenRatingAudit?: (ev: any) => void;
  }) => {
    const { user, profile } = useAuth();
    const { tx, td, tDisc, fmtDate, fmtDist, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const [, setPassRev] = useState(0);
    const [isFavLocal, setIsFavLocal] = useState(() => isEventFavoriteSync(item));
    useEffect(() => bovedaService.subscribePassesSnapshot(() => setPassRev((v) => v + 1)), []);
    useEffect(() => {
      setIsFavLocal(isEventFavoriteSync(item));
      return subscribeFavoriteEventsMemory(() => setIsFavLocal(isEventFavoriteSync(item)));
    }, [item]);
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const s = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseStyles, resolvedTheme, AGENDA_MEDIA_LOCK_KEYS), [resolvedTheme]);
    const onMediaAttr: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { dataSet: { kineticOnMedia: 'true' } } : {};
    const imgUri = useMemo(() => {
      const custom = item?.portada_url || item?.imagen_url || (Array.isArray(item?.galeria) ? item.galeria[0] : '');
      const cleanCustom = typeof custom === 'string' && custom.trim().length > 5 ? custom.trim() : '';
      return cleanCustom || resolveCategoryImage(item.categoria, item.nombre_servicio);
    }, [item?.portada_url, item?.imagen_url, item?.galeria, item.categoria, item.nombre_servicio]);
    const clubNombre = cleanLocationDisplay(item?.negocio_nombre || item?.club, language === 'en') || 'Kinetic Oficial';
    const isOfficialKinetic = isKineticOfficialClubName(clubNombre);
    const isInactiveEvent = Boolean(
      item?.isInactive ||
      item?.isExpired ||
      item?.esActivo === false ||
      item?.activo === false ||
      (!isOfficialKinetic && (item?.esClubActivo === false || item?.isGhost))
    );
    const clubLogoUri = useMemo(() => {
      const raw = item?.avatar_club || item?.avatar_url || item?.afiliado_avatar;
      if (raw && typeof raw === 'string' && raw.trim().length > 5) return getAvatarUrl(raw.trim(), 120);
      if (isOfficialKinetic) return null;
      const found = (KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || []).find(
        (a: any) => String(a.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === clubNombre.toLowerCase()
      );
      const fb = found?.avatar_url || found?.portada_url || resolveCategoryImage(item?.disciplina || item?.categoria, item?.categoria);
      return fb ? getAvatarUrl(fb, 120) : null;
    }, [item?.avatar_club, item?.avatar_url, item?.afiliado_avatar, isOfficialKinetic, clubNombre, item?.disciplina, item?.categoria]);
    const fallbackLogoUri = useMemo(
      () => resolveCategoryImage(item?.disciplina || item?.categoria, item?.categoria),
      [item?.disciplina, item?.categoria]
    );
    const effectiveIso = String(dateIso || item.fecha_proyectada || item.fecha || '').split('T')[0];
    const isAdminUser = isEventosAdminView(user, profile);
    const {
      basePriceKc, discountedTotalKc: totalKc, escrow40: escrowKc, puerta60: puertaKc,
      hasFlashDiscount, isFlashWindow, flashBadgeText,
    } = resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing({
      event: { ...item, precio_kc: item?.precio_kc, fecha: effectiveIso || item?.fecha, fecha_proyectada: effectiveIso },
      vipLevel: profile?.nivel, kcMembresia: profile?.kc_membresia, isAdmin: isAdminUser, isEn: language === 'en',
    });
    const horaTxt = String(item.hora_inicio || '08:00').substring(0, 5);
    const durMin = item.duracion_minutos || 60;
    const rawDateLbl = fmtDate(effectiveIso, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
    const cleanDateLbl = rawDateLbl ? rawDateLbl.replace(/\s+de\s+/gi, ' ').replace(/[.,]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : effectiveIso;
    const dateLabel = cleanDateLbl ? cleanDateLbl.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + cleanDateLbl.slice(1) : effectiveIso;
    const displayPriceKc = isAdminUser ? basePriceKc : totalKc;
    const cupoMax = Math.max(1, Number(item.cupo_maximo || 10));
    const cupoOcc = Math.max(0, Number(item.reservadosCount || item.cupo_actual || 0));
    const isFlashBadge = badgeMode === 'flash' || (!badgeMode && Boolean(item?.es_venta_flash || item?.isFlashSale || isFlashWindow));
    const resolvedBadgeMode: 'flash' | 'premium' | null = isFlashBadge
      ? 'flash'
      : isOfficialKinetic && (badgeMode === 'premium' || !badgeMode)
        ? 'premium'
        : null;
    const isUrgentFlash = Boolean(item?.isFlashUrgent3h || item?.flashUrgencyTag || isFlashWindow);
    const ubicacionShort = cleanLocationDisplay(item?.ubicacion || item?.sede || item?.direccion, language === 'en') || (isOfficialKinetic ? KINETIC_OFFICIAL_DEFAULT_LOCATION : '');
    const hasActivePass = typeof hasActivePassProp === 'boolean'
      ? hasActivePassProp
      : bovedaService.hasActivePassForEventSync(user?.id, item, effectiveIso);

    return (
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={[
          s.bannerCard, NO_SEL, cardWidth ? { width: cardWidth, marginBottom: 0 } : null,
          isInactiveEvent && { opacity: 0.6 },
          resolvedBadgeMode === 'premium' && { borderColor: 'rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.55)' },
          isLight && {
            backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: resolvedBadgeMode === 'premium' ? '#C084FC' : '#38BDF8',
            borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: resolvedBadgeMode === 'premium' ? '#7C3AED' : '#00B8D4',
            shadowColor: resolvedBadgeMode === 'premium' ? '#7C3AED' : '#00B8D4', shadowOpacity: 0.2,
          },
        ]}
        activeOpacity={0.88}
        onPress={() => {
          triggerHaptic(resolvedBadgeMode === 'flash' || item?.isFlashSale ? 'Warning' : 'Light');
          onSelect({ ...item, fecha_proyectada: effectiveIso, fecha_seleccionada: effectiveIso });
        }}
      >
        <View style={[s.bannerHero, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9' }]} {...({ dataSet: { kineticMedia: 'true' } } as any)}>
          <SmartImage source={{ uri: imgUri }} fallbackCategory={item.categoria || 'Fuerza'} style={s.fillImg} contentFit="cover" transition={0} />
          <View pointerEvents="none" style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, backgroundColor: isLight ? 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.05)' : 'rgba(5, 7, 12, 0.22)' }} />
          <View style={s.bannerTopRow}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, flexShrink: 1 }}>
              <View style={s.clubLogoMiniBox}>
                <SmartImage
                  source={clubLogoUri ? { uri: clubLogoUri } : KINETIC_OFFICIAL_LOGO}
                  fallbackUri={fallbackLogoUri} fallbackCategory={item?.categoria || 'Fuerza'}
                  style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" transition={0}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={[s.pillCyan, { backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan }]} {...({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as any)}>
                <Text style={[s.pillCyanTxt, { color: '#050505' }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {formatDisciplineBilingual(item.categoria || 'FUERZA', language, tDisc).toUpperCase()}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, flexShrink: 0 }}>
              {isInactiveEvent ? (
                <View style={s.pillInactiveMini} {...onMediaAttr}>
                  <Feather name="eye-off" size={9} color="#FCA5A5" />
                  <Text style={s.pillInactiveMiniTxt}>{tx('INACTIVO', 'INACTIVE')}</Text>
                </View>
              ) : resolvedBadgeMode === 'flash' ? (
                isUrgentFlash ? (
                  <View style={s.pillFlashUrgentMini} {...onMediaAttr}>
                    <Ionicons name="flash" size={9.5} color="#FBBF24" />
                    <Text style={s.pillFlashUrgentMiniTxt}>{flashBadgeText || tx('⚡ INICIA EN < 3H', '⚡ STARTS IN < 3H')}</Text>
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  <View style={s.pillFlashMini} {...onMediaAttr}>
                    <Ionicons name="flash" size={9} color={COLORS.cyan} />
                    <Text style={s.pillFlashMiniTxt}>{flashBadgeText || tx('VENTA FLASH', 'FLASH SALE')}</Text>
                  </View>
                )
              ) : resolvedBadgeMode === 'premium' ? (
                <View style={s.pillPremiumMini} {...onMediaAttr}>
                  <Ionicons name="sparkles" size={9} color="#E0AAFF" />
                  <Text style={s.pillPremiumMiniTxt}>{tx('PREMIUM', 'PREMIUM')}</Text>
                </View>
              ) : null}
            </View>
          </View>
          <View style={{ position: 'absolute', bottom: 8, right: 10, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              accessibilityRole="button"
              style={[
                s.starBadgeMini,
                isFavLocal && { borderColor: COLORS.cyan, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 12, 20, 0.94)' },
              ]}
              activeOpacity={0.82}
              onPress={(e: any) => {
                e?.stopPropagation?.();
                triggerHaptic('Selection');
                const { nextFavorite } = toggleFavoriteEventOptimistic({
                  ev: item,
                  userId: user?.id,
                  emitRadar: emitKineticRadarUpdated,
                });
                setIsFavLocal(nextFavorite);
              }}
              {...onMediaAttr}
            >
              <Ionicons name={isFavLocal ? 'flash' : 'flash-outline'} size={11} color={isFavLocal ? COLORS.cyan : '#FFFFFF'} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={s.starBadgeMini}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={(e: any) => {
                e?.stopPropagation?.();
                triggerHaptic('Selection');
                if (onOpenRatingAudit) onOpenRatingAudit(item);
                else openGlobalEventRatingAudit(item);
              }}
              {...onMediaAttr}
            >
              <Feather name="star" size={10} color="#FACC15" />
              <Text selectable={false} style={s.starBadgeMiniTxt}>{Number(item?.rating || 5.0).toFixed(1)}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
        <View style={[s.bannerFooter, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderTopColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}>
          <View style={s.bannerFooterMainRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, flexShrink: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[s.bannerTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, flexShrink: 1 }]} numberOfLines={2} ellipsizeMode="tail">
                {td(item.nombre_servicio || item.titulo || tx('Evento Kinetic', 'Kinetic Event'))}
              </Text>
              <Text
                style={[s.bannerMeta, { color: themeColors.textSecondary, marginTop: 3 }]}
                numberOfLines={2}
                ellipsizeMode="tail"
              >
                {`📍 ${clubNombre}${ubicacionShort && ubicacionShort.toLowerCase() !== clubNombre.toLowerCase() ? ` • ${ubicacionShort}` : ''} \u2060(${fmtDist(item.distNum || item.distanciaKm || 1.8)})\u2060`}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[s.bannerMeta, { color: themeColors.textSecondary, marginTop: 2 }]} numberOfLines={1} ellipsizeMode="tail">
                {`👤 ${
                  !item.instructor_nombre || String(item.instructor_nombre).trim() === 'Coach Oficial Kinetic'
                    ? tx('Coach Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Coach')
                    : td(item.instructor_nombre)
                }`}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <View style={{ alignItems: 'flex-end', flexShrink: 0 }}>
              <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>
                {hasFlashDiscount && (
                  <Text style={[s.bannerEscrow, { color: themeColors.textMuted, textDecorationLine: 'line-through' }]}>{basePriceKc}</Text>
                )}
                <Text style={[s.bannerPrice, { color: themeColors.cyanText, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>{displayPriceKc} KC</Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={[s.bannerEscrow, { color: isAdminUser ? themeColors.green : themeColors.purpleText, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }]}>
                {isAdminUser ? tx('PASE MAESTRO ADMIN (0 KC)', 'ADMIN MASTER PASS (0 KC)') : `40%: ${escrowKc} KC`}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
          <View style={[s.bannerBottomDateRow, isLight && { borderTopColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5, flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
              <Feather name="clock" size={11} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
              <Text style={[s.bannerDateRowTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, flexShrink: 1 }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {dateLabel} • {horaTxt} ({durMin}m)
              </Text>
              <Text style={[s.bannerSpotsTxt, { flexShrink: 0, color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>• {cupoOcc}/{cupoMax}</Text>
            </View>
            <View
              style={[
                hasActivePass ? s.cardCtaBtnEmerald : s.cardCtaBtnCyan,
                { flexShrink: 0 },
                hasActivePass && isLight && { backgroundColor: '#DCFCE7', borderColor: '#10B981', borderBottomColor: '#047857' },
              ]}
              {...(!hasActivePass ? ({ dataSet: { kineticInk: 'dark' } } as any) : {})}
            >
              <Feather
                name={hasActivePass ? 'check-circle' : 'calendar'}
                size={10}
                color={hasActivePass ? (isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : '#050505'}
              />
              <Text
                selectable={false}
                style={[
                  hasActivePass ? s.cardCtaBtnEmeraldTxt : s.cardCtaBtnCyanTxt,
                  hasActivePass && isLight && { color: '#047857' },
                ]}
              >
                {hasActivePass ? tx('✓ PASE ACTIVO', '✓ ACTIVE PASS') : tx('RESERVA AQUÍ', 'BOOK HERE')}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
  }
);

    // === src/components/eventos/EventosOverlaysAndCarousel.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Animated, Easing, Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { resolveCategoryImage } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { isEventosAdminView, isKineticOfficialClubName } from '../../services/eventosScreenHelpers';
import { resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing } from '../../services/kineticFlashSaleHelper';
import { COLORS, SPACING } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { s as baseEventosStyles } from '../../styles/tabs/eventos.styles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import {
  isEventFavoriteSync,
  subscribeFavoriteEventsMemory,
  toggleFavoriteEventOptimistic,
} from '../../utils/kineticFavoriteEventsMemoryLeaf';
import { SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { EventosFilterSortModals } from './EventosFilterSortModals';
import {
  AGENDA_MEDIA_LOCK_KEYS,
  MINICARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH,
  cleanLocationDisplay,
} from './EventosFlashAndAgendaViews';
import { s as agendaBaseStyles } from './EventosFlashAndAgendaViews.styles';

export { EventosFilterSortModals, cleanLocationDisplay };

const CompactPremiumEventMiniCard = memo(
  ({
    item,
    isFlash,
    onSelect,
  }: {
    item: any;
    isFlash: boolean;
    onSelect: (ev: any) => void;
  }) => {
    const { user, profile } = useAuth();
    const { tx, language, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
    const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
    const [isFavLocal, setIsFavLocal] = useState(() => isEventFavoriteSync(item));
    useEffect(() => {
      setIsFavLocal(isEventFavoriteSync(item));
      return subscribeFavoriteEventsMemory(() => setIsFavLocal(isEventFavoriteSync(item)));
    }, [item]);
    const rawImg = item?.portada_url || item?.imagen_url || (Array.isArray(item?.galeria) ? item.galeria[0] : '');
    const imgUri =
      typeof rawImg === 'string' && rawImg.trim().length > 5
        ? rawImg.trim()
        : resolveCategoryImage(item?.categoria, item?.nombre_servicio);
    const clubLabel = cleanLocationDisplay(item?.negocio_nombre || item?.club, language === 'en') || 'Kinetic Oficial';
    const titleLabel = String(item?.nombre_servicio || item?.titulo || tx('Sesión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Session')).trim();
    const isAdminUser = isEventosAdminView(user, profile);
    const fp = resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing({
      event: { ...(item || {}), ...(isFlash ? { es_venta_flash: true } : {}) },
      vipLevel: profile?.nivel,
      kcMembresia: profile?.kc_membresia,
      isAdmin: isAdminUser,
      isEn: language === 'en',
    });
    const priceKc = isAdminUser ? fp.basePriceKc : fp.discountedTotalKc;
    const timeLabel = String(item?.hora_inicio || item?.hora || '08:00').substring(0, 5);
    const spotsLeft = Math.max(0, (Number(item?.cupo_maximo) || 12) - (Number(item?.reservadosCount) || 0));

    return (
      <TouchableOpacity
        activeOpacity={0.86}
        onPress={() => {
          triggerHaptic('Selection');
          onSelect(item);
        }}
        style={[
          miniStyles.card,
          isFlash && { borderColor: '#FF3B5C', borderBottomColor: '#991B1B' },
          isLight && {
            backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
            borderColor: isFlash ? '#F87171' : '#C084FC',
            borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
            borderBottomColor: isFlash ? '#DC2626' : '#7C3AED',
          },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={[miniStyles.hero, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9' }]} {...({ dataSet: { kineticMedia: 'true' } } as any)}>
          <SmartImage
            source={{ uri: imgUri }}
            fallbackCategory={item?.categoria || 'Fuerza'}
            style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
            contentFit="cover"
            transition={0}
          />
          <View pointerEvents="none" style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(5, 7, 12, 0.24)' }]} />
          <View style={miniStyles.topBadges}>
            <View style={[miniStyles.modeBadge, { backgroundColor: isFlash ? 'rgba(220,38,38,0.88)' : 'rgba(124,58,237,0.88)' }]}>
              <Text style={miniStyles.badgeTxt} numberOfLines={1}>{fp.flashBadgeText || (isFlash ? 'FLASH' : 'PREMIUM')}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 5 }}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                accessibilityRole="button"
                activeOpacity={0.82}
                style={[
                  miniStyles.priceBadge,
                  { paddingHorizontal: 6, paddingVertical: 3 },
                  isFavLocal && { borderColor: COLORS.cyan, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 12, 20, 0.94)' },
                ]}
                onPress={(e) => {
                  e?.stopPropagation?.();
                  triggerHaptic('Selection');
                  const { nextFavorite } = toggleFavoriteEventOptimistic({
                    ev: item as Record<string, unknown>,
                    userId: user?.id,
                    emitRadar: emitKineticRadarUpdated,
                  });
                  setIsFavLocal(nextFavorite);
                }}
              >
                <Ionicons name={isFavLocal ? 'flash' : 'flash-outline'} size={11} color={isFavLocal ? COLORS.cyan : '#FFFFFF'} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <View style={[miniStyles.priceBadge, { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }]}>
                {fp.hasFlashDiscount && fp.basePriceKc > priceKc && (
                  <Text style={[miniStyles.badgeTxt, { color: '#CBD5E1', textDecorationLine: 'line-through', fontSize: 8.5 }]}>{fp.basePriceKc}</Text>
                )}
                <Text style={[miniStyles.badgeTxt, { color: COLORS.cyan }]}>{priceKc === 0 ? tx('CORTESÍA', 'FREE') : `${priceKc} KC`}</Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
        <View style={miniStyles.body}>
          <Text style={[miniStyles.clubTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {clubLabel}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[miniStyles.titleTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
            {titleLabel}
          </Text>
          <View style={miniStyles.metaRow}>
            <Feather name="clock" size={11} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            <Text style={[miniStyles.metaTxt, { color: themeColors.textMuted, flex: 1 }]} numberOfLines={1}>
              {`${timeLabel} • ${spotsLeft} ${tx('libres', 'left')}`}
            </Text>
            {isAdminUser && (
              <Text style={[miniStyles.metaTxt, { color: themeColors.green, fontSize: 9.5, flexShrink: 0 }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                {tx('PASE MAESTRO ADMIN (0 KC)', 'ADMIN MASTER PASS (0 KC)')}
              </Text>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
  }
);

const miniStyles = StyleSheet.create({

export const EventosClockwiseBoltBanner = memo(({ visible, isLight }: { visible: boolean; isLight: boolean }) => {
  const { tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const spinAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) {
      spinAnim.setValue(0);
      return;
    }
    const loop = Animated.loop(
      Animated.timing(spinAnim, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 900,
        easing: Easing.linear,
        useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
      })
    );
    loop.start();
    return () => loop.stop();
  }, [visible, spinAnim]);

  if (!visible) return null;

  const clockwiseSpin = spinAnim.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: ['0deg', '360deg'],
  });

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        baseEventosStyles.refreshBoltBanner,
        isLight && {
          backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderColor: '#06B6D4',
          borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF',
          borderBottomColor: '#0284C7',
        },
      ]}
    >
      <Animated.View style={{ transform: [{ rotate: clockwiseSpin }] }}>
        <Ionicons name="flash" size={17} color={isLight ? '#0284C7' : '#00F0FF'} />
      </Animated.View>
      <Text style={[baseEventosStyles.refreshBoltText, isLight && { color: '#006B82' }]}>
        {tx('ACTUALIZANDO CARTELERA EN VIVO...', 'REFRESHING LIVE SCHEDULE...')}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
});

export const EventosFlashCarouselSection = memo(
  ({
    recommendedEvents, activeCarouselIdx, setActiveCarouselIdx, topCarouselRef,
    topCarouselIdxRef, isUserTouchingCarouselRef, todayIso, onSelectEvent,
  }: {
    recommendedEvents: any[];
    activeCarouselIdx: number;
    setActiveCarouselIdx: (idx: number) => void;
    topCarouselRef: React.RefObject<ScrollView | null>;
    topCarouselIdxRef: React.MutableRefObject<number>;
    isUserTouchingCarouselRef: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>;
    todayIso: string;
    onSelectEvent: (ev: any) => void;
  }) => {
    const { tx, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
    const s = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(agendaBaseStyles, resolvedTheme, AGENDA_MEDIA_LOCK_KEYS), [resolvedTheme]);
    const touchEndTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
    useEffect(() => () => {
      if (touchEndTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(touchEndTimerRef.current);
    }, []);

    const activeOnlyRecommended = useMemo(
      () =>
        (recommendedEvents || []).filter((ev: any) => {
          if (!ev || ev.isInactive || ev.isExpired) return false;
          const isOfficial = isKineticOfficialClubName(ev.negocio_nombre || ev.club);
          const isFlash = Boolean(ev.es_venta_flash || ev.isFlashSale);
          const clubAlive = isOfficial || (ev.esClubActivo !== false && !ev.isGhost);
          return clubAlive && (isFlash || isOfficial);
        }),
      [recommendedEvents]
    );

    if (activeOnlyRecommended.length === 0) return null;
    const hasFlashUpcoming = activeOnlyRecommended.some((ev: any) => ev.es_venta_flash || ev.isFlashSale);
    const hasOfficialPremium = activeOnlyRecommended.some((ev: any) => isKineticOfficialClubName(ev.negocio_nombre || ev.club));

    const headerTitle = hasFlashUpcoming && hasOfficialPremium
      ? tx('VENTAS FLASH & EVENTOS PREMIUM', 'FLASH SALES & PREMIUM EVENTS')
      : hasFlashUpcoming
        ? tx('VENTAS FLASH', 'FLASH SALES')
        : tx('EVENTOS PREMIUM KINETIC', 'KINETIC PREMIUM EVENTS');

    return (
      <View style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <View style={{ paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg }}>
          <View style={s.recommendedHeaderRow}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flex: 1 }}>
              <View style={s.recommendedBoltBadge}>
                <Ionicons name="flash" size={12} color="#050505" />
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                  <Text style={[s.recommendedTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {headerTitle}
                  </Text>
                  {hasFlashUpcoming && (
                    <View style={{ backgroundColor: '#F59E0B', paddingHorizontal: 6, paddingVertical: 1.5, borderRadius: 5, elevation: 0 }}>
                      <Text style={{ color: '#050505', fontSize: 9, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.3 }}>
                        {tx('⏰ INICIA EN < 3 HORAS', '⏰ STARTS IN < 3 HOURS')}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </View>
                <Text style={[s.recommendedSub, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {hasFlashUpcoming
                    ? tx('Ocupación relámpago con Anticipo Protegido 40/60', 'Flash booking with 40/60 Protected Deposit')
                    : tx('Desliza o explora las experiencias oficiales ★ 5.0', 'Swipe or explore ★ 5.0 official experiences')}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
            <View style={[s.recommendedTopTag, themeColors.isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7' }]}>
              <Text style={[s.recommendedTopTagTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
                {Math.min(activeCarouselIdx + 1, activeOnlyRecommended.length)}/{activeOnlyRecommended.length}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
        <ScrollView
          ref={topCarouselRef}
          horizontal
          removeClippedSubviews={false}
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          decelerationRate="fast"
          snapToInterval={MINICARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH + 12}
          snapToAlignment="start"
          onTouchStart={() => {
            if (touchEndTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(touchEndTimerRef.current);
            isUserTouchingCarouselRef.current = true;
          }}
          onTouchEnd={() => {
            if (touchEndTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(touchEndTimerRef.current);
            touchEndTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => { isUserTouchingCarouselRef.current = false; }, 2200);
          }}
          onMomentumScrollEnd={(e) => {
            const idx = Math.round(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.x / (MINICARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH + 12));
            const safeIdx = Math.max(0, Math.min(idx, activeOnlyRecommended.length - 1));
            topCarouselIdxRef.current = safeIdx;
            setActiveCarouselIdx(safeIdx);
          }}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg, gap: 12 }}
        >
          {activeOnlyRecommended.map((ev: any) => {
            const isFlash = Boolean(ev.es_venta_flash || ev.isFlashSale);
            return (
              <CompactPremiumEventMiniCard
                key={`rec_mini_${ev.id}`}
                item={ev}
                isFlash={isFlash}
                onSelect={onSelectEvent}
              />
            );
          })}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>
    );
  }
);

    FILE: src/components/eventos/EventSubSheetCatalogBodyLeaf.tsx

    // === src/components/eventos/EventSubSheetCatalogBodyLeaf.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React from 'react';
import { FlatList, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { isWellnessCategory, SORTED_CATEGORIES } from '../../constants/exploreData';
import { KINETIC_16_WHAT_TO_BRING } from '../../constants/kineticEventCatalogs';
import { KineticListFilterSortBar } from '../shared/KineticListFilterSortBar';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export interface EventSubSheetCatalogOption {
  value: string;
  label: string;
}

export interface EventSubSheetCatalogBodyProps {
  activeSheet: string;
  isCompactSheet: boolean;
  isLight: boolean;
  isEn: boolean;
  sheetTitleText: string;
  discSearch: string;
  setDiscSearch: (v: string) => void;
  discFilter: 'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness';
  setDiscFilter: (v: 'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness') => void;
  discSort: 'az' | 'za' | 'wellness_first';
  setDiscSort: (v: 'az' | 'za' | 'wellness_first') => void;
  discCounts: { all: number; fitness: number; wellness: number };
  reqsVal: string[];
  setReqsVal: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string[]>>;
  resolvedCatalogOptions: EventSubSheetCatalogOption[];
  selectOptionValue: (sheet: string, val: string) => void;
  esStyles: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
  baseEsStyles: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
  themeColors: {
    textPrimary: string;
    textMuted: string;
    cyanText: string;
    purpleText: string;
  };
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
}

export const EventSubSheetCatalogBodyLeaf: React.FC<EventSubSheetCatalogBodyProps> = ({
  activeSheet,
  isCompactSheet,
  isLight,
  isEn,
  sheetTitleText,
  discSearch,
  setDiscSearch,
  discFilter,
  setDiscFilter,
  discSort,
  setDiscSort,
  discCounts,
  reqsVal,
  setReqsVal,
  resolvedCatalogOptions,
  selectOptionValue,
  esStyles,
  baseEsStyles,
  themeColors,
  tx,
}) => (
  <View style={isCompactSheet ? { flexShrink: 1 } : { flex: 1 }}>
    <Text style={[esStyles.sheetTitle, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{sheetTitleText}</Text>

    {activeSheet === 'disciplina' && (
      <KineticListFilterSortBar
        searchQuery={discSearch}
        onChangeSearch={setDiscSearch}
        searchPlaceholder={tx(
          `Buscar entre ${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} disciplinas...`,
          `Search ${SORTED_CATEGORIES.length} disciplines...`
        )}
        filterChips={[
          { key: 'all', label: tx('Todas', 'All'), count: discCounts.all },
          { key: 'fitness', label: tx('Deporte & Fitness', 'Sports & Fitness'), count: discCounts.fitness },
          { key: 'wellness', label: tx('Wellness', 'Wellness'), count: discCounts.wellness },
        ]}
        activeFilter={discFilter}
        onSelectFilter={(k) => setDiscFilter(k as 'all' | 'fitness' | 'wellness')}
        sortOptions={[
          { key: 'az', label: 'A → Z' },
          { key: 'za', label: 'Z → A' },
          { key: 'wellness_first', label: tx('Wellness Primero', 'Wellness First') },
        ]}
        activeSort={discSort}
        onSelectSort={(k) => setDiscSort(k as 'az' | 'za' | 'wellness_first')}
      />
    )}

    {activeSheet === 'requisitos' ? (
      <ScrollView key="sheet_reqs" style={{ flex: 1 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" showsVerticalScrollIndicator={true}>
        {KINETIC_16_WHAT_TO_BRING.map((item) => {
          const checked = reqsVal.some(
            (r) => r.toLowerCase() === item.es.toLowerCase() || r.toLowerCase() === item.en.toLowerCase()
          );
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={item.key}
              activeOpacity={0.84}
              style={[
                esStyles.reqBtn3D,
                isLight && baseEsStyles.reqBtn3DLight,
                checked && (isLight ? baseEsStyles.reqBtn3DActiveLight : baseEsStyles.reqBtn3DActiveDark),
              ]}
              onPress={() => {
                triggerHaptic('Selection');
                setReqsVal((prev) => {
                  const has = prev.some(
                    (x) => x.toLowerCase() === item.es.toLowerCase() || x.toLowerCase() === item.en.toLowerCase()
                  );
                  if (has) {
                    return prev.length > 1
                      ? prev.filter(
                          (x) => x.toLowerCase() !== item.es.toLowerCase() && x.toLowerCase() !== item.en.toLowerCase()
                        )
                      : prev;
                  }
                  return [...prev, item.es];
                });
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  esStyles.optTxt,
                  { color: checked ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: checked ? '900' : '700' },
                ]}
              >
                {isEn ? item.en : item.es}
              </Text>
              <View
                style={[
                  esStyles.chkBox,
                  isLight && !checked && { borderColor: '#94A3B8', backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC' },
                  checked && esStyles.chkBoxOn,
                ]}
              >
                {checked && <Feather name="check" size={13} color="#050505" />}
              </View>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </ScrollView>
    ) : (
      <FlatList
        key={'sheet_list_' + activeSheet}
        data={resolvedCatalogOptions}
        keyExtractor={(item) => item.value}
        initialNumToRender={18}
        maxToRenderPerBatch={24}
        windowSize={7}
        removeClippedSubviews={false}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        style={isCompactSheet ? { flexGrow: 0 } : { flex: 1 }}
        renderItem={({ item: opt }) => {
          const isDisc = activeSheet === 'disciplina';
          const isWell = isDisc && isWellnessCategory(opt.value);
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[esStyles.optRow, isLight && { borderBottomColor: '#E2E8F0' }]}
              onPress={() => selectOptionValue(activeSheet, opt.value)}
            >
              <View style={esStyles.optLeftRow}>
                {isDisc && (
                  <View
                    style={[
                      esStyles.medallion3d,
                      isWell
                        ? isLight ? baseEsStyles.medallionPurpleLight : baseEsStyles.medallionPurpleDark
                        : isLight ? baseEsStyles.medallionCyanLight : baseEsStyles.medallionCyanDark,
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Feather
                      name={isWell ? 'sun' : 'zap'}
                      size={12}
                      color={isWell ? themeColors.purpleText : themeColors.cyanText}
                    />
                  </View>
                )}
                <Text style={[esStyles.optTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{opt.label}</Text>
              </View>
              <Feather name="chevron-right" size={14} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        }}
      />
    )}
  </View>
);

    FILE: src/components/eventos/KineticStudioConfigPanel.tsx

    // === src/components/eventos/KineticStudioConfigPanel.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo } from 'react';
import { Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  EQUIPMENT_META,
  IStudioLayoutConfig,
  MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS,
  StudioEquipmentType,
} from '../../services/spotCollisionService';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

const NO_SEL: any = Platform.OS === 'web' ? { userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } : null;

export interface IKineticStudioConfigPanelProps {
  st: any;
  isLight: boolean;
  draft: IStudioLayoutConfig;
  setDraft: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<IStudioLayoutConfig>>;
  activeTotal: number;
  eqMeta: (typeof EQUIPMENT_META)[StudioEquipmentType];
  eqMenuOpen: boolean;
  setEqMenuOpen: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>;
  activeNumAxis: 'total' | 'rows' | 'cols' | null;
  setActiveNumAxis: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<'total' | 'rows' | 'cols' | null>>;
  numberPickerRange: number[];
  currentAxisVal: number;
  adjustDimension: (axis: 'total' | 'rows' | 'cols', delta: number) => void;
  applyDimension: (axis: 'total' | 'rows' | 'cols', exactVal: number) => void;
}

export const KineticStudioConfigPanel = memo((props: IKineticStudioConfigPanelProps) => {
  const {
    st, isLight, draft, setDraft, activeTotal, eqMeta,
    eqMenuOpen, setEqMenuOpen, activeNumAxis, setActiveNumAxis,
    numberPickerRange, currentAxisVal, adjustDimension, applyDimension,
  } = props;
  const { tx, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();

  const totalUnitLabel = draft.equipmentType === 'court' ? tx('CANCHAS', 'COURTS') : tx('EQUIPOS', 'SPOTS');

  return (
    <View style={[st.configPanel, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#7DD3FC' }]}>
      <TouchableOpacity
        activeOpacity={0.86}
        style={[st.eqDropBtn, isLight ? st.eqDropBtnLight : st.eqDropBtnDark, NO_SEL]}
        onPress={() => {
          triggerHaptic('Selection');
          setActiveNumAxis(null);
          setEqMenuOpen((p) => !p);
        }}
      >
        <View style={st.eqDropLeft}>
          <Feather name={eqMeta.icon} size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text selectable={false} style={[st.eqDropLabel, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
            {tx('Equipo:', 'Equipment:')}{' '}
            <Text style={{ color: themeColors.cyanText }}>
              {activeTotal} {tx(eqMeta.es, eqMeta.en)}
            </Text>
          </Text>
        </View>
        <Text selectable={false} style={[st.eqDropArrow, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
          {eqMenuOpen ? '▲' : '▼'}
        </Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>

      {eqMenuOpen && (
        <View style={[st.eqDropSheet, isLight ? st.eqDropSheetLight : st.eqDropSheetDark]}>
          {(Object.keys(EQUIPMENT_META) as StudioEquipmentType[]).map((eqKey) => {
            const active = draft.equipmentType === eqKey;
            const m = EQUIPMENT_META[eqKey];
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={eqKey}
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                style={[
                  st.eqOptionRow,
                  active && { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#ECFEFF' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)' },
                  NO_SEL,
                ]}
                onPress={() => {
                  triggerHaptic('Selection');
                  setDraft((p) => ({ ...p, equipmentType: eqKey }));
                  setEqMenuOpen(false);
                }}
              >
                <View style={st.eqDropLeft}>
                  <Feather name={m.icon} size={13} color={active ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted} />
                  <Text
                    selectable={false}
                    style={[
                      st.eqOptionTxt,
                      { color: active ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textPrimary, fontWeight: active ? '900' : '700' },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {tx(m.es, m.en)}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <Feather name={active ? 'check-circle' : 'circle'} size={14} color={active ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted} />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </View>
      )}

      <View style={st.stepperBar}>
        {([
          { id: 'total', lbl: totalUnitLabel, val: activeTotal },
          { id: 'rows', lbl: tx('FILAS', 'ROWS'), val: draft.rows },
          { id: 'cols', lbl: tx('COLUMNAS', 'COLS'), val: draft.cols },
        ] as const).map((ctl) => {
          const isAxisOpen = activeNumAxis === ctl.id;
          return (
            <View key={ctl.id} style={st.stepBlock}>
              <Text selectable={false} style={[st.stepLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                {ctl.lbl}
              </Text>
              <View style={st.stepRow}>
                <TouchableOpacity style={[st.stepBtn, NO_SEL]} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => adjustDimension(ctl.id, -1)}>
                  <Text selectable={false} style={st.stepBtnTxt}>-</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.85}
                  style={[
                    st.stepNumBtn,
                    isLight ? st.stepNumBtnLight : st.stepNumBtnDark,
                    isAxisOpen && { borderColor: isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan, backgroundColor: isLight ? '#CFFAFE' : '#0A2A36' },
                    NO_SEL,
                  ]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Selection');
                    setEqMenuOpen(false);
                    setActiveNumAxis((prev) => (prev === ctl.id ? null : ctl.id));
                  }}
                >
                  <Text selectable={false} style={[st.stepVal, { color: isAxisOpen ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                    {ctl.val}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
                <TouchableOpacity style={[st.stepBtn, NO_SEL]} activeOpacity={0.85} onPress={() => adjustDimension(ctl.id, 1)}>
                  <Text selectable={false} style={st.stepBtnTxt}>+</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            </View>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      {activeNumAxis && (
        <View style={[st.numPickerSheet, isLight ? st.numPickerSheetLight : st.numPickerSheetDark]}>
          <View style={st.numPickerHeader}>
            <Text selectable={false} style={[st.numPickerTitle, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
              {activeNumAxis === 'total'
                ? tx(`${totalUnitLabel} (1–${MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS})`, `TOTAL ${totalUnitLabel} (1–${MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS})`)
                : activeNumAxis === 'rows'
                  ? tx('FILAS (1–10)', 'ROWS (1–10)')
                  : tx('COLUMNAS (1–10)', 'COLUMNS (1–10)')}
            </Text>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setActiveNumAxis(null)} hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}>
              <Feather name="x" size={14} color={themeColors.textMuted} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
          <ScrollView nestedScrollEnabled showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false} style={{ maxHeight: 132 }}>
            <View style={st.numGridWrap}>
              {numberPickerRange.map((num) => {
                const active = currentAxisVal === num;
                return (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={`${activeNumAxis}_${num}`}
                    activeOpacity={0.85}
                    style={[st.numChip, isLight ? st.numChipLight : st.numChipDark, active && st.numChipActive, NO_SEL]}
                    onPress={() => {
                      applyDimension(activeNumAxis, num);
                      setActiveNumAxis(null);
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text selectable={false} style={[st.numChipTxt, { color: active ? '#050505' : themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
                      {num}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          </ScrollView>
        </View>
      )}

      <Text selectable={false} style={[st.aisleHint, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
        {tx('Toca el número central para salto rápido o una celda para pasillo (máx. 50).', 'Tap center numbers to jump or any cell to toggle aisle (max 50).')}
      </Text>
    </View>
  );
});

  numChipActive: { backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan, borderColor: '#5CEEFF', borderTopColor: '#D6FFFF', borderBottomColor: '#007B8A' },
  numChipTxt: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900', fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] },
  aisleHint: { fontSize: 9.2, fontWeight: '700', textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 6 },
  stageBar: {
    flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 8, minHeight: 36, borderRadius: 12,
    backgroundColor: '#080C14', borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: COLORS.cyan, borderTopWidth: 1.8,
    borderTopColor: '#B8FFFF', borderBottomWidth: 4.2, borderBottomColor: '#006B78', marginBottom: 12,
    shadowColor: COLORS.cyan, shadowOpacity: 0.36, shadowRadius: 8, elevation: 9,
  },
  stageTxt: { color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 1.2 },
  cinemaFloor: { gap: 8, paddingBottom: 8 },
  gridRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 7, justifyContent: 'center' },
  aisleSpacer: { flex: 1, minHeight: 54 },
  spotCard: {
    flex: 1, minHeight: 54, borderRadius: 11, paddingVertical: 5, paddingHorizontal: 3,
    alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', borderWidth: 1.2, borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderBottomWidth: 4.0,
    shadowColor: '#000000', shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 }, shadowOpacity: 0.3, shadowRadius: 6, elevation: 8,
  },
  spotAvailDark: { backgroundColor: '#071520', borderColor: 'rgba(0,240,255,0.55)', borderTopColor: '#B8FFFF', borderBottomColor: '#006B78' },
  spotAvailLight: { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#38BDF8', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0284C7' },
  spotOccDark: { backgroundColor: '#290910', borderColor: '#EF4444', borderTopColor: '#FCA5A5', borderBottomColor: '#7F1D1D' },
  spotOccLight: { backgroundColor: '#FEE2E2', borderColor: '#EF4444', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#B91C1C' },
  spotSelDark: { backgroundColor: '#062615', borderColor: COLORS.green, borderTopColor: '#B8FFD8', borderBottomColor: '#007A33' },
  spotSelLight: { backgroundColor: '#DCFCE7', borderColor: '#10B981', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#047857' },
  spotAisleCfg: { backgroundColor: '#0B101B', borderColor: 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.28)', borderTopColor: 'rgba(203, 213, 225, 0.35)', borderBottomColor: '#05080F' },
  spotAisleLight: { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
  spotNumTxt: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900', marginTop: 1, fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] },
  spotTagTxt: { fontSize: 7.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.3 },
  footerRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: 10, marginTop: 10 },
  closeBtn: {
    flex: 0.85, flexDirection: 'row', height: 48, borderRadius: 13, backgroundColor: '#0D111A',
    borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)', borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderTopColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)',
    borderBottomWidth: 4.2, borderBottomColor: '#04070D', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, elevation: 8,
  },
  closeBtnTxt: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900' },
  primaryBtn: {
    flex: 1.65, flexDirection: 'row', height: 48, borderRadius: 13, backgroundColor: COLORS.cyan,
    borderWidth: 1.2, borderColor: '#5CEEFF', borderTopWidth: 1.8, borderTopColor: '#D6FFFF',
    borderBottomWidth: 4.5, borderBottomColor: '#008B99', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', gap: 7, elevation: 10,
  },
  primaryBtnTxt: { color: '#050505', fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: 0.5 },
});

    FILE: src/components/eventos/KineticStudioLayoutModal.tsx

    // === src/components/eventos/KineticStudioLayoutModal.tsx === //
import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Alert, Platform, ScrollView, Text, TouchableOpacity, View, type ViewProps, type ViewStyle } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  buildGridForTotalSpots, clampStudioGridToMaxSpots, DEFAULT_SPOT_LOCK_MS, EQUIPMENT_META,
  fetchLiveOccupiedSpots, formatStudioCapacityLabel, formatStudioLayoutButtonLabel,
  getDisciplineEventTitlePlaceholder, getSpotCollisionAlertMessage, getSpotLockRemainingSec, GLOBAL_SPOTS_LOCKS,
  inferEquipmentByDiscipline, IStudioLayoutConfig, loadStudioLayoutLocal, MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS,
  registerTemporarySpotLock, resolveDefaultStudioLayout, saveStudioLayoutLocal, setSpotLock90s,
  SPOT_COLLISION_ERROR_CODE, STUDIO_LAYOUT_STORAGE_PREFIX, StudioEquipmentType, subscribeSpotCollisions,
  syncLayoutOnDisciplineChange, verifyLiveSpotAvailability,
} from '../../services/spotCollisionService';
import {
  formatStudioStationBadge,
  formatUseStudioStationCta,
  getLocalizedStudioUnitSingular,
  resolveStudioFrontStageLabel,
} from '../../services/studioLayoutGridHelper';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { adaptStyleSheetForTheme } from '../../utils/kineticContrastAnd3DEngine';
import { setKineticInlineOverlayOpen } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { IVolumetricStageCell, KineticVolumetricStudioStageLeaf } from '../shared/KineticVolumetricStudioStageLeaf';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { reconcileStudioOccupiedSpots } from './eventDetailsPureHelpers';
import { KineticStudioConfigPanel } from './KineticStudioConfigPanel';
import { baseSt } from './KineticStudioLayoutModal.styles';

export {
  buildGridForTotalSpots, clampStudioGridToMaxSpots, EQUIPMENT_META,
  formatStudioCapacityLabel, formatStudioLayoutButtonLabel,
  getDisciplineEventTitlePlaceholder, getSpotCollisionAlertMessage,
  getSpotLockRemainingSec, GLOBAL_SPOTS_LOCKS, inferEquipmentByDiscipline, loadStudioLayoutLocal,
  MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, resolveDefaultStudioLayout, saveStudioLayoutLocal, setSpotLock90s,
  SPOT_COLLISION_ERROR_CODE, STUDIO_LAYOUT_STORAGE_PREFIX, syncLayoutOnDisciplineChange,
  verifyLiveSpotAvailability, resolveStudioFrontStageLabel, getLocalizedStudioUnitSingular,
  formatStudioStationBadge, formatUseStudioStationCta,
};
export type { IStudioLayoutConfig, StudioEquipmentType };

const NO_SEL: ViewStyle | undefined = Platform.OS === 'web'
  ? ({ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none' } as unknown as ViewStyle)
  : undefined;

export interface IKineticStudioLayoutModalProps {
  visible: boolean; onClose: () => void; inlineOverlay?: boolean; eventKey?: string; serviceId?: string; fechaClase?: string;
  currentUserId?: string; eventTitle?: string; discipline?: string; maxCupo: number; occupiedCount: number;
  initialLayout?: IStudioLayoutConfig | null; selectedSpot?: number | null;
  onSelectSpot?: (spotNumber: number | null) => void; onSpotCollision?: (message: string) => void;
  canConfigure?: boolean; initialConfigMode?: boolean; onSaveLayout?: (layout: IStudioLayoutConfig) => void;
}

export const KineticStudioLayoutModal = memo((props: IKineticStudioLayoutModalProps) => {
  const {
    visible, onClose, inlineOverlay = false, eventKey, serviceId, fechaClase, currentUserId, eventTitle, discipline, maxCupo, occupiedCount,
    initialLayout, selectedSpot, onSelectSpot, onSpotCollision, canConfigure = false, initialConfigMode = false, onSaveLayout,
  } = props;
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { tx, language, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const effectiveUserId = currentUserId || user?.id || null;
  const st = useMemo(() => adaptStyleSheetForTheme(baseSt, resolvedTheme, [
    'stageBar', 'stageTxt', 'spotAvailDark', 'spotOccDark', 'spotSelDark', 'spotAisleCfg',
    'primaryBtn', 'primaryBtnTxt', 'stepBtn', 'stepBtnTxt', 'cfgToggleBtnOn',
    'eqDropBtnDark', 'eqDropSheetDark', 'stepNumBtnDark', 'numPickerSheetDark', 'numChipActive', 'collisionBannerDark',
  ]), [resolvedTheme]);

  const [configMode, setConfigMode] = useState(Boolean(initialConfigMode));
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<IStudioLayoutConfig>(() => resolveDefaultStudioLayout(discipline, maxCupo, initialLayout, eventTitle));
  const [pickedSpot, setPickedSpot] = useState<number | null>(selectedSpot ?? null);
  const [lockSeconds, setLockSeconds] = useState<number>(() => getSpotLockRemainingSec(eventKey, selectedSpot));
  const [liveOccupiedSpots, setLiveOccupiedSpots] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [collisionAlertMsg, setCollisionAlertMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isVerifyingSpot, setIsVerifyingSpot] = useState(false);
  const [eqMenuOpen, setEqMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeNumAxis, setActiveNumAxis] = useState<'total' | 'rows' | 'cols' | null>(null);
  const hydratedOpenRef = useRef<string | null>(null);

  const gridCells = useMemo(() => {
    const safeDraft = clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(draft.rows, draft.cols, draft.disabledCells, draft.equipmentType);
    const disabledSet = new Set(safeDraft.disabledCells);
    const list: { key: string; row: number; col: number; disabled: boolean; spotNum: number | null }[] = [];
    let counter = 1;
    for (let r = 0; r < safeDraft.rows; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < safeDraft.cols; c++) {
        const key = `${r}-${c}`;
        const isDis = disabledSet.has(key) || counter > MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS;
        list.push({ key, row: r, col: c, disabled: isDis, spotNum: isDis ? null : counter++ });
      }
    }
    return { list, activeTotal: Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, Math.max(1, counter - 1)) };
  }, [draft]);

  const pickedSpotRef = useRef<number | null>(pickedSpot);
  pickedSpotRef.current = pickedSpot;
  const languageRef = useRef(language);
  languageRef.current = language;
  const clearCollidedPick = useCallback((msg: string) => {
    triggerHaptic('Error');
    setPickedSpot(null); setLockSeconds(0); setCollisionAlertMsg(msg);
    onSelectSpot?.(null); onSpotCollision?.(msg);
  }, [onSelectSpot, onSpotCollision]);
  const clearCollidedPickRef = useRef(clearCollidedPick);
  clearCollidedPickRef.current = clearCollidedPick;

  const refreshLiveSpots = useCallback(async (checkSelectedCollision = false) => {
    const snap = await fetchLiveOccupiedSpots({
      serviceId, eventKey, eventTitle, fechaClase, userId: effectiveUserId,
      maxSpots: gridCells.activeTotal, fallbackOccupiedCount: occupiedCount,
    });
    setLiveOccupiedSpots(snap.occupiedSpots);
    const activePick = pickedSpotRef.current;
    if (checkSelectedCollision && activePick && (snap.dbBookedSpots.includes(activePick) || snap.lockedByOthersSpots.includes(activePick))) {
      clearCollidedPickRef.current(getSpotCollisionAlertMessage(languageRef.current));
    }
  }, [serviceId, eventKey, eventTitle, fechaClase, effectiveUserId, gridCells.activeTotal, occupiedCount]);
  const refreshLiveSpotsRef = useRef(refreshLiveSpots);
  refreshLiveSpotsRef.current = refreshLiveSpots;

  const hadActiveLockRef = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => { setPickedSpot(selectedSpot ?? null); }, [selectedSpot]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (pickedSpot && lockSeconds > 0) {
      hadActiveLockRef.current = true;
      const interval = setInterval(() => {
        setLockSeconds((prev) => (prev <= 1 ? 0 : prev - 1));
      }, 1000);
      return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }
    if (hadActiveLockRef.current && lockSeconds === 0) {
      hadActiveLockRef.current = false;
      if (pickedSpot) void refreshLiveSpotsRef.current(true);
    }
  }, [pickedSpot, lockSeconds]);

  const openHydrateSig = visible
    ? `${eventKey || ''}::${discipline || ''}::${maxCupo}::${initialLayout?.equipmentType || ''}::${initialLayout?.rows || ''}::${initialLayout?.cols || ''}::${initialLayout?.totalSpots || ''}`
    : '';
  const prevVisibleRef = useRef(false);
  if (visible && !prevVisibleRef.current) {
    prevVisibleRef.current = true;
    hydratedOpenRef.current = null;
  } else if (!visible && prevVisibleRef.current) {
    prevVisibleRef.current = false;
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) {
      hydratedOpenRef.current = null; setEqMenuOpen(false); setActiveNumAxis(null); setCollisionAlertMsg(null); setIsVerifyingSpot(false);
      return;
    }
    if (inlineOverlay) setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(true);
    void refreshLiveSpotsRef.current(false);
    const unsub = subscribeSpotCollisions((key, collidedSpot, occ) => {
      const matchKey = String(eventKey || serviceId || eventTitle || '').trim();
      if (!matchKey || key === matchKey || key === serviceId || key === eventKey) {
        setLiveOccupiedSpots(occ);
        if (pickedSpotRef.current === collidedSpot) {
          clearCollidedPickRef.current(getSpotCollisionAlertMessage(languageRef.current));
        }
      }
    });
    const sessionSig = `${openHydrateSig}::${initialConfigMode ? '1' : '0'}::${fechaClase || ''}`;
    if (hydratedOpenRef.current !== sessionSig) {
      hydratedOpenRef.current = sessionSig;
      setConfigMode(Boolean(initialConfigMode && canConfigure));
      setDraft(resolveDefaultStudioLayout(discipline, maxCupo, initialLayout, eventTitle));
      if (!initialLayout && eventKey && !String(eventKey).startsWith('draft_')) {
        loadStudioLayoutLocal(eventKey).then((c) => {
          if (c && hydratedOpenRef.current === sessionSig) {
            setDraft(resolveDefaultStudioLayout(discipline, maxCupo, c, eventTitle));
          }
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
    }
    return () => {
      unsub();
      if (inlineOverlay) setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(false);
    };
  }, [visible, inlineOverlay, openHydrateSig, initialConfigMode, canConfigure, discipline, maxCupo, initialLayout, eventTitle, eventKey, serviceId, fechaClase]);

  const reconciledCapacity = useMemo(
    () => reconcileStudioOccupiedSpots(liveOccupiedSpots, gridCells.activeTotal, occupiedCount, pickedSpot),
    [liveOccupiedSpots, gridCells.activeTotal, occupiedCount, pickedSpot]
  );
  const occupiedSpotSet = reconciledCapacity.occupiedSet;
  const safeOccupied = reconciledCapacity.reconciledCount;
  const freeSpots = reconciledCapacity.freeSpots;
  const eqMeta = EQUIPMENT_META[draft.equipmentType] || EQUIPMENT_META.spot;

  const toggleAisleCell = useCallback((cellKey: string, isCurrentlyDisabled: boolean) => {
    if (isCurrentlyDisabled && gridCells.activeTotal >= MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      setCollisionAlertMsg(tx('Máximo 50 estaciones alcanzado.', 'Maximum 50 stations reached.'));
      return;
    }
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setCollisionAlertMsg(null);
    setDraft((prev) => {
      if (!isCurrentlyDisabled && gridCells.activeTotal <= 1) return prev;
      const rawDis = isCurrentlyDisabled ? prev.disabledCells.filter((k) => k !== cellKey) : [...prev.disabledCells, cellKey];
      return clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(prev.rows, prev.cols, rawDis, prev.equipmentType);
    });
  }, [gridCells.activeTotal, tx]);

  const applyDimension = useCallback((axis: 'total' | 'rows' | 'cols', exactVal: number) => {
    if (axis === 'total' && exactVal > MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      setCollisionAlertMsg(tx('Máximo 50 estaciones alcanzado.', 'Maximum 50 stations reached.'));
      return;
    }
    setDraft((prev) => {
      if (axis === 'total') {
        setCollisionAlertMsg(null);
        triggerHaptic('Selection');
        return buildGridForTotalSpots(Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, exactVal)), prev.equipmentType);
      }
      const nextAxis = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Math.round(exactVal)));
      const nextRows = axis === 'rows' ? nextAxis : prev.rows;
      const nextCols = axis === 'cols' ? nextAxis : prev.cols;
      if (nextRows * nextCols > MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS) {
        if (MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS % nextAxis === 0 && MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS / nextAxis <= 10) {
          const companion = MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS / nextAxis;
          setCollisionAlertMsg(null);
          triggerHaptic('Selection');
          return clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(
            axis === 'rows' ? nextAxis : companion,
            axis === 'cols' ? nextAxis : companion,
            [],
            prev.equipmentType
          );
        }
        if (gridCells.activeTotal < MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS) {
          setCollisionAlertMsg(null);
          triggerHaptic('Selection');
          return buildGridForTotalSpots(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, prev.equipmentType);
        }
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        setCollisionAlertMsg(tx('Máximo 50 estaciones alcanzado.', 'Maximum 50 stations reached.'));
        return prev;
      }
      setCollisionAlertMsg(null);
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      return clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(nextRows, nextCols, prev.disabledCells, prev.equipmentType);
    });
  }, [gridCells.activeTotal, tx]);

  const adjustDimension = useCallback((axis: 'total' | 'rows' | 'cols', delta: number) => {
    const curVal = axis === 'total' ? gridCells.activeTotal : axis === 'rows' ? draft.rows : draft.cols;
    const rawTarget = curVal + delta;
    if (delta > 0 && gridCells.activeTotal >= MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS) {
      if (axis === 'total' || (draft.rows * draft.cols >= MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS && !(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS % rawTarget === 0 && rawTarget <= 10))) {
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        setCollisionAlertMsg(tx('Máximo 50 estaciones alcanzado.', 'Maximum 50 stations reached.'));
        return;
      }
    }
    applyDimension(axis, rawTarget);
  }, [applyDimension, gridCells.activeTotal, draft.rows, draft.cols, tx]);

  const handleConfirmSaveLayout = async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Success');
    const finalLayout = clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(draft.rows, draft.cols, draft.disabledCells, draft.equipmentType);
    // Sincronización atómica inmediata antes del cierre para no revertir a 12 spots / 3x4 en la UI padre
    onSaveLayout?.(finalLayout);
    if (initialConfigMode) onClose(); else setConfigMode(false);
    void saveStudioLayoutLocal(eventKey, finalLayout);
  };

  const handleConfirmSpotSelection = async () => {
    if (!pickedSpot) { onClose(); return; }
    if (isVerifyingSpot) return;
    setIsVerifyingSpot(true);
    try {
      const check = await verifyLiveSpotAvailability({ serviceId, eventKey, eventTitle, fechaClase, spotNumber: pickedSpot, userId: effectiveUserId, maxSpots: gridCells.activeTotal, lang: language });
      if (!check.available) {
        setLiveOccupiedSpots(check.occupiedSpots);
        clearCollidedPick(check.message);
        if (Platform.OS !== 'web') Alert.alert(tx('LUGAR OCUPADO', 'SPOT TAKEN'), check.message);
        return;
      }
      setLiveOccupiedSpots(check.occupiedSpots);
      void registerTemporarySpotLock({ serviceId, eventKey, fechaClase, spotNumber: pickedSpot, userId: effectiveUserId, ttlMs: DEFAULT_SPOT_LOCK_MS });
      setLockSeconds(Math.ceil(DEFAULT_SPOT_LOCK_MS / 1000));
      triggerHaptic('Success'); onSelectSpot?.(pickedSpot); onClose();
    } finally { setIsVerifyingSpot(false); }
  };

  const numberPickerRange = useMemo(() => (!activeNumAxis ? [] : Array.from({ length: activeNumAxis === 'total' ? MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS : 10 }, (_, i) => i + 1)), [activeNumAxis]);
  const currentAxisVal = activeNumAxis === 'total' ? gridCells.activeTotal : activeNumAxis === 'rows' ? draft.rows : draft.cols;

  if (inlineOverlay && !visible) return null;

  const sheetContent = (
    <View style={[
      globalStyles.bottomSheet, st.sheet,
      inlineOverlay ? { height: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top + 8, 16), borderTopLeftRadius: 0, borderTopRightRadius: 0 } : KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
      isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#0284C7' },
      { paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 16) + 8 }, NO_SEL,
    ]}>
      <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} />

      <View style={st.headerRow}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Text selectable={false} style={[st.microLbl, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
            {tx(`MAPA 3D DEL ESTUDIO • ${gridCells.activeTotal} ${eqMeta.es.toUpperCase()}`, `3D STUDIO MAP • ${gridCells.activeTotal} ${eqMeta.en.toUpperCase()}`)}
          </Text>
          <Text selectable={false} style={[st.titleTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>{eventTitle || tx('Distribución del Estudio', 'Studio Floor Layout')}</Text>
        </View>
        {canConfigure && (
          <TouchableOpacity style={[st.cfgToggleBtn, isLight && !configMode && st.cfgToggleBtnLightOff, configMode && st.cfgToggleBtnOn, NO_SEL]} onPress={() => { triggerHaptic('Selection'); setConfigMode((p) => !p); setEqMenuOpen(false); setActiveNumAxis(null); }}>
            <Feather name="sliders" size={13} color={configMode ? '#050505' : themeColors.cyanText} />
            <Text selectable={false} style={[st.cfgToggleTxt, { color: configMode ? '#050505' : themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tx('DISEÑAR B2B', 'B2B CONFIG')}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      <View style={[st.summaryStrip, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]}>
        <View style={st.legendRow}>
          {[
            { c: isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan, l: `${tx('Libre', 'Open')} (${freeSpots})` },
            { c: isLight ? '#059669' : COLORS.green, l: tx('Tu Selección', 'Selected') },
            { c: isLight ? '#DC2626' : '#EF4444', l: `${tx('Ocupado', 'Occupied')} (${safeOccupied})` },
          ].map((it) => (
            <View key={it.l} style={st.legendItem}>
              <View style={[st.dot3d, { backgroundColor: it.c }]} />
              <Text selectable={false} style={[st.legendTxt, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{it.l}</Text>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
        <Text selectable={false} style={[st.anonShieldTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>
          🔒 {safeOccupied}/{gridCells.activeTotal} {tx('Confirmados', 'Confirmed')} • {freeSpots} {tx('libres', 'open')} • {gridCells.activeTotal} {tx(eqMeta.es, eqMeta.en)}
          {lockSeconds > 0 && pickedSpot ? ` • ⏱️ #${pickedSpot} (${lockSeconds}s)` : ''}
        </Text>
      </View>

      {collisionAlertMsg && (
        <View style={[st.collisionBanner, isLight ? st.collisionBannerLight : st.collisionBannerDark]}>
          <Feather name="alert-triangle" size={14} color={isLight ? '#B91C1C' : '#FCA5A5'} />
          <Text selectable={false} style={[st.collisionBannerTxt, { color: isLight ? '#991B1B' : '#FECACA' }]}>{collisionAlertMsg}</Text>
        </View>
      )}

      {configMode && canConfigure && (
        <KineticStudioConfigPanel
          st={st} isLight={isLight} draft={draft} setDraft={setDraft} activeTotal={gridCells.activeTotal}
          eqMeta={eqMeta} eqMenuOpen={eqMenuOpen} setEqMenuOpen={setEqMenuOpen} activeNumAxis={activeNumAxis}
          setActiveNumAxis={setActiveNumAxis} numberPickerRange={numberPickerRange} currentAxisVal={currentAxisVal}
          adjustDimension={adjustDimension} applyDimension={applyDimension}
        />
      )}

      <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1 }} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        <KineticVolumetricStudioStageLeaf
          mode={configMode ? 'config_editor' : 'athlete_picker'}
          rows={draft.rows}
          cols={draft.cols}
          stageLabel={resolveStudioFrontStageLabel(discipline, draft.equipmentType, language === 'en')}
          isLight={isLight}
          iconName={eqMeta.icon as keyof typeof Feather.glyphMap}
          disabledAction={isVerifyingSpot}
          aisleLabel={tx('+ PASILLO', '+ AISLE')}
          cells={gridCells.list.map((c): IVolumetricStageCell => {
            const num = c.spotNum || 1;
            const isOcc = !configMode && occupiedSpotSet.has(num);
            const isSel = !configMode && !isOcc && pickedSpot === num;
            return {
              key: c.key,
              row: c.row,
              col: c.col,
              disabled: c.disabled,
              spotNum: c.spotNum,
              status: c.disabled ? 'aisle_cfg' : isSel ? 'selected' : isOcc ? 'occupied' : 'free',
              labelTop: c.disabled ? undefined : `#${num}`,
              labelBottom: c.disabled
                ? undefined
                : isOcc
                  ? tx('OCUPADO', 'TAKEN')
                  : getLocalizedStudioUnitSingular(draft.equipmentType, language === 'en'),
              isInteractive: configMode || (!isOcc && !isVerifyingSpot),
            };
          })}
          onPressCell={(cell) => {
            if (configMode) {
              toggleAisleCell(cell.key, cell.disabled);
              return;
            }
            const num = cell.spotNum || 1;
            if (occupiedSpotSet.has(num) || isVerifyingSpot) return;
            triggerHaptic('Selection');
            setCollisionAlertMsg(null); setPickedSpot(num);
            setSpotLock90s(eventKey, num, DEFAULT_SPOT_LOCK_MS);
            void registerTemporarySpotLock({ serviceId, eventKey, fechaClase, spotNumber: num, userId: effectiveUserId, ttlMs: DEFAULT_SPOT_LOCK_MS });
            setLockSeconds(Math.ceil(DEFAULT_SPOT_LOCK_MS / 1000));
            onSelectSpot?.(num); onClose();
          }}
        />
      </ScrollView>
      <View style={st.footerRow}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={[st.closeBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }, NO_SEL]} onPress={onClose}>
          <Feather name="arrow-left" size={14} color={themeColors.textPrimary} />
          <Text selectable={false} style={[st.closeBtnTxt, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{tx('ATRÁS', 'BACK')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        {configMode && canConfigure ? (
          <TouchableOpacity style={[st.primaryBtn, NO_SEL]} onPress={handleConfirmSaveLayout}>
            <Feather name="save" size={14} color="#050505" />
            <Text selectable={false} style={st.primaryBtnTxt}>{tx(`FIJAR MAPA (${Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, gridCells.activeTotal)} LUGARES)`, `SAVE MAP (${Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, gridCells.activeTotal)} SPOTS)`)}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ) : (
          <TouchableOpacity style={[st.primaryBtn, NO_SEL]} disabled={isVerifyingSpot} onPress={handleConfirmSpotSelection}>
            <Ionicons name="checkmark-circle" size={15} color="#050505" />
            <Text selectable={false} style={st.primaryBtnTxt}>
              {isVerifyingSpot ? tx('VERIFICANDO LUGAR...', 'VERIFYING SPOT...') : pickedSpot ? formatUseStudioStationCta(draft.equipmentType, pickedSpot, language === 'en') : tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT')}
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );

  if (inlineOverlay) {
    return (
      <View {...(Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } as unknown as ViewProps) : {})} style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, zIndex: 980, backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#06090F', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
        {sheetContent}
      </View>
    );
  }
  return (
    <ReactNativeModal isVisible={Boolean(visible)} onBackdropPress={onClose} onBackButtonPress={onClose} style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }} backdropColor="#04060B" backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.55 : 0.96} backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0} hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false} animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180} useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'}>
      {sheetContent}
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
});

export default KineticStudioLayoutModal;

const getTrafficLightColor = (ocupados: number, maximo: number, isLight: boolean) => {
  if (ocupados >= maximo) return isLight ? '#DC2626' : COLORS.red;
  if (ocupados >= maximo * 0.6) return isLight ? '#B45309' : COLORS.yellow;
  return isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green;
};

export const ReservationTacticalAlertModal = memo(({ alertConfig, setAlertConfig }: {
  alertConfig: IReservationAlertConfig;
  setAlertConfig: Dispatch<SetStateAction<IReservationAlertConfig>>;
}) => {
  const { themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);

  );

  return (
    <Modal
      visible={Boolean(alertConfig.visible)}
      animationType="fade"
      transparent
      onRequestClose={() => setAlertConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }))}
    >
      <Pressable
        style={styles.tacticalOverlayDark}
        onPress={() => setAlertConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }))}
      >
        <Pressable
          style={[styles.tacticalAlertBox, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#0284C7' }]}
          onPress={() => {}}
        >
          <View style={styles.tacticalAlertHeader}>
            <Feather name={alertConfig.type === 'confirm' ? 'shield' : 'activity'} size={20} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
