          </View>
          <Text style={[styles.tacticalAlertDesc, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{alertConfig.message}</Text>
          {alertConfig.type === 'confirm' ? (
            <View style={styles.tacticalAlertActions}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.tacticalCancelBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8', elevation: 0 }]}
                onPress={() => setAlertConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }))}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.tacticalCancelBtnText, { color: isLight ? '#0F172A' : themeColors.textPrimary }]}>{alertConfig.cancelText}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.tacticalConfirmBtn,
                  isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1', elevation: 0 },
                ]}
                onPress={() => {
                  const confirmFn = alertConfig.onConfirm;
                  setAlertConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }));
                  if (confirmFn) confirmFn();
                }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.tacticalConfirmBtnText, { color: isLight ? '#0369A1' : themeColors.cyanText }]}>{alertConfig.confirmText}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          ) : (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.tacticalAlertBtn,
                isLight && { backgroundColor: '#ECFEFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderTopColor: '#FFFFFF', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1', elevation: 0 },
              ]}
              onPress={() => setAlertConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }))}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.tacticalAlertBtnText, { color: isLight ? '#0369A1' : themeColors.cyanText }]}>{alertConfig.confirmText}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
          {Boolean(alertConfig.secondaryActionText && alertConfig.onSecondaryAction) && (
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={{ marginTop: 12, paddingVertical: 8, alignItems: 'center' }}
              onPress={() => {
                const secFn = alertConfig.onSecondaryAction;
                setAlertConfig((prev) => ({ ...prev, visible: false }));
                if (secFn) secFn();
              }}
            >
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800', color: themeColors.cyanText, textDecorationLine: 'underline' }}>
                {alertConfig.secondaryActionText}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
        </Pressable>
      </Pressable>
    </Modal>
  );
});

    FILE: src/components/eventos/SplitEscrowInboxSection.tsx

    // === src/components/eventos/SplitEscrowInboxSection.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, Text, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  fetchPendingSplitInvites,
  IPendingSplitEscrowInvite,
  respondSplitEscrowInvite,
} from '../../services/splitEscrowInvitesService';
import { patchKinetiClubTabAlertState } from '../../services/kineticlubTabAlertStore';
import { COLORS } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { SmartImage, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';

export interface ISplitEscrowInboxSectionProps {
  userId?: string | null;
  onAcceptInvite?: (invite: IPendingSplitEscrowInvite) => void;
  onCountChange?: (count: number) => void;
  onLoadingChange?: (loading: boolean) => void;
}

export const SplitEscrowInboxSection = memo(({ userId, onAcceptInvite, onCountChange, onLoadingChange }: ISplitEscrowInboxSectionProps) => {
  const { tx, td, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const [invites, setInvites] = useState<IPendingSplitEscrowInvite[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(() => Boolean(userId));
  const [busyId, setBusyId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const reqSeqRef = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      onLoadingChange?.(false);
    };
  }, [onLoadingChange]);

  const reload = useCallback(async () => {
    const reqId = ++reqSeqRef.current;
    if (!userId) {
      if (isMountedRef.current) {
        setInvites([]);
        setLoading(false);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (isMountedRef.current) setLoading(true);
    try {
      const rows = await fetchPendingSplitInvites(userId);
      if (isMountedRef.current && reqId === reqSeqRef.current) {
        setInvites(Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []);
      }
    } catch {
      if (isMountedRef.current && reqId === reqSeqRef.current) {
        setInvites([]);
      }
    } finally {
      if (isMountedRef.current && reqId === reqSeqRef.current) {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }
  }, [userId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void reload();
  }, [reload]);

  useEffect(() => {
    onLoadingChange?.(loading);
  }, [loading, onLoadingChange]);

  useEffect(() => {
    onCountChange?.(invites.length);
    if (!loading && userId) {
      patchKinetiClubTabAlertState({ pendingSplitInvitesCount: invites.length }, userId);
    }
  }, [invites.length, loading, userId, onCountChange]);

  const handleRespond = useCallback(
    async (inv: IPendingSplitEscrowInvite, estado: 'aceptada' | 'rechazada') => {
      if (busyId) return;
      triggerHaptic(estado === 'aceptada' ? 'Success' : 'Selection');
      setBusyId(inv.id);
      try {
        await respondSplitEscrowInvite(inv.id, estado);
        if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
        setInvites((prev) => {
          const next = prev.filter((item) => item.id !== inv.id);
          if (userId) patchKinetiClubTabAlertState({ pendingSplitInvitesCount: next.length }, userId);
          return next;
        });
        if (estado === 'aceptada') onAcceptInvite?.(inv);
      } catch {
      } finally {
        if (isMountedRef.current) setBusyId(null);
      }
    },
    [busyId, onAcceptInvite, userId]
  );

  if (loading && invites.length === 0) return null;
  if (invites.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <View style={ib.wrap}>
      <Text style={[ib.header, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>
        {tx(`INVITACIONES SPLIT-ESCROW EN KINETIC (${invites.length})`, `KINETIC SPLIT-ESCROW INVITES (${invites.length})`)}
      </Text>
      {invites.map((inv) => {
        const isBusy = busyId === inv.id;
        return (
          <View
            key={inv.id}
            style={[
              ib.card,
              isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FAF5FF', borderColor: '#C4B5FD', borderBottomColor: '#7C3AED' },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={ib.topRow}>
              <View style={ib.avatarBox}>
                {inv.anfitrion_avatar ? (
                  <SmartImage source={{ uri: inv.anfitrion_avatar }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
                ) : (
                  <Feather name="user" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                )}
              </View>
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={[ib.title, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {td(inv.servicio_nombre)}
                </Text>
                <Text style={[ib.sub, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]} numberOfLines={2}>
                  {tx(
                    `${inv.anfitrion_nombre} te invita • ${inv.negocio_nombre} • Tu parte 40%: ${inv.monto_kc_parte} KC`,
                    `${inv.anfitrion_nombre} invites you • ${inv.negocio_nombre} • Your 40% share: ${inv.monto_kc_parte} KC`
                  )}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
            <View style={ib.btnRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                disabled={isBusy}
                onPress={() => void handleRespond(inv, 'rechazada')}
                style={[ib.declineBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FEF2F2', borderColor: '#F87171' }]}
              >
                <Text style={[ib.declineTxt, { color: isLight ? '#DC2626' : COLORS.red }]}>
                  {tx('RECHAZAR', 'DECLINE')}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                disabled={isBusy}
                onPress={() => void handleRespond(inv, 'aceptada')}
                style={ib.acceptBtn}
              >
                {isBusy ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#050505" />
                ) : (
                  <Text style={ib.acceptTxt}>{tx('✓ ACEPTAR EN KINETIC', '✓ ACCEPT IN KINETIC')}</Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        );
      })}
    </View>
  );
});

  card: {

import { Feather, Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { memo, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import * as RN from 'react-native';
import { ActivityIndicator, Keyboard, Platform, ScrollView, Share, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import ReactNativeModal from 'react-native-modal';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { computeSplitEscrow1NMath } from '../../services/kineticSplitPreBookingStore';
import { createSplitEscrowInvites, ISplitInviteAthlete, loadSplitEscrowDirectory, searchPublicAthletesForSplit } from '../../services/splitEscrowInvitesService';
import { COLORS, globalStyles } from '../../styles/globalStyles';
import { openExternalLinkSafe } from '../../utils/externalLinks';
import { setKineticInlineOverlayOpen } from '../../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { SmartImage, triggerArcadeHaptic, triggerHaptic } from '../SharedUI';
import { KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS, KineticSwipeCloseHeader } from '../shared/KineticModalHeader3D';
import { buildCleanSquadDeepLink, buildCleanSquadInviteMessage, translateAthleteTierLabel } from './eventDetailsPureHelpers';
import { SplitEscrowInboxSection } from './SplitEscrowInboxSection';
import { splitInviteStyles as st } from './SplitEscrowInviteModal.styles';

const SplitEscrowDirectorySkeleton = memo(({ isLight }: { isLight: boolean }) => {
  const bar = isLight ? 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.28)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.16)';
  const border = isLight ? '#E2E8F0' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 6, marginTop: 2 }}>
      {[0, 1, 2].map((idx) => (
        <View key={`split_skel_${idx}`} style={[st.friendRow, { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0B101B', borderColor: border, marginBottom: 0 }]}>
          <View style={[st.avatarCircle, { backgroundColor: bar, borderColor: border }]} />
          <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 6 }}>
            <View style={[st.skeletonBlock, { width: idx === 1 ? '62%' : '74%', height: 12, backgroundColor: bar }]} />
            <View style={[st.skeletonBlock, { width: '46%', height: 9, backgroundColor: bar }]} />
          </View>
          <View style={[st.skeletonBlock, { width: 62, height: 24, borderRadius: 8, backgroundColor: bar }]} />
        </View>
      ))}
    </View>
  );
});

export { SplitEscrowInboxSection };

export const shareTacticalMessageSafe = async (title: string, message: string, url?: string) => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    const nav: any = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator : null;
    const cleanText = url ? message.replace(url, '').trim() : message;
    if (nav && typeof nav.share === 'function') {
      try {
        await nav.share({ title, text: cleanText, ...(url ? { url } : {}) });
        return;
      } catch (err: any) {
        if (String(err?.name || '') === 'AbortError') return;
      }
    }
    try { if (nav?.clipboard?.writeText) await nav.clipboard.writeText(message); } catch {}
    await openExternalLinkSafe(`https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(message)}`);
    return;
  }
  try {
    await Share.share({ title, message, ...(Platform.OS === 'ios' && url ? { url } : {}) });
  } catch {}
};

export interface IPreBookingSplitPayload {
  splitCount: number;
  selectedGuests: ISplitInviteAthlete[];
  perPersonEscrow: number;
  perPersonDoor: number;
}

export interface ISplitEscrowInviteModalProps {
  visible: boolean; onClose: () => void; inlineOverlay?: boolean; eventId?: string | number; reservaId?: string | null;
  title: string; discipline: string; clubName: string; location: string; readableDate: string;
  totalKc: number; escrow40: number; maxPersonas?: 2 | 3 | 4;
  initialSplitCount?: number;
  initialSelectedGuests?: ISplitInviteAthlete[];
  onApplyPreBookingSplit?: (cfg: IPreBookingSplitPayload) => void;
  onResetIndividualSplit?: () => void;
}

export const SplitEscrowInviteModal = memo((props: ISplitEscrowInviteModalProps) => {
  const {
    visible, onClose, inlineOverlay = false, eventId, reservaId, title, discipline, clubName,
    location, readableDate, totalKc, escrow40, maxPersonas = 4,
    initialSplitCount, initialSelectedGuests, onApplyPreBookingSplit, onResetIndividualSplit,
  } = props;

  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { user, profile } = useAuth();
  const { tx, tDisc, language, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
  const isLight = Boolean(themeColors.isLight);
  const isPreBookingMode = Boolean(!reservaId && onApplyPreBookingSplit);

  const safeMax: 2 | 3 | 4 = maxPersonas === 2 ? 2 : maxPersonas === 3 ? 3 : 4;
  const countOptions = useMemo(() => [2, 3, 4].filter((n) => n <= safeMax), [safeMax]);
  const [splitCount, setSplitCount] = useState<number>(2);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [directory, setDirectory] = useState<ISplitInviteAthlete[]>([]);
  const [liveResults, setLiveResults] = useState<ISplitInviteAthlete[] | null>(null);
  const [loadingAthletes, setLoadingAthletes] = useState(false);
  const [selectedGuests, setSelectedGuests] = useState<ISplitInviteAthlete[]>([]);
  const [isSendingKinetic, setIsSendingKinetic] = useState(false);
  const isSendingKineticRef = React.useRef(false);
  const [statusNotice, setStatusNotice] = useState<{ text: string; tone: 'ok' | 'warn' } | null>(null);
  const [keyboardOpen, setKeyboardOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const showSub = Keyboard.addListener(Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'keyboardWillShow' : 'keyboardDidShow', () => setKeyboardOpen(true));
    const hideSub = Keyboard.addListener(Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'keyboardWillHide' : 'keyboardDidHide', () => setKeyboardOpen(false));
    return () => { showSub.remove(); hideSub.remove(); };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!visible) {
      setSearchQuery(''); setStatusNotice(null); setLiveResults(null); setKeyboardOpen(false);
      return;
    }
    if (inlineOverlay) setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(true);
    const nextSplit =
      typeof initialSplitCount === 'number' && initialSplitCount >= 2 && initialSplitCount <= safeMax
        ? initialSplitCount
        : 2;
    setSplitCount(nextSplit);
    setSelectedGuests(
      Array.isArray(initialSelectedGuests) ? initialSelectedGuests.slice(0, nextSplit - 1) : []
    );
    let active = true;
    setLoadingAthletes(true);
    loadSplitEscrowDirectory(user?.id, { myEmail: user?.email, myName: profile?.nombre })
      .then((rows) => { if (active) setDirectory(rows); })
      .catch(() => {})
      .finally(() => { if (active) setLoadingAthletes(false); });
    return () => {
      active = false;
      if (inlineOverlay) setKineticInlineOverlayOpen(false);
    };
  }, [visible, inlineOverlay, safeMax, user?.id, user?.email, profile?.nombre, initialSplitCount, initialSelectedGuests]);

  const allyIdSet = useMemo(() => new Set(directory.filter((d) => d.isAlly).map((d) => d.id)), [directory]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim();
    if (!visible || !q) { setLiveResults(null); return; }
    let active = true;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      searchPublicAthletesForSplit(q, user?.id, allyIdSet, { myEmail: user?.email, myName: profile?.nombre })
        .then((rows) => { if (active) setLiveResults(rows); })
        .catch(() => {});
    }, 240);
    return () => { active = false; clearTimeout(timer); };
  }, [visible, searchQuery, user?.id, user?.email, profile?.nombre, allyIdSet]);

  const cleanEscrow = Math.max(0, Number(escrow40) || Math.round((Number(totalKc) || 0) * 0.4));
  const puerta60 = Math.max(0, (Number(totalKc) || 0) - cleanEscrow);
  const cleanTotal = Math.max(Number(totalKc) || 0, cleanEscrow + puerta60);
  const splitMath = useMemo(
    () => computeSplitEscrow1NMath(cleanTotal, splitCount, cleanEscrow),
    [cleanTotal, splitCount, cleanEscrow]
  );
  const perPersonEscrow = splitMath.effectiveEscrowKc;
  const perPersonDoor = splitMath.effectiveDoorKc;

  const deepLinkUrl = useMemo(
    () => buildCleanSquadDeepLink(eventId, title, splitCount, user?.id),
    [eventId, title, splitCount, user?.id]
  );

  const buildInviteText = useCallback(
    (guestName?: string) => {
      const raw = buildCleanSquadInviteMessage({
        guestName, eventId, title, discipline: language === 'en' ? tDisc(discipline) : discipline,
        clubName, location, readableDate, splitCount, totalKc: cleanTotal, hostId: user?.id, language,
      }).message;
      return raw.replace(/\bEscuadr[óo]n\b/g, 'Equipo').replace(/\bSquad\b/g, 'Team');
    },
    [language, splitCount, eventId, title, tDisc, discipline, clubName, location, readableDate, cleanTotal, user?.id]
  );

  const copyTextSilently = useCallback(async (textToCopy: string) => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const nav: any = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator : null;
      try { if (nav?.clipboard?.writeText) { await nav.clipboard.writeText(textToCopy); return; } } catch {}
      try {
        if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
          const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
          ta.value = textToCopy; ta.style.position = 'fixed'; ta.style.opacity = '0';
          document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy'); document.body.removeChild(ta);
        }
      } catch {}
      return;
    }
    try { (RN as any).Clipboard?.setString?.(textToCopy); } catch {}
  }, []);

  const handleToggleSelectAthlete = useCallback((athlete: ISplitInviteAthlete) => {
    const aid = String(athlete?.id || '');
    if (!aid) return;
    setStatusNotice(null);
    setSelectedGuests((prev) => {
      if (prev.some((x) => x.id === aid)) {
        triggerHaptic('Selection');
        return prev.filter((x) => x.id !== aid);
      }
      if (prev.length >= splitCount - 1) {
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        return prev;
      }
      triggerArcadeHaptic('unlock');
      return [...prev, athlete];
    });
  }, [splitCount]);

  const handleSendKineticInvites = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isPreBookingMode && onApplyPreBookingSplit) {
      Keyboard.dismiss();
      triggerArcadeHaptic('unlock');
      onApplyPreBookingSplit({
        splitCount,
        selectedGuests: selectedGuests.slice(0, splitCount - 1),
        perPersonEscrow,
        perPersonDoor,
      });
      onClose?.();
      return;
    }
    if (isSendingKineticRef.current || isSendingKinetic || selectedGuests.length === 0 || !user?.id) return;
    isSendingKineticRef.current = true;
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    setIsSendingKinetic(true);
    setStatusNotice(null);
    try {
      const res = await createSplitEscrowInvites({
        reservaId, eventId, title, anfitrionId: user.id, invitados: selectedGuests, montoKcParte: perPersonEscrow,
      });
      if (res.requiresReserva) {
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        setStatusNotice({
          tone: 'warn',
          text: tx(
            'Reserva primero tu lugar (40% Escrow) para enviar invitaciones en app, o confirma por WhatsApp ahora.',
            'Confirm your own booking (40% Escrow) first to send in-app invites, or confirm via WhatsApp now.'
          ),
        });
        return;
      }
      triggerArcadeHaptic('unlock');
      setSelectedGuests([]);
      setStatusNotice({
        tone: 'ok',
        text: tx(`✓ ${res.insertedCount} invitación(es) enviadas en Kinetic`, `✓ ${res.insertedCount} Kinetic invite(s) sent`),
      });
    } catch (err: any) {
      triggerHaptic('Error');
      setStatusNotice({ tone: 'warn', text: err?.message || tx('No se pudo enviar la invitación.', 'Could not send invite.') });
    } finally {
      isSendingKineticRef.current = false;
      setIsSendingKinetic(false);
    }
  }, [
    isPreBookingMode, onApplyPreBookingSplit, splitCount, selectedGuests, perPersonEscrow, perPersonDoor,
    onClose, isSendingKinetic, user?.id, reservaId, eventId, title, tx,
  ]);

  const handleWhatsAppShare = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Medium'); triggerArcadeHaptic('unlock');
    const names = selectedGuests.map((g) => g.nombre).join(', ');
    openExternalLinkSafe(`https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(buildInviteText(names || undefined))}`);
  }, [buildInviteText, selectedGuests]);

  const handleCopyDeepLink = useCallback(async () => {
    await copyTextSilently(deepLinkUrl);
    triggerHaptic('Success'); triggerArcadeHaptic('unlock');
    setStatusNotice({ tone: 'ok', text: tx('✓ ¡Enlace directo copiado!', '✓ Direct link copied!') });
  }, [copyTextSilently, deepLinkUrl, tx]);

  const filteredAthletes = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    const baseList = liveResults ?? directory;
    if (!q) return baseList;
    return baseList.filter((a) => String(a?.nombre || '').toLowerCase().includes(q));
  }, [directory, liveResults, searchQuery]);

  if (inlineOverlay && !visible) return null;

  const sheetBody = (
    <View style={[
      globalStyles.bottomSheet, st.sheet,
      inlineOverlay
        ? { height: '100%', maxHeight: '100%', borderTopLeftRadius: 0, borderTopRightRadius: 0, paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top + 8, 14) }
        : KINETIC_TOP_SHEET_METRICS,
      isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC', borderColor: '#7C3AED' },
      { paddingBottom: insets.bottom > 0 ? insets.bottom + 16 : 24 },
    ]}>
      <KineticSwipeCloseHeader onClose={onClose} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }} />

      <Text style={[st.title, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]}>
        {tx('Dividir Cuenta • Equipo 40% Escrow', 'Split Bill • 40% Team Escrow')}
      </Text>
      <Text style={[st.sub, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]} numberOfLines={2}>
        {title} • {clubName}
      </Text>

      {!keyboardOpen && (
        <View style={[st.summaryCard, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' }]}>
          <Text style={[st.secLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted }]}>
            {tx(`1. NÚMERO DE INTEGRANTES (2 A ${safeMax} PERSONAS)`, `1. TEAM SIZE (2 TO ${safeMax} PEOPLE)`)}
          </Text>
          <View style={st.pillsRow}>
            {countOptions.map((cnt) => {
              const active = splitCount === cnt;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={cnt} activeOpacity={0.85}
                  onPress={() => {
                    triggerHaptic('Selection');
                    setSplitCount(cnt);
                    setSelectedGuests((prev) => prev.slice(0, cnt - 1));
                  }}
                  style={[
                    st.countPill,
                    isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1', borderBottomColor: '#94A3B8' },
                    active && (isLight ? st.countPillActiveLight : st.countPillActiveDark),
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text style={[st.countPillTop, { color: active ? themeColors.cyanText : themeColors.textMuted }]}>
                    {cnt} {tx('Personas', 'People')}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[st.countPillVal, { color: active ? themeColors.textPrimary : themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
                    {computeSplitEscrow1NMath(cleanTotal, cnt, cleanEscrow).effectiveEscrowKc} KC {tx('c/u', 'ea.')}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
          <View style={st.mathRow}>
            <Text style={[st.mathLbl, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('Custodia 40% por integrante hoy:', '40% Escrow per member today:')}</Text>
            <Text style={[st.mathVal, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{perPersonEscrow} KC</Text>
          </View>
          <View style={st.mathRow}>
            <Text style={[st.mathLbl, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>{tx('Liquidación 60% en QR puerta c/u:', '60% QR door settlement ea.:')}</Text>
            <Text style={[st.mathVal, { color: themeColors.purpleText }]}>{perPersonDoor} KC</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      )}

      <Text style={[st.secLbl, { color: themeColors.textMuted, marginTop: 10 }]}>
        {tx(`2. ELEGIR EQUIPO KINETICLUB (${selectedGuests.length}/${splitCount - 1})`, `2. SELECT KINETICLUB TEAM (${selectedGuests.length}/${splitCount - 1})`)}
      </Text>
      <View style={[st.searchBox, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' }]}>
        <Feather name="search" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
        <TextInput
          style={[st.searchInput, { color: themeColors.textPrimary, fontSize: 16 }]}
          value={searchQuery} onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
          placeholder={tx('Buscar atleta en KinetiClub...', 'Search KinetiClub athlete...')}
          placeholderTextColor={themeColors.textMuted}
        />
      </View>

      <ScrollView style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 110 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        {loadingAthletes && directory.length === 0 ? (
          <SplitEscrowDirectorySkeleton isLight={isLight} />
        ) : filteredAthletes.length === 0 ? (
          <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 12, textAlign: 'center', marginVertical: 14 }}>
            {tx('Usa WhatsApp o Enlace Directo para invitar a tu equipo.', 'Use WhatsApp or Direct Link to invite your team.')}
          </Text>
        ) : (
          filteredAthletes.map((ath) => {
            const selected = selectedGuests.some((g) => g.id === ath.id);
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={ath.id} activeOpacity={0.84} onPress={() => handleToggleSelectAthlete(ath)}
                style={[st.friendRow, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#E2E8F0' }, selected && { borderColor: isLight ? '#059669' : COLORS.green }]}
              >
                <View style={st.avatarCircle}>
                  {ath.avatar_url ? (
                    <SmartImage source={{ uri: ath.avatar_url }} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%' }} contentFit="cover" />
                  ) : (
                    <Feather name="user" size={14} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
                  )}
                </View>
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
                    <Text style={[st.friendName, { color: themeColors.textPrimary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                      {ath.nombre || tx('Atleta KinetiClub', 'KinetiClub Athlete')}
                    </Text>
                    {ath.isAlly && (
                      <Text style={{ fontSize: 8.5, fontWeight: '900', color: themeColors.purpleText }}>★ {tx('ALIADO', 'ALLY')}</Text>
                    )}
                  </View>
                  <Text style={{ color: themeColors.textMuted, fontSize: 10.5, fontWeight: '700' }}>
                    {translateAthleteTierLabel(ath.nivel, language)} • {perPersonEscrow} KC (40%)
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={[
                  st.inviteChip,
                  selected
                    ? { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#DCFCE7' : 'rgba(0, 255, 102, 0.16)', borderColor: isLight ? '#059669' : COLORS.green }
                    : { backgroundColor: isLight ? '#ECFEFF' : 'rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.14)', borderColor: isLight ? '#0284C7' : COLORS.cyan },
                ]}>
                  <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900', color: selected ? (isLight ? '#047857' : COLORS.green) : themeColors.cyanText }}>
                    {selected ? tx('✓ ELEGIDO', '✓ SELECTED') : tx('ELEGIR', 'SELECT')}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })
        )}
      </ScrollView>

      {statusNotice && (
        <Text style={{ color: statusNotice.tone === 'ok' ? themeColors.green : '#F59E0B', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800', textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 6 }}>
          {statusNotice.text}
        </Text>
      )}

      <TouchableOpacity
        style={[
          st.kineticPrimaryBtn,
          ((!isPreBookingMode && selectedGuests.length === 0) || isSendingKinetic) && { opacity: 0.45 },
        ]}
        activeOpacity={0.86}
        disabled={(!isPreBookingMode && selectedGuests.length === 0) || isSendingKinetic}
        onPress={handleSendKineticInvites}
      >
        {isSendingKinetic ? (
          <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#050505" />
        ) : (
          <Text style={st.kineticPrimaryTxt} numberOfLines={1} adjustsFontSizeToFit>
            {isPreBookingMode
              ? tx(`⚡ APLICAR DIVISIÓN 1/${splitCount} • ${perPersonEscrow} KC HOY`, `⚡ APPLY 1/${splitCount} SPLIT • ${perPersonEscrow} KC TODAY`)
              : tx(`⚡ ENVIAR INVITACIÓN EN KINETIC (${selectedGuests.length})`, `⚡ SEND KINETIC INVITE (${selectedGuests.length})`)}
          </Text>
        )}
      </TouchableOpacity>
      {isPreBookingMode && ((initialSplitCount ?? 1) > 1 || selectedGuests.length > 0) && onResetIndividualSplit && (
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[st.resetSoloBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#F1F5F9', borderColor: '#CBD5E1' }]}
          activeOpacity={0.84}
          onPress={() => {
            triggerHaptic('Selection');
            onResetIndividualSplit();
            onClose?.();
          }}
        >
          <Text style={[st.resetSoloTxt, { color: themeColors.textSecondary }]}>
            {tx('Volver a 1 persona (Individual)', 'Reset to 1 person (Individual)')}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      )}

      <View style={st.actionsRow}>
        <TouchableOpacity style={st.whatsappBtn} activeOpacity={0.86} onPress={handleWhatsAppShare}>
          <Ionicons name="logo-whatsapp" size={16} color="#050505" />
          <Text style={st.whatsappBtnTxt}>{tx('📲 CONFIRMAR POR WHATSAPP', '📲 CONFIRM VIA WHATSAPP')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[st.copyLinkBtn, isLight && { backgroundColor: '#FFFFFF', borderColor: '#0284C7', borderBottomColor: '#0369A1' }]}
          activeOpacity={0.86} onPress={handleCopyDeepLink}
        >
          <Feather name="link" size={15} color={themeColors.cyanText} />
          <Text style={[st.copyLinkTxt, { color: themeColors.cyanText }]}>{tx('DEEP LINK', 'DEEP LINK')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </View>
  );

  if (inlineOverlay) {
    return (
      <View
        {...(Platform.OS === 'web' ? ({ dataSet: { kineticSheetOpen: 'true' } } as any) : {})}
        style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 0, zIndex: 980, backgroundColor: isLight ? '#F8FAFC' : '#080B12', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}
      >
        {sheetBody}
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ReactNativeModal
      isVisible={visible} onBackdropPress={onClose} onBackButtonPress={onClose}
      style={{ margin: 0, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }} backdropColor="#04060B" backdropOpacity={isLight ? 0.55 : 0.96}
      backdropTransitionOutTiming={0} backdropTransitionInTiming={0} hideModalContentWhileAnimating={false}
      animationInTiming={200} animationOutTiming={180}
      useNativeDriver={Platform.OS !== 'web'} useNativeDriverForBackdrop={Platform.OS !== 'web'} avoidKeyboard={true}
    >
      {sheetBody}
    </ReactNativeModal>
  );
});

    FILE: src/components/partner-web/PartnerWebEventsCrudView.tsx

    // === src/components/partner-web/PartnerWebEventsCrudView.tsx === //
import { Feather } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { ActivityIndicator, Image, StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, TouchableOpacity, View } from 'react-native';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { usePartnerWebEventsCrud } from '../../hooks/partner-web/usePartnerWebEventsCrud';
import { computeServiceMinRequiredSpots, PARTNER_EVENT_DISCIPLINES, PARTNER_EVENT_FLASH_PCTS, PARTNER_EVENT_FREQS, PARTNER_EVENT_SPLIT_OPTS } from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebMediaAndCatalogService';
import type { IPartnerWebAffiliateRow, IPartnerWebReservationRow, IPartnerWebServiceRow } from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebTypes';
import { buildGridForTotalSpots, clampStudioGridToMaxSpots, EQUIPMENT_META, inferEquipmentByDiscipline, MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, type IStudioLayoutConfig, type StudioEquipmentType } from '../../services/spotCollisionService';
import { resolveStudioFrontStageLabel } from '../../services/studioLayoutGridHelper';
import { preservesBookedSpotPositions } from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebMediaAndCatalogService';
import { KineticDualConfirmModal } from '../shared/KineticDualConfirmModal';
import { PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, type IPartnerWebPalette, partnerWebSharedStyles } from './partnerWebThemeStyles';

const EQ_KEYS: StudioEquipmentType[] = ['reformer', 'bike', 'barre', 'bag', 'court', 'spot'];

function StudioGridMatrixEditor({
  pal, layout, discipline, minSpots, occupiedSpots, disabled, onChange,
}: {
  pal: IPartnerWebPalette; layout: IStudioLayoutConfig; discipline: string;
  minSpots: number; occupiedSpots?: Set<number>; disabled?: boolean; onChange: (next: IStudioLayoutConfig) => void;
}) {
  const { language, tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const disabledSet = useMemo(() => new Set(layout.disabledCells || []), [layout.disabledCells]);
  const effectiveMin = useMemo(() => Math.max(minSpots, ...(occupiedSpots && occupiedSpots.size > 0 ? Array.from(occupiedSpots) : [1])), [minSpots, occupiedSpots]);
  const adjustAxis = (dR: number, dC: number) => {
    if (disabled) return;
    const next = clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(Math.max(1, Math.min(10, layout.rows + dR)), Math.max(1, Math.min(10, layout.cols + dC)), layout.disabledCells, layout.equipmentType);
    if (preservesBookedSpotPositions(layout, next, minSpots, occupiedSpots)) onChange(next);
  };
  const toggleCell = (key: string) => {
    if (disabled) return;
    const nextDis = disabledSet.has(key) ? (layout.disabledCells || []).filter((k) => k !== key) : [...(layout.disabledCells || []), key];
    const next = clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(layout.rows, layout.cols, nextDis, layout.equipmentType);
    if (preservesBookedSpotPositions(layout, next, minSpots, occupiedSpots)) onChange(next);
  };
  let seq = 0;
  return (
    <View style={[s.matrixBox, { backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle }]}>
      <View style={s.rowBetweenWrap}>
        <Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx(`Matriz Sala 3D (${layout.totalSpots}/${MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS} | mín. ${effectiveMin})`, `3D Studio Grid (${layout.totalSpots}/${MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS} | min ${effectiveMin})`)}</Text>
        <View style={s.wrapRow}>
          {EQ_KEYS.map((eq) => (
            <TouchableOpacity key={eq} disabled={disabled} onPress={() => onChange({ ...layout, equipmentType: eq })} style={[s.chipBtn, { borderColor: layout.equipmentType === eq ? pal.cyanText : pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: layout.equipmentType === eq ? pal.translucentCyanBg : pal.cardBg }]}>
              <Text style={{ color: layout.equipmentType === eq ? pal.cyanText : pal.textMuted, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '800' }}>{EQUIPMENT_META[eq].tag}</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
        </View>
      </View>
      <View style={s.rowBetweenWrap}>
        {([['Filas', 'Rows', layout.rows, 1, 0], ['Cols', 'Cols', layout.cols, 0, 1]] as const).map(([esLbl, enLbl, val, dR, dC]) => (
          <View key={esLbl} style={s.stepperRow}>
            <Text style={{ color: pal.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '700' }}>{tx(`${esLbl}: ${val}`, `${enLbl}: ${val}`)}</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity disabled={disabled || val <= 1} onPress={() => adjustAxis(-dR, -dC)} style={[s.stepBtn, { borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.cardBg }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontWeight: '900' }}>-</Text></TouchableOpacity>
            <TouchableOpacity disabled={disabled || val >= 10} onPress={() => adjustAxis(dR, dC)} style={[s.stepBtn, { borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.cardBg }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontWeight: '900' }}>+</Text></TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>
      <View style={[s.stageBar, { backgroundColor: pal.translucentPurpleBg, borderColor: pal.purple }]}>
        <Text style={{ color: pal.purpleText, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '900' }}>{resolveStudioFrontStageLabel(discipline, layout.equipmentType, language === 'en')}</Text>
      </View>
      <View style={{ gap: 6 }}>
        {Array.from({ length: layout.rows }).map((_, r) => (
          <View key={r} style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 }}>
            {Array.from({ length: layout.cols }).map((__, c) => {
              const key = `${r}-${c}`;
              const off = disabledSet.has(key);
              const spotNum = off ? 0 : ++seq;
              const isBooked = spotNum > 0 && Boolean(occupiedSpots?.has(spotNum));
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity key={key} disabled={disabled || isBooked} onPress={() => toggleCell(key)} style={[s.gridCell, { borderColor: off ? pal.borderSubtle : isBooked ? pal.amberText : pal.cyanText, backgroundColor: off ? 'transparent' : isBooked ? pal.translucentAmberBg : pal.cardBg, opacity: off ? 0.45 : 1 }]}>
                  <Text style={{ color: off ? pal.textMuted : isBooked ? pal.amberText : pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '900' }}>{off ? '—' : `#${spotNum}`}</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

interface PartnerWebEventsCrudViewProps {
  pal: IPartnerWebPalette; actorUserId: string; isAdmin: boolean; isB2B: boolean;
  business: IPartnerWebAffiliateRow | null; services: IPartnerWebServiceRow[];
  activeReservations: IPartnerWebReservationRow[]; completedReservations: IPartnerWebReservationRow[];
  onRefreshWorkspace: () => Promise<void>;
}

export function PartnerWebEventsCrudView(props: PartnerWebEventsCrudViewProps) {
  const { pal, business, services, activeReservations, completedReservations } = props;
  const { tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const {
    filterKey, setFilterKey, editorModal, setForm, busy, studioPanel, feedback, canMutate, editFloor, editBookedSet,
    selectedService, selectedFloor, selectedBookedSet, filteredServices, openEditor, closeEditor, clearCoverImage,
    setConfirmDeleteId, setEditorLayout, handleUploadEventCover, handleSaveEvent, handleInspectStudio, handleSaveLiveLayout,
    handleTogglePause, handleDelete,
  } = usePartnerWebEventsCrud(props);

  const { open: formOpen, editingService, form } = editorModal;
  const { saving, uploadingCover, confirmDeleteId, deletingId, togglingId, savingLayout } = busy;
  const { roster: studioRoster, loading: loadingStudio, editorLayout, selectedServiceId } = studioPanel;

  const priceNum = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(form.precio_kc) || 0));
  const escrowKc = Math.round(priceNum * 0.4);
  const inpBg = { backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, color: pal.textPrimary };

  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 16 }}>
      {!canMutate.ok && (
        <View style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.translucentPill, { borderRadius: 14, padding: 14, backgroundColor: pal.translucentAmberBg, borderColor: pal.amberText }]}>
          <Text style={{ color: pal.amberText, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800' }}>{canMutate.reasonCode === 'venue_not_verified' ? tx('Sede en revisión: puedes consultar catálogo y Sala 3D; publicar/editar requiere verificación activa.', 'Venue under review: read-only catalog & 3D preview until verified.') : tx('Sin permisos B2B de escritura sobre esta sede.', 'No B2B write access for this venue.')}</Text>
        </View>
      )}
      <View style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.card3D, s.headerCard, { backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, borderTopColor: pal.cyanText, borderBottomColor: pal.borderBottom3D }]}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 240, gap: 6 }}>

          <View style={s.wrapRow}>
            {([['all', tx(`Todos (${services.length})`, `All (${services.length})`)], ['active', tx('Activos', 'Active')], ['flash', 'Flash <3h'], ['paused', tx('Pausados', 'Paused')]] as const).map(([k, lbl]) => (
              <TouchableOpacity key={k} onPress={() => setFilterKey(k)} style={[s.chipBtn, { borderColor: filterKey === k ? pal.cyanText : pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: filterKey === k ? pal.translucentCyanBg : pal.recessedBg }]}>
                <Text style={{ color: filterKey === k ? pal.cyanText : pal.textMuted, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{lbl}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </View>
        </View>
        <TouchableOpacity disabled={!canMutate.ok} onPress={() => openEditor(null)} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnPrimaryCyan, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, { opacity: canMutate.ok ? 1 : 0.45 }]}>
          <Feather name="plus" size={16} color="#050505" />
          <Text style={{ color: '#050505', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900' }}>{tx('Nueva Experiencia', 'New Experience')}</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {Boolean(feedback) && (
        <View style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.translucentPill, { borderRadius: 12, padding: 12, backgroundColor: feedback?.type === 'ok' ? pal.translucentCyanBg : pal.translucentAmberBg, borderColor: feedback?.type === 'ok' ? pal.cyanText : pal.redText }]}>
          <Text style={{ color: feedback?.type === 'ok' ? pal.cyanText : pal.redText, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800' }}>{feedback?.text}</Text>
        </View>
      )}

      {formOpen && (
        <View testID="partner-web-event-form" style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.card3D, { backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: pal.purple, borderTopColor: pal.purple, borderBottomColor: pal.borderBottom3D, gap: 12 }]}>
          <View style={s.rowBetweenWrap}>

            <TouchableOpacity onPress={closeEditor} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, { borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('Cerrar', 'Close')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

            <TextInput value={form.nombre_servicio} onChangeText={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, nombre_servicio: v }))} placeholder={tx('Título del evento/clase', 'Class or event title')} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, s.halfInput, { fontSize: 16 }, inpBg]} />
            <TextInput value={form.categoria} onChangeText={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, categoria: v, studio_layout: { ...p.studio_layout, equipmentType: inferEquipmentByDiscipline(v, p.nombre_servicio) } }))} placeholder={tx('Disciplina / Categoría', 'Discipline')} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, s.halfInput, { fontSize: 16 }, inpBg]} />
          </View>
          <View style={s.wrapRow}>
            {PARTNER_EVENT_DISCIPLINES.map((d) => (
              <TouchableOpacity key={d} onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, categoria: d, studio_layout: { ...p.studio_layout, equipmentType: inferEquipmentByDiscipline(d, p.nombre_servicio) } }))} style={[s.chipBtn, { borderColor: form.categoria === d ? pal.purple : pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: form.categoria === d ? pal.translucentPurpleBg : pal.recessedBg }]}>
                <Text style={{ color: form.categoria === d ? pal.purpleText : pal.textMuted, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{d}</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </View>
          <View style={s.gridTwo}>
            <TextInput value={form.instructor_nombre} onChangeText={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, instructor_nombre: v }))} placeholder={tx('Coach / Instructor', 'Coach / Instructor')} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, s.halfInput, { fontSize: 16 }, inpBg]} />
            <TextInput value={form.ubicacion} onChangeText={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, ubicacion: v }))} placeholder={tx('Ubicación / Zona', 'Location')} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, s.halfInput, { fontSize: 16 }, inpBg]} />
            <TextInput value={form.fecha} onChangeText={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, fecha: v }))} placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD" placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, s.thirdInput, { fontSize: 16 }, inpBg]} />
            <TextInput value={form.hora_inicio} onChangeText={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, hora_inicio: v }))} placeholder="HH:MM" placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, s.thirdInput, { fontSize: 16 }, inpBg]} />
            <TextInput value={form.duracion_minutos} onChangeText={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, duracion_minutos: v.replace(/\D/g, '') }))} keyboardType="numeric" placeholder={tx('Duración min', 'Minutes')} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, s.thirdInput, { fontSize: 16 }, inpBg]} />
          </View>
          <View style={s.gridTwo}>
            <View style={[s.halfInput, { gap: 4 }]}>
              <View style={s.stepperRow}>
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, precio_kc: String(Math.max(0, (Math.round(Number(p.precio_kc) / 10) || 0) * 10 - 10)) }))} style={[s.stepBtn, { borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontWeight: '900' }}>-10</Text></TouchableOpacity>
                <TextInput value={form.precio_kc} onChangeText={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, precio_kc: v.replace(/\D/g, '') }))} keyboardType="numeric" placeholder="Precio KC (10)" placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, { fontSize: 16, flex: 1 }, inpBg]} />
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, precio_kc: String(Math.min(50000, (Math.round(Number(p.precio_kc) / 10) || 0) * 10 + 10)) }))} style={[s.stepBtn, { borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontWeight: '900' }}>+10</Text></TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
              <Text style={{ color: pal.cyanText, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx(`Escrow 40%: ${escrowKc} KC • Puerta 60%: ${Math.max(0, priceNum - escrowKc)} KC`, `40% Escrow: ${escrowKc} KC • 60% Door: ${Math.max(0, priceNum - escrowKc)} KC`)}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[s.halfInput, { gap: 4 }]}>
              <TextInput value={form.cupo_maximo} editable={editBookedSet.size === 0} onChangeText={(v) => { const clean = v.replace(/\D/g, ''); const n = Number(clean); setForm((p) => ({ ...p, cupo_maximo: clean, studio_layout: editBookedSet.size === 0 && clean && n >= editFloor && n <= MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS ? buildGridForTotalSpots(n, p.studio_layout.equipmentType) : p.studio_layout })); }} keyboardType="numeric" placeholder={tx(`Aforo (${editFloor}-50)`, `Capacity (${editFloor}-50)`)} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, { fontSize: 16, opacity: editBookedSet.size > 0 ? 0.65 : 1 }, inpBg]} />
              <Text style={{ color: pal.textMuted, fontSize: 11 }}>{editBookedSet.size > 0 ? tx(`Aforo controlado en Matriz 3D (mín. ${editFloor} ocupadas)`, `Capacity managed in 3D Grid (min ${editFloor} booked)`) : tx(`Piso reservas activas: ${editFloor} estaciones`, `Active bookings floor: ${editFloor} spots`)}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
          <View style={s.rowBetweenWrap}>
            <View style={s.wrapRow}>
              {PARTNER_EVENT_FREQS.map((f) => (<TouchableOpacity key={f} onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, frecuencia: f }))} style={[s.chipBtn, { borderColor: form.frecuencia === f ? pal.cyanText : pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: form.frecuencia === f ? pal.translucentCyanBg : pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: form.frecuencia === f ? pal.cyanText : pal.textMuted, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{f}</Text></TouchableOpacity>))}
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, estado: p.estado === 'pausado' ? 'aprobado' : 'pausado' }))} style={[s.chipBtn, { borderColor: form.estado === 'pausado' ? pal.amberText : pal.greenText, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: form.estado === 'pausado' ? pal.amberText : pal.greenText, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{form.estado === 'pausado' ? tx('Pausado', 'Paused') : tx('Publicado', 'Published')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
            <View style={s.wrapRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, es_venta_flash: !p.es_venta_flash }))} style={[s.chipBtn, { borderColor: form.es_venta_flash ? pal.amberText : pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: form.es_venta_flash ? pal.translucentAmberBg : pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: form.es_venta_flash ? pal.amberText : pal.textMuted, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>Flash {form.es_venta_flash ? `-${form.descuento_flash_pct}%` : 'Off'}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
              {form.es_venta_flash && PARTNER_EVENT_FLASH_PCTS.map((pct) => (<TouchableOpacity key={pct} onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, descuento_flash_pct: pct }))} style={[s.chipBtn, { borderColor: form.descuento_flash_pct === pct ? pal.amberText : pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '800' }}>-{pct}%</Text></TouchableOpacity>))}
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, split_escrow_enabled: !p.split_escrow_enabled }))} style={[s.chipBtn, { borderColor: form.split_escrow_enabled ? pal.purple : pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: form.split_escrow_enabled ? pal.translucentPurpleBg : pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: form.split_escrow_enabled ? pal.purpleText : pal.textMuted, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>Split {form.split_escrow_enabled ? `x${form.split_max_personas}` : 'Off'}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
              {form.split_escrow_enabled && PARTNER_EVENT_SPLIT_OPTS.map((sp) => (<TouchableOpacity key={sp} onPress={() => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, split_max_personas: sp }))} style={[s.chipBtn, { borderColor: form.split_max_personas === sp ? pal.purple : pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: '800' }}>x{sp}</Text></TouchableOpacity>))}
            </View>
          </View>
          <TextInput value={form.descripcion} onChangeText={(v) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, descripcion: v }))} multiline numberOfLines={3} placeholder={tx('Descripción de la experiencia...', 'Experience description...')} placeholderTextColor={pal.textMuted} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.input16, { fontSize: 16, minHeight: 72 }, inpBg]} />
          <View style={[s.rowBetweenWrap, { padding: 10, borderRadius: 12, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg }]}>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, flex: 1 }}>
              {Boolean(form.portada_url) && <Image source={{ uri: form.portada_url }} style={{ width: 64, height: 48, borderRadius: 8 }} />}
              <Text numberOfLines={1} style={{ color: pal.textSecondary, fontSize: 12, flex: 1 }}>{form.portada_url || tx('Sin portada personalizada (Storage avatars)', 'No custom cover uploaded')}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8 }}>
              <TouchableOpacity disabled={uploadingCover} onPress={handleUploadEventCover} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, { borderColor: pal.cyanText, backgroundColor: pal.cardBg }]}>{uploadingCover ? <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={pal.cyanText} /> : <Text style={{ color: pal.cyanText, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('Subir Foto Evento', 'Upload Event Photo')}</Text>}</TouchableOpacity>
              {Boolean(form.portada_url) && <TouchableOpacity onPress={clearCoverImage} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, { borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.cardBg }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.redText, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('Quitar', 'Clear')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>}
            </View>
          </View>
          <StudioGridMatrixEditor pal={pal} layout={form.studio_layout} discipline={form.categoria} minSpots={editFloor} occupiedSpots={editBookedSet} onChange={(next) => setForm((p) => ({ ...p, studio_layout: next, cupo_maximo: String(next.totalSpots) }))} />
          <TouchableOpacity disabled={saving} onPress={handleSaveEvent} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnPrimaryCyan, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, { alignSelf: 'flex-end' }]}>
            {saving ? <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#050505" /> : <Text style={{ color: '#050505', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '900' }}>{editingService ? tx('Guardar Cambios', 'Save Changes') : tx('Publicar Experiencia', 'Publish Experience')}</Text>}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      )}

      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 14 }}>
        {filteredServices.length === 0 ? (
          <View style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.card3D, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, alignItems: 'center', padding: 28 }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textMuted, fontSize: 14 }}>{tx('No hay experiencias en esta vista.', 'No experiences match this filter.')}</Text></View>
        ) : filteredServices.map((srv) => {
          const paused = String(srv.estado || '').toLowerCase() === 'pausado';
          const floor = computeServiceMinRequiredSpots(srv, activeReservations, completedReservations);
          const attrsObj = srv.atributos && typeof srv.atributos === 'object' ? (srv.atributos as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
          const coverUri = String(srv.imagen_url || srv.portada_url || attrsObj.imagen_url || attrsObj.portada_url || '').trim();
          return (
            <View key={srv.id} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.card3D, s.srvCard, { backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: selectedServiceId === srv.id ? pal.cyanText : pal.borderSubtle, borderTopColor: selectedServiceId === srv.id ? pal.cyanText : pal.borderTop3D, borderBottomColor: pal.borderBottom3D }]}>
              {Boolean(coverUri) && <Image source={{ uri: coverUri }} style={{ width: '100%', height: 112, borderRadius: 10 }} />}
              <View style={s.rowBetweenWrap}>
                <Text numberOfLines={1} style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800', flex: 1 }}>{srv.nombre_servicio || srv.titulo || tx('Clase Kinetic', 'Kinetic Class')}</Text>
                <Text style={{ color: pal.cyanText, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: '900' }}>{`${Number(srv.precio_kc) || 0} KC`}</Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={{ color: pal.textSecondary, fontSize: 13 }}>{srv.categoria || business?.disciplina || 'Fitness'} • {srv.fecha || tx('Recurrente', 'Recurring')} {srv.hora_inicio || ''}</Text>
              <Text style={{ color: pal.textMuted, fontSize: 12 }}>{tx('Aforo', 'Capacity')}: {srv.cupo_actual ?? 0}/{srv.cupo_maximo ?? 12} • {paused ? tx('PAUSADO', 'PAUSED') : tx('ACTIVO', 'ACTIVE')}{srv.es_venta_flash ? ` • FLASH -${srv.descuento_flash_pct ?? 15}%` : ''}</Text>
              <View style={s.wrapRow}>
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => handleInspectStudio(srv)} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, { borderColor: pal.cyanText, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, paddingVertical: 6 }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.cyanText, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('Sala 3D', '3D Map')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
                {canMutate.ok && (<>
                  <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => openEditor(srv)} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, { borderColor: pal.purple, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, paddingVertical: 6 }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.purpleText, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('Editar', 'Edit')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
                  <TouchableOpacity disabled={togglingId === srv.id} onPress={() => handleTogglePause(srv)} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, { borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, paddingVertical: 6 }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textSecondary, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{paused ? tx('Activar', 'Resume') : tx('Pausar', 'Pause')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
                  <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setConfirmDeleteId(srv.id)} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnGhost3D, { borderColor: pal.redText, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg, paddingVertical: 6 }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.redText, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('Eliminar', 'Delete')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
                </>)}
              </View>
            </View>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      <KineticDualConfirmModal
        visible={Boolean(confirmDeleteId)}
        title={tx('Eliminar Experiencia', 'Delete Experience')}
        message={tx(
          'Se aplicará reembolso 100% Escrow automático a las reservas activas de esta experiencia.',
          'All active bookings on this experience will receive an automatic 100% Escrow refund.'
        )}
        confirmLabel={tx('Eliminar Experiencia', 'Delete Experience')}
        cancelLabel={tx('Cancelar', 'Cancel')}
        tone="danger"
        loading={Boolean(confirmDeleteId && deletingId === confirmDeleteId)}
        onConfirm={() => {
          const target = filteredServices.find((s) => s.id === confirmDeleteId);
          setConfirmDeleteId(null);
          if (target) handleDelete(target);
        }}
        onCancel={() => setConfirmDeleteId(null)}
      />

      {(loadingStudio || studioRoster) && (
        <View style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.card3D, { backgroundColor: pal.cardBg, borderColor: pal.borderSubtle, borderTopColor: pal.purple, borderBottomColor: pal.borderBottom3D, gap: 12 }]}>
          <Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800' }}>{tx('Sala 3D en Vivo & Editor de Estaciones', 'Live 3D Studio & Station Editor')}</Text>
          {loadingStudio ? <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={pal.cyanText} /> : studioRoster ? (<>
            <Text style={{ color: pal.textSecondary, fontSize: 13 }}>{tx(`Estaciones: ${studioRoster.totalSpots} • Ocupadas: ${studioRoster.occupiedCount} • Check-ins: ${studioRoster.checkedInCount} • Libres: ${studioRoster.freeCount}`, `Stations: ${studioRoster.totalSpots} • Occupied: ${studioRoster.occupiedCount} • Checked-in: ${studioRoster.checkedInCount} • Free: ${studioRoster.freeCount}`)}</Text>
            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>{studioRoster.spots.map((sp) => (<View key={sp.spotNumber} style={[s.spotCell, { borderColor: sp.status === 'checked_in' ? pal.greenText : sp.status === 'reserved' ? pal.cyanText : pal.borderSubtle, backgroundColor: pal.recessedBg }]}><Text style={{ color: pal.textPrimary, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '900' }}>#{sp.spotNumber}</Text><Text numberOfLines={1} style={{ color: pal.textMuted, fontSize: 11 }}>{sp.athleteShortName || tx('Libre', 'Free')}</Text></View>))}</View>
            {canMutate.ok && editorLayout && (<View style={{ gap: 10 }}>
              <StudioGridMatrixEditor pal={pal} layout={editorLayout} discipline={selectedService?.categoria || 'Fuerza'} minSpots={selectedFloor} occupiedSpots={selectedBookedSet} onChange={setEditorLayout} />
              <TouchableOpacity disabled={savingLayout} onPress={handleSaveLiveLayout} style={[partnerWebSharedStyles.btnPrimaryPurple, PARTNER_NO_SELECT_STYLE, { alignSelf: 'flex-start' }]}>{savingLayout ? <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#FFFFFF" /> : <Text style={{ color: '#FFFFFF', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: '900' }}>{tx('Guardar Sala 3D', 'Save 3D Layout')}</Text>}</TouchableOpacity>
            </View>)}
          </>) : null}
        </View>
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

const s = StyleSheet.create({

export const KINETIC_20_ENVIRONMENT_LABELS_ES: string[] = KINETIC_20_ENVIRONMENTS.map((item) => item.es);

export const normalizeEnvironmentLabel = (raw?: string | null): string => {
  const clean = String(raw || '').trim();
  if (!clean) return KINETIC_20_ENVIRONMENTS[0].es;
  if (/^canche\s+exterior$/i.test(clean)) return 'Cancha Exterior';
  if (/^estudio\s+indoor$/i.test(clean)) return 'Estudio Interior (Climatizado)';
  if (/^outdoor\s*\/\s*playa$/i.test(clean)) return 'Playa / Arena';
  if (/^arena\s+techada$/i.test(clean)) return 'Arena Techada / Domo';
  const matched = KINETIC_20_ENVIRONMENTS.find(
    (item) => item.es.toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase() || item.en.toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase()
  );
  return matched ? matched.es : clean;
};

export const KINETIC_OPERATING_HOURS_30M: string[] = (() => {
  const slots: string[] = [];
  for (let h = 6; h <= 22; h++) {
    const hh = String(h).padStart(2, '0');
    slots.push(`${hh}:00`);
    if (h < 22) slots.push(`${hh}:30`);
  }
  return slots;
})();

export const KINETIC_AGE_POLICIES: IBilingualCatalogItem[] = [
  { key: 'age_18', es: '18+ Adultos', en: '18+ Adults' },
