export const normalizeAgePolicy = (
  raw?: string | null
): '18+ Adultos' | '13+ Teens (Con Tutor)' => {
  const clean = String(raw || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (
    clean.includes('13+') ||
    clean.includes('teens') ||
    clean.includes('tutor') ||
    clean.includes('guardian')
  ) {
    return '13+ Teens (Con Tutor)';
  }
  return '18+ Adultos';
};

export const toSafeLocalIsoDate = (raw?: unknown, fallback?: string): string => {
  const now = new Date();
  const fb =
    fallback ||
    `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const str = String(raw || '').trim();
  if (!str) return fb;
  const m = str.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
  if (m) return `${m[1]}-${m[2]}-${m[3]}`;
  const parsed = new Date(str);
  if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return fb;
  return `${parsed.getFullYear()}-${String(parsed.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(parsed.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
};

export const parseIsoToLocalDate = (iso?: string | null): Date => {
  const clean = toSafeLocalIsoDate(iso);
  const [y, m, d] = clean.split('-').map(Number);
  return new Date(y || 2026, (m || 1) - 1, d || 1, 12, 0, 0);
};

export const EVENT_CATALOGS_EN_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
  ...Object.fromEntries(KINETIC_20_ENVIRONMENTS.map((i) => [i.es, i.en])),
  ...Object.fromEntries(KINETIC_7_DURATIONS.map((d) => [d.label, d.labelEn])),
  ...Object.fromEntries(KINETIC_16_WHAT_TO_BRING.map((i) => [i.es, i.en])),
  ...Object.fromEntries(KINETIC_OPERATING_DAYS_OPTIONS.map((i) => [i.es, i.en])),
  ...Object.fromEntries(KINETIC_AGE_POLICIES.map((i) => [i.es, i.en])),
  ...LEGACY_REQ_TRANSLATIONS,
};

export const describeAgePolicyCard = (rawAge?: string | null, language?: string) => {
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const norm = normalizeAgePolicy(rawAge);
  const isTeens = norm === '13+ Teens (Con Tutor)';
  return {
    icon: 'shield' as const,
    cat: isEn ? 'AGE POLICY' : 'POLÍTICA DE EDAD',
    title: isTeens
      ? isEn ? '13+ Teens (With Guardian)' : '13+ Teens (Con Tutor)'
      : isEn ? '18+ Adults' : '18+ Adultos',
    desc: isTeens
      ? isEn
        ? 'Teens 13+ allowed when accompanied by a parent or guardian.'
        : 'Adolescentes 13+ permitidos acompañados por madre, padre o tutor.'
      : isEn
        ? 'Exclusive for adults 18+ with valid official ID.'
        : 'Exclusivo para mayores de 18 años con identificación oficial.',
  };
};

export const describeWhatToBringCard = (rawItem: string, language?: string) => {
  const isEn = language === 'en';
  const clean = String(rawItem || '').trim();
  const matched = KINETIC_16_WHAT_TO_BRING.find(
    (i) => i.es.toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase() || i.en.toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase()
  );
  const title = matched
    ? isEn ? matched.en : matched.es
    : isEn ? EVENT_CATALOGS_EN_MAP[clean] || clean : clean;
  const low = `${matched?.es || clean}`.toLowerCase();

  if (low.includes('agua') || low.includes('hidrat')) {
    return {
      icon: 'droplet',
      cat: isEn ? 'WHAT TO BRING' : 'QUÉ TRAER',
      title,
      desc: isEn ? 'Stay properly hydrated throughout the entire session.' : 'Mantén hidratación óptima durante toda la sesión.',
    };
  }
  if (low.includes('toalla')) {
    return {
      icon: 'wind',
      cat: isEn ? 'WHAT TO BRING' : 'QUÉ TRAER',
      title,
      desc: isEn ? 'Required for studio hygiene on stations and gear.' : 'Requerida por higiene en estaciones y equipamiento.',
    };
  }
  if (low.includes('tenis') || low.includes('calcetines') || low.includes('sandalias')) {
    return {
      icon: 'award',
      cat: isEn ? 'WHAT TO BRING' : 'QUÉ TRAER',
      title,
      desc: isEn ? 'Safe traction and care of the training surface.' : 'Tracción segura y cuidado de la superficie deportiva.',
    };
  }
  if (low.includes('mat')) {
    return {
      icon: 'layers',
      cat: isEn ? 'WHAT TO BRING' : 'QUÉ TRAER',
      title,
      desc: isEn ? 'Personal floor support for posture and mobility.' : 'Soporte personal de piso para postura y movilidad.',
    };
  }
  if (low.includes('pala') || low.includes('raqueta') || low.includes('guantes') || low.includes('calleras')) {
    return {
      icon: 'target',
      cat: isEn ? 'WHAT TO BRING' : 'QUÉ TRAER',
      title,
      desc: isEn ? 'Personal technical gear for safe grip and impact.' : 'Equipo técnico personal para agarre e impacto seguro.',
    };
  }
  if (low.includes('traje') || low.includes('lentes') || low.includes('bloqueador') || low.includes('gorra')) {
    return {
      icon: 'sun',
      cat: isEn ? 'WHAT TO BRING' : 'QUÉ TRAER',
      title,
      desc: isEn ? 'Aquatic or UV gear for pool and outdoor sessions.' : 'Protección acuática o solar para sesiones al aire libre.',
    };
  }
  return {
    icon: 'check-circle',
    cat: isEn ? 'WHAT TO BRING' : 'QUÉ TRAER',
    title,
    desc: isEn ? 'Breathable athletic attire for full range of motion.' : 'Prenda deportiva cómoda para total libertad de movimiento.',
  };
};

    FILE: src/hooks/eventos/useEventDetailsMediaAndLayout.ts

    // === src/hooks/eventos/useEventDetailsMediaAndLayout.ts === //
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { Keyboard } from 'react-native';
import { MAX_EXTRA_EVENT_PHOTOS } from '../../components/eventos/EventEditorB2BView';
import {
  IStudioLayoutConfig,
  MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS,
  saveStudioLayoutLocal,
} from '../../components/eventos/KineticStudioLayoutModal';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { syncStudioLayoutToCloud } from '../../services/terminalSpeiAndStudioService';
import { markTransientNativeOverlayActive } from '../../utils/kineticBiometricGateMachineLeaf';
import {
  pickEventCoverWithCdnAction,
  pickEventGalleryWithCdnAction,
} from '../../utils/eventModalActions';

export function useEventDetailsMediaAndLayout(params: {
  currentEv?: { id?: string | number | null; [k: string]: unknown } | null;
  isCreateMode?: boolean;
  clubName: string;
  titleVal: string;
  standardDisciplineImage: string;
  galleryUris: string[];
  eventPersistKey: string;
  studioDirtyRef: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>;
  setCustomCoverUri: (u: string) => void;
  setActivePhotoIdx: (i: number) => void;
  setGalleryUris: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string[]>>;
  setStudioLayout: (lay: IStudioLayoutConfig) => void;
  setCupoVal: (val: string) => void;
}) {
  const {
    currentEv, isCreateMode = false, clubName, titleVal, standardDisciplineImage, galleryUris,
    eventPersistKey, studioDirtyRef, setCustomCoverUri, setActivePhotoIdx,
    setGalleryUris, setStudioLayout, setCupoVal,
  } = params;

  const pickCoverImage = useCallback(async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    markTransientNativeOverlayActive('image_picker', true);
    try {
      triggerHaptic('Light');
      await pickEventCoverWithCdnAction(
        (currentEv?.id != null ? String(currentEv.id) : '') || clubName || titleVal,
        setCustomCoverUri,
        setActivePhotoIdx,
        () => triggerHaptic('Success')
      );
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; } finally {
      markTransientNativeOverlayActive('image_picker', false);
    }
  }, [currentEv?.id, clubName, titleVal, setCustomCoverUri, setActivePhotoIdx]);

  const removeCustomCover = useCallback(() => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    setCustomCoverUri('');
    setActivePhotoIdx(0);
  }, [setCustomCoverUri, setActivePhotoIdx]);

  const pickGalleryImages = useCallback(async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    markTransientNativeOverlayActive('image_picker', true);
    try {
      triggerHaptic('Light');
      await pickEventGalleryWithCdnAction(
        Math.max(1, MAX_EXTRA_EVENT_PHOTOS - galleryUris.length),
        (currentEv?.id != null ? String(currentEv.id) : '') || clubName || titleVal,
        standardDisciplineImage,
        MAX_EXTRA_EVENT_PHOTOS,
        setGalleryUris,
        () => triggerHaptic('Success')
      );
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; } finally {
      markTransientNativeOverlayActive('image_picker', false);
    }
  }, [
    galleryUris.length, currentEv?.id, clubName, titleVal,
    standardDisciplineImage, setGalleryUris,
  ]);

  const removeGalleryPhotoAt = useCallback(
    (idxToRemove: number) => {
      triggerHaptic('Selection');
      setGalleryUris((prev) => prev.filter((_, idx) => idx !== idxToRemove));
      setActivePhotoIdx(0);
    },
    [setGalleryUris, setActivePhotoIdx]
  );

  const handleSaveStudioLayout = useCallback(
    (layout: IStudioLayoutConfig) => {
      const cappedSpots = Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, Math.max(1, layout.totalSpots));
      const safeLay = { ...layout, totalSpots: cappedSpots };
      studioDirtyRef.current = true;
      setStudioLayout(safeLay);
      setCupoVal(String(cappedSpots));
      if (eventPersistKey) saveStudioLayoutLocal(eventPersistKey, safeLay);
      if (!isCreateMode && currentEv?.id) {
        currentEv.cupo_maximo = cappedSpots;
        currentEv.studio_layout_json = safeLay;
        currentEv.atributos = { ...(currentEv.atributos || {}), studio_layout: safeLay };
        void syncStudioLayoutToCloud(String(currentEv.id), safeLay);
      }
    },
    [eventPersistKey, isCreateMode, currentEv, studioDirtyRef, setStudioLayout, setCupoVal]
  );

  return {
    pickCoverImage,
    removeCustomCover,
    pickGalleryImages,
    removeGalleryPhotoAt,
    handleSaveStudioLayout,
  };
}

    FILE: src/hooks/eventos/useEventDetailsSplitAndSpot.ts

    // === src/hooks/eventos/useEventDetailsSplitAndSpot.ts === //
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { resolveCleanVipLevel } from '../../components/boveda/TopUpPackagesSection';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import {
  clearPendingSplitConfig,
  computeSplitEscrow1NMath,
  getPendingSplitConfig,
  IPendingSplitEscrowConfig,
  persistSelectedSpotSession,
  rehydratePersistedSpotForEvent,
  setPendingSplitConfig,
} from '../../services/kineticSplitPreBookingStore';
import { ISplitInviteAthlete } from '../../services/splitEscrowInvitesService';
import { useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel } from '../boveda/useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel';

export interface IUseEventDetailsSplitAndSpotParams {
  userId?: string | null;
  userObj?: any;
  userLevel?: string | null;
  userKcMembresia?: number | null;
  localBookingDateIso?: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  isCreateMode: boolean;
  eventPersistKey: string;
  evIdStr: string;
  titleVal: string;
  totalKc: number;
  escrow40: number;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  splitEscrowEnabled: boolean;
  availableKc: number;
  refreshProfile?: () => Promise<void> | void;
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
  triggerAlert: (title: string, msg: string, type?: string) => void;
}

export function useEventDetailsSplitAndSpot(params: IUseEventDetailsSplitAndSpotParams) {
  const {
    userId, userObj, userLevel, userKcMembresia, localBookingDateIso, isOpen, isCreateMode, eventPersistKey, evIdStr, titleVal,
    totalKc, escrow40, isAdmin, splitEscrowEnabled, availableKc,
    refreshProfile, tx, triggerAlert,
  } = params;
  const { language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const datedEventKey = localBookingDateIso && (eventPersistKey || titleVal)
    ? `${eventPersistKey || titleVal}::${localBookingDateIso}`
    : '';
  const datedEvIdKey = localBookingDateIso && evIdStr ? `${evIdStr}::${localBookingDateIso}` : '';

  const [selectedSpot, setSelectedSpot] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [pendingSplitConfig, setPendingSplitState] = useState<IPendingSplitEscrowConfig | null>(null);
  const [insufficientKcPrompt, setInsufficientKcPrompt] = useState<{
    missingKc: number; availableKc: number; requiredEscrow: number;
  } | null>(null);
  const [isMembershipModalOpen, setIsMembershipModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isProcessingMembership, setIsProcessingMembership] = useState(false);
  const [processingPlanId, setProcessingPlanId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const modalTimersRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    modalTimersRef.current.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));
    modalTimersRef.current = [];
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) {
      setInsufficientKcPrompt(null);
      return;
    }
    if (isCreateMode) {
      setSelectedSpot(null);
      setPendingSplitState(null);
      return;
    }
    const lookupKeys = localBookingDateIso
      ? [datedEventKey, datedEvIdKey, titleVal ? `${titleVal}::${localBookingDateIso}` : ''].filter(Boolean)
      : [eventPersistKey, evIdStr, titleVal].filter(Boolean);
    setSelectedSpot(rehydratePersistedSpotForEvent(...lookupKeys));
    setPendingSplitState(getPendingSplitConfig(eventPersistKey, evIdStr, titleVal));
  }, [isOpen, isCreateMode, datedEventKey, datedEvIdKey, eventPersistKey, evIdStr, titleVal, localBookingDateIso]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || isCreateMode || selectedSpot == null) return;
    persistSelectedSpotSession(eventPersistKey || titleVal, selectedSpot, evIdStr, localBookingDateIso);
  }, [isOpen, isCreateMode, selectedSpot, availableKc, isMembershipModalOpen, eventPersistKey, evIdStr, titleVal, localBookingDateIso]);

  const persistAndSelectSpot = useCallback(
    (spot: number | null) => {
      setSelectedSpot(spot);
      persistSelectedSpotSession(eventPersistKey || titleVal, spot, evIdStr, localBookingDateIso);
    },
    [eventPersistKey, evIdStr, titleVal, localBookingDateIso]
  );

  const applyPreBookingSplit = useCallback(
    (cfg: { splitCount: number; selectedGuests: ISplitInviteAthlete[] }) => {
      const saved = setPendingSplitConfig(eventPersistKey || titleVal, cfg, evIdStr);
      setPendingSplitState(saved);
    },
    [eventPersistKey, evIdStr, titleVal]
  );

  const resetPreBookingSplit = useCallback(() => {
    clearPendingSplitConfig(eventPersistKey, evIdStr, titleVal);
    setPendingSplitState(null);
  }, [eventPersistKey, evIdStr, titleVal]);

  const { handlePurchase: handleMembershipPurchase } = useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel({
    user: userObj || (userId ? { id: userId } : null),
    isEn: language === 'en',
    tx,
    cleanVipLevel: resolveCleanVipLevel(userLevel, userKcMembresia),
    refreshProfile: async () => {
      await refreshProfile?.();
      if (selectedSpot != null) {
        persistSelectedSpotSession(eventPersistKey || titleVal, selectedSpot, evIdStr, localBookingDateIso);
      }
      setInsufficientKcPrompt(null);
    },
    fetchData: async () => {},
    getCostoTotal: () => 0,
    isProcessingPayment: isProcessingMembership,
    setIsProcessingPayment: setIsProcessingMembership,
    setProcessingPlanId,
    isCanceling: false,
    setIsCanceling: () => {},
    setIsMembershipModalOpen,
    setSelectedQR: () => {},
    scheduleBovedaTimeout: (fn, ms) => {
      const t = setTimeout(fn, ms);
      modalTimersRef.current.push(t);
    },
    showTacticalAlert: triggerAlert,
  });

  const splitMath = useMemo(
    () =>
      computeSplitEscrow1NMath(
        totalKc,
        splitEscrowEnabled && pendingSplitConfig ? pendingSplitConfig.splitCount : 1,
        escrow40,
        isAdmin
      ),
    [totalKc, splitEscrowEnabled, pendingSplitConfig, escrow40, isAdmin]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (insufficientKcPrompt && availableKc >= splitMath.effectiveEscrowKc) {
      setInsufficientKcPrompt(null);
    }
  }, [insufficientKcPrompt, availableKc, splitMath.effectiveEscrowKc]);

  return {
    selectedSpot, setSelectedSpot, persistAndSelectSpot,
    pendingSplitConfig, applyPreBookingSplit, resetPreBookingSplit,
    effectiveSplitCount: splitMath.safeSplit,
    effectiveEscrowKc: splitMath.effectiveEscrowKc,
    effectiveDoorKc: splitMath.effectiveDoorKc,
    perPersonTotalKc: splitMath.perPersonTotalKc,
    insufficientKcPrompt, setInsufficientKcPrompt,
    isMembershipModalOpen, setIsMembershipModalOpen,
    isProcessingMembership, processingPlanId, handleMembershipPurchase,
  };
}

    FILE: src/hooks/eventos/useEventosBookingAndDeepLink.ts

    // === src/hooks/eventos/useEventosBookingAndDeepLink.ts === //
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../../components/SharedUI';
import { supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
import { calcDistanceKm, isValidUuid, normTxt } from '../../services/eventosHelpers';
import {
  buildEventosClubLookup,
  getLocalTodayIso,
  mapServiceRowToEventosItem,
  matchDeepLinkEventInCatalog,
} from '../../services/eventosScreenHelpers';
import { eventsService } from '../../services/eventsService';
import { resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing } from '../../services/kineticFlashSaleHelper';
import { isStrictB2BPartnerAccount } from '../../services/kineticlubHelpers';
import { verifyAndPurgeConfirmedGhostService } from '../../services/kineticGhostEventsPurgeHelper';
import { clearPendingSplitConfig, computeSplitEscrow1NMath, persistSelectedSpotSession } from '../../services/kineticSplitPreBookingStore';
import { createSplitEscrowInvites } from '../../services/splitEscrowInvitesService';
import { SERVICIOS_RADAR_COLS } from '../../services/exploreService';
import { notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed } from '../../utils/eventModalActions';
import { buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel } from '../../utils/kineticDigitalPassViewModelLeaf';

export interface IReserveOutcome {
  ok: boolean;
  insufficientKc?: boolean;
  missingKc?: number;
  availableKc?: number;
  requiredEscrow?: number;
}

interface IUseEventosBookingAndDeepLinkParams {
  routeEvento?: string | string[];
  routeEvent?: string | string[];
  catalog: any[];
  hasLoadedInitial: boolean;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  user: any;
  profile: any;
  refreshProfile?: () => Promise<any>;
  viewMode: 'agenda' | 'lista';
  searchQuery: string;
  selectedIsoDate: string;
  language: string;
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
  isMountedRef: React.MutableRefObject<boolean>;
  setSelectedEvent: (ev: any | null, explicitDateIso?: string) => void;
  setAlertConfig: (cfg: any) => void;
  setIsMembershipOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
  applySeatDelta: (evId: string, delta: number) => void;
  onRefreshCatalogAfterReserve: () => void;
}

export const useEventosBookingAndDeepLink = ({
  routeEvento,
  routeEvent,
  catalog,
  hasLoadedInitial,
  isAdmin,
  user,
  profile,
  refreshProfile,
  viewMode,
  searchQuery,
  selectedIsoDate,
  language,
  tx,
  isMountedRef,
  setSelectedEvent,
  setAlertConfig,
  setIsMembershipOpen,
  applySeatDelta,
  onRefreshCatalogAfterReserve,
}: IUseEventosBookingAndDeepLinkParams) => {
  const handledDeepLinkRef = useRef<string>('');
  const isReservingRef = useRef(false);
  const alertTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const membershipOpenTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (alertTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(alertTimerRef.current);
      if (membershipOpenTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(membershipOpenTimerRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const rawParam = Array.isArray(routeEvento)
      ? routeEvento[0]
      : routeEvento || (Array.isArray(routeEvent) ? routeEvent[0] : routeEvent) || '';
    const trimmedParam = String(rawParam || '').trim();
    if (!trimmedParam) return;
    let target = trimmedParam;
    try {
      target = decodeURIComponent(trimmedParam).trim();
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    if (!target || handledDeepLinkRef.current === target) return;

    const found = matchDeepLinkEventInCatalog(catalog, target, normTxt);
    if (found) {
      handledDeepLinkRef.current = target;
      setSelectedEvent(found, found.fecha_proyectada || found.fecha);
      return;
    }

    if (!hasLoadedInitial) return;
    handledDeepLinkRef.current = target;
    if (isValidUuid(target)) {
      Promise.resolve(
        supabase.from('servicios').select(SERVICIOS_RADAR_COLS).eq('id', target).maybeSingle()
      )
        .then(({ data }) => {
          const mapped = data
            ? mapServiceRowToEventosItem(data, 0, buildEventosClubLookup([]), calcDistanceKm)
            : null;
          if (mapped && (isAdmin || !mapped.isInactive)) {
            setSelectedEvent(mapped, mapped.fecha_proyectada || mapped.fecha);
          }
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    }
  }, [routeEvento, routeEvent, catalog, hasLoadedInitial, isAdmin, setSelectedEvent]);

  const handleReserve = useCallback(
    async (ev: any): Promise<boolean> => {
      if (!ev || isReservingRef.current) return false;
      const queueAlert = (cfg: any, ms = 120) => {
        if (alertTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(alertTimerRef.current);
        alertTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
          if (isMountedRef.current) setAlertConfig({ visible: true, ...cfg });
        }, ms);
      };

      if (!user?.id) {
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        queueAlert({
          title: tx('ACCESO REQUERIDO', 'SIGN IN REQUIRED'),
          type: 'warning',
          message: tx(
            'Inicia sesión o crea tu cuenta en Kinetic para apartar tu lugar con Anticipo Protegido.',
            'Sign in or create your Kinetic account to lock your spot with Protected Deposit.'
          ),
        });
        return false;
      }

      if (isStrictB2BPartnerAccount(user, profile)) {
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        queueAlert({
          title: tx('SOLO CUENTAS DE ATLETA', 'ATHLETE ACCOUNTS ONLY'),
          type: 'error',
          message: tx(
            'Las cuentas operativas B2B no pueden realizar reservas de atleta.',
            'B2B operator accounts cannot create athlete bookings.'
          ),
        });
        return false;
      }

      const splitCount = Math.min(4, Math.max(1, Number(ev?.split_count || ev?.splitCount) || 1));
      const useMasterPass = Boolean(isAdmin && (ev?.isMasterPass || Number(ev?.precio_kc) === 0));
      const fp = resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing({
        event: ev,
        vipLevel: profile?.nivel,
        kcMembresia: profile?.kc_membresia,
        isAdmin: useMasterPass,
      });
      const fullTotal = useMasterPass ? 0 : fp.discountedTotalKc;
      const escMath = computeSplitEscrow1NMath(fullTotal, splitCount, fp.escrow40, useMasterPass);
      const total = fullTotal;
      const esc = escMath.effectiveEscrowKc;
      const availableKc = Math.max(
        0,
        (Number(profile?.kc_membresia) || 0) + (Number(profile?.kc_topup) || 0)
      );

      // [V14-07] NO cerrar ni perder selectedEvent si faltan KC para el 40% Escrow
      if (!useMasterPass && esc > 0 && availableKc < esc) {
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        if (!ev._fromDetailsModal) {
          const missingKc = Math.max(1, esc - availableKc);
          queueAlert({
            title: tx('SALDO INSUFICIENTE', 'INSUFFICIENT BALANCE'),
            type: 'confirm',
            confirmText: tx('⚡ RECARGAR KINETICOINS / SUBIR NIVEL', '⚡ TOP UP KINETICOINS / UPGRADE'),
            message: tx(
              `Te faltan ${missingKc} KC • Disponible: ${availableKc} KC (Requiere ${esc} KC de Anticipo Protegido 40%).`,
              `You need ${missingKc} more KC • Available: ${availableKc} KC (Requires ${esc} KC 40% Protected Deposit).`
            ),
            onConfirm: () => {
              if (membershipOpenTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(membershipOpenTimerRef.current);
              membershipOpenTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setIsMembershipOpen(true), 120);
            },
          });
        }
        return false;
      }

      const rawDateStr = String(
        ev.fecha_clase ||
          ev.fecha_seleccionada ||
          ev.fecha_proyectada ||
          (viewMode === 'agenda' && !searchQuery.trim() ? selectedIsoDate : '') ||
          ev.fecha ||
          getLocalTodayIso()
      )
        .split('T')[0]
        .trim();
      const localBookingDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(rawDateStr)
        ? rawDateStr
        : getLocalTodayIso();
      const pickedSpot = ev.spot_number ?? ev.spot ?? ev.estacion ?? ev.selectedSpot ?? null;
      const evId = String(ev.id || '');

      isReservingRef.current = true;
      if (evId) applySeatDelta(evId, 1);
      try {
        const resOut = await eventsService.procesarReserva(
          user.id,
          String(ev.negocio_nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial'),
          String(ev.nombre_servicio || ev.titulo || 'Sesión Kinetic'),
          total,
          evId,
          {
            fechaClase: localBookingDate,
            selectedDate: localBookingDate,
            horaInicio: ev.hora_inicio || null,
            spot: pickedSpot,
            spotNumber: pickedSpot,
            splitCount,
            isMasterPass: useMasterPass,
            cupoMaximo: ev.cupo_maximo,
            lang: language,
          },
          pickedSpot
        );
        const createdReservaId = String((resOut as any)?.reserva_id || (resOut as any)?.id || '').trim();
        if (splitCount > 1 && Array.isArray(ev?.splitGuests) && ev.splitGuests.length > 0) {
          await createSplitEscrowInvites({
            reservaId: createdReservaId || undefined,
            eventId: evId,
            title: String(ev.nombre_servicio || ev.titulo || 'Sesión Kinetic'),
            anfitrionId: user.id,
            invitados: ev.splitGuests,
            montoKcParte: esc,
          }).catch(() => {});
        }
        clearPendingSplitConfig(evId, ev.nombre_servicio, ev.titulo);
        persistSelectedSpotSession(evId, null, ev.nombre_servicio || ev.titulo);
        if (!isMountedRef.current) return true;
        if (!ev._fromDetailsModal) {
          setSelectedEvent(null);
        }
        notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed();
        if (total > 0) Promise.resolve(refreshProfile?.()).catch(() => {});
        onRefreshCatalogAfterReserve();

        if (!ev._fromDetailsModal) {
          const passVm = buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel(
            {
              negocio_nombre: ev.negocio_nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial',
              servicio: ev.nombre_servicio || ev.titulo || 'Sesión Kinetic',
              categoria: ev.categoria || 'Fuerza',
              fecha_clase: localBookingDate,
              hora_inicio: ev.hora_inicio || '08:00',
              duracion_minutos: ev.duracion_minutos || 60,
              ubicacion: ev.ubicacion || '',
              instructor_nombre: ev.instructor_nombre || '',
              spot_number: pickedSpot,
              es_pase_maestro: useMasterPass,
              precio_kc: total,
              monto_escrow: esc,
              monto_puerta: escMath.effectiveDoorKc,
            },
            { isEn: language === 'en', isAdminOverride: useMasterPass }
          );
          const stationLine = passVm.stationBadge ? `\n🎯 ${passVm.stationBadge}` : '';
          const masterLine = passVm.masterPassBadge ? `\n🛡️ ${passVm.masterPassBadge}` : '';
          queueAlert({
            title: passVm.headerTitle,
            message:
              `${passVm.sessionTitle} • ${passVm.disciplineLabel}\n` +
              `📍 ${passVm.venueName} (${passVm.zoneAndCoachLabel})\n` +
              `🗓️ ${passVm.fechaDisplayLabel} (${passVm.fechaClaseIso}) • ${passVm.scheduleLabel}` +
              `${stationLine}${masterLine}\n` +
              `• ${passVm.depositLabel}: ${passVm.depositValueLabel}\n` +
              `• ${passVm.settlementLabel}: ${passVm.settlementValueLabel}`,
            type: 'success',
          });
        }
        return true;
      } catch (err: any) {
        if (evId) applySeatDelta(evId, -1);
        if (!isMountedRef.current) return false;
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        const msg = String(err?.message || '');
        const confirmedRetired = evId ? await verifyAndPurgeConfirmedGhostService(supabase, evId) : false;
        if (confirmedRetired) {
          onRefreshCatalogAfterReserve();
          const ghostMsg = tx(
            'Esta experiencia ya no está disponible y fue retirada de la cartelera.',
            'This experience is no longer available and was removed from the schedule.'
          );
          if (ev._onGhostPurged) {
            ev._onGhostPurged(ghostMsg);
          } else {
            setSelectedEvent(null);
            queueAlert({
              title: tx('EXPERIENCIA RETIRADA', 'EXPERIENCE RETIRED'),
              message: ghostMsg,
              type: 'warning',
            });
          }
          return false;
        }
        if (/SALDO_INSUFICIENTE|FONDOS_INSUFICIENTES/i.test(msg)) {
          if (ev._onInsufficientKc) {
            ev._onInsufficientKc(Math.max(1, esc - availableKc), availableKc, esc);
          } else if (!ev._fromDetailsModal) {
            queueAlert({
              title: tx('SALDO INSUFICIENTE', 'INSUFFICIENT BALANCE'),
              type: 'confirm',
              confirmText: tx('⚡ RECARGAR KINETICOINS / SUBIR NIVEL', '⚡ TOP UP KINETICOINS / UPGRADE'),
              message: msg,
              onConfirm: () => {
                if (membershipOpenTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(membershipOpenTimerRef.current);
                membershipOpenTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => setIsMembershipOpen(true), 120);
              },
            });
          }
          return false;
        }
        if (ev._onBookingError) {
          ev._onBookingError(msg || tx('Intenta nuevamente.', 'Please try again.'));
        } else if (!ev._fromDetailsModal) {
          queueAlert({
            title: tx('AVISO DE RESERVA', 'BOOKING NOTICE'),
            message: msg || tx('Intenta nuevamente.', 'Please try again.'),
            type: 'error',
          });
        }
        return false;
      } finally {
        isReservingRef.current = false;
      }
    },
    [
      isMountedRef,
      user?.id,
      isAdmin,
      profile?.kc_membresia,
      profile?.kc_topup,
      viewMode,
      searchQuery,
      selectedIsoDate,
      language,
      tx,
      applySeatDelta,
      setSelectedEvent,
      setAlertConfig,
      setIsMembershipOpen,
      refreshProfile,
      onRefreshCatalogAfterReserve,
    ]
  );

  return { handleReserve };
};

    FILE: src/hooks/partner-web/usePartnerWebEventsCrud.ts

    // === src/hooks/partner-web/usePartnerWebEventsCrud.ts === //
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { loadPartnerServiceStudioRoster } from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebDataService';
import { canPartnerMutateCatalog } from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebGuards';
import {
  buildPartnerEventFormState,
  computeServiceMinRequiredSpots,
  deletePartnerWebEventService,
  framePartnerWebPickedImage,
  pickWebImageFileAsBytes,
  removePartnerOwnedAvatarObjectsBatchSafe,
  savePartnerWebEventService,
  savePartnerWebStudioLayoutOnly,
  togglePartnerWebEventState,
  uploadPartnerImageToAvatarsBucket,
  type IPartnerEventFormState,
} from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebMediaAndCatalogService';
import { preloadImageInMemory } from '../../utils/kineticImageFramePureLeaf';
import type { IPartnerWebAffiliateRow, IPartnerWebReservationRow, IPartnerWebServiceRow } from '../../services/partner-web/partnerWebTypes';
import { MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, resolveDefaultStudioLayout, type IStudioLayoutConfig } from '../../services/spotCollisionService';
import type { ILiveStudioRosterResult } from '../../services/terminalSpeiAndStudioService';

export interface IUsePartnerWebEventsCrudParams {
  actorUserId: string;
  isAdmin: boolean;
  isB2B: boolean;
  business: IPartnerWebAffiliateRow | null;
  services: IPartnerWebServiceRow[];
  activeReservations: IPartnerWebReservationRow[];
  completedReservations: IPartnerWebReservationRow[];
  onRefreshWorkspace: () => Promise<void>;
}

interface IEditorModalState {
  open: boolean;
  editingService: IPartnerWebServiceRow | null;
  form: IPartnerEventFormState;
}

interface ICrudBusyState {
  saving: boolean;
  uploadingCover: boolean;
  confirmDeleteId: string | null;
  deletingId: string | null;
  togglingId: string | null;
  savingLayout: boolean;
}

interface IStudioPanelState {
  selectedServiceId: string | null;
  roster: ILiveStudioRosterResult | null;
  loading: boolean;
  editorLayout: IStudioLayoutConfig | null;
}

const INITIAL_BUSY: ICrudBusyState = { saving: false, uploadingCover: false, confirmDeleteId: null, deletingId: null, togglingId: null, savingLayout: false };
const INITIAL_STUDIO: IStudioPanelState = { selectedServiceId: null, roster: null, loading: false, editorLayout: null };

export function usePartnerWebEventsCrud(params: IUsePartnerWebEventsCrudParams) {
  const { actorUserId, isAdmin, isB2B, business, services, activeReservations, completedReservations, onRefreshWorkspace } = params;
  const { tx } = useKineticPreferences();
  const [filterKey, setFilterKey] = useState<'all' | 'active' | 'flash' | 'paused'>('all');
  const [editorModal, setEditorModal] = useState<IEditorModalState>(() => ({ open: false, editingService: null, form: buildPartnerEventFormState(null, business) }));
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<ICrudBusyState>(INITIAL_BUSY);
  const [studioPanel, setStudioPanel] = useState<IStudioPanelState>(INITIAL_STUDIO);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<{ type: 'ok' | 'err'; text: string } | null>(null);

  const mountedRef = useRef(true), busyRef = useRef(false), studioReqIdRef = useRef(0), formSessionRef = useRef(0);
  const replacedCoverUrlsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const bizIdRef = useRef<string | null>(business?.id || null);
  bizIdRef.current = business?.id || null;

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => { mountedRef.current = false; studioReqIdRef.current += 1; formSessionRef.current += 1; };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    studioReqIdRef.current += 1; formSessionRef.current += 1; replacedCoverUrlsRef.current.clear();
    setEditorModal({ open: false, editingService: null, form: buildPartnerEventFormState(null, business) });
    setBusy(INITIAL_BUSY); setStudioPanel(INITIAL_STUDIO); setFeedback(null);
  }, [business?.id]);

  const canMutate = useMemo(() => canPartnerMutateCatalog({ actorUserId, isAdmin, isB2B, business }), [actorUserId, isAdmin, isB2B, business]);
  const editFloorInfo = useMemo(() => (editorModal.editingService ? computeServiceMinRequiredSpots(editorModal.editingService, activeReservations, completedReservations) : { activeBookedCount: 0, highestBookedSpot: 0, minRequiredSpots: 1, occupiedSpots: [] }), [editorModal.editingService, activeReservations, completedReservations]);
  const editFloor = editFloorInfo.minRequiredSpots;
  const editBookedSet = useMemo(() => new Set(editFloorInfo.occupiedSpots), [editFloorInfo.occupiedSpots]);

  const selectedService = useMemo(() => services.find((srv) => srv.id === studioPanel.selectedServiceId) || null, [services, studioPanel.selectedServiceId]);
  const selectedFloorInfo = useMemo(() => (selectedService ? computeServiceMinRequiredSpots(selectedService, activeReservations, completedReservations, (studioPanel.roster?.spots || []).filter((sp) => sp.status === 'reserved' || sp.status === 'checked_in').map((sp) => sp.spotNumber)) : { activeBookedCount: 0, highestBookedSpot: 0, minRequiredSpots: 1, occupiedSpots: [] }), [selectedService, activeReservations, completedReservations, studioPanel.roster]);
  const selectedFloor = selectedFloorInfo.minRequiredSpots;
  const selectedBookedSet = useMemo(() => new Set(selectedFloorInfo.occupiedSpots), [selectedFloorInfo.occupiedSpots]);

  const filteredServices = useMemo(() => (services || []).filter((srv) => {
    const st = String(srv.estado || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return filterKey === 'paused' ? st === 'pausado' : filterKey === 'flash' ? Boolean(srv.es_venta_flash) : filterKey === 'active' ? st !== 'pausado' : true;
  }), [services, filterKey]);

  const showErr = (err: unknown, fallback: string) => {
    if (mountedRef.current) setFeedback({ type: 'err', text: err instanceof Error ? err.message : fallback });
  };

  const setForm = (updater: (prev: IPartnerEventFormState) => IPartnerEventFormState) => {
    setEditorModal((prev) => ({ ...prev, form: updater(prev.form) }));
  };

  const openEditor = (srv: IPartnerWebServiceRow | null) => {
    if (!canMutate.ok || busyRef.current) return;
    formSessionRef.current += 1; replacedCoverUrlsRef.current.clear(); setFeedback(null);
    setEditorModal({ open: true, editingService: srv, form: buildPartnerEventFormState(srv, business) });
  };

  const closeEditor = () => {
    formSessionRef.current += 1; replacedCoverUrlsRef.current.clear();
    setEditorModal((prev) => ({ ...prev, open: false }));
  };

  const clearCoverImage = () => {
    if (editorModal.form.portada_url) replacedCoverUrlsRef.current.add(editorModal.form.portada_url);
    setForm((p) => ({ ...p, portada_url: '' }));
  };

  const handleUploadEventCover = async () => {
    if (busyRef.current || busy.uploadingCover || busy.saving || !canMutate.ok) return;
    busyRef.current = true; setFeedback(null); setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, uploadingCover: true }));
    const sessionId = formSessionRef.current;
    try {
      const picked = await pickWebImageFileAsBytes();
      if (!picked) return;
      const framed = await framePartnerWebPickedImage(picked, 'event');
      if (!framed) return;
      const publicUrl = await uploadPartnerImageToAvatarsBucket({ ...framed, slot: 'event', isAdmin });
      await preloadImageInMemory(publicUrl);
      if (!mountedRef.current || formSessionRef.current !== sessionId) {
        void removePartnerOwnedAvatarObjectsBatchSafe([publicUrl], actorUserId, isAdmin);
        return;
      }
      setForm((prev) => {
        if (prev.portada_url && prev.portada_url !== publicUrl) replacedCoverUrlsRef.current.add(prev.portada_url);
        return { ...prev, portada_url: publicUrl };
      });
      setFeedback({ type: 'ok', text: tx('Foto del evento cargada a Storage (avatars). Guarda el evento para publicar.', 'Event photo uploaded to Storage (avatars). Save event to publish.') });
    } catch (err: unknown) { showErr(err, tx('Error subiendo foto del evento.', 'Event photo upload failed.')); }
    finally { busyRef.current = false; if (mountedRef.current) setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, uploadingCover: false })); }
  };

  const handleSaveEvent = async () => {
    if (busyRef.current || busy.saving || busy.uploadingCover || !canMutate.ok) return;
    busyRef.current = true; setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, saving: true })); setFeedback(null);
    const { form, editingService } = editorModal;
    const startBizId = bizIdRef.current, rawCupoTrim = form.cupo_maximo.trim(), parsedCupo = /^\d+$/.test(rawCupoTrim) ? Number(rawCupoTrim) : Number.NaN;
    const cupoValue = !Number.isSafeInteger(parsedCupo) || parsedCupo < editFloor || parsedCupo > MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS ? parsedCupo : form.studio_layout.totalSpots;
    try {
      await savePartnerWebEventService({
        actorUserId, isAdmin, isB2B, business, editingService, activeReservations, completedReservations,
        rawDraft: { ...form, duracion_minutos: form.duracion_minutos.trim() ? Number(form.duracion_minutos.trim()) : Number.NaN, precio_kc: form.precio_kc.trim(), cupo_maximo: cupoValue },
      });
      const staleUrls = [...replacedCoverUrlsRef.current].filter((u) => u !== form.portada_url);
      if (staleUrls.length > 0) void removePartnerOwnedAvatarObjectsBatchSafe(staleUrls, actorUserId, isAdmin);
      replacedCoverUrlsRef.current.clear();
      await onRefreshWorkspace();
      if (mountedRef.current && bizIdRef.current === startBizId) {
        formSessionRef.current += 1;
        setEditorModal((p) => ({ ...p, open: false, editingService: null }));
        setFeedback({ type: 'ok', text: tx('Experiencia y fotografía guardadas en catálogo.', 'Experience & photo saved to catalog.') });
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) { showErr(err, tx('No se pudo guardar.', 'Save failed.')); }
    finally { busyRef.current = false; if (mountedRef.current) setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, saving: false })); }
  };

  const handleInspectStudio = async (srv: IPartnerWebServiceRow) => {
    const reqId = ++studioReqIdRef.current, startBizId = bizIdRef.current;
    const attrs = srv.atributos && typeof srv.atributos === 'object' ? (srv.atributos as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
    const fallbackLayout = resolveDefaultStudioLayout(srv.categoria || 'Fuerza', Number(srv.cupo_maximo) || 12, srv.studio_layout_json || attrs.studio_layout, srv.nombre_servicio || srv.titulo || '');
    setStudioPanel((p) => ({ ...p, selectedServiceId: srv.id, loading: true, editorLayout: fallbackLayout }));
    try {
      const roster = await loadPartnerServiceStudioRoster(srv, activeReservations, completedReservations);
      if (mountedRef.current && reqId === studioReqIdRef.current && bizIdRef.current === startBizId) {
        setStudioPanel((p) => ({ ...p, roster, editorLayout: roster.layout }));
      }
    } catch (rosterErr: unknown) {
      if (mountedRef.current && reqId === studioReqIdRef.current) {
        setStudioPanel((p) => ({ ...p, roster: null }));
        showErr(rosterErr, tx('No se pudo cargar el mapa de Sala 3D.', 'Unable to load 3D Studio map.'));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mountedRef.current && reqId === studioReqIdRef.current) setStudioPanel((p) => ({ ...p, loading: false }));
    }
  };

  const handleSaveLiveLayout = async () => {
    if (busyRef.current || !selectedService || !studioPanel.editorLayout || busy.savingLayout || !canMutate.ok) return;
    busyRef.current = true; setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, savingLayout: true })); setFeedback(null);
    const startBizId = bizIdRef.current, targetSrv = selectedService, nextLayout = studioPanel.editorLayout;
    try {
      await savePartnerWebStudioLayoutOnly({ actorUserId, isAdmin, isB2B, business, service: targetSrv, activeReservations, completedReservations, layout: nextLayout });
      await onRefreshWorkspace();
      if (!mountedRef.current || bizIdRef.current !== startBizId) return;
      await handleInspectStudio({ ...targetSrv, cupo_maximo: nextLayout.totalSpots, studio_layout_json: nextLayout });
      if (mountedRef.current && bizIdRef.current === startBizId) setFeedback({ type: 'ok', text: tx('Mapa Sala 3D y aforo actualizados.', '3D Studio map and capacity updated.') });
    } catch (err: unknown) { showErr(err, tx('Error guardando Sala 3D.', '3D map save failed.')); }
    finally { busyRef.current = false; if (mountedRef.current) setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, savingLayout: false })); }
  };

  const handleTogglePause = async (srv: IPartnerWebServiceRow) => {
    if (busyRef.current || busy.togglingId || !canMutate.ok) return;
    busyRef.current = true; setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, togglingId: srv.id }));
    try { await togglePartnerWebEventState({ actorUserId, isAdmin, isB2B, business, service: srv }); await onRefreshWorkspace(); }
    catch (err: unknown) { showErr(err, tx('Error actualizando estado.', 'Status update failed.')); }
    finally { busyRef.current = false; if (mountedRef.current) setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, togglingId: null })); }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (srv: IPartnerWebServiceRow) => {
    if (busyRef.current || busy.deletingId || !canMutate.ok) return;
    busyRef.current = true; setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, deletingId: srv.id }));
    const startBizId = bizIdRef.current;
    try {
      await deletePartnerWebEventService({ actorUserId, isAdmin, isB2B, business, service: srv });
      if (mountedRef.current && bizIdRef.current === startBizId) {
        if (studioPanel.selectedServiceId === srv.id) setStudioPanel(INITIAL_STUDIO);
        setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, confirmDeleteId: null }));
      }
      await onRefreshWorkspace();
      if (mountedRef.current && bizIdRef.current === startBizId) setFeedback({ type: 'ok', text: tx('Experiencia eliminada y Escrow reembolsado.', 'Experience deleted & Escrow refunded.') });
    } catch (err: unknown) { showErr(err, tx('Error eliminando.', 'Delete failed.')); }
    finally { busyRef.current = false; if (mountedRef.current) setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, deletingId: null })); }
  };

  return {
    filterKey, setFilterKey, editorModal, setForm, busy, studioPanel, feedback, canMutate, editFloor, editBookedSet,
    selectedService, selectedFloor, selectedBookedSet, filteredServices, openEditor, closeEditor, clearCoverImage,
    setConfirmDeleteId: (id: string | null) => setBusy((p) => ({ ...p, confirmDeleteId: id })),
    setEditorLayout: (layout: IStudioLayoutConfig) => setStudioPanel((p) => ({ ...p, editorLayout: layout })),
    handleUploadEventCover, handleSaveEvent, handleInspectStudio, handleSaveLiveLayout, handleTogglePause, handleDelete,
  };
}

export interface UseEventDetailsModalParams {

  isVisible?: boolean;
  event?: EventAttrsRecord | null;
  selectedEvent?: EventAttrsRecord | null;
  isCreateMode?: boolean;
  defaultClubName?: string;
  onClose?: () => void;
  onReserve?: (ev: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<boolean | string | { reservaId?: string; id?: string } | void> | boolean | string | { reservaId?: string; id?: string } | void;
  onRefreshEvents?: () => Promise<void> | void;
  onNavigateVault?: () => void;
  [k: string]: unknown;
}

export const useEventDetailsModal = ({
  visible, isVisible, event, selectedEvent, isCreateMode = false,
  defaultClubName, onClose, onReserve, onRefreshEvents,
}: UseEventDetailsModalParams) => {
  const { user, session, profile, refreshProfile } = useAuth();
  const { tx, td, tDisc, fmtDate, language, themeColors } = useKineticPreferences();
  const router = useRouter();
  const scrollRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null);

  const isOpen = Boolean(visible ?? isVisible);
  const rawIncoming = event ?? selectedEvent;
  const lastValidDataRef = useRef<EventAttrsRecord | null>(null);
  if (isCreateMode && !rawIncoming) lastValidDataRef.current = null;
  else if (rawIncoming) lastValidDataRef.current = rawIncoming;
  const currentEv = rawIncoming || lastValidDataRef.current;

  const [isFavEvent, setIsFavEvent] = useState(false); const [isEditMode, setIsEditMode] = useState(false);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false); const [isStoryOpen, setIsStoryOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeSheet, setActiveSheet] = useState<ActiveSheetType>(null);

  });

  const saveExperience = async () => {
    Keyboard.dismiss();
    const cleanT = titleVal.trim();
    if (!cleanT) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning'); scrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true });
      triggerAlert(tx('TÍTULO REQUERIDO', 'TITLE REQUIRED'), tx('Escribe el nombre del evento deportivo en el campo superior.', 'Enter the sports event title in the top field.'), 'error', () => scrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true }));
      return;
    }
    if (!isEventEditorFormValid({ titleVal: cleanT, discVal, dateVal, timeVal, priceVal, cupoVal })) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      triggerAlert(tx('CAMPOS INVÁLIDOS', 'INVALID FIELDS'), tx('Verifica fecha, horario, cupo (1-200) y precio múltiplo de 10 KC (0 a 3,000 KC).', 'Verify date, time, capacity (1-200), and price in multiples of 10 KC (0 to 3,000 KC).'), 'warning');
      return;
    }
    setIsSaving(true);
    try {
      const { cleanExtras, safeAge, resolvedGps, coverHttpsUrl } = await persistEventExperienceAction({
        cleanT, currentEv, isCreateMode, isAdmin, canManage, clubName, coverUri: primaryCoverUri, galleryUris, standardDisciplineImage,

      setGalleryUris(cleanExtras); setAgeVal(safeAge);
      if (resolvedGps) setLocCoords({ lat: resolvedGps.latitud, lng: resolvedGps.longitud });
      invalidateEventosMemoryCache(); await onRefreshEvents?.();
      setIsEditMode(false); setActivePhotoIdx(0); triggerHaptic('Success');
      if (isCreateMode) onClose?.();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : '';
      triggerHaptic('Error'); triggerAlert(tx('FRICCIÓN', 'FRICTION'), msg || tx('No se pudo guardar el evento.', 'Could not save event.'), 'error');
    } finally { setIsSaving(false); }
  };

  const selectOptionValue = (sheet: ActiveSheetType, val: string) => {
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    if (sheet === 'disciplina' || sheet === 'cupo') studioDirtyRef.current = true;
    applyEventSheetOptionSelection(sheet, val, {

  const handleConfirmBookingAction = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isConfirmingBookingRef.current || isBookingBusy || !currentEv) return;
    if (bookingLockState.isLocked) {
      setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'COLLISION_RESET' }) === 2);
      triggerAlert(tx('SESIÓN NO DISPONIBLE', 'SESSION UNAVAILABLE'), bookingLockState.label, 'warning');
      return;
    }
    if (isStrictB2B || isTerminalOperational) {
      setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'COLLISION_RESET' }) === 2);
      triggerAlert(tx('SOLO CUENTAS DE ATLETA', 'ATHLETE ACCOUNTS ONLY'), tx('Las cuentas operativas B2B no pueden realizar reservas de atleta.', 'B2B operator accounts cannot book as athletes.'), 'error');
      return;
    }
    const requiredEscrow = isAdmin ? 0 : effectiveEscrowKc;
    if (!isAdmin && requiredEscrow > 0 && availableKc < requiredEscrow) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'INSUFFICIENT_KC' }) === 2);
      setInsufficientKcPrompt({ missingKc: Math.max(1, requiredEscrow - availableKc), availableKc, requiredEscrow });
      return;
    }
    isConfirmingBookingRef.current = true;
    setIsConfirmingBooking(true);
    setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'SEAL_START' }) === 2);
    const bookingStartedIso = new Date(Date.now() - 4000).toISOString();
    try {
      const realTitle = String(titleVal || currentEv?.nombre_servicio || currentEv?.titulo || tx('Evento Kinetic', 'Kinetic Event')).trim();
      const payloadEv = buildBookingConfirmPayload({
        currentEv, realTitle, localBookingDateIso, timeVal, selectedSpot, effectiveSplitCount, selectedGuests: pendingSplitConfig?.selectedGuests || [], isAdmin, totalKc,
        onInsufficientKc: (missingKc, avail, req) => {
          setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'INSUFFICIENT_KC' }) === 2);
          setInsufficientKcPrompt({ missingKc, availableKc: avail, requiredEscrow: req });
        },
        onBookingError: (msg) => {
          if (/ocupad|ocupar|taken|took\s+your\s+spot|tom[oó]\s+tu\s+lugar|se\s+agotan|colisi|agotad|sold\s*out|spot_ocupado|23505/i.test(String(msg || ''))) {
            persistAndSelectSpot(null);
            setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'COLLISION_RESET' }) === 2);
          }
          triggerAlert(tx('AVISO DE RESERVA', 'BOOKING NOTICE'), msg, 'error');
        },
        onGhostPurged: (msg) => { resetPreBookingSplit(); persistAndSelectSpot(null); onClose?.(); if (navTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(navTimeoutRef.current); navTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => triggerAlert(tx('EXPERIENCIA RETIRADA', 'EXPERIENCE RETIRED'), msg, 'warning'), 220); },
      });
      const reserveOutcome = await onReserve?.(payloadEv);
      if (reserveOutcome === false) return;
      const calLocation = String(locVal || currentEv?.ubicacion || '').trim() || null;
      if (user?.id) {
        const outObj = reserveOutcome && typeof reserveOutcome === 'object' ? (reserveOutcome as { reserva_id?: unknown; id?: unknown }) : null;
        const confirmedId = String(outObj?.reserva_id || outObj?.id || '').trim() || resolveRecentCreatedReservaId(currentEv?.id ?? null, localBookingDateIso) || '';
        const exists = (getBovedaSWRReservas(user.id) || []).some((r) => (confirmedId && String(r?.id || '') === confirmedId) || (currentEv?.id && String(r?.servicio_id || '') === String(currentEv.id) && String(r?.fecha_clase || r?.fecha || '').slice(0, 10) === localBookingDateIso));
        if (!exists) injectConfirmedBovedaPass(user.id, { id: confirmedId, servicioId: currentEv?.id ?? null, title: realTitle, venueName: clubName, fechaIso: localBookingDateIso, horaInicio: timeVal || currentEv?.hora_inicio || '08:00', spotNumber: selectedSpot ?? null, totalKc, escrowKc: effectiveEscrowKc, puertaKc: Math.max(0, totalKc - effectiveEscrowKc), ubicacion: calLocation, categoria: discVal, instructorNombre: coachVal, duracionMinutos: Number(durVal) || 60, isAdmin });
      }
      if (localBookingTrackingKey) setLocalBookedEventId(localBookingTrackingKey);
      resetPreBookingSplit(); persistAndSelectSpot(null); notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed(); onClose?.();
      if (navTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(navTimeoutRef.current);
      navTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
        setAlertConfig(buildBookingSuccessAlertConfig({
          realTitle, localBookingDateIso, timeVal, clubName, discVal, locVal: calLocation || locVal, coachVal, durVal,
          selectedSpot, equipmentType: studioLayout?.equipmentType || null, totalKc, escrowKc: effectiveEscrowKc,
          puertaKc: Math.max(0, totalKc - effectiveEscrowKc), isAdmin, language, fmtDate, tx,
          onConfirm: () => router.push('/(tabs)/boveda'),
          secondaryActionText: tx('Sincronizar con mi Calendario', 'Sync to Calendar'),
          onSecondaryAction: () => {
            void syncConfirmedModalBookingCalendar({
              returnedOutcome: reserveOutcome, serviceId: currentEv?.id, title: realTitle, venueName: clubName, location: calLocation, fechaIso: localBookingDateIso, horaInicio: timeVal, spotNumber: selectedSpot, escrowKc: effectiveEscrowKc, puertaKc: Math.max(0, totalKc - effectiveEscrowKc), language,
              lookupFallbackReservaId: user?.id ? async () => String((await verifyReservaSavedInDb(user.id, currentEv?.id != null ? String(currentEv.id) : null, realTitle, localBookingDateIso, selectedSpot, bookingStartedIso))?.id || '') || null : undefined,
            });
          },
        }));
      }, 160);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      triggerHaptic('Error');
      triggerAlert(tx('AVISO DE RESERVA', 'BOOKING NOTICE'), (err instanceof Error ? err.message : '') || tx('Intenta nuevamente.', 'Please try again.'), 'error');
    } finally { isConfirmingBookingRef.current = false; setIsConfirmingBooking(false); }
  }, [isBookingBusy, currentEv, availableKc, isAdmin, effectiveEscrowKc, effectiveSplitCount, pendingSplitConfig, titleVal, localBookingDateIso, timeVal, selectedSpot, totalKc, onReserve, localBookingTrackingKey, resetPreBookingSplit, persistAndSelectSpot, fmtDate, onClose, tx, router, triggerAlert, clubName, locVal, user?.id, language]);

  const handleConfirmWithAntiCollision = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isConfirmingBookingRef.current || antiCollisionBusyRef.current || isBookingBusy || !currentEv) return;
    antiCollisionBusyRef.current = true;
    try {
      if (selectedSpot != null) {
        const liveCheck = await verifySpotAntiCollisionLive({ serviceId: currentEv?.id != null ? String(currentEv.id) : undefined, serviceTitle: String(titleVal || currentEv?.nombre_servicio || currentEv?.titulo || ''), localIsoDate: localBookingDateIso, selectedSpot, currentUserId: user?.id });
        if (liveCheck.liveCount > 0) setConfirmedReservationsCount((prev) => Math.max(prev, liveCheck.liveCount));
        if (liveCheck.collided || liveCheck.liveCount >= maxCupo) {
          triggerHaptic('Warning'); persistAndSelectSpot(null);
          setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen(stepStudioBookingStepMachine(2, { type: 'COLLISION_RESET' }) === 2);
          setStudioInitialConfigMode(false); setIsStudioMapOpen(true);
          setAlertConfig(buildSpotCollisionAlertConfig({ tx, onConfirm: () => { setStudioInitialConfigMode(false); setIsStudioMapOpen(true); } }));
          return;
        }
        persistAndSelectSpot(selectedSpot);
      }
      await handleConfirmBookingAction();
    } finally { antiCollisionBusyRef.current = false; }
  }, [isBookingBusy, currentEv, selectedSpot, persistAndSelectSpot, titleVal, localBookingDateIso, user?.id, maxCupo, tx, handleConfirmBookingAction]);

  const storyOwner = useMemo<IKineticStoryOwner>(() => ({
    id: String(currentEv?.afiliado_id || clubName), name: clubName, avatarUrl: clubLogo, isOfficialKinetic: isOfficial, discipline: discVal,
    canUpload: canManage, subtitle: `${tDisc(discVal)} • ${titleVal || tx('Evento Kinetic', 'Kinetic Event')}`,
  }), [currentEv?.afiliado_id, clubName, discVal, titleVal, clubLogo, isOfficial, canManage, tDisc, tx]);

  return {
    userId: user?.id, router, scrollRef, isOpen, currentEv, tx, td, tDisc, language, themeColors, bookingLockState, isFavEvent,
    handleToggleFavorite, isEditMode, setIsEditMode, cancelEditMode, isSaving, isStoryOpen, setIsStoryOpen, activeSheet,
    setActiveSheet, openInternalSheet, minTomorrowIso, maxFutureIso, calMonth, changeCalMonth, alertConfig, setAlertConfig,
    triggerAlert, titleVal, setTitleVal, discVal, freqVal, dateVal, setDateVal, timeVal, durVal, coachVal, setCoachVal,
    locVal, handleLocInput, locCoords, detectedZone, cupoVal, priceVal, ageVal, reqsVal, setReqsVal, descVal, setDescVal,
    attrInt, attrEnf, attrImp, attrNiv, attrEnt, galleryUris, activePhotoIdx, setActivePhotoIdx, isPhotoLightboxOpen,
    setIsPhotoLightboxOpen, splitEscrowEnabled, setSplitEscrowEnabled, splitMaxPersonas, setSplitMaxPersonas, studioLayout,
    selectedSpot, setSelectedSpot, persistAndSelectSpot, pendingSplitConfig, applyPreBookingSplit, resetPreBookingSplit,
    effectiveSplitCount, effectiveEscrowKc, effectiveDoorKc, perPersonTotalKc, insufficientKcPrompt, setInsufficientKcPrompt,
    isStudioMapOpen, setIsStudioMapOpen, isStudioInitialConfigMode: studioInitialConfigMode, studioInitialConfigMode,
    setStudioInitialConfigMode, handleSaveStudioLayout, eventPersistKey, isPreBookingConfirmOpen, setIsPreBookingConfirmOpen,
    isBookingBusy, hasActiveBookingForThis, handleConfirmBookingAction, handleConfirmWithAntiCollision, navTimeoutRef, isAdmin,
    canManage, isStrictB2B, isTerminalOperational, clubName, clubLogo, standardDisciplineImage, allDisplayPhotos, coverUri,
    totalKc, basePriceKc, hasFlashDiscount, flashBadgeText, escrow40, puerta60, availableKc, maxCupo, confirmedCount,
    freeSpots, readableDate, localBookingDateIso, openMapsNavigation, handleShareExperience, customCoverUri, pickCoverImage,
    removeCustomCover, pickGalleryImages, removeGalleryPhotoAt, saveExperience, selectOptionValue, storyOwner,
    isMembershipModalOpen, setIsMembershipModalOpen, isProcessingMembership, processingPlanId, handleMembershipPurchase,
    userLevel: profile?.nivel, userKcMembresia: profile?.kc_membresia,
  };
};
