import { useFocusEffect, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Animated, Easing, PanResponder, Platform, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { triggerHaptic } from '../components/SharedUI';
import { MINICARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH } from '../components/eventos/EventosFlashAndAgendaViews';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { EVENT_SORT_OPTIONS, EventSortKey, calcDistanceKm, normTxt, projectEventDateForSearch } from '../services/eventosHelpers';
import {
  FLASH_CAROUSEL_INTERVAL_MS, type EventosRecord, type MappedEventoRecord, addDaysToIso,
  buildAgendaDaySessions, buildAgendaMonthCells, buildAgendaWeekCells, buildRecommendedActiveEvents,
  fetchEventosLiveSupabasePayload, filterAndSortCatalogEvents, getLocalTodayIso, isEventosAdminView,
  isEventosB2BAffiliate, mapSupabaseEventosPayload, mapServiceRowToEventosItem, matchesDaySchedule,
  parseIsoLocal, resolveEventosInitialCache, toIsoLocal,
} from '../services/eventosScreenHelpers';
import { exploreService } from '../services/exploreService';
import { filterOutServiceId, reconcileActiveLiveServicesSnapshot, registerGhostPurgeHook } from '../services/kineticGhostEventsPurgeHelper';
import { registerSessionCleanupHook } from '../services/kineticSessionPersistence';
import {
  getEventosSwrSync,
  hydrateEventosSwrEncrypted,
  setEventosSwrSync,
  type SwrRecord,
} from '../services/kineticSwrMemoryHub';
import { createDebouncedEventRunner, getFavoriteEventsIds, isFavoriteOnlyRadarPulse, subscribeKineticRadarUpdated, subscribeKineticRootRefresh } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { subscribeFavoriteEventsMemory } from '../utils/kineticFavoriteEventsMemoryLeaf';
import { useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel } from './boveda/useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel';
import { useEventosBookingAndDeepLink } from './eventos/useEventosBookingAndDeepLink';
import { getExploreMemoryCache, getExploreServiciosMemoryCache } from './useExplorar';

export { EVENT_SORT_OPTIONS, EventSortKey };

const EVENTOS_EGRESS_TTL_MS = 120_000;
let eventosLastFetchedAt = 0;
let eventosFavsCache: { uid: string; ids: string[]; ts: number } | null = null;
let eventosMemoryCache: MappedEventoRecord[] | null = null;
let eventosMemoryCacheMode: 'admin' | 'public' | null = null;

export const invalidateEventosMemoryCache = (): void => {
  eventosMemoryCache = null;
  eventosMemoryCacheMode = null;
  eventosLastFetchedAt = 0;
};
registerSessionCleanupHook(() => {
  eventosFavsCache = null;
  invalidateEventosMemoryCache();
});

export const useEventosScreen = () => {
  const { user, session, profile, refreshProfile } = useAuth();
  const { themeColors, t, tx, tDisc, language } = useKineticPreferences();
  const routeParams = useLocalSearchParams<{ evento?: string | string[]; event?: string | string[] }>();

  const isAdmin = useMemo(() => isEventosAdminView(user, profile, session?.access_token), [user, profile, session?.access_token]);
  const canCreateEvents = useMemo(() => isEventosB2BAffiliate(user, profile, session?.access_token), [user, profile, session?.access_token]);
  const defaultCreatorClubName = useMemo(
    () => (isAdmin ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : String(profile?.nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial').trim()),
    [isAdmin, profile?.nombre]
  );

  const initialCache = useMemo(() => resolveEventosInitialCache({
    isAdminMode: isAdmin, cachedCatalog: eventosMemoryCache, cachedMode: eventosMemoryCacheMode,
    swrPayload: getEventosSwrSync(user?.id) as { servData?: EventosRecord[]; afData?: EventosRecord[]; resData?: EventosRecord[] } | null,
    exploreServicios: getExploreServiciosMemoryCache() as EventosRecord[] | null,
    exploreAffiliates: getExploreMemoryCache() as EventosRecord[] | null,
    calcDistFn: calcDistanceKm, mapPayloadFn: mapSupabaseEventosPayload, mapSingleFn: mapServiceRowToEventosItem,
  }), [isAdmin, user?.id]);
  if (initialCache.length > 0 && eventosMemoryCache === null) {
    eventosMemoryCache = initialCache;
    eventosMemoryCacheMode = isAdmin ? 'admin' : 'public';
    const swrTs = getEventosSwrSync(user?.id)?.fetchedAt;
    if (swrTs) eventosLastFetchedAt = swrTs;
  }
  const hasLoadedInitialRef = useRef<boolean>(Boolean(initialCache.length));
  const isFetchingRef = useRef<boolean>(false); const isMountedRef = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const isTabFocusedRef = useRef<boolean>(true); const alertTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const lastValidDataRef = useRef<MappedEventoRecord[] | null>(initialCache.length ? initialCache : null);
  const fetchSeqRef = useRef<number>(0); const handledDeepLinkRef = useRef<string>('');
  const [hasLoadedInitial, setHasLoadedInitial] = useState<boolean>(Boolean(initialCache.length));
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'agenda' | 'lista'>('agenda');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(!initialCache.length);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [catalog, setCatalog] = useState<MappedEventoRecord[]>(() => initialCache);
  const [favoriteIds, setFavoriteIds] = useState<string[]>(() => (user?.id && eventosFavsCache?.uid === user.id ? eventosFavsCache?.ids || [] : []));
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState('Todas'); const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [quickChip, setQuickChip] = useState<'todas' | 'wellness' | 'favoritos' | 'mas_buscados'>('todas');
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<EventSortKey>('relevancia');
  const [catPickerOpen, setCatPickerOpen] = useState(false); const [sortPickerOpen, setSortPickerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedEvent, setSelectedEventState] = useState<EventosRecord | null>(null);
  const [isCreateEventOpen, setIsCreateEventOpen] = useState(false); const [isMembershipOpen, setIsMembershipOpen] = useState(false);
  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<Record<string, unknown> & { visible: boolean }>({ visible: false });
  const todayIso = getLocalTodayIso();
  const [selectedIsoDate, setSelectedIsoDate] = useState<string>(() => getLocalTodayIso());
  const [activeMonth, setActiveMonth] = useState<Date>(() => { const d = parseIsoLocal(getLocalTodayIso()); return new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1); });
  const [monthOpen, setMonthOpen] = useState(false);
  const initDateDoneRef = useRef(Boolean(initialCache.length)); const userPickedDateRef = useRef(false);
  const swipedRecentlyRef = useRef<number>(0); const lastWeekStepAtRef = useRef<number>(0); const slideAnimX = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const topCarouselRef = useRef<ScrollView>(null); const topCarouselIdxRef = useRef<number>(0);
  const isUserTouchingCarouselRef = useRef<boolean>(false); const [activeCarouselIdx, setActiveCarouselIdx] = useState(0);

  const selectAgendaDate = useCallback((iso: string, isManualUserAction = true) => {
    const cleanIso = String(iso || '').split('T')[0].trim();
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(cleanIso)) return;
    if (isManualUserAction) userPickedDateRef.current = true;
    setSelectedIsoDate(cleanIso);
    const d = parseIsoLocal(cleanIso);
    setActiveMonth(new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1));
  }, []);

  const setSelectedEvent = useCallback(
    (ev: EventosRecord | null, explicitDateIso?: string) => {
      if (!ev) { setSelectedEventState(null); return; }
      const rawCand = String(
        explicitDateIso ||
          ev?.fecha_seleccionada ||
          (viewMode === 'agenda' && !searchQuery.trim() ? selectedIsoDate : '') ||
          ev?.fecha_proyectada ||
          ev?.fecha ||
          getLocalTodayIso()
      ).split('T')[0].trim();
      const localTargetIso = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(rawCand) ? rawCand : getLocalTodayIso();
      setSelectedEventState({
        ...ev,
        fecha: localTargetIso,
        fecha_proyectada: localTargetIso,
        fecha_programada: localTargetIso,
        openInEditMode: Boolean(ev?.openInEditMode),
      });
    },
    [viewMode, searchQuery, selectedIsoDate]
  );

  const runSingleSwipeTransition = useCallback((dir: -1 | 1, onStateUpdate: () => void) => {
    onStateUpdate();
    slideAnimX.setValue(dir * 34);
    Animated.timing(slideAnimX, {
      toValue: 0, duration: 165, easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic), useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web',
    }).start();
  }, [slideAnimX]);

  const fetchCatalog = useCallback(async (silent = false, force = false) => {
    if (isFetchingRef.current && !force) return;
    const modeKey: 'admin' | 'public' = isAdmin ? 'admin' : 'public';
    if (force) {
      invalidateEventosMemoryCache();
    }
    const cacheMatchesMode = eventosMemoryCache !== null && eventosMemoryCacheMode === modeKey;
    if (!force && cacheMatchesMode && Date.now() - eventosLastFetchedAt < EVENTOS_EGRESS_TTL_MS) {
      hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
      setHasLoadedInitial((p) => (p ? p : true));
      setIsLoading((p) => (p ? false : p));
      setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
      return;
    }
    isFetchingRef.current = true;
    const seq = ++fetchSeqRef.current;
    if (!silent && !hasLoadedInitialRef.current && !cacheMatchesMode) setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const [servRes, afilRes, bookRes] = await fetchEventosLiveSupabasePayload(isAdmin);
      if ((servRes.error || afilRes.error) && !servRes.data && lastValidDataRef.current?.length && !force) return;

      const hasBothPrimaryErrors = Boolean(servRes?.error && afilRes?.error);
      if (hasBothPrimaryErrors && lastValidDataRef.current?.length) {
        if (isMountedRef.current && seq === fetchSeqRef.current) setCatalog(lastValidDataRef.current);
        return;
      }
      const nowLocal = new Date();
      const reconciledServicios = reconcileActiveLiveServicesSnapshot(
        Array.isArray(servRes.data) ? servRes.data : [],
        { allowAdminDrafts: isAdmin }
      );
      const items = mapSupabaseEventosPayload(
        reconciledServicios,
        (afilRes.data || []) as unknown as EventosRecord[],
        (bookRes.data || []) as unknown as EventosRecord[],
        isAdmin,
        calcDistanceKm,
        nowLocal
      );
      if (servRes?.error && items.length === 0 && lastValidDataRef.current?.length) return;
      if (seq !== fetchSeqRef.current) return;
      eventosMemoryCache = items; eventosMemoryCacheMode = modeKey;
      lastValidDataRef.current = items; eventosLastFetchedAt = Date.now();
      setEventosSwrSync({ userId: user?.id ? String(user.id) : undefined, servData: reconciledServicios as SwrRecord[], afData: (afilRes.data || []) as unknown as SwrRecord[], resData: (bookRes.data || []) as unknown as SwrRecord[], fetchedAt: eventosLastFetchedAt });
      if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
      setCatalog(items);
      if (items.length > 0) {
        const baseIso = getLocalTodayIso(nowLocal);
        setSelectedIsoDate((curIso) => {
          const effectiveCur = curIso < baseIso ? baseIso : curIso;
          if (effectiveCur < baseIso || (!initDateDoneRef.current && !userPickedDateRef.current)) {
            initDateDoneRef.current = true;
            if (!items.some((ev) => matchesDaySchedule(ev, effectiveCur, baseIso, { includeInactiveAdmin: isAdmin, nowRef: nowLocal }))) {
              for (let i = 0; i <= 90; i++) {
                const cand = addDaysToIso(baseIso, i);
                if (items.some((ev) => matchesDaySchedule(ev, cand, baseIso, { includeInactiveAdmin: isAdmin, nowRef: nowLocal }))) {
                  const d = parseIsoLocal(cand);
                  setActiveMonth(new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1));
                  return cand;
                }
              }
            }
          }
          initDateDoneRef.current = true;
          return effectiveCur;
        });
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; } finally {
      isFetchingRef.current = false; hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
      if (isMountedRef.current && seq === fetchSeqRef.current) {
        setHasLoadedInitial((p) => (p ? p : true)); setIsLoading((p) => (p ? false : p)); setRefreshing((p) => (p ? false : p));
      }
    }
  }, [isAdmin, user?.id]);

  const applyFavIdsIfChanged = useCallback((nextIds: string[]) => {
    setFavoriteIds((prev) =>
      prev.length === nextIds.length && prev.every((v, idx) => v === nextIds[idx]) ? prev : nextIds
    );
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    if (!eventosMemoryCache?.length) {
      hydrateEventosSwrEncrypted(user?.id).then((swr) => {
        if (!isMountedRef.current || eventosMemoryCache?.length || !swr?.servData?.length) return;
        const restored = mapSupabaseEventosPayload(swr.servData as EventosRecord[], (swr.afData || []) as EventosRecord[], (swr.resData || []) as EventosRecord[], isAdmin, calcDistanceKm, new Date());
        if (restored.length > 0) {
          eventosMemoryCache = restored; eventosMemoryCacheMode = isAdmin ? 'admin' : 'public';
          lastValidDataRef.current = restored; hasLoadedInitialRef.current = true;
          setCatalog(restored); setHasLoadedInitial(true); setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }).catch(() => {});
    }
    fetchCatalog(Boolean(eventosMemoryCache?.length), false);
    const syncFavEvents = () => {
      getFavoriteEventsIds()
        .then((evIds: string[]) => {
          const clubIds = user?.id && eventosFavsCache?.uid === user.id ? eventosFavsCache?.ids || [] : [];
          applyFavIdsIfChanged(Array.from(new Set([...clubIds, ...(evIds || [])])));
        })
        .catch(() => {});
    };
    syncFavEvents();
    const unsubFavMem = subscribeFavoriteEventsMemory((evIds) => {
      const clubIds = user?.id && eventosFavsCache?.uid === user.id ? eventosFavsCache?.ids || [] : [];
      applyFavIdsIfChanged(Array.from(new Set([...clubIds, ...(evIds || [])])));
    });
    const debouncedReload = createDebouncedEventRunner(() => {
      invalidateEventosMemoryCache();
      fetchCatalog(true, true);
    }, 150);
    const debouncedFavSync = createDebouncedEventRunner(syncFavEvents, 150);
    const unsubRadar = subscribeKineticRadarUpdated(() => {
      if (isFavoriteOnlyRadarPulse()) return;
      debouncedReload.trigger();
    });
    const unsubRoot = subscribeKineticRootRefresh(debouncedReload.trigger);
    const unsubGhost = registerGhostPurgeHook('useEventos', (cleanId) => {
      if (eventosMemoryCache) eventosMemoryCache = filterOutServiceId(eventosMemoryCache, cleanId);
      if (isMountedRef.current) setCatalog((prev) => filterOutServiceId(prev, cleanId));
    });
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.addEventListener === 'function') {
      window.addEventListener('kinetic:fav-events-updated', debouncedFavSync.trigger);
      window.addEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedReload.trigger);
    }
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      debouncedReload.cancel(); debouncedFavSync.cancel();
      if (alertTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(alertTimerRef.current);
      unsubFavMem(); unsubRadar(); unsubRoot(); unsubGhost();
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.removeEventListener === 'function') {
        window.removeEventListener('kinetic:fav-events-updated', debouncedFavSync.trigger);
        window.removeEventListener('kinetic:boveda-updated', debouncedReload.trigger);
      }
    };
  }, [fetchCatalog, user?.id, applyFavIdsIfChanged]);

  useFocusEffect(useCallback(() => {
    isTabFocusedRef.current = true;
    const freshToday = getLocalTodayIso();
    setSelectedIsoDate((prev) => {
      if (prev < freshToday) { const dNow = parseIsoLocal(freshToday); setActiveMonth(new Date(dNow.getFullYear(), dNow.getMonth(), 1)); return freshToday; }
      return prev;
    });
    fetchCatalog(true, false);
    if (user?.id) {
      const favHit = eventosFavsCache;
      if (favHit && favHit.uid === user.id && Date.now() - favHit.ts < EVENTOS_EGRESS_TTL_MS) {
        getFavoriteEventsIds().then((evIds: string[]) => applyFavIdsIfChanged(Array.from(new Set([...(favHit.ids || []), ...(evIds || [])])))).catch(() => {});
      } else {
        Promise.all([exploreService.fetchUserFavorites(user.id).catch(() => []), getFavoriteEventsIds().catch(() => [])]).then(([clubIds, evIds]) => {
          eventosFavsCache = { uid: user.id, ids: clubIds || [], ts: Date.now() };
          applyFavIdsIfChanged(Array.from(new Set([...(clubIds || []), ...(evIds || [])])));
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
    }
    return () => { isTabFocusedRef.current = false; };
  }, [user?.id, fetchCatalog, applyFavIdsIfChanged]));

  const processedEvents = useMemo(
    () => filterAndSortCatalogEvents({ catalog, isAdminView: isAdmin, selectedCategory, searchQuery, quickChip, sortBy, favoriteIds, tDisc, normTxt }),
    [catalog, isAdmin, selectedCategory, searchQuery, quickChip, sortBy, favoriteIds, tDisc]
  );
  const recommendedEvents = useMemo(() => buildRecommendedActiveEvents(processedEvents), [processedEvents]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (viewMode !== 'lista' || recommendedEvents.length <= 1 || Boolean(selectedEvent) || isCreateEventOpen || catPickerOpen || sortPickerOpen || isMembershipOpen) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      if (isUserTouchingCarouselRef.current || !isTabFocusedRef.current) return;
      const nextIdx = (topCarouselIdxRef.current + 1) % recommendedEvents.length;
      topCarouselIdxRef.current = nextIdx;
      setActiveCarouselIdx(nextIdx);
      topCarouselRef.current?.scrollTo({ x: nextIdx * (MINICARD_CAROUSEL_WIDTH + 12), animated: true });
    }, FLASH_CAROUSEL_INTERVAL_MS);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [viewMode, recommendedEvents.length, selectedEvent, isCreateEventOpen, catPickerOpen, sortPickerOpen, isMembershipOpen]);

  const prevDaySessionsRef = useRef<MappedEventoRecord[]>([]);
  const daySessions = useMemo(() => {
    const computed = buildAgendaDaySessions(processedEvents, selectedIsoDate, todayIso, searchQuery, isAdmin, projectEventDateForSearch);
    if (!hasLoadedInitial && computed.length === 0 && catalog.length === 0 && prevDaySessionsRef.current.length > 0) {
      return prevDaySessionsRef.current;
    }
    prevDaySessionsRef.current = computed;
    return computed;
  }, [hasLoadedInitial, processedEvents, catalog.length, selectedIsoDate, todayIso, searchQuery, isAdmin]);
  const weekCells = useMemo(() => buildAgendaWeekCells(selectedIsoDate, processedEvents, todayIso, isAdmin), [selectedIsoDate, processedEvents, todayIso, isAdmin]);
  const monthGridCells = useMemo(() => buildAgendaMonthCells(activeMonth, processedEvents, todayIso, isAdmin), [activeMonth, processedEvents, todayIso, isAdmin]);

  const stepWeek = useCallback((dir: -1 | 1) => {
    const nowMs = Date.now();
    if (nowMs - lastWeekStepAtRef.current < 220) return;
    lastWeekStepAtRef.current = nowMs;
    const cand = addDaysToIso(selectedIsoDate, dir * 7);
    const nextIso = dir === -1 && cand < todayIso ? todayIso : cand;
    userPickedDateRef.current = true;
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    runSingleSwipeTransition(dir, () => {
      setSelectedIsoDate(nextIso);
      const d = parseIsoLocal(nextIso);
      setActiveMonth(new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1));
    });
  }, [selectedIsoDate, todayIso, runSingleSwipeTransition]);

  const stepMonth = useCallback((dir: -1 | 1) => {
    const dToday = parseIsoLocal(todayIso);
    const minM = new Date(dToday.getFullYear(), dToday.getMonth(), 1);
    const nextM = new Date(activeMonth.getFullYear(), activeMonth.getMonth() + dir, 1);
    if (nextM < minM) {
      triggerHaptic('Warning');
      Animated.timing(slideAnimX, { toValue: 0, duration: 130, useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web' }).start();
      return;
    }
    userPickedDateRef.current = true;
    triggerHaptic('Selection');
    runSingleSwipeTransition(dir, () => {
      setActiveMonth(nextM);
      const firstIso = toIsoLocal(nextM);
      setSelectedIsoDate(firstIso < todayIso ? todayIso : firstIso);
    });
  }, [activeMonth, todayIso, runSingleSwipeTransition, slideAnimX]);

  const handleSwipeEnd = useCallback(
    (dx: number, vx = 0) => {
      if (Math.abs(dx) > 32 || Math.abs(vx) > 0.35) {
        swipedRecentlyRef.current = Date.now();
        if (monthOpen) stepMonth(dx < 0 ? 1 : -1); else stepWeek(dx < 0 ? 1 : -1);
      } else {
        Animated.timing(slideAnimX, { toValue: 0, duration: 140, easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic), useNativeDriver: Platform.OS !== 'web' }).start();
      }
    },
    [monthOpen, stepMonth, stepWeek, slideAnimX]
  );

  const calendarSwipeResponder = useMemo(
    () =>
      PanResponder.create({
        onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: (_, gs) => Math.abs(gs.dx) > 10 && Math.abs(gs.dx) > Math.abs(gs.dy) * 1.2,
        onPanResponderMove: (_, gs) => slideAnimX.setValue(Math.max(-96, Math.min(96, gs.dx * 0.72))),
        onPanResponderRelease: (_, gs) => handleSwipeEnd(gs.dx, gs.vx),
        onPanResponderTerminate: (_, gs) => handleSwipeEnd(gs.dx, gs.vx),
      }),
    [handleSwipeEnd, slideAnimX]
  );

  const groupedByCategory = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, MappedEventoRecord[]>();
    processedEvents.forEach((ev) => { const cat = String(ev.categoria || 'Fuerza'); map.set(cat, [...(map.get(cat) || []), ev]); });
    return Array.from(map.entries()).map(([title, items]) => ({ title, items }));
  }, [processedEvents]);

  const activeSortOption = useMemo(() => EVENT_SORT_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.key === sortBy) || EVENT_SORT_OPTIONS[0], [sortBy]);

  const applySeatDelta = useCallback((evId: string, delta: number) => {
    setCatalog((prev) => {
      const next = prev.map((it) => String(it?.id) !== evId ? it : (() => {
        const occ = Math.max(0, (Number(it.cupo_actual ?? it.reservadosCount) || 0) + delta);
        return { ...it, cupo_actual: occ, reservadosCount: occ, ocupacionRatio: occ / Math.max(1, Number(it.cupo_maximo) || 12) };
      })());
      eventosMemoryCache = next;
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const refreshAfterMutation = useCallback(() => {
    invalidateEventosMemoryCache();
    void fetchCatalog(true, true);
  }, [fetchCatalog]);

  const [isCheckoutProcessing, setIsCheckoutProcessing] = useState(false);

  const { handleReserve } = useEventosBookingAndDeepLink({
    routeEvento: routeParams?.evento, routeEvent: routeParams?.event, catalog, hasLoadedInitial, isAdmin,
    user, profile, refreshProfile, viewMode, searchQuery, selectedIsoDate, language, tx, isMountedRef,
    setSelectedEvent, setAlertConfig, setIsMembershipOpen, applySeatDelta, onRefreshCatalogAfterReserve: refreshAfterMutation,
  });

  const { handlePurchase: handleMembershipPurchase } = useBovedaPurchaseAndCancel({
    user, isEn: language === 'en', tx, cleanVipLevel: String(profile?.nivel || 'Acceso Base'),
    refreshProfile, fetchData: async () => {}, getCostoTotal: () => 0,
    isProcessingPayment: isCheckoutProcessing, setIsProcessingPayment: setIsCheckoutProcessing,
    setProcessingPlanId: () => {}, isCanceling: false, setIsCanceling: () => {},
    setIsMembershipModalOpen: setIsMembershipOpen, setSelectedQR: () => {},
    scheduleBovedaTimeout: (fn, ms) => { alertTimerRef.current = setTimeout(fn, ms); },
    showTacticalAlert: (title, message, type = 'success') => setAlertConfig({ visible: true, title, message, type }),
  });

  return {
    themeColors, t, tx, tDisc, language, isAdmin, canCreateEvents, defaultCreatorClubName,
    viewMode, setViewMode, isLoading, hasLoadedInitial, refreshing, setRefreshing, catalog,
    selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory, searchQuery, setSearchQuery, quickChip, setQuickChip, sortBy, setSortBy,
    catPickerOpen, setCatPickerOpen, sortPickerOpen, setSortPickerOpen, selectedEvent, setSelectedEvent,
    isCreateEventOpen, setIsCreateEventOpen, isMembershipOpen, setIsMembershipOpen, alertConfig, setAlertConfig,
    todayIso, selectedIsoDate, setSelectedIsoDate, selectAgendaDate, activeMonth, setActiveMonth, monthOpen, setMonthOpen,
    swipedRecentlyRef, slideAnimX, topCarouselRef, topCarouselIdxRef, isUserTouchingCarouselRef,
    activeCarouselIdx, setActiveCarouselIdx, fetchCatalog, recommendedEvents, daySessions, weekCells,
    monthGridCells, stepWeek, stepMonth, calendarSwipeResponder, groupedByCategory, activeSortOption, handleReserve,
    handleMembershipPurchase, isCheckoutProcessing,
  };
};

    FILE: src/hooks/useReservationDetailScreen.ts

    // === src/hooks/useReservationDetailScreen.ts === //
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { IReservationAlertConfig, IReservationServiceItem } from '../components/eventos/ReservationDetailBreakdownCard';
import { triggerArcadeHaptic, triggerHaptic } from '../components/SharedUI';
import { parseIsoToLocalDate, toSafeLocalIsoDate } from '../constants/kineticEventCatalogs';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useKineticPreferences } from '../context/KineticPreferencesContext';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { shouldAutoSyncCalendarReminders, syncReservationToNativeCalendar } from '../services/kineticCalendarSyncService';
import { resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing } from '../services/kineticFlashSaleHelper';
import { injectConfirmedBovedaPass } from '../services/kineticOptimisticStore';
import { isKineticMasterAdminEmail, isRealSupabaseJwt } from '../services/kineticSessionPersistence';
import { getTomorrowIsoStr } from '../utils/eventEditorHelpers';
import { notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed } from '../utils/eventModalActions';
import { useCheckout } from './useCheckout';

export const isReservationUuidStr = (v: string) =>
  /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(String(v || '').trim());

const SRV_COLS =
  'id, negocio_nombre, nombre_servicio, duracion_minutos, duracion_min, precio_kc, cupo_maximo, hora_inicio, descripcion, imagen_url, portada_url, fecha, estado, categoria, ubicacion, latitud, longitud, atributos, nivel_requerido, instructor_nombre, requisitos_traer, edad_minima, frecuencia, es_venta_flash';

export const useReservationDetailScreen = () => {
  const { gymName, evento, event, split, host, fecha } = useLocalSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, session, profile, refreshProfile } = useAuth();
  const { tx, fmtDate, themeColors, resolvedTheme } = useKineticPreferences();

  const targetEventParam = String(evento || event || '').trim();
  const splitCount = Math.min(4, Math.max(1, parseInt(String(split || '1'), 10) || 1));
  const isSplitGuestMode = Boolean(split && splitCount >= 2);
  const hostIdParam = String(host || '').trim();

  const [servicios, setServicios] = useState<IReservationServiceItem[]>([]);
  const [hostName, setHostName] = useState<string>('');
  const [isFetching, setIsFetching] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [bookingId, setBookingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [reservedServiceIds, setReservedServiceIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [isMembershipModalOpen, setIsMembershipModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [processingPlanId, setProcessingPlanId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<IReservationAlertConfig>({
    visible: false,
    title: '',
    message: '',
    type: 'alert',
    onConfirm: null,
    confirmText: 'OK',
    cancelText: 'CANCEL',
  });

  const isMountedRef = useRef(true);
  const isSubmittingRef = useRef(false);
  const pendingTimersRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[]>([]);

  const scheduleSafeTimeout = useCallback((fn: () => void, ms: number) => {
    const id = setTimeout(fn, ms);
    pendingTimersRef.current.push(id);
    return id;
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      isMountedRef.current = false;
      pendingTimersRef.current.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));
      pendingTimersRef.current = [];
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!hostIdParam || !isReservationUuidStr(hostIdParam)) {
      setHostName(tx('Anfitrión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Host'));
      return;
    }
    supabase
      .from('perfiles_publicos')
      .select('id, nombre')
      .eq('id', hostIdParam)
      .maybeSingle()
      .then(
        ({ data }) => {
          if (isMountedRef.current) {
            setHostName(String(data?.nombre || '').trim() || tx('Anfitrión Kinetic', 'Kinetic Host'));
          }
        },
        () => {}
      );
  }, [hostIdParam, tx]);

  const isAdminView = Boolean(
    user?.id &&
      isRealSupabaseJwt(session?.access_token) &&
      Boolean((user as { email_confirmed_at?: unknown } | null)?.email_confirmed_at) &&
      isKineticMasterAdminEmail(user?.email)
  );

  const fetchData = useCallback(
    async (isSilent = false) => {
      if (!gymName && !targetEventParam) {
        if (isMountedRef.current) setIsFetching(false);
        return;
      }
      if (!isSilent && isMountedRef.current) setIsFetching(true);
      try {
        let servPromise;
        if (targetEventParam && isReservationUuidStr(targetEventParam)) {
          servPromise = supabase.from('servicios').select(SRV_COLS).eq('id', targetEventParam).limit(5);
        } else if (targetEventParam && !gymName) {
          const decoded = decodeURIComponent(targetEventParam).trim();
          servPromise = supabase.from('servicios').select(SRV_COLS).ilike('nombre_servicio', `%${decoded}%`).limit(20);
        } else {
          const cleanFilter = String(gymName).trim();
          const baseServ = supabase.from('servicios').select(SRV_COLS).ilike('negocio_nombre', `%${cleanFilter}%`).limit(80);
          servPromise = isAdminView
            ? baseServ.neq('estado', 'inactivo')
            : baseServ.in('estado', ['aprobado', 'activo']);
        }

        const [servReq, resReq] = await Promise.all([
          servPromise,
          supabase
            .from('reservas')
            .select('servicio_id, usuario_id, estado')
            .in('estado', ['pendiente', 'completada', 'activa', 'confirmada'])
            .limit(200),
        ]);
        if (!isMountedRef.current || servReq.error) return;
        const rawServices = (servReq.data || []) as unknown as IReservationServiceItem[];
        const todosLosServicios = rawServices.filter((s) => s?.activo !== false || isAdminView);
        const reservasData = (resReq.data || []) as unknown as {
          servicio_id?: string | null;
          usuario_id?: string | null;
          estado?: string | null;
        }[];
        const nombreGymLimpio = gymName ? String(gymName).trim().toLowerCase() : '';
        const serviciosFiltrados =
          nombreGymLimpio && !targetEventParam
            ? todosLosServicios.filter(
                (s) => s.negocio_nombre && s.negocio_nombre.trim().toLowerCase().includes(nombreGymLimpio)
              )
            : todosLosServicios;

        const myReservedIds: string[] = [];
        const serviciosConCupo = serviciosFiltrados.map((servicio) => {
          const reservasServ = reservasData.filter((r) => r.servicio_id === servicio.id);
          if (user?.id && reservasServ.some((r) => r.usuario_id === user.id && r.estado !== 'completada')) {
            myReservedIds.push(servicio.id);
          }
          return { ...servicio, ocupados: reservasServ.length };
        });
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setServicios(serviciosConCupo);
          if (myReservedIds.length > 0) {
            setReservedServiceIds((prev) => Array.from(new Set([...prev, ...myReservedIds])));
          }
        }
      } catch (err: unknown) { void err; } finally {
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setIsFetching(false);
          setRefreshing(false);
        }
      }
    },
    [gymName, targetEventParam, user?.id, isAdminView]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchData(false);
  }, [fetchData]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    triggerHaptic('Light');
    setRefreshing(true);
    await Promise.all([fetchData(true), refreshProfile?.()]);
  }, [fetchData, refreshProfile]);

  const { startCheckout, isProcessing: isProcessingPayment } = useCheckout({
    onSuccess: (item) => {
      setIsMembershipModalOpen(false);
      scheduleSafeTimeout(() => {
        if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
        setAlertConfig({
          visible: true,
          title: item.tipo === 'topup' ? tx('RECARGA TOP-UP APROBADA', 'TOP-UP APPROVED') : tx('ASCENSO VIP APROBADO', 'VIP UPGRADE APPROVED'),
          message:
            item.tipo === 'topup'
              ? tx(`Se inyectaron +${item.kcOtorgados} KC en tu Billetera Top-Up.`, `Added +${item.kcOtorgados} KC to your Top-Up Wallet.`)
              : tx(`Has adquirido ${item.nombre}. +${item.kcOtorgados} KC añadidos a tu Membresía.`, `Unlocked ${item.nombre}. +${item.kcOtorgados} KC added to Membership.`),
          type: 'alert',
          confirmText: tx('CONTINUAR', 'CONTINUE'),
          cancelText: tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
          onConfirm: null,
        });
      }, 260);
    },
    onError: (err) => {
      setIsMembershipModalOpen(false);
      scheduleSafeTimeout(() => {
        if (!isMountedRef.current) return;
        setAlertConfig({
          visible: true,
          title: tx('FRICCIÓN BANCARIA', 'BANKING FRICTION'),
          message: err.message,
          type: 'alert',
          confirmText: 'OK',
          cancelText: tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
          onConfirm: null,
        });
      }, 260);
    },
  });

  const handlePurchase = useCallback(
    async (plan: {
      id?: string;
      nivel?: string;
      tipo?: string;
      nombre?: string;
      kc_otorgados?: number | string;
      precio_mxn?: number | string;
      precio_mensual_mxn?: number | string;
    }) => {
      if (!user?.id || isProcessingPayment) return;
      setProcessingPlanId(String(plan.id || plan.nivel || ''));
      try {
        const kcVal = Number(plan.kc_otorgados) || 0;
        const isTopup = plan.tipo === 'topup';
        await startCheckout({
          userId: user.id,
          tipo: isTopup ? 'topup' : 'membresia',
          planId: String(plan.id || plan.nivel || 'plan'),
          nombre: isTopup ? `Top-Up +${kcVal} KC` : String(plan.nombre || plan.nivel),
          kcOtorgados: kcVal,
          precioMxn: Math.max(0, Number(plan?.precio_mxn ?? plan?.precio_mensual_mxn) || kcVal),
        });
      } finally {
        if (isMountedRef.current) setProcessingPlanId(null);
      }
    },
    [user?.id, isProcessingPayment, startCheckout]
  );

  const procesarReserva = useCallback(
    async (servicio: IReservationServiceItem, customSplitCount = 1) => {
      triggerHaptic('Light');
      if (bookingId || isSubmittingRef.current) return;
      if (!user?.id) {
        setAlertConfig({
          visible: true,
          title: tx('ACCESO DENEGADO', 'ACCESS DENIED'),
          message: tx('Inicia sesión para reservar esta experiencia.', 'Sign in to book this experience.'),
          type: 'alert',
          confirmText: tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'),
          cancelText: tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
          onConfirm: null,
        });
        return;
      }
      const cupoMaximo = Number(servicio.cupo_maximo) || 15;
      if ((servicio.ocupados ?? 0) >= cupoMaximo) {
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        setAlertConfig({
          visible: true,
          title: tx('CUPO AGOTADO', 'SOLD OUT'),
          message: tx('Ya no hay lugares disponibles para este horario.', 'No spots remaining for this schedule.'),
          type: 'alert',
          confirmText: tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'),
          cancelText: tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
          onConfirm: null,
        });
        return;
      }

      const safeSplit = Math.min(4, Math.max(1, Number(customSplitCount) || 1));
      const fp = resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing({
        event: { ...servicio, fecha: String(fecha || servicio.fecha_proyectada || servicio.fecha || '') },
        vipLevel: profile?.nivel,
        kcMembresia: profile?.kc_membresia,
        isAdmin: isAdminView,
      });
      const fullPrecioKc = isAdminView ? 0 : fp.discountedTotalKc;
      const effectivePrecioKc = isAdminView ? 0 : safeSplit > 1 ? Math.round(fullPrecioKc / safeSplit) : fullPrecioKc;
      const montoEscrowRequerido = isAdminView ? 0 : Math.round(effectivePrecioKc * 0.4);
      const saldoTotalBoveda = (Number(profile?.kc_membresia) || 0) + (Number(profile?.kc_topup) || 0);

      if (!isAdminView && montoEscrowRequerido > 0 && saldoTotalBoveda < montoEscrowRequerido) {
        triggerHaptic('Warning');
        const missingKc = Math.max(1, montoEscrowRequerido - saldoTotalBoveda);
        setAlertConfig({
          visible: true,
          title: tx('SALDO INSUFICIENTE', 'INSUFFICIENT BALANCE'),
          message: tx(
            `Te faltan ${missingKc} KC • Disponible: ${saldoTotalBoveda} KC (Requiere ${montoEscrowRequerido} KC de 40% Escrow).`,
            `You need ${missingKc} more KC • Available: ${saldoTotalBoveda} KC (Requires ${montoEscrowRequerido} KC 40% Escrow).`
          ),
          type: 'confirm',
          confirmText: tx('⚡ RECARGAR KINETICOINS / SUBIR NIVEL', '⚡ TOP UP KINETICOINS / UPGRADE'),
          cancelText: tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
          onConfirm: () => {
            scheduleSafeTimeout(() => {
              if (isMountedRef.current) setIsMembershipModalOpen(true);
            }, 180);
          },
        });
        return;
      }

      const realServiceTitle = String(servicio.nombre_servicio || servicio.titulo || tx('Evento Kinetic', 'Kinetic Event')).trim();
      const clubLabel = String(gymName || servicio.negocio_nombre || tx('Sede Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Venue')).trim();
      const localDateIso = toSafeLocalIsoDate(String(fecha || servicio.fecha_proyectada || servicio.fecha || ''), getTomorrowIsoStr());
      const horaLocal = String(servicio.hora_inicio || '08:00').substring(0, 5);
      const niceLocalDate = fmtDate(parseIsoToLocalDate(localDateIso), { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' }) || localDateIso;

      if (!isReservationUuidStr(String(servicio.id || ''))) {
        throw new Error(tx('Experiencia sin identificador válido del servidor.', 'Experience missing valid server identifier.'));
      }
      isSubmittingRef.current = true;
      setBookingId(servicio.id);
      try {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('procesar_reserva_escrow', {
          p_usuario_id: user.id,
          p_negocio_nombre: clubLabel,
          p_servicio_nombre: realServiceTitle,
          p_precio_kc: isAdminView ? 0 : fullPrecioKc,
          p_servicio_id: servicio.id,
          p_fecha_clase: localDateIso,
          p_spot_number: servicio.spot_number ?? null,
          p_split_count: safeSplit,
          p_es_pase_maestro: isAdminView,
        });
        if (error) throw error;

        const resultRow = Array.isArray(data) ? data[0] : data;
        await refreshProfile();
        notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed();
        triggerArcadeHaptic('checkIn');
        const montoRetenido = resultRow?.monto_escrow ?? resultRow?.monto_congelado ?? montoEscrowRequerido;
        const montoPuerta = Math.max(0, effectivePrecioKc - montoRetenido);
        const confirmedReservaId = String(resultRow?.reserva_id ?? resultRow?.id ?? '').trim();
        injectConfirmedBovedaPass(user.id, {
          id: confirmedReservaId, servicioId: servicio.id ?? null, title: realServiceTitle, venueName: clubLabel,
          fechaIso: localDateIso, horaInicio: horaLocal, spotNumber: servicio.spot_number ?? null,
          totalKc: effectivePrecioKc, escrowKc: Number(montoRetenido) || 0, puertaKc: montoPuerta,
          ubicacion: servicio.ubicacion ? String(servicio.ubicacion) : null, isAdmin: isAdminView,
        });
        const calInput = confirmedReservaId
          ? {
              reservaId: confirmedReservaId,
              title: realServiceTitle,
              venueName: clubLabel,
              location: servicio.ubicacion ? String(servicio.ubicacion) : null,
              fechaIso: localDateIso,
              horaInicio: horaLocal,
              durationMinutes: Number(servicio.duracion_minutos || servicio.duracion_min) || 60,
              spotNumber: servicio.spot_number ? Number(servicio.spot_number) : null,
              escrowKc: Number(montoRetenido) || 0,
              puertaKc: Number(montoPuerta) || 0,
              status: 'CONFIRMED' as const,
            }
          : null;
        if (calInput) {
          shouldAutoSyncCalendarReminders(user.id)
            .then((ok) => {
              if (ok) void syncReservationToNativeCalendar({ input: calInput, silent: true });
            })
            .catch(() => {});
        }

        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setReservedServiceIds((prev) => (prev.includes(servicio.id) ? prev : [...prev, servicio.id]));
          setAlertConfig({
            visible: true,
            title: isAdminView
              ? tx('PASE DIGITAL KINETIC • MAESTRO', 'KINETIC DIGITAL PASS • MASTER')
              : safeSplit > 1
                ? tx(`PASE DIGITAL KINETIC • DIVISIÓN 1/${safeSplit}`, `KINETIC DIGITAL PASS • 1/${safeSplit} SPLIT`)
                : tx('PASE DIGITAL KINETIC', 'KINETIC DIGITAL PASS'),
            message: isAdminView
              ? tx(
                  `Pase Maestro (0 KC) confirmado para "${realServiceTitle}" el ${niceLocalDate} (${horaLocal} hrs) en ${clubLabel}.`,
                  `Master Pass (0 KC) confirmed for "${realServiceTitle}" on ${niceLocalDate} (${horaLocal} hrs) at ${clubLabel}.`
                )
              : tx(
                  `Lugar asegurado en "${realServiceTitle}" (${clubLabel}) para el ${niceLocalDate} (${horaLocal} hrs).\n\n• Anticipo Protegido (40%)${safeSplit > 1 ? ` (1/${safeSplit})` : ''}: ${montoRetenido} KC\n• Liquidación en Recepción (60%): ${montoPuerta} KC\n\nTu Pase Digital Kinetic te espera en Mi Bóveda.`,
                  `Spot secured in "${realServiceTitle}" (${clubLabel}) for ${niceLocalDate} (${horaLocal} hrs).\n\n• Protected Deposit (40%)${safeSplit > 1 ? ` (1/${safeSplit})` : ''}: ${montoRetenido} KC\n• Front Desk Settlement (60%): ${montoPuerta} KC\n\nYour Kinetic Digital Pass is ready in My Vault.`
                ),
            type: 'confirm',
            confirmText: tx('IR A MI BÓVEDA QR', 'OPEN MY QR VAULT'),
            cancelText: tx('SEGUIR EXPLORANDO', 'KEEP EXPLORING'),
            onConfirm: () => router.replace('/(tabs)/boveda'),
            secondaryActionText: calInput ? tx('Sincronizar con mi Calendario', 'Sync to Calendar') : undefined,
            onSecondaryAction: calInput ? () => { void syncReservationToNativeCalendar({ input: calInput, silent: false }); } : null,
          } as typeof alertConfig);
        }
        fetchData(true);
      } catch (error: unknown) {
        const errMsg =
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : typeof error === 'object' && error !== null && 'message' in error
              ? String((error as { message?: unknown }).message || '')
              : '';
        triggerHaptic('Error');
        if (isMountedRef.current) {
          setAlertConfig({
            visible: true,
            title: tx('FRICCIÓN EN BÓVEDA', 'VAULT FRICTION'),
            message: errMsg || tx('No se pudo procesar la reserva.', 'Could not process booking.'),
            type: 'alert',
            confirmText: tx('ENTENDIDO', 'GOT IT'),
            cancelText: tx('CANCELAR', 'CANCEL'),
            onConfirm: null,
          });
        }
      } finally {
        isSubmittingRef.current = false;
        if (isMountedRef.current) setBookingId(null);
      }
    },
    [bookingId, user?.id, isAdminView, profile?.kc_membresia, profile?.kc_topup, gymName, fecha, fmtDate, tx, refreshProfile, router, fetchData, scheduleSafeTimeout]
  );

  const saldoTotal = (Number(profile?.kc_membresia) || 0) + (Number(profile?.kc_topup) || 0);
  const resolvedHeaderTitle = useMemo(
    () => gymName || servicios[0]?.negocio_nombre || servicios[0]?.nombre_servicio || tx('Sede Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Venue'),
    [gymName, servicios, tx]
  );

  return {
    router, profile, tx, themeColors, resolvedTheme, isLight: Boolean(themeColors.isLight),
    splitCount, isSplitGuestMode, hostName, preferredDateIso: fecha ? String(fecha) : undefined,
    servicios, isFetching, refreshing, onRefresh,
    bookingId, reservedServiceIds, isMembershipModalOpen, setIsMembershipModalOpen,
    isProcessingPayment, processingPlanId, alertConfig, setAlertConfig,
    procesarReserva, handlePurchase, saldoTotal, resolvedHeaderTitle,
  };
};

    import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { CABO_BASE_COORDS } from '../constants/exploreData';

export type EventSortKey = 'relevancia' | 'distancia' | 'rating_desc' | 'rating_asc' | 'horario' | 'demanda' | 'precio_asc' | 'precio_desc';

export const EVENT_SORT_OPTIONS: { key: EventSortKey; es: string; en: string; shortEs: string; shortEn: string }[] = [

  { key: 'rating_asc', es: 'Valoración (Menor a Mayor ★)', en: 'Rating (Low to High ★)', shortEs: 'Estrellas (Menor ★)', shortEn: 'Lowest Rated ★' },
  { key: 'horario', es: 'Horario más próximo', en: 'Earliest Start Time', shortEs: 'Horario', shortEn: 'Start Time' },
  { key: 'demanda', es: 'Mayor Demanda (Cupos Reservados)', en: 'Most Booked (High Demand)', shortEs: 'Mayor Demanda', shortEn: 'Most Booked' },
  { key: 'precio_asc', es: 'Precio (Menor a Mayor KC)', en: 'Price (Low to High KC)', shortEs: 'Menor Precio', shortEn: 'Lowest Price' },
  { key: 'precio_desc', es: 'Precio (Mayor a Menor KC)', en: 'Price (High to Low KC)', shortEs: 'Mayor Precio', shortEn: 'Highest Price' },
];

export const normTxt = (raw: string) => String(raw || '').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase();

export const projectEventDateForSearch = (
  ev: { fecha?: string | null; frecuencia?: string | null; descripcion?: string | null; hora_inicio?: string | null; hora?: string | null; [k: string]: unknown } | null | undefined,
  todayIso?: string
): string => {
  const mz = getMazatlanNowParts();
  const safeToday = normalizeLocalIsoDate(todayIso || mz.isoDate);
  const rawIso = normalizeLocalIsoDate(ev?.fecha || safeToday);
  const freq = String(ev?.frecuencia || getFreqFromDesc(ev?.descripcion || undefined)).toLowerCase();
  if (/diario|semanal|mensual|daily|weekly|monthly/.test(freq)) {
    if (safeToday === mz.isoDate && !todayIso) {
      return computeEventLocalLifecycle(ev).fechaProyectada;
    }
    const rawHora = String(ev?.hora_inicio || ev?.hora || '').trim();
    const hm = rawHora.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})\s*(a\.?m\.?|p\.?m\.?)?/i);
    let hh = hm ? parseInt(hm[1], 10) : 23;
    const mm = hm ? parseInt(hm[2], 10) || 0 : 59;
    const ampm = (hm?.[3] || '').toLowerCase();
    if (ampm.startsWith('p') && hh < 12) hh += 12;
    if (ampm.startsWith('a') && hh === 12) hh = 0;
    const evHHMM = `${String(Math.min(23, Math.max(0, hh))).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(Math.min(59, Math.max(0, mm))).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    const passedToday = rawIso === safeToday && safeToday === mz.isoDate && Boolean(rawHora) && evHHMM < mz.hhmm;
    if (rawIso < safeToday || passedToday) {
      const startOff = passedToday ? 1 : 0;
      for (let i = startOff; i <= 366; i++) {
        const cand = addDaysToIso(safeToday, i);
        if (matchesDaySchedule(ev, cand, safeToday)) return cand;
      }
      return passedToday ? addDaysToIso(safeToday, 1) : safeToday;
    }
  }
  return rawIso;
};

export const calcDistanceKm = (lat2?: number, lon2?: number): number => {
  void CABO_BASE_COORDS;
  return computeCanonicalVenueDistanceKm(lat2, lon2);
};

export const ACTIVE_RESERVA_STATES = ['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada'] as const;
export const DELETED_SERVICE_STATES = new Set([
  'eliminado',
  'eliminada',
  'borrado',
  'cancelado',
  'cancelada',
  'rechazado',
]);

const _globalProcessedReservaIds = new Set<string>();

export const isValidUuid = (val?: unknown): boolean =>
  /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(
    String(val || '').trim()
  );

export const hasReservaBeenProcessed = (reservaId?: unknown): boolean => {
  const clean = String(reservaId || '').trim();
  return Boolean(clean && _globalProcessedReservaIds.has(clean));
};

export const markReservaProcessed = (reservaId?: unknown): void => {
  const clean = String(reservaId || '').trim();
  if (clean) _globalProcessedReservaIds.add(clean);
};

const LEGACY_PASS_STORAGE_BASE_KEYS = [
  '@kinetic_boveda_passes_cache_v1',
  '@kinetic_boveda_cache',
  '@kinetic_boveda_cache_v1',
  '@kinetic_boveda_cache_v2',
  '@kinetic_local_passes',
  '@kinetic_local_passes_v1',
  '@kinetic_local_passes_v2',
  '@kinetic_admin_passes',
  '@kinetic_admin_passes_v1',
  '@kinetic_admin_passes_v2',
  '@kinetic_optimistic_passes',
  '@kinetic_optimistic_passes_v1',
];
const LEGACY_PASS_KEY_REGEX =
  /^@kinetic_(local_passes|boveda_passes|boveda_res|boveda_cache|admin_passes|optimistic_passes)/i;

export const purgeGhostPassStorageKeys = async (userId?: string): Promise<void> => {
  const keys = new Set<string>(LEGACY_PASS_STORAGE_BASE_KEYS);
  if (userId) {
    keys.add(`@kinetic_boveda_res_v2_${userId}`);
    keys.add(`@kinetic_boveda_cache_${userId}`);
    keys.add(`@kinetic_local_passes_${userId}`);
    keys.add(`@kinetic_admin_passes_${userId}`);
    keys.add(`@kinetic_optimistic_passes_${userId}`);
  }
  try {
    const allKeys = await AsyncStorage.getAllKeys();
    for (const k of allKeys) {
      if (LEGACY_PASS_KEY_REGEX.test(k)) keys.add(k);
    }
  } catch {}
  try {
    if (keys.size > 0) await AsyncStorage.multiRemove(Array.from(keys));
  } catch {}
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.localStorage) {
    try {
      for (let i = window.localStorage.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const lk = window.localStorage.key(i);
        if (lk && (keys.has(lk) || LEGACY_PASS_KEY_REGEX.test(lk))) {
          window.localStorage.removeItem(lk);
        }
      }
    } catch {}
  }
};

export const creditWalletSafe = async (
  targetOwnerId: string,
  amount: number,
  wallet: 'membresia' | 'topup'
): Promise<void> => {
  if (!targetOwnerId || amount <= 0) return;
  const { error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('inyectar_saldo_boveda', {
    p_user_id: targetOwnerId,
    p_cantidad: amount,
    p_billetera: wallet,
  });
  if (rpcErr) {
    throw new Error(rpcErr.message || 'ERROR_RPC_INYECTOR_BOVEDA');
  }
};

export const claimRefundAndDeleteOrphanRow = async (
  r: any,
  fallbackUserId: string = '',
  defaultEscrowKc: number = 0
): Promise<{ refunded: boolean; refundAmount: number }> => {
  const resId = String(r?.id || '').trim();
  const ownerId = String(r?.usuario_id || fallbackUserId || '').trim();
  if (!resId || !isValidUuid(resId)) return { refunded: false, refundAmount: 0 };
  if (_globalProcessedReservaIds.has(resId)) {
    return { refunded: false, refundAmount: 0 };
  }

  const st = String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  const isActiveState = st === 'pendiente' || st === 'activa' || st === 'confirmada';
  const memOrigin = Math.max(0, Number(r?.escrow_membresia) || 0);
  const topupOrigin = Math.max(0, Number(r?.escrow_topup) || 0);
  const rawEscrow = Math.max(
    0,
    Number(r?.monto_escrow ?? r?.anticipo_kc ?? defaultEscrowKc) || 0
  );
  const refundTotal = memOrigin + topupOrigin > 0 ? memOrigin + topupOrigin : rawEscrow;

  if (isActiveState) {
    try {
      const { data: rpcRes, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('cancelar_reserva_escrow_72h', {
        p_reserva_id: resId,
      });
      if (!rpcErr) {
        const resObj = (Array.isArray(rpcRes) ? rpcRes[0] : rpcRes) as Record<string, unknown> | null;
        _globalProcessedReservaIds.add(resId);
        try {
          await supabase.from('reservas').delete().eq('id', resId).eq('monto_escrow', 0);
        } catch {}
        purgePassById(resId).catch(() => {});
        return {
          refunded: Boolean(resObj?.refunded),
          refundAmount: Number(resObj?.refundAmount) || 0,
        };
      }
      if (
        rpcErr.code !== 'PGRST202' &&
        rpcErr.code !== '42883' &&
        !String(rpcErr.message || '').includes('cancelar_reserva_escrow_72h')
      ) {
        return { refunded: false, refundAmount: 0 };
      }
    } catch {
      return { refunded: false, refundAmount: 0 };
    }
  }

  let wonClaim = false;
  let alreadyDeleted = false;

  if (isActiveState) {
    try {
      const { data: casFull, error: casFullErr } = await supabase
        .from('reservas')
        .update({ estado: 'cancelada', monto_escrow: 0, escrow_membresia: 0, escrow_topup: 0 })
        .eq('id', resId)
        .in('estado', [...ACTIVE_RESERVA_STATES])
        .select('id');
      if (!casFullErr && Array.isArray(casFull) && casFull.length > 0) {
        wonClaim = true;
      } else if (casFullErr) {
        const { data: casMin, error: casMinErr } = await supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .update({ estado: 'cancelada', monto_escrow: 0 })
          .eq('id', resId)
          .in('estado', [...ACTIVE_RESERVA_STATES])
          .select('id');
        if (!casMinErr && Array.isArray(casMin) && casMin.length > 0) wonClaim = true;
      }
    } catch {}

    if (!wonClaim) {
      try {
        const { data: delClaim, error: delClaimErr } = await supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .delete()
          .eq('id', resId)
          .in('estado', [...ACTIVE_RESERVA_STATES])
          .select('id');
        if (!delClaimErr && Array.isArray(delClaim) && delClaim.length > 0) {
          wonClaim = true;
          alreadyDeleted = true;
        }
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  let refundedAmount = 0;
  let refundSucceeded = !isActiveState || refundTotal <= 0 || !ownerId;
  if (wonClaim && refundTotal > 0 && ownerId) {
    try {
      if (memOrigin > 0 || topupOrigin > 0) {
        if (memOrigin > 0) await creditWalletSafe(ownerId, memOrigin, 'membresia');
        if (topupOrigin > 0) await creditWalletSafe(ownerId, topupOrigin, 'topup');
      } else {
        await creditWalletSafe(ownerId, rawEscrow, 'topup');
      }
      refundedAmount = refundTotal;
      refundSucceeded = true;
    } catch {
      refundSucceeded = false;
    }
  }

  let deleteSucceeded = alreadyDeleted;
  if (!alreadyDeleted && (!isActiveState || (wonClaim && refundSucceeded))) {
    try {
      const { error: delErr } = await supabase.from('reservas').delete().eq('id', resId);
      if (!delErr) deleteSucceeded = true;
    } catch {}
  }
  if ((wonClaim && refundSucceeded) || (!isActiveState && deleteSucceeded)) {
    _globalProcessedReservaIds.add(resId);
  }
  purgePassById(resId).catch(() => {});
  return { refunded: refundedAmount > 0, refundAmount: refundedAmount };
};

export const cascadeRefundAndDeleteServiceReservas = async (
  serviceId: string,
  strictServiceLookup: boolean = false
): Promise<{ servData: any; affectedActiveCount: number }> => {
  const cleanSid = String(serviceId || '').trim();
  const { data: servData, error: fetchErr } = await supabase
    .from('servicios')
    .select('id, nombre_servicio, negocio_nombre, precio_kc')
    .eq('id', cleanSid)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (strictServiceLookup && fetchErr) {
    throw new Error(`Evento no encontrado: ${fetchErr.message}`);
  }
  if (strictServiceLookup && !servData) {
    throw new Error('Evento no encontrado en catálogo activo.');
  }

  const nombreServicio = String(servData?.nombre_servicio || '').trim();
  const negocioNombre = String(servData?.negocio_nombre || '').trim();
  const costoRetencionDefault = Math.floor((Number(servData?.precio_kc) || 0) * 0.4);
  const resSelectCols =
    'id, usuario_id, negocio_nombre, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, estado, monto_escrow, escrow_membresia, escrow_topup';

  const [byIdRes, byNullSidRes] = await Promise.all([
    isValidUuid(cleanSid)
      ? supabase.from('reservas').select(resSelectCols).eq('servicio_id', cleanSid)
      : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }),
    nombreServicio && negocioNombre
      ? supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .select(resSelectCols)
          .is('servicio_id', null)
          .eq('negocio_nombre', negocioNombre)
          .in('estado', [...ACTIVE_RESERVA_STATES])
      : Promise.resolve({ data: [] }),
  ]);

  const targetNameNorm = nombreServicio.toLowerCase();
  const targetNegNorm = negocioNombre.toLowerCase();
  const nullSidMatches = (byNullSidRes.data || []).filter((r: any) => {
    const names = [r?.servicio, r?.nombre_servicio, r?.titulo_clase]
      .map((v) => String(v || '').trim().toLowerCase())
      .filter(Boolean);
    if (!names.includes(targetNameNorm)) return false;
    const rNeg = String(r?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return !targetNegNorm || !rNeg || rNeg === targetNegNorm;
  });

  const mergedById = new Map<string, any>();
  for (const r of [...(byIdRes.data || []), ...nullSidMatches]) {
    if (r?.id) mergedById.set(String(r.id), r);
  }

  const allMatched = Array.from(mergedById.values());
  const activeToPurge = allMatched.filter((r) =>
    ACTIVE_RESERVA_STATES.includes(String(r?.estado || '').toLowerCase().trim() as any)
  );

  const safeDeleteIds: string[] = [];
  let hasUnrefundedEscrow = false;

  for (const res of activeToPurge) {
    const memPlusTop =
      Math.max(0, Number(res?.escrow_membresia) || 0) +
      Math.max(0, Number(res?.escrow_topup) || 0);
    const resEscrow =
      memPlusTop > 0
        ? memPlusTop
        : Math.max(0, Number(res?.monto_escrow ?? costoRetencionDefault) || 0);
    const outcome = await claimRefundAndDeleteOrphanRow(
      res,
      String(res?.usuario_id || ''),
      costoRetencionDefault
    );
    const resIdStr = String(res?.id || '').trim();
    if (resEscrow <= 0 || outcome.refunded) {
      if (isValidUuid(resIdStr)) safeDeleteIds.push(resIdStr);
    } else {
      hasUnrefundedEscrow = true;
    }
  }

  if (hasUnrefundedEscrow) {
    throw new Error(
      'BLOQUEO_ESCROW_ACTIVO: Existen reservas con anticipo activo que requieren cancelación desde Admin Maestro.'
    );
  }

  if (safeDeleteIds.length > 0) {
    await supabase.from('reservas').delete().in('id', safeDeleteIds);
  }
  if (isValidUuid(cleanSid)) {
    await supabase
      .from('reservas')
      .delete()
      .eq('servicio_id', cleanSid)
      .lte('monto_escrow', 0)
      .in('estado', [...ACTIVE_RESERVA_STATES]);
  }
  if (nombreServicio && negocioNombre) {
    await Promise.allSettled([
      supabase
        .from('reservas')
        .delete()
        .is('servicio_id', null)
        .eq('negocio_nombre', negocioNombre)
        .lte('monto_escrow', 0)
        .in('estado', [...ACTIVE_RESERVA_STATES])
        .eq('servicio', nombreServicio),
      supabase
        .from('reservas')
        .delete()
        .is('servicio_id', null)
        .eq('negocio_nombre', negocioNombre)
        .lte('monto_escrow', 0)
        .in('estado', [...ACTIVE_RESERVA_STATES])
        .eq('nombre_servicio', nombreServicio),
    ]);
  }

  if (isValidUuid(cleanSid)) {
    const { error: unlinkErr } = await supabase
      .from('reservas')
      .update({ servicio_id: null })
      .eq('servicio_id', cleanSid);
    if (unlinkErr) {
      await supabase
        .from('reservas')
        .delete()
        .eq('servicio_id', cleanSid)
        .lte('monto_escrow', 0);
    }
  }

  if (activeToPurge.length > 0 && negocioNombre) {
    try {
      const { data: afRow } = await supabase
        .from('afiliados')
        .select('id, strikes_operativos')
        .ilike('nombre', negocioNombre)
        .maybeSingle();
      if (afRow?.id) {
        await supabase
          .from('afiliados')
          .update({ strikes_operativos: (Number(afRow.strikes_operativos) || 0) + 1 })
          .eq('id', afRow.id);
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  await purgePassesForDeletedService(cleanSid, nombreServicio);
  return { servData, affectedActiveCount: activeToPurge.length };
};

    FILE: src/services/eventosScheduleHelpers.ts

import {

export type EventosRecord = Record<string, unknown>;

const DELETED_ESTADOS_SET = new Set(['eliminado', 'eliminada', 'borrado', 'cancelado', 'cancelada']);

export const normalizeSimpleText = (raw?: unknown): string =>
  String(raw ?? '').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');

export const isKineticOfficialClubName = (rawName?: unknown): boolean =>
  normalizeSimpleText(rawName) === 'kinetic oficial';

export const toIsoLocal = (d: Date): string =>
  `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

export const getLocalTodayIso = (nowRef?: Date): string => getMazatlanNowParts(nowRef).isoDate;

export const getLocalCurrentHHMM = (nowRef?: Date): string => getMazatlanNowParts(nowRef).hhmm;

export const parseIsoLocal = (iso: string): Date => {
  const parts = String(iso || '').split('T')[0].trim().split('-').map(Number);
  if (parts.length === 3 && !parts.some((n) => Number.isNaN(n))) {
    return new Date(parts[0], parts[1] - 1, parts[2], 12, 0, 0, 0);
  }
  const mz = getMazatlanNowParts();
  return new Date(mz.year, mz.month - 1, mz.day, 12, 0, 0, 0);
};

export const toMondayFirstDayIndex = (jsDay: number): number =>
  ((((Number(jsDay) || 0) % 7) + 7) % 7 + 6) % 7;

export const addDaysToIso = (iso: string, days: number): string => {
  const clean = String(iso || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  const parts = clean.split('-').map(Number);
  const mz = getMazatlanNowParts();
  const baseY = parts.length === 3 && !parts.some((n) => Number.isNaN(n)) ? parts[0] : mz.year;
  const baseM = parts.length === 3 && !parts.some((n) => Number.isNaN(n)) ? parts[1] : mz.month;
  const baseD = parts.length === 3 && !parts.some((n) => Number.isNaN(n)) ? parts[2] : mz.day;
  const utcMidday = new Date(Date.UTC(baseY, baseM - 1, baseD + Math.trunc(Number(days) || 0), 12, 0, 0));
  return `${utcMidday.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(utcMidday.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(utcMidday.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
};

export const normalizeEventHHMM = (rawHora?: unknown, fallback = '23:59'): string => {
  const m = String(rawHora ?? '').trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\s*(a\.?m\.?|p\.?m\.?)?/i);
  if (!m) return fallback;
  let hh = parseInt(m[1], 10);
  const mm = Math.max(0, Math.min(59, parseInt(m[2], 10) || 0));
  const ampm = (m[3] || '').toLowerCase();
  if (ampm.startsWith('p') && hh < 12) hh += 12;
  if (ampm.startsWith('a') && hh === 12) hh = 0;
  if (Number.isNaN(hh)) return fallback;
  return `${String(Math.max(0, Math.min(23, hh))).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(mm).padStart(2, '0')}`;
};

export const getFreqFromDesc = (desc?: string): string =>
  String(desc || '').match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*(.*?)\]/i)?.[1]?.trim() || 'Única Vez';

export const resolveCanonicalRecurrence = (
  ev: EventosRecord | null | undefined
): 'Única Vez' | 'Diario' | 'Semanal' | 'Mensual' => {
  const attrs = ev?.atributos as EventosRecord | undefined;
  const raw = normalizeSimpleText(
    ev?.frecuencia || attrs?.frecuencia || getFreqFromDesc(typeof ev?.descripcion === 'string' ? ev.descripcion : undefined)
  );
  if (raw.includes('diario') || raw.includes('diaria') || raw.includes('daily')) return 'Diario';
  if (raw.includes('semanal') || raw.includes('weekly')) return 'Semanal';
  if (raw.includes('mensual') || raw.includes('monthly')) return 'Mensual';
  return 'Única Vez';
};

export const computeEventLocalLifecycle = (
  ev?: EventosRecord | null,
  clubInfo?: EventosRecord | null,
  nowRef?: Date
) => {
  const evAttrs = ev?.atributos as EventosRecord | undefined;
  const now = nowRef || new Date();
  const todayLocalIso = getLocalTodayIso(now);
  const currentLocalHHMM = getLocalCurrentHHMM(now);
  const rawClub = String(ev?.negocio_nombre || ev?.club || 'Kinetic Oficial').trim();
  const isOfficialKinetic = isKineticOfficialClubName(rawClub);
  const negocioNombre = isOfficialKinetic ? 'Kinetic Oficial' : rawClub || 'Kinetic Oficial';

  let esClubActivo = true;
  let clubEstado = 'activo';
  if (!isOfficialKinetic) {
    const rawClubEst = normalizeSimpleText(clubInfo?.estado ?? ev?.clubEstado ?? '');
    if (rawClubEst) {
      esClubActivo = rawClubEst === 'activo';
      clubEstado = rawClubEst;
    } else if (
      clubInfo?.esClubActivo === false ||
      clubInfo?.esActivo === false ||
      clubInfo?.activo === false ||
      ev?.esClubActivo === false ||
      ev?.isGhost === true
    ) {
      esClubActivo = false;
      clubEstado = 'inactivo';
    }
  }

  const rawServEstado = normalizeSimpleText(ev?.estado || 'aprobado');
  const tituloNorm = normalizeSimpleText(ev?.nombre_servicio || ev?.titulo || '');
  const isDeleted =
    Boolean(ev?.isMock || ev?.isDummy || ev?.es_demo || ev?.eliminado === true || evAttrs?.eliminado === true || evAttrs?.cancelado === true) ||
    DELETED_ESTADOS_SET.has(rawServEstado) ||
    tituloNorm.includes('borrado') ||
    tituloNorm.includes('eliminado');
  const isPausedOrInactiveFlag = ev?.activo === false || ev?.esActivo === false || evAttrs?.activo === false;
  const isServiceApproved =
    !isPausedOrInactiveFlag &&
    (rawServEstado === 'aprobado' || rawServEstado === 'activo' || rawServEstado === '');

  const frecuencia = resolveCanonicalRecurrence(ev);
  const rawDateIso = String(ev?.fecha_original || ev?.fechaInicioOriginal || ev?.fecha || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  const hasValidDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(rawDateIso);
  const endDateIso = String(ev?.fecha_fin || evAttrs?.fecha_fin || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  const hasEndDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(endDateIso);
  const rawHoraStr = String(ev?.hora_inicio || ev?.hora || '').trim();
  const hasExplicitHora = rawHoraStr.length > 0;
  const horaComparable = normalizeEventHHMM(rawHoraStr, '23:59');

  let fechaProyectada = hasValidDate ? rawDateIso : todayLocalIso;
  let isExpired = false;

  if (frecuencia === 'Única Vez') {
    if (!hasValidDate || rawDateIso < todayLocalIso || (rawDateIso === todayLocalIso && hasExplicitHora && horaComparable < currentLocalHHMM)) {
      isExpired = true;
    }
    if (hasEndDate && endDateIso < todayLocalIso) isExpired = true;
  } else {
    const baseIso = hasValidDate ? rawDateIso : todayLocalIso;
    if (baseIso > todayLocalIso || (baseIso === todayLocalIso && (!hasExplicitHora || horaComparable >= currentLocalHHMM))) {
      fechaProyectada = baseIso;
    } else if (frecuencia === 'Diario') {
      fechaProyectada = !hasExplicitHora || horaComparable >= currentLocalHHMM ? todayLocalIso : addDaysToIso(todayLocalIso, 1);
    } else if (frecuencia === 'Semanal') {
      let candIso = baseIso;
      for (let g = 0; g < 520 && (candIso < todayLocalIso || (candIso === todayLocalIso && hasExplicitHora && horaComparable < currentLocalHHMM)); g++) {
        candIso = addDaysToIso(candIso, 7);
      }
      fechaProyectada = candIso;
    } else {
      const baseD = parseIsoLocal(baseIso);
      let cand = baseD;
      for (let step = 1; step <= 240 && (toIsoLocal(cand) < todayLocalIso || (toIsoLocal(cand) === todayLocalIso && hasExplicitHora && horaComparable < currentLocalHHMM)); step++) {
        const y = baseD.getFullYear(), m0 = baseD.getMonth() + step;
        cand = new Date(y, m0, Math.min(baseD.getDate(), new Date(y, m0 + 1, 0).getDate()));
      }
      fechaProyectada = toIsoLocal(cand);
    }
    if (hasEndDate && fechaProyectada > endDateIso) isExpired = true;
  }

  const hhmmStr = normalizeEventHHMM(rawHoraStr, '08:00');
  const nextTimestamp = parseMazatlanTimestampMs(`${fechaProyectada}T${hhmmStr}:00-07:00`);
  const isInactive = isExpired || !esClubActivo || !isServiceApproved;

  return {
    isDeleted, isOfficialKinetic, negocioNombre, esClubActivo, clubEstado, isServiceApproved,
    frecuencia, fechaOriginal: hasValidDate ? rawDateIso : fechaProyectada, fechaProyectada,
    isExpired, isInactive, nextTimestamp,
  };
};

export const matchesDaySchedule = (
  ev: EventosRecord | null | undefined,
  dayIso: string,
  minIso: string,
  options?: { includeInactiveAdmin?: boolean; nowRef?: Date }
): boolean => {
  if (!dayIso || dayIso < minIso) return false;
  const includeAdmin = Boolean(options?.includeInactiveAdmin);
  const now = options?.nowRef || new Date();
  const todayIso = getLocalTodayIso(now);
  if (!includeAdmin && (ev?.isInactive || ev?.isExpired || ev?.esClubActivo === false)) return false;
  const evAttrs = ev?.atributos as EventosRecord | undefined;
  const endDateIso = String(ev?.fecha_fin || evAttrs?.fecha_fin || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(endDateIso) && dayIso > endDateIso) return false;
  const baseIso = String(ev?.fecha_original || ev?.fechaInicioOriginal || ev?.fecha || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(baseIso)) return false;
  if (!includeAdmin && dayIso === todayIso) {
    const rawHora = String(ev?.hora_inicio || ev?.hora || '').trim();
    if (rawHora && normalizeEventHHMM(rawHora, '23:59') < getLocalCurrentHHMM(now)) return false;
  }
  const freq = resolveCanonicalRecurrence(ev);
  if (freq === 'Única Vez') return baseIso === dayIso;
  if (dayIso < baseIso) return false;
  if (freq === 'Diario') return true;
  const sDate = parseIsoLocal(baseIso), tDate = parseIsoLocal(dayIso);
  if (freq === 'Semanal') return sDate.getDay() === tDate.getDay();
  if (freq === 'Mensual') return tDate.getDate() === Math.min(sDate.getDate(), new Date(tDate.getFullYear(), tDate.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate());
  return false;
};

export const buildAgendaWeekCells = (selectedIsoDate: string, processedEvents: EventosRecord[], todayIso: string, isAdmin: boolean) => {
  const c = parseIsoLocal(selectedIsoDate);
  const mon = new Date(c.getFullYear(), c.getMonth(), c.getDate() - toMondayFirstDayIndex(c.getDay()));
  return Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => {
    const d = new Date(mon.getFullYear(), mon.getMonth(), mon.getDate() + i);
    const iso = toIsoLocal(d);
    const isPast = iso < todayIso;
    const count = isPast ? 0 : processedEvents.filter((ev) => matchesDaySchedule(ev, iso, todayIso, { includeInactiveAdmin: isAdmin })).length;
    return { iso, dayNum: d.getDate(), count, isPast };
  });
};

export const buildAgendaMonthCells = (activeMonth: Date, processedEvents: EventosRecord[], todayIso: string, isAdmin: boolean) => {
  const y = activeMonth.getFullYear(), m = activeMonth.getMonth();
  const startW = toMondayFirstDayIndex(new Date(y, m, 1).getDay()), totalD = new Date(y, m + 1, 0).getDate();
  const arr: Array<{ key: string; iso?: string; dayNum?: number; count?: number; isPast?: boolean }> = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < startW; i++) arr.push({ key: `blank_${i}` });
  for (let day = 1; day <= totalD; day++) {
    const iso = toIsoLocal(new Date(y, m, day));
    const isPast = iso < todayIso;
    const count = isPast ? 0 : processedEvents.filter((ev) => matchesDaySchedule(ev, iso, todayIso, { includeInactiveAdmin: isAdmin })).length;
    arr.push({ key: `m_day_${day}`, iso, dayNum: day, count, isPast });
  }
  return arr;
};
