export interface EventosAuthUserLike {
  id?: string;
  email?: string | null;
  app_metadata?: { rol?: unknown; tipo_cuenta?: unknown };
}

export interface EventosProfileLike {
  email?: string | null;
  rol?: unknown;
  tipo_cuenta?: unknown;
  es_afiliado?: boolean | null;
}

export const KINETIC_OFFICIAL_LOGO = require('../../assets/logo.png');
export const KINETIC_OFFICIAL_DEFAULT_LOCATION = 'Los Cabos, B.C.S.';
export const FLASH_CAROUSEL_INTERVAL_MS = 3000;

export const isEventosAdminView = (
  user?: EventosAuthUserLike | null,
  profile?: EventosProfileLike | null,
  accessToken?: string | null
): boolean => {
  if (!user?.id || !isRealSupabaseJwt(accessToken)) return false;
  const emails = [user?.email, profile?.email].map((e) => normalizeRoleToken(e)).filter(Boolean);
  if (emails.some((em) => isMasterAdminEmailOnly(em))) return true;
  const primaryEmail = emails[0] || '';
  const roles = [profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.app_metadata?.rol]
    .map((r) => normalizeRoleToken(r))
    .filter(Boolean);
  return roles.some((r) => isKineticMasterAdminRole(r, primaryEmail));
};

export const isEventosB2BAffiliate = (
  user?: EventosAuthUserLike | null,
  profile?: EventosProfileLike | null,
  accessToken?: string | null
): boolean => {
  if (!user?.id || !isRealSupabaseJwt(accessToken)) return false;
  if (isEventosAdminView(user, profile, accessToken) || profile?.es_afiliado === true) return true;
  const roles = [profile?.rol, profile?.tipo_cuenta, user?.app_metadata?.rol]
    .map((r) => normalizeRoleToken(r))
    .filter(Boolean);
  return roles.some((r) => isKineticB2BRoleText(r));
};

export const buildEventosClubLookup = (dbAffiliates?: EventosRecord[]): Map<string, EventosRecord> => {
  const clubLookup = new Map<string, EventosRecord>();
  (KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || []).forEach((rawA) => {
    const a = rawA as unknown as EventosRecord;
    if (a?.nombre) clubLookup.set(normalizeSimpleText(a.nombre), a);
  });
  (dbAffiliates || []).forEach((dbA) => {
    if (dbA?.nombre) {
      const k = normalizeSimpleText(dbA.nombre);
      const hasEst = typeof dbA.estado === 'string' && dbA.estado.trim().length > 0;
      const act = hasEst ? normalizeSimpleText(dbA.estado) === 'activo' : undefined;
      clubLookup.set(k, { ...(clubLookup.get(k) || {}), ...dbA, ...(act !== undefined ? { activo: act, esClubActivo: act } : {}) });
    }
  });
  return clubLookup;
};

const computeAuditColsAvg = (src: EventosRecord | undefined): number => {
  if (!src) return 0;
  const cols = [src.rating_limpieza, src.rating_seguridad, src.rating_equipo, src.rating_atencion, src.rating_comunidad]
    .map((v) => Number(v))
    .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 && n <= 5);
  return cols.length > 0 ? Number((cols.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / cols.length).toFixed(1)) : 0;
};

export const mapServiceRowToEventosItem = (
  srv: EventosRecord, idx: number, clubLookup: Map<string, EventosRecord>, calcDistanceKmFn: (lat?: number, lon?: number) => number,
  resCountOverride?: number, nowRef?: Date
): MappedEventoRecord | null => {
  if (!srv || typeof srv !== 'object') return null;
  const cInfo = clubLookup.get(normalizeSimpleText(srv.negocio_nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial'));
  const life = computeEventLocalLifecycle(srv, cInfo, nowRef);
  if (life.isDeleted) return null;
  const useClubGps = Boolean(cInfo) && !life.isOfficialKinetic && srv?.es_sede_externa !== true;
  const canonVenue = useClubGps ? resolveCanonicalVenueCoordinates(cInfo, undefined, idx) : null;
  const lat = canonVenue ? canonVenue.latitud : Number(srv.latitud ?? cInfo?.latitud ?? cInfo?.latitude ?? CABO_BASE_COORDS.latitude + (idx % 5) * 0.004);
  const lon = canonVenue ? canonVenue.longitud : Number(srv.longitud ?? cInfo?.longitud ?? cInfo?.longitude ?? CABO_BASE_COORDS.longitude + (idx % 4) * 0.004);
  const distNum = canonVenue ? computeCanonicalVenueDistanceKm(lat, lon) : calcDistanceKmFn(lat, lon);
  const cupoMax = Math.max(1, Number(srv.cupo_maximo) || 12);
  const baseBooked = Number(srv.cupo_actual ?? srv.cupos_ocupados) || 0;
  const totalBooked = resCountOverride !== undefined ? Math.max(baseBooked, resCountOverride) : baseBooked;
  const srvAudit = computeAuditColsAvg(srv);
  const clubAudit = computeAuditColsAvg(cInfo);
  const explicitSrvRating = Number(srv.rating);
  const explicitClubRating = Number(cInfo?.rating);
  const ratingNum =
    srvAudit > 0
      ? srvAudit
      : Number.isFinite(explicitSrvRating) && explicitSrvRating > 0 && explicitSrvRating !== Number(srv.estrellas_exclusividad)
        ? explicitSrvRating
        : clubAudit > 0
          ? clubAudit
          : Number.isFinite(explicitClubRating) && explicitClubRating > 0 && explicitClubRating !== Number(cInfo?.estrellas_exclusividad)
            ? explicitClubRating
            : 5.0;
  const isSponsored = life.isOfficialKinetic;
  const isOfficialPremium = life.isOfficialKinetic;
  const nowMs = (nowRef || new Date()).getTime();
  const diffFlashMs = life.nextTimestamp - nowMs;
  const isUrgent3h = diffFlashMs > 0 && diffFlashMs <= 180 * 60_000;
  const isFlashSale = Boolean(srv?.es_venta_flash || srv?.isFlashSale || isUrgent3h);
  const resolvedUbicacion = String(srv.ubicacion || cInfo?.ubicacion || '').trim() || (life.isOfficialKinetic ? KINETIC_OFFICIAL_DEFAULT_LOCATION : life.negocioNombre);

  return {
    ...srv,
    id: srv.id !== undefined ? String(srv.id) : undefined,
    nombre_servicio: typeof srv.nombre_servicio === 'string' ? srv.nombre_servicio : undefined,
    titulo: typeof srv.titulo === 'string' ? srv.titulo : undefined,
    categoria: typeof srv.categoria === 'string' ? srv.categoria : undefined,
    hora_inicio: typeof srv.hora_inicio === 'string' ? srv.hora_inicio : undefined,
    negocio_nombre: life.negocioNombre, esKineticOficial: life.isOfficialKinetic,
    isPremium: isOfficialPremium, es_venta_flash: isFlashSale, isFlashSale, isFlashUrgent3h: isUrgent3h,
    esClubActivo: life.esClubActivo, clubEstado: life.clubEstado,
    officialLogoAsset: life.isOfficialKinetic ? KINETIC_OFFICIAL_LOGO : undefined,
    ubicacion: resolvedUbicacion, lugar: resolvedUbicacion, frecuencia: life.frecuencia,
    fecha_original: life.fechaOriginal, fechaInicioOriginal: life.fechaOriginal,
    fecha_proyectada: life.fechaProyectada, fecha: life.fechaProyectada,
    isExpired: life.isExpired, isArchived: Boolean(life.isExpired && life.frecuencia === 'Única Vez'),
    isInactive: life.isInactive, nextTimestamp: life.nextTimestamp,
    reservadosCount: totalBooked, cupo_actual: totalBooked, cupo_maximo: cupoMax,
    ocupacionRatio: totalBooked / cupoMax, rating: ratingNum, isSponsored,
    recommendationScore: (isSponsored ? 50 : 0) + ratingNum * 12 + totalBooked * 15 - distNum * 0.5,
    avatar_club: srv.avatar_club || srv.avatar_url || cInfo?.avatar_url || null,
    distNum, distanciaKm: `${distNum}\u00A0km`,
  };
};

export const mapSupabaseEventosPayload = (
  rawServices: EventosRecord[], rawAffiliates: EventosRecord[], activeBookings: EventosRecord[], isAdmin: boolean,
  calcDistanceKmFn: (lat?: number, lon?: number) => number, nowRef?: Date
): MappedEventoRecord[] => {
  const clubLookup = buildEventosClubLookup(rawAffiliates);
  const gpsHydratedServices = hydrateServicesGpsWithClubCoords(rawServices || [], rawAffiliates || []);
  const countById = new Map<string, number>();
  const countByName = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const r of activeBookings || []) {
    const sid = String(r?.servicio_id || '').trim();
    if (sid) countById.set(sid, (countById.get(sid) || 0) + 1);
    const sname = String(r?.servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (sname) countByName.set(sname, (countByName.get(sname) || 0) + 1);
  }
  const now = nowRef || new Date();
  return gpsHydratedServices
    .map((srv, idx) => {
      const sid = String(srv?.id || '').trim();
      const sname = String(srv?.nombre_servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
      const resCount = Math.max(sid ? countById.get(sid) || 0 : 0, sname ? countByName.get(sname) || 0 : 0);
      return mapServiceRowToEventosItem(srv, idx, clubLookup, calcDistanceKmFn, resCount, now);
    })
    .filter((it): it is MappedEventoRecord => it !== null && (isAdmin || !it.isInactive));
};

export const filterAndSortCatalogEvents = (params: {
  catalog: MappedEventoRecord[]; isAdminView: boolean; selectedCategory: string; searchQuery: string;
  quickChip: 'todas' | 'wellness' | 'favoritos' | 'mas_buscados'; sortBy: string;
  favoriteIds: string[]; tDisc: (c: string) => string; normTxt: (s: string) => string;
}): MappedEventoRecord[] => {
  const { catalog, isAdminView, selectedCategory, searchQuery, quickChip, sortBy, favoriteIds, tDisc, normTxt } = params;
  const now = new Date();
  const q = normTxt(searchQuery.trim());
  const refreshed = catalog
    .map((ev): MappedEventoRecord | null => {
      const life = computeEventLocalLifecycle(ev, undefined, now);
      return life.isDeleted ? null : { ...ev, esClubActivo: life.esClubActivo, clubEstado: life.clubEstado, fecha_proyectada: life.fechaProyectada, fecha: life.fechaProyectada, isExpired: life.isExpired, isInactive: life.isInactive };
    })
    .filter((it): it is MappedEventoRecord => it !== null);
  const visible = isAdminView ? refreshed : refreshed.filter((ev) => !ev.isInactive);
  const maxBooked = Math.max(0, ...visible.map((c) => Number(c.reservadosCount) || 0));
  const filtered = visible.filter((ev) => {
    if (selectedCategory !== 'Todas' && ev.categoria !== selectedCategory) return false;
    if (q) {
      const blob = normTxt(`${ev.nombre_servicio || ''} ${ev.titulo || ''} ${ev.categoria || ''} ${tDisc(String(ev.categoria || ''))} ${ev.negocio_nombre || ''} ${ev.instructor_nombre || ''} ${ev.ubicacion || ''} ${ev.descripcion || ''}`);
      if (!blob.includes(q)) return false;
    }
    const canonKey = getCanonicalFixedEventKey(ev);
    const isFav =
      favoriteIds.includes(String(ev.id)) ||
      favoriteIds.includes(String(ev.nombre_servicio || ev.titulo || '')) ||
      (Boolean(canonKey) && favoriteIds.includes(canonKey));
    if (quickChip === 'wellness' && !isWellnessCategory(String(ev.categoria || ''))) return false;
    if (quickChip === 'favoritos' && !isFav) return false;
    if (quickChip === 'mas_buscados') return maxBooked > 0 ? (Number(ev.reservadosCount) || 0) >= Math.max(1, maxBooked * 0.5) : TRENDING_CATEGORIES.includes(String(ev.categoria || ''));
    return true;
  });
  return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => {
    if (isAdminView && Boolean(a.isInactive) !== Boolean(b.isInactive)) return a.isInactive ? 1 : -1;
    if (sortBy === 'distancia') return (Number(a.distNum) || 1.8) - (Number(b.distNum) || 1.8);
    if (sortBy === 'rating_desc') return (Number(b.rating) || 5) - (Number(a.rating) || 5);
    if (sortBy === 'rating_asc') return (Number(a.rating) || 5) - (Number(b.rating) || 5);
    if (sortBy === 'horario') return String(a.hora_inicio || '').localeCompare(String(b.hora_inicio || ''));
    if (sortBy === 'demanda') return (Number(b.reservadosCount) || 0) - (Number(a.reservadosCount) || 0);
    if (sortBy === 'precio_asc') return (Number(a.precio_kc) || 0) - (Number(b.precio_kc) || 0);
    if (sortBy === 'precio_desc') return (Number(b.precio_kc) || 0) - (Number(a.precio_kc) || 0);
    return (Number(b.recommendationScore) || 0) - (Number(a.recommendationScore) || 0) || (Number(b.reservadosCount) || 0) - (Number(a.reservadosCount) || 0);
  });
};

export const buildRecommendedActiveEvents = (processedEvents: MappedEventoRecord[]): MappedEventoRecord[] => {
  const now = new Date();
  return processedEvents
    .filter((ev) => {
      if (ev?.isInactive || ev?.isExpired || ev?.esClubActivo === false) return false;
      const life = computeEventLocalLifecycle(ev, undefined, now);
      if (life.isInactive || life.isDeleted) return false;
      const isFlash = Boolean(ev?.es_venta_flash || ev?.isFlashSale);
      return isFlash || life.isOfficialKinetic;
    })
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const aF = Boolean(a.es_venta_flash || a.isFlashSale) ? 1 : 0, bF = Boolean(b.es_venta_flash || b.isFlashSale) ? 1 : 0;
      const aOff = isKineticOfficialClubName(a.negocio_nombre) ? 1 : 0, bOff = isKineticOfficialClubName(b.negocio_nombre) ? 1 : 0;
      return bF - aF || bOff - aOff || (Number(b.recommendationScore) || 0) - (Number(a.recommendationScore) || 0) || (Number(b.rating) || 5) - (Number(a.rating) || 5);
    })
    .slice(0, 8);
};

export const buildAgendaDaySessions = (
  processedEvents: MappedEventoRecord[], selectedIsoDate: string, todayIso: string, searchQuery: string, isAdmin: boolean,
  projectDateFn: (ev: MappedEventoRecord, tIso: string) => string
): MappedEventoRecord[] => {
  const now = new Date();
  const curHHMM = getLocalCurrentHHMM(now);
  if (searchQuery.trim().length > 0) {
    return processedEvents
      .map((ev): MappedEventoRecord => { const proj = projectDateFn(ev, todayIso); return { ...ev, fecha: proj, fecha_proyectada: proj, fecha_seleccionada: proj }; })
      .sort((a, b) => String(a.fecha_proyectada || '').localeCompare(String(b.fecha_proyectada || '')) || String(a.hora_inicio || '').localeCompare(String(b.hora_inicio || '')));
  }
  return processedEvents
    .filter((ev) => matchesDaySchedule(ev, selectedIsoDate, todayIso, { includeInactiveAdmin: isAdmin, nowRef: now }))
    .map((ev): MappedEventoRecord => {
      const rawHora = String(ev?.hora_inicio || ev?.hora || '').trim();
      const expToday = selectedIsoDate === todayIso && Boolean(rawHora) && normalizeEventHHMM(rawHora, '23:59') < curHHMM;
      return { ...ev, fecha_proyectada: selectedIsoDate, fecha_seleccionada: selectedIsoDate, isInactive: Boolean(ev.isInactive || expToday) };
    });
};

export const matchDeepLinkEventInCatalog = <T extends EventosRecord>(
  catalog: T[],
  target: string,
  normTxtFn: (s: string) => string
): T | undefined => {
  const targetLow = target.toLowerCase();
  const targetSlug = normTxtFn(target).replace(/\s+/g, '-');
  return catalog.find((ev) => {
    if (String(ev?.id || '').trim() === target) return true;
    const evTitle = String(ev?.nombre_servicio || ev?.titulo || '');
    const evSlug = normTxtFn(evTitle).replace(/\s+/g, '-');
    return evTitle.trim().toLowerCase() === targetLow || Boolean(evSlug && evSlug === targetSlug);
  });
};

export async function fetchEventosLiveSupabasePayload(isAdmin: boolean) {
  const servQuery = supabase.from('servicios').select(SERVICIOS_RADAR_COLS).order('fecha', { ascending: true }).limit(150);
  const finalServQuery = isAdmin ? servQuery : servQuery.in('estado', ['aprobado', 'activo']);
  return Promise.all([
    finalServQuery,
    supabase.from('afiliados').select(AFILIADOS_RADAR_COLS).limit(120),
    supabase.from('reservas').select('id, servicio_id, servicio, estado, fecha_clase').in('estado', ['activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada']).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(200),
  ]);
}

export const resolveEventosInitialCache = (params: {
  isAdminMode: boolean;
  cachedCatalog: MappedEventoRecord[] | null;
  cachedMode: 'admin' | 'public' | null;
  swrPayload?: { servData?: EventosRecord[]; afData?: EventosRecord[]; resData?: EventosRecord[] } | null;
  exploreServicios?: EventosRecord[] | null;
  exploreAffiliates?: EventosRecord[] | null;
  calcDistFn: (lat?: number, lon?: number) => number;
  mapPayloadFn: (
    servicios: EventosRecord[],
    afiliados: EventosRecord[],
    reservas: EventosRecord[],
    isAdmin: boolean,
    calcDist: (lat?: number, lon?: number) => number,
    nowRef?: Date
  ) => MappedEventoRecord[];
  mapSingleFn: (
    srv: EventosRecord,
    idx: number,
    clubLookup: Map<string, EventosRecord>,
    calcDist: (lat?: number, lon?: number) => number,
    resCountOverride?: number,
    nowRef?: Date
  ) => MappedEventoRecord | null;
}): MappedEventoRecord[] => {
  const modeKey: 'admin' | 'public' = params.isAdminMode ? 'admin' : 'public';
  if (params.cachedCatalog !== null && params.cachedMode === modeKey) {
    return params.cachedCatalog;
  }
  const now = new Date();
  if (params.swrPayload?.servData?.length) {
    return params.mapPayloadFn(
      params.swrPayload.servData,
      params.swrPayload.afData || [],
      params.swrPayload.resData || [],
      params.isAdminMode,
      params.calcDistFn,
      now
    );
  }
  const rawServ = params.exploreServicios;
  if (!rawServ?.length) return [];
  const clubLookup = buildEventosClubLookup(params.exploreAffiliates || []);
  return rawServ
    .map((srv, idx) => params.mapSingleFn(srv, idx, clubLookup, params.calcDistFn, undefined, now))
    .filter((it): it is MappedEventoRecord => Boolean(it) && (params.isAdminMode || !it?.isInactive));
};

import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { readMediaUriAsUint8Array, validateSafeStorageImagePayload } from '../utils/kineticBinaryMediaHelper';
import { extractSyncEventGpsCoordinates } from '../utils/eventModalActions';
import { cascadeRefundAndDeleteServiceReservas } from './eventosHelpers';

export type EventServiceRecord = Record<string, unknown> & {
  id?: string | number | null;
  usuario_id?: string | null;
  precio_kc?: number | string | null;
  fecha?: string | null;
  hora_inicio?: string | null;
  duracion_minutos?: number | string | null;
  cupo_maximo?: number | string | null;
  cupo_actual?: number | string | null;
  negocio_nombre?: string | null;
  ubicacion?: string | null;
  latitud?: number | string | null;
  longitud?: number | string | null;
  es_sede_externa?: boolean;
  es_venta_flash?: boolean;
  atributos?: { ubicacion?: string | null; latitud?: number | null; longitud?: number | null; [k: string]: unknown } | null;
};

export const sanitizeEventGeoPayload = (payload: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined) => {
  if (!payload || typeof payload !== 'object') return payload;
  const clean: Record<string, unknown> & {
    coords?: { latitude?: unknown; longitude?: unknown };
    latitud?: unknown;
    longitud?: unknown;
    ubicacion?: unknown;
    cupo_actual?: unknown;
  } = { ...payload };
  if (clean.coords) {
    if (clean.coords.latitude !== undefined) clean.latitud = parseFloat(String(clean.coords.latitude));
    if (clean.coords.longitude !== undefined) clean.longitud = parseFloat(String(clean.coords.longitude));
    delete clean.coords;
  }
  if (clean.ubicacion !== undefined || clean.latitud !== undefined || clean.longitud !== undefined) {
    const gps = extractSyncEventGpsCoordinates(
      typeof clean.ubicacion === 'string' ? clean.ubicacion : undefined,
      clean.latitud as number | string | null | undefined,
      clean.longitud as number | string | null | undefined
    );
    clean.latitud = gps.latitud;
    clean.longitud = gps.longitud;
  }
  delete clean.cupo_actual;
  return clean;
};

export async function eliminarEventoConReembolsoCore(idAEliminar: string): Promise<boolean> {
  await cascadeRefundAndDeleteServiceReservas(idAEliminar, true);
  const { data: deletedService, error: delServError } = await supabase
    .from('servicios')
    .delete()
    .eq('id', idAEliminar)
    .select();
  if (delServError) throw new Error(`Falla en Supabase: ${delServError.message}`);
  if (!deletedService || deletedService.length === 0) throw new Error('BLOQUEO RLS: Supabase denegó el borrado.');
  return true;
}

export async function uploadEventImageCore(uri: string, path: string): Promise<string> {
  const cleanUri = String(uri || '').trim();
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(cleanUri)) return cleanUri;
  const bytes = await readMediaUriAsUint8Array(cleanUri);
  const check = validateSafeStorageImagePayload({ bytes, uriOrName: cleanUri });
  if (!check.ok || !bytes) {
    throw new Error(
      check.reason === 'file_too_large'
        ? 'La imagen supera el peso máximo permitido (8 MB).'
        : 'Formato de imagen inválido o inseguro. Usa JPG, PNG, WEBP o HEIC.'
    );
  }
  const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
    .from('imagenes')
    .upload(path, bytes, { contentType: check.contentType || 'image/jpeg', cacheControl: '604800', upsert: true });
  if (uploadError) throw new Error(uploadError.message);
  const { data } = supabase.storage.from('imagenes').getPublicUrl(path);
  return data.publicUrl;
}

export interface IProcesarReservaOptions {

export type { EventServiceRecord };

const EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_events_snapshot_v2';
let _memoryEventsSnapshot: {
  servData: EventServiceRecord[];
  resData: EventServiceRecord[];
  perfData: EventServiceRecord[];
  afData: EventServiceRecord[];
} | null = null;

registerGhostPurgeHook('events', (cleanId) => {
  if (_memoryEventsSnapshot) {
    _memoryEventsSnapshot = { ..._memoryEventsSnapshot, servData: filterOutServiceId(_memoryEventsSnapshot.servData, cleanId) };
  }
});

export const eventsService = {
  // 1. CARGA GLOBAL EN PARALELO CON PROYECCIÓN ANTI-EGRESS Y RESILIENCIA HTTP 402
  async fetchGlobalEvents() {
    const readFallback = async () => {
      const mem = _memoryEventsSnapshot;
      if (mem) {
        return {
          ...mem,
          servData: (mem.servData || []).filter((s) => !isPurgedGhostServiceId(s?.id)),
        };
      }
      try {
        const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (raw) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as {
            servData?: EventServiceRecord[];
            resData?: EventServiceRecord[];
            perfData?: EventServiceRecord[];
            afData?: EventServiceRecord[];
          };
          if (parsed && Array.isArray(parsed.servData)) {
            const hydrated = {
              servData: parsed.servData.filter((s) => !isPurgedGhostServiceId(s?.id)),
              resData: Array.isArray(parsed.resData) ? parsed.resData : [],
              perfData: Array.isArray(parsed.perfData) ? parsed.perfData : [],
              afData: Array.isArray(parsed.afData) ? parsed.afData : [],
            };
            _memoryEventsSnapshot = hydrated;
            return hydrated;
          }
        }
      } catch {}
      return { servData: [], resData: [], perfData: [], afData: [] };
    };

    if (isSupabaseQuotaExceeded()) {
      return readFallback();
    }

    try {
      type QueryOut = { data: EventServiceRecord[] | null; error: { message?: string } | null };
      const [sRes, resReq, afReq] = await Promise.all([
        supabase
          .from('servicios')
          .select(SERVICIOS_RADAR_COLS)
          .order('fecha', { ascending: true })
          .limit(150),
        supabase
          .from('reservas')
          .select('servicio_id, usuario_id, estado, fecha_clase')
          .not('estado', 'in', '("cancelada","no_show")')
          .limit(250),
        supabase
          .from('afiliados')
          .select(
            'id, nombre, avatar_url, portada_url, categoria, ubicacion, latitud, longitud, estado, rating_limpieza, rating_seguridad, rating_equipo, rating_atencion, rating_comunidad'
          )
          .limit(120),
      ]);
      let servReq: QueryOut = {
        data: (sRes.data as unknown as EventServiceRecord[] | null) ?? null,
        error: sRes.error,
      };
      if (servReq.error && !isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(servReq.error)) {
        const legRes = await supabase
          .from('servicios')
          .select(SERVICIOS_RADAR_COLS_LEGACY)
          .order('fecha', { ascending: true })
          .limit(150);
        servReq = {
          data: (legRes.data as unknown as EventServiceRecord[] | null) ?? null,
          error: legRes.error,
        };
      }

      const anyErr = servReq.error || resReq.error || afReq.error;
      if (anyErr) {
        if (isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(anyErr)) {
          setSupabaseQuotaExceeded(true);
          return readFallback();
        }
        if (_memoryEventsSnapshot) return readFallback();
        throw new Error(`Error cargando cartelera: ${anyErr.message}`);
      }

      const resData = (resReq.data || []) as EventServiceRecord[];
      const uniqueUserIds = Array.from(
        new Set(resData.map((r) => r.usuario_id).filter(Boolean))
      ).slice(0, 80) as string[];

      let perfData: EventServiceRecord[] = [];
      if (uniqueUserIds.length > 0) {
        const { data: pData, error: perfError } = await supabase
          .from('perfiles')
          .select('id, nombre, avatar_url')
          .in('id', uniqueUserIds);

        if (perfError && isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(perfError)) {
          setSupabaseQuotaExceeded(true);
        } else {
          perfData = (pData || []) as EventServiceRecord[];
        }
      }

      const afList = (afReq.data || []) as EventServiceRecord[];
      const afByName = new Map<string, EventServiceRecord>();
      const afById = new Map<string, EventServiceRecord>();
      for (const a of afList) {
        if (a?.id != null) afById.set(String(a.id).trim(), a);
        const rawNm = String(a?.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (rawNm) afByName.set(rawNm, a);
        const canonNm = canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(a?.nombre);
        if (canonNm) afByName.set(canonNm, a);
      }

      const reconciledRawServices = reconcileActiveLiveServicesSnapshot(servReq.data || [], {
        allowAdminDrafts: true,
      }) as EventServiceRecord[];
      const snapshot = {
        servData: reconciledRawServices.map((srv) => {
          const sid = String(srv?.afiliado_id || srv?.club_id || '').trim();
          const af =
            (sid ? afById.get(sid) : null) ||
            afByName.get(canonicalizeAffiliateLookupKey(srv?.negocio_nombre)) ||
            afByName.get(String(srv?.negocio_nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase());
          if (af && srv?.es_sede_externa !== true) {
            const canonGps = resolveCanonicalVenueCoordinates(af);
            return {
              ...srv,
              latitud: canonGps.latitud,
              longitud: canonGps.longitud,
              latitude: canonGps.latitud,
              longitude: canonGps.longitud,
            };
          }
          const gps = extractSyncEventGpsCoordinates(
            `${srv?.ubicacion || ''} ${srv?.atributos?.ubicacion || ''} ${af?.ubicacion || ''}`.trim(),
            srv?.latitud ?? srv?.atributos?.latitud ?? af?.latitud,
            srv?.longitud ?? srv?.atributos?.longitud ?? af?.longitud,
            srv?.negocio_nombre ?? undefined
          );
          return { ...srv, latitud: gps.latitud, longitud: gps.longitud };
        }),
        resData,
        perfData,
        afData: afList,
      };
      _memoryEventsSnapshot = snapshot;
      AsyncStorage.setItem(EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(snapshot)).catch(() => {});
      return snapshot;
    } catch (err) {
      if (isSupabaseQuotaOr402Error(err)) {
        setSupabaseQuotaExceeded(true);
        return readFallback();
      }
      if (_memoryEventsSnapshot) return readFallback();
      throw err;
    }
  },

  // 2. RESERVA ATÓMICA VÍA RPC + ANTI-COLISIÓN DE SPOTS 3D + CERO DESFASE UTC + PASES 0 KC
  async procesarReserva(
    p_usuario_id: string,
    p_negocio_nombre: string,
    p_servicio_nombre: string,
    p_precio_kc: number | string,
    p_servicio_id: string,
    p_fecha_or_opts?: string | IProcesarReservaOptions | null,
    p_spot_optional?: number | null
  ) {
    const jwtCheck = await reconcileRealSupabaseJwtSession(supabase, 3500);
    const authUid = String(jwtCheck.user?.id || '').trim();
    const isVerifiedAdmin = Boolean(jwtCheck.user?.email_confirmed_at && isKineticMasterAdminEmail(jwtCheck.user?.email));
    if (jwtCheck.status !== 'valid' || !authUid || (authUid !== String(p_usuario_id || '').trim() && !isVerifiedAdmin)) {
      throw new Error('No autorizado para reservar a nombre de otro usuario.');
    }
    const targetUserId = isVerifiedAdmin ? p_usuario_id : authUid;
    const serviceUuidOk = isValidUuid(p_servicio_id);
    if (!serviceUuidOk && !isKineticDevRuntime()) {
      throw new Error('Identificador de experiencia inválido.');
    }

    const optsObj = typeof p_fecha_or_opts === 'object' && p_fecha_or_opts !== null ? p_fecha_or_opts : null;
    let rawDateInput =
      typeof p_fecha_or_opts === 'string'
        ? p_fecha_or_opts
        : optsObj?.fechaClase || optsObj?.selectedDate || null;
    let maxCupo = optsObj?.cupoMaximo;
    let horaInicio = optsObj?.horaInicio || null;
    let dbPrecioKc: number | null = null;
    let dbSrvForFlash: EventServiceRecord | null = null;
    let perfVipRow: { nivel?: string | null; kc_membresia?: number | null } | null = null;

    if (serviceUuidOk) {
      const [{ data: srv, error: srvErr }, perfLookup] = await Promise.all([
        supabase
          .from('servicios')
          .select('id, estado, activo, fecha, hora_inicio, frecuencia, descripcion, cupo_maximo, precio_kc, es_venta_flash, atributos')
          .eq('id', p_servicio_id)
          .maybeSingle(),
        isValidUuid(targetUserId)
          ? supabase.from('perfiles').select('nivel, kc_membresia').eq('id', targetUserId).maybeSingle()
          : Promise.resolve({ data: null }),
      ]);
      if (srvErr) {
        throw new Error(srvErr.message || 'No se pudo consultar la sesión en la nube.');
      }
      if (!srv || isConfirmedRetiredServiceRecord(srv)) {
        purgeGhostServiceEverywhere(p_servicio_id);
        throw new Error('La experiencia seleccionada ya no existe.');
      }
      if (!rawDateInput && srv.fecha) {
        rawDateInput = projectEventDateForSearch(srv, getTodayLocalIsoDate());
      }
      if (!maxCupo && srv.cupo_maximo) maxCupo = Number(srv.cupo_maximo);
      if (!horaInicio && srv.hora_inicio) horaInicio = String(srv.hora_inicio);
      dbPrecioKc = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(srv.precio_kc) || 0));
      dbSrvForFlash = srv as unknown as EventServiceRecord;
      perfVipRow = (perfLookup?.data as { nivel?: string | null; kc_membresia?: number | null } | null) || null;
    }

    const exactLocalDate = normalizeLocalIsoDate(rawDateInput);
    const spotNum =
      p_spot_optional ??
      optsObj?.spotNumber ??
      optsObj?.spot ??
      resolveClientPickedSpot(p_servicio_id, p_servicio_nombre);
    const clientPrecioKc = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(p_precio_kc) || 0));
    if (dbPrecioKc !== null && dbPrecioKc > 0) {
      assertValidKcMultipleOfTen(dbPrecioKc, 'ReservaEscrow');
    } else if (dbPrecioKc === null && clientPrecioKc > 0 && !dbSrvForFlash) {
      assertValidKcMultipleOfTen(clientPrecioKc, 'ReservaEscrow');
    }
    const flashRes = dbSrvForFlash
      ? resolveFlashVipDiscountPricing({
          event: { ...dbSrvForFlash, fecha: exactLocalDate || dbSrvForFlash.fecha, hora_inicio: horaInicio || dbSrvForFlash.hora_inicio },
          vipLevel: perfVipRow?.nivel,
          kcMembresia: perfVipRow?.kc_membresia,
          isAdmin: isVerifiedAdmin,
        })
      : null;
    const precioTotal = dbPrecioKc !== null
      ? (isVerifiedAdmin && clientPrecioKc === 0 ? 0 : (flashRes ? flashRes.discountedTotalKc : dbPrecioKc))
      : clientPrecioKc;

    // [V7-02] Verificación en vivo de estación/spot antes de cualquier cargo
    await assertLiveSpotAvailableOrThrow({
      serviceId: p_servicio_id,
      eventKey: p_servicio_id,
      eventTitle: p_servicio_nombre,
      fechaClase: exactLocalDate,
      spotNumber: spotNum,
      userId: targetUserId,
      maxSpots: maxCupo,
      lang: optsObj?.lang,
    });

    const splitCount = Math.min(4, Math.max(1, Number(optsObj?.splitCount) || 1));
    const isMasterPass = Boolean(
      isVerifiedAdmin && (optsObj?.isMasterPass || clientPrecioKc === 0)
    );

    // [V12-02] Si el servicio no es UUID de DB (mock/sandbox local) y es 0 KC, usar inserción local directa
    if (!serviceUuidOk && (precioTotal <= 0 || isMasterPass)) {
      return insertZeroKcReservaDirect({
        userId: targetUserId,
        serviceId: p_servicio_id,
        negocioNombre: p_negocio_nombre || 'Kinetic Oficial',
        servicioNombre: p_servicio_nombre || 'Sesión Kinetic',
        exactLocalDate,
        horaInicio,
        spotNumber: spotNum,
        cupoMaximo: maxCupo,
        lang: optsObj?.lang,
      });
    }

    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('procesar_reserva_escrow', {
      p_usuario_id: targetUserId,
      p_negocio_nombre,
      p_servicio_nombre,
      p_precio_kc: isMasterPass ? 0 : precioTotal,
      p_servicio_id: serviceUuidOk ? p_servicio_id : null,
      p_fecha_clase: exactLocalDate,
      p_spot_number: spotNum ?? null,
      p_split_count: splitCount,
      p_es_pase_maestro: isMasterPass,
    });

    if (error) {
      await verifyAndPurgeConfirmedGhostService(supabase, p_servicio_id);
      const cleanMsg = error.message.includes('|')
        ? error.message.split('|')[1].trim()
        : error.message;
      throw new Error(cleanMsg);
    }

    const baseResult = Array.isArray(data) ? data[0] : data;
    const confirmedResId = String(baseResult?.id || baseResult?.reserva_id || '').trim();
    if (confirmedResId) {
      rememberCreatedReservaId(p_servicio_id, exactLocalDate, confirmedResId);
    }
    clearSpotLockAndIntent(p_servicio_id, p_servicio_nombre);
    void registerTemporarySpotLock({
      serviceId: serviceUuidOk ? p_servicio_id : null,
      eventKey: p_servicio_id || p_servicio_nombre,
      fechaClase: exactLocalDate,
      spotNumber: null,
      userId: targetUserId,
    });
    notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed();
    return {
      ...(baseResult && typeof baseResult === 'object' ? baseResult : {}),
      reserva_id: baseResult?.id || baseResult?.reserva_id,
      fecha_clase: baseResult?.fecha_clase || exactLocalDate,
      fecha: baseResult?.fecha_clase || exactLocalDate,
      spot_number: baseResult?.spot_number ?? spotNum ?? null,
      monto_congelado: baseResult?.monto_escrow ?? Math.round((isMasterPass ? 0 : precioTotal) * 0.4),
    };
  },

  // 3. ACTUALIZACIÓN DE EXPERIENCIA SANITIZADA (CON SOPORTE DE GALERÍA)
  async updateServicio(id: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    const cleanPayload = sanitizeEventGeoPayload(payload) || {};
    const { error } = await supabase.from('servicios').update(cleanPayload).eq('id', id);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);
    return true;
  },

  // 4. REEMBOLSO MASIVO BLINDADO + BORRADO EN CASCADA DE PASES ACTIVOS Y +1 STRIKE B2B
  async eliminarEventoConReembolso(idAEliminar: string, _ignoradoCosto = 0) {
    await eliminarEventoConReembolsoCore(idAEliminar);
    _memoryEventsSnapshot = null;
    AsyncStorage.removeItem(EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY).catch(() => {});
    emitKineticRadarUpdated();
    return true;
  },

  async deleteEvent(idAEliminar: string) {
    return this.eliminarEventoConReembolso(idAEliminar, 0);
  },

  // 5. SUBIDA BINARIA UINT8ARRAY AL CDN
  async uploadImage(uri: string, path: string) {
    return uploadEventImageCore(uri, path);
  },
};

    FILE: src/services/kineticGhostEventsPurgeHelper.ts

    // === src/services/kineticGhostEventsPurgeHelper.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { emitKineticRadarUpdated } from '../utils/kineticEliteHelpers';
import { clearPendingSplitConfig } from './kineticSplitPreBookingStore';
import { clearSpotLockAndIntent } from './spotCollisionService';

const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
const POISON_KEYS = new Set(['__proto__', 'constructor', 'prototype', 'tostring', 'valueof']);
const INACTIVE_SERVICE_ESTADOS = new Set([
  'eliminado',
  'cancelado',
  'inactivo',
  'rechazado',
  'archivado',
  'borrado',
  'suspendido',
  'oculto',
]);
const DRAFT_ESTADOS = new Set(['pendiente', 'borrador', 'draft']);

export const EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_events_snapshot_v2';
export const RADAR_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_radar_snapshot_v2';

type GhostPurgeHook = (cleanId: string) => void;
const _purgeHooks = new Map<string, GhostPurgeHook>();
const _purgedGhostIds = new Set<string>();

export const registerGhostPurgeHook = (key: string, fn: GhostPurgeHook): (() => void) => {
  _purgeHooks.set(key, fn);
  return () => {
    _purgeHooks.delete(key);
  };
};

export const sanitizeGhostServiceId = (rawId: unknown): string | null => {
  if (typeof rawId !== 'string' && typeof rawId !== 'number') return null;
  const trimmed = String(rawId).trim();
  if (!trimmed || POISON_KEYS.has(trimmed.toLowerCase())) return null;
  if (!UUID_RE.test(trimmed)) return null;
  return trimmed;
};

export const isPurgedGhostServiceId = (rawId: unknown): boolean => {
  const id = sanitizeGhostServiceId(rawId);
  return Boolean(id && _purgedGhostIds.has(id.toLowerCase()));
};

export const clearPurgedGhostIdsForTests = (): void => {
  _purgedGhostIds.clear();
};

export const unpurgeActiveServicesFromCache = (liveRows: unknown): void => {
  if (!Array.isArray(liveRows)) return;
  for (const raw of liveRows) {
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object' || Array.isArray(raw)) continue;
    const rec = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
    const cleanId = sanitizeGhostServiceId(rec.id);
    if (!cleanId) continue;
    if (rec.activo === false || rec.eliminado === true) continue;
    const attrs = rec.atributos && typeof rec.atributos === 'object' ? (rec.atributos as Record<string, unknown>) : null;
    if (attrs && (attrs.eliminado === true || attrs.cancelado === true)) continue;
    const estadoRaw = rec.estado;
    const estadoLow = typeof estadoRaw === 'string' ? estadoRaw.trim().toLowerCase() : 'aprobado';
    if (!estadoLow || (!INACTIVE_SERVICE_ESTADOS.has(estadoLow) && !DRAFT_ESTADOS.has(estadoLow))) {
      _purgedGhostIds.delete(cleanId.toLowerCase());
    }
  }
};

export const filterOutServiceId = <T extends { id?: unknown }>(
  rows: readonly T[] | null | undefined,
  serviceId: string
): T[] => {
  if (!Array.isArray(rows)) return [];
  const targetLow = serviceId.toLowerCase();
  return rows.filter((row) => {
    if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') return false;
    const rid = String(row.id ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return Boolean(rid) && rid !== targetLow;
  });
};

export const reconcileActiveLiveServicesSnapshot = (
  liveRows: unknown,
  opts?: { allowAdminDrafts?: boolean }
): any[] => {
  if (!Array.isArray(liveRows)) return [];
  unpurgeActiveServicesFromCache(liveRows);
  const allowDrafts = Boolean(opts?.allowAdminDrafts);
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const cleanOut: any[] = [];

  for (const raw of liveRows) {
    if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object' || Array.isArray(raw)) continue;
    const rec = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
    const hasOwnId = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec, 'id');
    const cleanId = sanitizeGhostServiceId(hasOwnId ? rec.id : undefined);
    if (!cleanId) continue;

    const idLow = cleanId.toLowerCase();
    if (seen.has(idLow) || _purgedGhostIds.has(idLow)) continue;
    if (rec.activo === false || rec.eliminado === true) continue;
    const attrs = rec.atributos && typeof rec.atributos === 'object' ? (rec.atributos as Record<string, unknown>) : null;
    if (attrs && (attrs.eliminado === true || attrs.cancelado === true)) {
      continue;
    }

    const estadoRaw = rec.estado;
    const estadoLow = typeof estadoRaw === 'string' ? estadoRaw.trim().toLowerCase() : 'aprobado';
    if (estadoLow && INACTIVE_SERVICE_ESTADOS.has(estadoLow)) continue;
    if (!allowDrafts && estadoLow && DRAFT_ESTADOS.has(estadoLow)) continue;

    seen.add(idLow);
    cleanOut.push(raw);
  }
  return cleanOut;
};

export const isGhostServiceBookingError = (err: unknown): boolean => {
  const msg = String((err as any)?.message || (err as any)?.details || err || '').toLowerCase();
  const code = String((err as any)?.code || '').trim();
  if (
    /la experiencia seleccionada ya no existe|experiencia.*ya no existe|servicio no existe|servicio no disponible|no longer exists|service.*does not exist/i.test(
      msg
    )
  ) {
    return true;
  }
  if (code === '23503' && /servicio|servicios|reservas_servicio_id_fkey/i.test(msg)) {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
};

const stripGhostFromStoredSnapshot = async (storageKey: string, listField: 'servData' | 'servicios', cleanId: string) => {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (!raw) return;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || !Array.isArray(parsed[listField])) return;
    const nextList = filterOutServiceId(parsed[listField], cleanId);
    if (nextList.length !== parsed[listField].length) {
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, [listField]: nextList }));
    }
  } catch {}
};

export const EXPLICIT_RETIRED_SERVICE_STATES = [
  'inactivo',
  'eliminado',
  'rechazado',
  'cancelado',
] as const;
const EXPLICIT_RETIRED_SET = new Set<string>(EXPLICIT_RETIRED_SERVICE_STATES);

export const isConfirmedRetiredServiceRecord = (row: unknown): boolean => {
  if (!row || typeof row !== 'object') return true;
  const r = row as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (r.activo === false) return true;
  const attrs = r.atributos;
  if (attrs && typeof attrs === 'object' && ((attrs as Record<string, unknown>).eliminado === true || (attrs as Record<string, unknown>).cancelado === true)) {
    return true;
  }
  const stLow = String(r.estado ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
  return EXPLICIT_RETIRED_SET.has(stLow);
};

export interface IServiciosLookupClient {
  from: (tbl: string) => {
    select: (cols: string) => {
      eq: (col: string, val: string) => {
        maybeSingle: () => PromiseLike<{ data: unknown; error: unknown }>;
      };
    };
  };
}

const isServiciosLookupClient = (val: unknown): val is IServiciosLookupClient =>
  Boolean(val && typeof val === 'object' && typeof (val as { from?: unknown }).from === 'function');

export const confirmServiceMissingOrInactiveInDb = async (
  supabaseClient: unknown,
  serviceId: unknown
): Promise<boolean> => {
  const cleanId = sanitizeGhostServiceId(serviceId);
  if (!cleanId || !isServiciosLookupClient(supabaseClient)) return false;
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabaseClient
      .from('servicios')
      .select('id, estado, activo, atributos')
      .eq('id', cleanId)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error) return false;
    return !data || isConfirmedRetiredServiceRecord(data);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
};

export const verifyAndPurgeConfirmedGhostService = async (
  supabaseClient: unknown,
  serviceId: unknown
): Promise<boolean> => {
  const confirmed = await confirmServiceMissingOrInactiveInDb(supabaseClient, serviceId);
  if (!confirmed) return false;
  purgeGhostServiceEverywhere(serviceId);
  return true;
};

export interface IMazatlanRecurrenceInput {
  requestedDate?: string | null;
  baseDate?: string | null;
  horaInicio?: string | null;
  frecuencia?: string | null;
  descripcion?: string | null;
  atributos?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  nowRef?: Date;
}

const getMazatlanNowParts = (nowRef?: Date): { todayIso: string; hhmm: string } => {
  const ref = nowRef instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(nowRef.getTime()) ? nowRef : new Date();
  try {
    const fmt = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {
      timeZone: 'America/Mazatlan',
      year: 'numeric',
      month: '2-digit',
      day: '2-digit',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      hour12: false,
    });
    const parts = Object.fromEntries(fmt.formatToParts(ref).map((p) => [p.type, p.value]));
    const hh = parts.hour === '24' ? '00' : String(parts.hour || '08').padStart(2, '0');
    const mm = String(parts.minute || '00').padStart(2, '0');
    return { todayIso: `${parts.year}-${parts.month}-${parts.day}`, hhmm: `${hh}:${mm}` };
  } catch {
    const utcMs = ref.getTime() - 7 * 3600_000;
    const d = new Date(utcMs);
    return {
      todayIso: d.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      hhmm: d.toISOString().slice(11, 16),
    };
  }
};

const addDaysIsoUtc = (iso: string, days: number): string => {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-').map(Number);
  const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, (m || 1) - 1, (d || 1) + days));
  return dt.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

export const evaluateMazatlanRecurrenceSchedule = (
  input: IMazatlanRecurrenceInput
): { valid: boolean; resolvedDate: string; recurrence: 'unica' | 'diario' | 'semanal' | 'mensual'; reason: string } => {
  const { todayIso, hhmm } = getMazatlanNowParts(input.nowRef);
  const rawDescFreq = String(input.descripcion || '').match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*([^\]]+)\]/i)?.[1] || '';
  const freqRaw = String(input.frecuencia || input.atributos?.frecuencia || rawDescFreq || 'unica')
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase();
  const recurrence: 'unica' | 'diario' | 'semanal' | 'mensual' =
    /diari|daily/.test(freqRaw) ? 'diario'
      : /seman|week/.test(freqRaw) ? 'semanal'
        : /mensu|month/.test(freqRaw) ? 'mensual'
          : 'unica';

  const baseRaw = String(input.baseDate || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  const baseDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(baseRaw) ? baseRaw : null;
  const reqRaw = String(input.requestedDate || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  const hasExplicitHora = Boolean(String(input.horaInicio || '').trim());
  const horaMatch = String(input.horaInicio || '').trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  const targetHHMM = horaMatch
    ? `${String(Math.min(23, Math.max(0, Number(horaMatch[1])))).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(Math.min(59, Math.max(0, Number(horaMatch[2])))).padStart(2, '0')}`
    : '23:59';

  const [curH, curM] = hhmm.split(':').map(Number);
  const [tgtH, tgtM] = targetHHMM.split(':').map(Number);
  const timePassedToday = hasExplicitHora && (tgtH * 60 + tgtM) < (curH * 60 + curM - 15);

  let targetDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(reqRaw) ? reqRaw : '';
  if (!targetDate) {
    if (recurrence === 'unica') targetDate = baseDate || todayIso;
    else if (recurrence === 'diario') {
      targetDate = baseDate && baseDate > todayIso ? baseDate : todayIso;
      if (targetDate === todayIso && timePassedToday) targetDate = addDaysIsoUtc(todayIso, 1);
    } else if (recurrence === 'semanal') {
      targetDate = baseDate || todayIso;
      for (let g = 0; g < 520 && (targetDate < todayIso || (targetDate === todayIso && timePassedToday)); g++) {
        targetDate = addDaysIsoUtc(targetDate, 7);
      }
    } else {
      targetDate = baseDate || todayIso;
      const [by, bm, bd] = targetDate.split('-').map(Number);
      for (let step = 1; step <= 240 && (targetDate < todayIso || (targetDate === todayIso && timePassedToday)); step++) {
        const lastDom = new Date(Date.UTC(by, (bm || 1) + step, 0)).getUTCDate();
        const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(by, (bm || 1) - 1 + step, Math.min(bd || 1, lastDom)));
        targetDate = dt.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      }
    }
  }

  const endRaw = String(input.atributos?.fecha_fin || '').split('T')[0].trim();
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(endRaw) && targetDate > endRaw) {
    return { valid: false, resolvedDate: targetDate, recurrence, reason: 'HORARIO_NO_DISPONIBLE' };
  }
  if (targetDate < todayIso || (targetDate === todayIso && timePassedToday)) {
    return { valid: false, resolvedDate: targetDate, recurrence, reason: 'HORARIO_NO_DISPONIBLE' };
  }
  if (recurrence === 'unica' && baseDate && targetDate !== baseDate) {
    return { valid: false, resolvedDate: targetDate, recurrence, reason: 'HORARIO_NO_DISPONIBLE' };
  }
  if (recurrence === 'diario' && baseDate && targetDate < baseDate) {
    return { valid: false, resolvedDate: targetDate, recurrence, reason: 'HORARIO_NO_DISPONIBLE' };
  }
  if (recurrence === 'semanal' && baseDate) {
    const bDow = new Date(`${baseDate}T12:00:00Z`).getUTCDay();
    const tDow = new Date(`${targetDate}T12:00:00Z`).getUTCDay();
    if (targetDate < baseDate || bDow !== tDow) {
      return { valid: false, resolvedDate: targetDate, recurrence, reason: 'HORARIO_NO_DISPONIBLE' };
    }
  }
  if (recurrence === 'mensual' && baseDate) {
    const bDom = Number(baseDate.slice(8, 10));
    const [ty, tm, td] = targetDate.split('-').map(Number);
    const lastDay = new Date(Date.UTC(ty, tm, 0)).getUTCDate();
    const matchesDom = td === bDom || (bDom > lastDay && td === lastDay);
    if (targetDate < baseDate || !matchesDom) {
      return { valid: false, resolvedDate: targetDate, recurrence, reason: 'HORARIO_NO_DISPONIBLE' };
    }
  }
  return { valid: true, resolvedDate: targetDate, recurrence, reason: 'OK' };
};

export const purgeGhostServiceEverywhere = (
  serviceId: unknown
): { purged: boolean; serviceId: string | null } => {
  const cleanId = sanitizeGhostServiceId(serviceId);
  if (!cleanId) return { purged: false, serviceId: null };

  _purgedGhostIds.add(cleanId.toLowerCase());
  clearSpotLockAndIntent(cleanId);
  clearPendingSplitConfig(cleanId);

  _purgeHooks.forEach((hook) => {
    try {
      hook(cleanId);
    } catch {}
  });

  void stripGhostFromStoredSnapshot(EVENTS_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY, 'servData', cleanId);
  void stripGhostFromStoredSnapshot(RADAR_SNAPSHOT_STORAGE_KEY, 'servicios', cleanId);

  try {
    emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  } catch {}

  return { purged: true, serviceId: cleanId };
};

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

export const EQUIPMENT_META: Record<
  StudioEquipmentType,
  { tag: string; es: string; en: string; icon: any }
> = {
  reformer: { tag: 'REFORMER', es: 'Camas Reformer', en: 'Reformer Beds', icon: 'layers' },
  bike: { tag: 'BIKE', es: 'Bicis Indoor', en: 'Indoor Bikes', icon: 'disc' },
  barre: { tag: 'BARRE', es: 'Estaciones Barre', en: 'Barre Stations', icon: 'sliders' },
  bag: { tag: 'BAG', es: 'Costales Box', en: 'Heavy Bags', icon: 'target' },
  court: { tag: 'COURT', es: 'Canchas', en: 'Courts', icon: 'grid' },
  spot: { tag: 'SPOT', es: 'Estaciones', en: 'Stations', icon: 'zap' },

export const inferEquipmentByDiscipline = (
  discipline?: string,
  title?: string
): StudioEquipmentType => {
  const txt = `${discipline || ''} ${title || ''}`.toLowerCase();
  if (txt.includes('pilates') || txt.includes('reformer')) return 'reformer';
  if (
    txt.includes('cycling') ||
    txt.includes('spinning') ||
    txt.includes('bici') ||
    txt.includes('indoor bike')
  ) {
    return 'bike';
  }
  if (txt.includes('barre') || txt.includes('ballet')) return 'barre';
  if (txt.includes('box') || txt.includes('muay') || txt.includes('kick') || txt.includes('mma')) {
    return 'bag';
  }
  if (
    txt.includes('padel') ||
    txt.includes('pádel') ||
    txt.includes('tenis') ||
    txt.includes('tennis') ||
    txt.includes('pickleball') ||
    txt.includes('voleibol') ||
    txt.includes('volleyball') ||
    txt.includes('badminton') ||
    txt.includes('bádminton') ||
    txt.includes('squash') ||
    txt.includes('racquet') ||
    txt.includes('frontenis') ||
    txt.includes('cancha') ||
    txt.includes('court')
  ) {
    return 'court';
  }
  return 'spot';
};

export const resolveStudioFrontStageLabel = (
  discipline?: string | null,
  equipmentType?: StudioEquipmentType | string | null,
  isEn = false
): string => {
  const norm = `${discipline || ''} ${equipmentType || ''}`
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase();
  if (/(padel|tenis|tennis|pickleball|voleibol|volleyball|badminton|squash|racquet|frontenis|cancha|court)/.test(norm)) {
    return isEn ? 'CENTER NET / COURT FRONT' : 'RED CENTRAL / FRENTE DE CANCHA';
  }
  if (/(natacion|nado|swim|alberca|pool|acuaerobic|aqua|clavados|waterpolo|apnea)/.test(norm)) {
    return isEn ? 'LANE HEADWALL' : 'CABECERA DE CARRILES';
  }
  if (/(fisio|physio|rehab|quiropr|masaje|massage|acupuntura|osteop|nutrici|podolog|consultorio|camilla|especialista)/.test(norm)) {
    return isEn ? 'SPECIALIST ZONE' : 'ZONA DE ESPECIALISTA';
  }
  return isEn ? 'COACH STAGE • MIRROR ZONE' : 'TARIMA DEL COACH • ZONA ESPEJO';
};

export const getLocalizedStudioUnitSingular = (
  equipmentType?: StudioEquipmentType | string | null,
  isEn = false
): string => {
  if (equipmentType === 'court') return isEn ? 'COURT' : 'CANCHA';
  if (equipmentType === 'reformer') return 'REFORMER';
  if (equipmentType === 'bike') return isEn ? 'BIKE' : 'BICI';
  if (equipmentType === 'barre') return isEn ? 'BARRE' : 'BARRA';
  if (equipmentType === 'bag') return isEn ? 'BAG' : 'COSTAL';
  return isEn ? 'STATION' : 'ESTACIÓN';
};

  equipmentType: StudioEquipmentType | string | null | undefined,
  spotNum: number | string,
  isEn = false
): string => `${getLocalizedStudioUnitSingular(equipmentType, isEn)} #${spotNum}`;

export const formatUseStudioStationCta = (
  equipmentType: StudioEquipmentType | string | null | undefined,
  spotNum: number | string,
  isEn = false
): string => {
  const badge = formatStudioStationBadge(equipmentType, spotNum, isEn);
  return isEn ? `USE ${badge}` : `USAR ${badge}`;
};

export const resolveDefaultStudioLayout = (
  discipline?: string,
  maxCupo?: number,
  saved?: any,
  title?: string
): IStudioLayoutConfig => {
  const fallbackEq = inferEquipmentByDiscipline(discipline, title);
  if (saved && typeof saved === 'object' && Number(saved.rows) >= 1 && Number(saved.cols) >= 1) {
    const disabled = Array.isArray(saved.disabledCells) ? saved.disabledCells.map(String) : [];
    const savedEq = saved.equipmentType as StudioEquipmentType | undefined;
    const eqType =
      savedEq && EQUIPMENT_META[savedEq]
        ? savedEq === 'spot' && fallbackEq !== 'spot'
          ? fallbackEq
          : savedEq
        : fallbackEq;
    return clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(
      Number(saved.rows) || 3,
      Number(saved.cols) || 4,
      disabled,
      eqType
    );
  }
  return buildGridForTotalSpots(
    Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, Math.max(1, Number(maxCupo) || 12)),
    fallbackEq
  );
};

export const syncLayoutOnDisciplineChange = (
  prev: IStudioLayoutConfig | null | undefined,
  discipline?: string,
  maxCupo: number | string = 12,
  title?: string
): IStudioLayoutConfig => {
  const nextEq = inferEquipmentByDiscipline(discipline, title);
  const targetSpots = Math.min(
    MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS,
    Math.max(1, Number(prev?.totalSpots || maxCupo) || 12)
  );
  if (prev && prev.rows > 0 && prev.cols > 0) {
    return clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(
      Number(prev.rows) || 3,
      Number(prev.cols) || 4,
      Array.isArray(prev.disabledCells) ? prev.disabledCells : [],
      nextEq
    );
  }
  return buildGridForTotalSpots(targetSpots, nextEq);
};

export const formatStudioCapacityLabel = (
  cupoVal: string | number | undefined,
  studioLayout: IStudioLayoutConfig | null | undefined,
  discipline?: string,
  title?: string,
  tx?: (es: string, en: string) => string
): string => {
  const activeSpots = Math.min(
    MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS,
    Math.max(1, Number(studioLayout?.totalSpots || cupoVal) || 12)
  );
  const t = tx || ((es: string) => es);
  const eq = studioLayout?.equipmentType || inferEquipmentByDiscipline(discipline, title);
  if (eq === 'bike') return t(`${activeSpots} Bicis`, `${activeSpots} Bikes`);
  if (eq === 'reformer') return `${activeSpots} Reformers`;
  if (eq === 'court') return t(`${activeSpots} Canchas/Cupos`, `${activeSpots} Court Spots`);
  return t(`${activeSpots} Atletas`, `${activeSpots} Athletes`);
};

export const formatStudioLayoutButtonLabel = (
  studioLayout: IStudioLayoutConfig | null | undefined,
  cupoVal: string | number | undefined,
  discipline?: string,
  title?: string,
  isEn = false
): string => {
  const fallbackEq = inferEquipmentByDiscipline(discipline, title);
  const safeCap = Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, Math.max(1, Number(cupoVal) || 12));
  const resolved =
    studioLayout && studioLayout.rows > 0 && studioLayout.cols > 0
      ? studioLayout
      : buildGridForTotalSpots(safeCap, fallbackEq);
  const meta = EQUIPMENT_META[resolved.equipmentType] || EQUIPMENT_META.spot;
  const label = isEn ? meta.en : meta.es;
  return `${resolved.totalSpots} ${label} ${resolved.rows}x${resolved.cols}`;
};

export const getDisciplineEventTitlePlaceholder = (
  discipline?: string,
  isEn = false
): string => {
  const low = String(discipline || '').toLowerCase();
  if (/padel|pádel|tenis|tennis|pickleball|squash/.test(low)) {
    return isEn ? 'e.g., Americano Doubles / Padel Clinic' : 'Ej: Torneo Americano / Clínica Pádel Pro';
  }
  if (/cycling|spinning|bici|velo|cycle|ciclismo/.test(low)) {
    return isEn ? 'e.g., Neon Rhythm Ride 45' : 'Ej: Ride Nocturno Sprint 45';
  }
  if (/pilates|reformer/.test(low)) {
    return isEn ? 'e.g., Core & Glutes Reformer 50' : 'Ej: Reformer Core & Glúteos 50';
  }
  if (/barre/.test(low)) {
    return isEn ? 'e.g., Sculpt & Pulse Barre 50' : 'Ej: Barre Sculpt & Flow 50';
  }
  if (/box|mma|muay|kickbox/.test(low)) {
    return isEn ? 'e.g., Heavy Bag & Footwork Drills' : 'Ej: Boxeo Técnico & Saco Heavy';
  }
  if (/yoga|medit|breath|sound|hielo|cold/.test(low)) {
    return isEn ? 'e.g., Sunset Vinyasa & Breathwork' : 'Ej: Vinyasa Flow & Respiro Sunset';
  }
  if (/run|trail|atletismo|marat/.test(low)) {
    return isEn ? 'e.g., Social 5K Marina Run' : 'Ej: Social Run 5K Malecón';
  }
  if (/fisio|rehab|quirop|osteop|masaj/.test(low)) {
    return isEn ? 'e.g., Sports Recovery & Mobility' : 'Ej: Sesión Descarga & Movilidad';
  }
  const cleanDisc = String(discipline || '').trim();
  if (cleanDisc && cleanDisc.toLowerCase() !== 'todas') {
    return isEn ? `e.g., ${cleanDisc} Masterclass 60'` : `Ej: Masterclass ${cleanDisc} 60'`;
  }
  return isEn ? 'e.g., Sunrise Kinetic Training 60' : 'Ej: Sesión Especial Kinetic 60';
};

export const loadStudioLayoutLocal = async (
  eventKey?: string
): Promise<IStudioLayoutConfig | null> => {
  if (!eventKey) return null;
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(`${STUDIO_LAYOUT_STORAGE_PREFIX}${eventKey}`);
    return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
};

export const saveStudioLayoutLocal = async (
  eventKey: string | undefined,
  layout: IStudioLayoutConfig
): Promise<void> => {
  if (!eventKey) return;
  const safe = clampStudioGridToMaxSpots(
    layout.rows,
    layout.cols,
    layout.disabledCells,
    layout.equipmentType
  );
  try {
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(`${STUDIO_LAYOUT_STORAGE_PREFIX}${eventKey}`, JSON.stringify(safe));
  } catch {}
};

export const formatIsoLocal = (d: Date) =>
  `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

export const getTomorrowIsoStr = () => {
  const mz = getMazatlanNowParts();
  return new Date(Date.UTC(mz.year, mz.month - 1, mz.day + 1)).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

export const getThreeMonthsIsoStr = () => {
  const mz = getMazatlanNowParts();
  const targetM0 = mz.month - 1 + 3;
  const ty = mz.year + Math.floor(targetM0 / 12);
  const m0 = ((targetM0 % 12) + 12) % 12;
  const maxD = new Date(Date.UTC(ty, m0 + 1, 0)).getUTCDate();
  return new Date(Date.UTC(ty, m0, Math.min(mz.day, maxD))).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
};

export type ActiveSheetType =
  | null | 'lista_confirmados' | 'desglose_escrow' | 'auditoria_rating'
  | 'fecha' | 'hora' | 'disciplina' | 'frecuencia' | 'duracion'
  | 'cupo' | 'precio' | 'edad' | 'requisitos'
  | 'intensidad' | 'enfoque' | 'impacto' | 'nivel' | 'entorno';

const SLOTS_30_MIN: { value: string; label: string; labelEn?: string }[] = [];
for (let h = 6; h <= 22; h++) {
  const hh = String(h).padStart(2, '0');
  SLOTS_30_MIN.push({ value: `${hh}:00`, label: `${hh}:00 hrs`, labelEn: `${hh}:00 hrs` });
  if (h < 22) SLOTS_30_MIN.push({ value: `${hh}:30`, label: `${hh}:30 hrs`, labelEn: `${hh}:30 hrs` });
}

  const v = String(i + 1);
  return { value: v, label: `${v} ${i === 0 ? 'Atleta' : 'Atletas'}`, labelEn: `${v} ${i === 0 ? 'Athlete' : 'Athletes'}` };
});

  return { value: v, label: v === '0' ? '0 KC (Cortesía)' : `${v} KC`, labelEn: v === '0' ? '0 KC (Courtesy)' : `${v} KC` };
});
