export const translateEventCatalogTerm = (raw: string, language?: string): string => {
  const trimmed = String(raw || '').trim();
  if (!trimmed) return '';
  if (/^(18\+|13\+|14\+|16\+|todas las edades|all ages|familiar)/i.test(trimmed)) {
    const normAge = normalizeAgePolicy(trimmed);
    return language === 'en' ? EN_CATALOG_DICT[normAge] || normAge : normAge;
  }
  const fixedEs = /^canche\s+exterior$/i.test(trimmed) ? 'Cancha Exterior' : trimmed;
  if (language !== 'en') return fixedEs;
  if (EN_CATALOG_DICT[fixedEs]) return EN_CATALOG_DICT[fixedEs];
  const normEnv = normalizeEnvironmentLabel(fixedEs);
  if (EN_CATALOG_DICT[normEnv]) return EN_CATALOG_DICT[normEnv];
  const athMatch = fixedEs.match(/^(\d+)\s+Atleta(s)?$/i);
  if (athMatch) return `${athMatch[1]} ${athMatch[1] === '1' ? 'Athlete' : 'Athletes'}`;
  const hrMatch = fixedEs.match(/^(\d+)\s+hora(s)?$/i);
  if (hrMatch) return `${hrMatch[1]} ${hrMatch[1] === '1' ? 'hour' : 'hours'}`;
  return translateDynamicContent(fixedEs, 'en');
};

export const normalizeCityLabel = (raw: string): string => cleanLocationDisplay(raw, '');

export const normalizeEventTitleKey = (raw?: string | null): string =>
  String(raw || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');

export const saveEventAttrsDualCache = async (
  keys: (string | null | undefined)[],
  atributosObj: Record<string, any>,
  studioLayout?: IStudioLayoutConfig | null
): Promise<void> => {
  const uniqueKeys = Array.from(
    new Set(
      keys
        .flatMap((k) => {
          const raw = String(k || '').trim();
          return raw ? [raw, normalizeEventTitleKey(raw)] : [];
        })
        .filter(Boolean)
    )
  );
  const serialized = JSON.stringify(atributosObj);
  await Promise.all(
    uniqueKeys.map(async (key) => {
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(`${ATTR_STORAGE_PREFIX}${key}`, serialized).catch(() => {});
      if (studioLayout) await saveStudioLayoutLocal(key, studioLayout);
    })
  );
};

export const loadEventAttrsDualCache = async (
  id?: string | null,
  title?: string | null
): Promise<Record<string, any> | null> => {
  const candidates = Array.from(
    new Set(
      [
        String(id || '').trim(),
        normalizeEventTitleKey(title),
        String(title || '').trim(),
      ].filter(Boolean)
    )
  );
  for (const key of candidates) {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(`${ATTR_STORAGE_PREFIX}${key}`);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') return parsed;
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  return null;
};

export const resolveSplitEscrowConfig = (evOrAttrs: any): { enabled: boolean; maxPersonas: 2 | 3 | 4 } => {
  const attrs = evOrAttrs?.atributos && typeof evOrAttrs.atributos === 'object' ? evOrAttrs.atributos : evOrAttrs || {};
  const explicitBool = attrs?.split_escrow_enabled ?? evOrAttrs?.split_escrow_enabled;
  const enabled =
    typeof explicitBool === 'boolean'
      ? explicitBool
      : Boolean(attrs?.permite_dividir_cuenta === true || evOrAttrs?.permite_dividir_cuenta === true);
  const rawMax = Number(
    attrs?.split_max_personas ?? evOrAttrs?.split_max_personas ?? attrs?.max_division_personas ?? evOrAttrs?.max_division_personas ?? 4
  );
  const maxPersonas: 2 | 3 | 4 = rawMax === 2 ? 2 : rawMax === 3 ? 3 : 4;
  return { enabled, maxPersonas };
};

export const mergeAdminEventWithCachedAttrs = (prev: any, cached: Record<string, any>) => {
  const prevAttrs = prev?.atributos || {};
  const cachedGal = Array.isArray(prev?.galeria) && prev.galeria.length > 0
    ? prev.galeria
    : Array.isArray(prevAttrs.galeria) && prevAttrs.galeria.length > 0
      ? prevAttrs.galeria
      : Array.isArray(cached.galeria) ? cached.galeria : [];
  const splitCfg = resolveSplitEscrowConfig({ ...cached, ...prevAttrs, ...prev });
  const safeAge = normalizeAgePolicy(prev?.edad_minima || prevAttrs.edad_minima || cached.edad_minima);
  const mergedAttrs = {
    ...cached,
    ...prevAttrs,
    intensidad: prevAttrs.intensidad || cached.intensidad || 'Intensidad Media',
    enfoque: prevAttrs.enfoque || cached.enfoque || 'Técnica y Precisión',
    impacto: prevAttrs.impacto || cached.impacto || 'Impacto Moderado',
    nivel: prevAttrs.nivel || cached.nivel || 'Multi-Nivel (Todos)',
    entorno: normalizeEnvironmentLabel(prevAttrs.entorno || cached.entorno),
    edad_minima: safeAge,
    split_escrow_enabled: splitCfg.enabled,
    split_max_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas,
    permite_dividir_cuenta: splitCfg.enabled,
    max_division_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas,
    galeria: cachedGal,
  };
  return {
    ...prev,
    frecuencia: prev?.frecuencia || cached.frecuencia || 'Única Vez',
    duracion_minutos: prev?.duracion_minutos ?? cached.duracion_minutos ?? 60,
    requisitos_traer: prev?.requisitos_traer || cached.requisitos_traer || 'Agua / Hidratación personal',
    edad_minima: safeAge,
    precio_kc: prev?.precio_kc ?? cached.precio_kc ?? '200',
    split_escrow_enabled: splitCfg.enabled,
    split_max_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas,
    permite_dividir_cuenta: splitCfg.enabled,
    max_division_personas: splitCfg.maxPersonas,
    galeria: cachedGal,
    imagen_url: prev?.imagen_url || cachedGal[0] || '',
    studio_layout_json: prev?.studio_layout_json || prevAttrs.studio_layout || cached.studio_layout || null,
    atributos: mergedAttrs,
  };
};

export const writeServiceToSupabaseSafe = async (params: {
  isUpdate: boolean;
  eventId?: string | null;
  clubName: string;
  coreData: Record<string, any>;
  extData: Record<string, any>;
}): Promise<any> => {
  const { isUpdate, eventId, clubName, coreData, extData } = params;
  const payload: Record<string, any> = { ...extData };
  if (!isUpdate) payload.negocio_nombre = clubName;

  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 10; attempt++) {
    const res = isUpdate
      ? await supabase.from('servicios').update(payload).eq('id', eventId).select('*').maybeSingle()
      : await supabase.from('servicios').insert([payload]).select('*').maybeSingle();
    if (!res.error) {
      emitKineticRadarUpdated();
      return res.data || null;
    }
    const errMsg = String(res.error.message || '');
    const colMatch =
      errMsg.match(/Could not find the '([^']+)' column/i) ||
      errMsg.match(/column "([^"]+)" of relation/i);
    if (colMatch?.[1] && colMatch[1] in payload) {
      delete payload[colMatch[1]];
      continue;
    }
    const fallbackPayload: Record<string, any> = { ...coreData };
    if (!isUpdate) fallbackPayload.negocio_nombre = clubName;
    const fallbackRes = isUpdate
      ? await supabase.from('servicios').update(fallbackPayload).eq('id', eventId).select('*').maybeSingle()
      : await supabase.from('servicios').insert([fallbackPayload]).select('*').maybeSingle();
    if (fallbackRes.error) throw fallbackRes.error;
    emitKineticRadarUpdated();
    return fallbackRes.data || null;
  }
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  return null;
};

export const parseRequirementsInput = (raw: unknown): string[] => {
  if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw.map((x) => String(x || '').trim()).filter(Boolean);
  if (typeof raw === 'string' && raw.trim().length > 0) return raw.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return [];
};

export const extractExtraEventPhotos = (src: any, stdCatImg: string, coverUrl = '', maxPhotos = 10): string[] =>
  Array.isArray(src)
    ? src
        .map((u) => (typeof u === 'string' ? u.trim() : ''))
        .filter((u) => u.length > 5 && u !== stdCatImg && (!coverUrl || u !== coverUrl))
        .slice(0, maxPhotos)
    : [];

export const hydrateEventFormState = (currentEv: any, isCreateMode: boolean, minTomorrowIso: string) => {
  const evIdStr = String(currentEv?.id || '').trim();
  if (isCreateMode || !evIdStr) {
    const initCat = currentEv?.categoria || currentEv?.disciplina || 'Fuerza';
    return {
      isEditMode: true, titleVal: '', discVal: initCat, freqVal: 'Única Vez', dateVal: minTomorrowIso,
      timeVal: '08:00', durVal: '60', coachVal: 'Coach Oficial Kinetic', locVal: '', mapsDeepUrl: '',
      locCoords: { lat: null as number | null, lng: null as number | null }, detectedZone: null as string | null,
      cupoVal: '12', priceVal: '200', ageVal: '18+ Adultos', reqsVal: ['Agua / Hidratación personal'], descVal: '',
      attrInt: 'Intensidad Alta', attrEnf: 'Cardio / Quema', attrImp: 'Alto Impacto Articular',
      attrNiv: 'Multi-Nivel (Todos)', attrEnt: KINETIC_20_ENVIRONMENTS[0].es, coverUri: '', galleryUris: [] as string[],
      studioLayout: resolveDefaultStudioLayout(initCat, 12, null, ''),
      splitEscrowEnabled: false, splitMaxPersonas: 4 as 2 | 3 | 4,
      dbAttrs: null as Record<string, any> | null, stdCatImg: resolveCategoryImage(initCat, ''), rawLoc: '',
    };
  }
  const dbAttrs = currentEv?.atributos && typeof currentEv.atributos === 'object' ? currentEv.atributos : null;
  const splitCfg = resolveSplitEscrowConfig(currentEv);
  const rawD = String(currentEv?.descripcion || '');
  const fMatch = rawD.match(/\[Frecuencia Programada:\s*(.*?)\]/i);
  const parsedF = currentEv?.frecuencia || dbAttrs?.frecuencia || fMatch?.[1]?.trim() || 'Única Vez';
  const cleanD = rawD.replace(/\[Frecuencia Programada:.*?\]/gi, '').trim();
  const initReqs = parseRequirementsInput(currentEv?.requisitos_traer ?? dbAttrs?.requisitos_traer);
  const rawLoc = String(currentEv?.ubicacion ?? dbAttrs?.ubicacion ?? '').trim();
  const parsedLoc = extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords(rawLoc);
  const rawDateIso = toSafeLocalIsoDate(currentEv?.fecha_proyectada || currentEv?.fecha, minTomorrowIso);
  const effectiveIso = currentEv?.openInEditMode && rawDateIso < minTomorrowIso ? minTomorrowIso : rawDateIso;
  const currentCat = currentEv?.categoria || currentEv?.disciplina || 'Fuerza';
  const currentTit = currentEv?.nombre_servicio || currentEv?.titulo || '';
  const stdCatImg = resolveCategoryImage(currentCat, currentTit);
  const currentMaxCupo = Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, Math.max(1, Number(currentEv?.cupo_maximo || 10)));
  const gallerySrc = Array.isArray(currentEv?.galeria) && currentEv.galeria.length > 0 ? currentEv.galeria : dbAttrs?.galeria;
  const rawExplicitCover = String(
    currentEv?.portada_url || currentEv?.imagen_url || dbAttrs?.portada_url || dbAttrs?.imagen_url || ''
  ).trim();
  const customCoverUri =
    isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(rawExplicitCover) && rawExplicitCover !== stdCatImg
      ? rawExplicitCover
      : isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(Array.isArray(gallerySrc) ? gallerySrc[0] : '') &&
          String(gallerySrc[0]).trim() !== stdCatImg
        ? String(gallerySrc[0]).trim()
        : '';
  return {
    isEditMode: Boolean(currentEv?.openInEditMode), titleVal: currentTit, discVal: currentCat, freqVal: parsedF,
    dateVal: effectiveIso, timeVal: String(currentEv?.hora_inicio || '08:00').substring(0, 5),
    durVal: String(currentEv?.duracion_minutos ?? dbAttrs?.duracion_minutos ?? 60),
    coachVal: currentEv?.instructor_nombre || 'Coach Oficial Kinetic', locVal: rawLoc,
    mapsDeepUrl: parsedLoc.url || '',
    locCoords: {
      lat: (currentEv?.latitud ?? dbAttrs?.latitud ?? parsedLoc.lat ?? null) as number | null,
      lng: (currentEv?.longitud ?? dbAttrs?.longitud ?? parsedLoc.lng ?? null) as number | null,
    },
    detectedZone: parsedLoc.detectedZone, cupoVal: String(currentMaxCupo),
    priceVal: String(currentEv?.precio_kc ?? dbAttrs?.precio_kc ?? 180),
    ageVal: normalizeAgePolicy(currentEv?.edad_minima || dbAttrs?.edad_minima),
    reqsVal: initReqs.length > 0 ? initReqs : ['Agua / Hidratación personal'], descVal: cleanD,
    attrInt: dbAttrs?.intensidad || 'Intensidad Alta', attrEnf: dbAttrs?.enfoque || 'Cardio / Quema',
    attrImp: dbAttrs?.impacto || 'Alto Impacto Articular', attrNiv: dbAttrs?.nivel || 'Multi-Nivel (Todos)',
    attrEnt: normalizeEnvironmentLabel(dbAttrs?.entorno),
    coverUri: customCoverUri,
    galleryUris: extractExtraEventPhotos(gallerySrc, stdCatImg, customCoverUri),
    studioLayout: resolveDefaultStudioLayout(currentCat, currentMaxCupo, currentEv?.studio_layout_json || dbAttrs?.studio_layout, currentTit),
    splitEscrowEnabled: splitCfg.enabled, splitMaxPersonas: splitCfg.maxPersonas,
    dbAttrs, stdCatImg, rawLoc,
  };
};

export const buildEventSavePayloads = (p: {
  cleanT: string; discVal: string; freqVal: string; dateVal: string; minTomorrowIso: string;
  timeVal: string; durVal: string; coachVal: string; locVal: string; mapsDeepUrl: string;
  locCoords: { lat: number | null; lng: number | null }; cupoVal: string; totalKc: number;
  ageVal: string; reqsVal: string[]; descVal: string; attrInt: string; attrEnf: string;
  attrImp: string; attrNiv: string; attrEnt: string; studioLayout: IStudioLayoutConfig | null;
  coverHttpsUrl?: string; fullGallery: string[]; status: string; splitEscrowEnabled?: boolean; splitMaxPersonas?: number;
}) => {
  const finalD = `${p.descVal.trim()} [Frecuencia Programada: ${p.freqVal}]`.trim();
  const parsedLoc = extractGoogleMapsUrlAndCoords(p.locVal);
  const dbLoc = parsedLoc.cleanValue || p.mapsDeepUrl || '';
  const validFutureDate = p.dateVal < p.minTomorrowIso ? p.minTomorrowIso : p.dateVal;
  const finalCupo = Math.min(MAX_STUDIO_SPOTS, Math.max(1, parseInt(p.cupoVal, 10) || 12));
  const durMinInt = Math.max(15, parseInt(p.durVal, 10) || 60);
  const reqsJoined = p.reqsVal.join(', ');
  const safeAge = normalizeAgePolicy(p.ageVal);
  const normalizedEnv = normalizeEnvironmentLabel(p.attrEnt);
  const effectiveLayout = p.studioLayout || resolveDefaultStudioLayout(p.discVal, finalCupo, null, p.cleanT);
  const finalLat = parsedLoc.lat ?? p.locCoords.lat;
  const finalLng = parsedLoc.lng ?? p.locCoords.lng;
  const splitOn = Boolean(p.splitEscrowEnabled);
  const splitMax: 2 | 3 | 4 = p.splitMaxPersonas === 2 ? 2 : p.splitMaxPersonas === 3 ? 3 : 4;
  const safeGallery = (p.fullGallery || []).filter((u) => isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(u)).slice(0, 11);
  const resolvedCoverUrl =
    (p.coverHttpsUrl && isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(p.coverHttpsUrl) ? p.coverHttpsUrl : '') ||
    safeGallery[0] ||
    '';
  const atributosObj: Record<string, any> = {
    intensidad: p.attrInt, enfoque: p.attrEnf, impacto: p.attrImp, nivel: p.attrNiv, entorno: normalizedEnv,
    studio_layout: effectiveLayout, galeria: safeGallery, frecuencia: p.freqVal, duracion_minutos: durMinInt,
    requisitos_traer: reqsJoined, edad_minima: safeAge, precio_kc: p.totalKc, ubicacion: dbLoc,
    ...(resolvedCoverUrl ? { portada_url: resolvedCoverUrl, imagen_url: resolvedCoverUrl } : {}),
    split_escrow_enabled: splitOn, split_max_personas: splitMax,
    permite_dividir_cuenta: splitOn, max_division_personas: splitMax,
    ...(finalLat != null && finalLng != null ? { latitud: finalLat, longitud: finalLng } : {}),
  };
  const coreData: Record<string, any> = {
    nombre_servicio: p.cleanT, categoria: p.discVal, fecha: validFutureDate, hora_inicio: p.timeVal,
    duracion_minutos: durMinInt, instructor_nombre: p.coachVal.trim() || 'Coach Oficial Kinetic',
    ubicacion: dbLoc, cupo_maximo: finalCupo, precio_kc: p.totalKc, edad_minima: safeAge,
    requisitos_traer: reqsJoined, descripcion: finalD, estado: p.status,
  };
  const extData: Record<string, any> = {
    ...coreData, frecuencia: p.freqVal, galeria: safeGallery, atributos: atributosObj, studio_layout_json: effectiveLayout,
    ...(resolvedCoverUrl ? { portada_url: resolvedCoverUrl, imagen_url: resolvedCoverUrl } : {}),
    ...(finalLat != null && finalLng != null ? { latitud: finalLat, longitud: finalLng } : {}),
  };
  return { atributosObj, coreData, extData, effectiveLayout, finalCupo, durMinInt, reqsJoined, dbLoc, safeAge, finalD, validFutureDate, resolvedCoverUrl };
};

export const verifySpotAntiCollisionLive = async (params: {
  serviceId?: string | null; serviceTitle?: string; localIsoDate: string; selectedSpot: number; currentUserId?: string | null;
}): Promise<{ collided: boolean; liveCount: number }> => {
  const { serviceId, serviceTitle, localIsoDate, selectedSpot, currentUserId } = params;
  const sid = String(serviceId || '').trim();
  const isUuid = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(sid);
  if (!isUuid && !serviceTitle) return { collided: false, liveCount: 0 };
  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('obtener_ocupacion_spots_servicio', {
      p_servicio_id: isUuid ? sid : null,
      p_servicio_nombre: !isUuid ? String(serviceTitle || '').trim() : null,
      p_fecha_clase: localIsoDate,
    });
    if (!error && data && typeof data === 'object') {
      const booked = Array.isArray((data as any).booked_spots) ? (data as any).booked_spots.map(Number) : [];
      const locked = Array.isArray((data as any).locked_other_spots) ? (data as any).locked_other_spots.map(Number) : [];
      return { collided: booked.includes(selectedSpot) || locked.includes(selectedSpot), liveCount: Number((data as any).active_count) || 0 };
    }
    const runQuery = (cols: string) => {
      const q = supabase.from('reservas').select(cols).in('estado', ['pendiente', 'activa', 'confirmada']).limit(60);
      return isUuid ? q.eq('servicio_id', sid) : q.eq('servicio', String(serviceTitle).trim());
    };
    let res = await runQuery('id, usuario_id, estado, fecha_clase, fecha_hora, estacion, spot, notas');
    if (res.error) res = await runQuery('id, usuario_id, estado, fecha_clase');
    const dateRows = (res.data || []).filter((r: any) => {
      const rawD = String(r?.fecha_clase || r?.fecha_hora || '').slice(0, 10);
      return !rawD || rawD === localIsoDate;
    });
    const spotTaken = dateRows.some((r: any) => {
      if (currentUserId && String(r?.usuario_id || '') === String(currentUserId)) return false;
      if (Number(r?.estacion ?? r?.spot) === selectedSpot) return true;
      const m = `${r?.estacion || ''} ${r?.spot || ''} ${r?.notas || ''}`.match(/(?:#|spot\s*|estaci[oó]n\s*)(\d+)\b/i);
      return Boolean(m && Number(m[1]) === selectedSpot);
    });
    return { collided: spotTaken, liveCount: dateRows.length };
  } catch {
    return { collided: false, liveCount: 0 };
  }
};

    FILE: src/utils/eventMediaCdnHelper.ts

    // === src/utils/eventMediaCdnHelper.ts === //
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';
import { openKineticImageFrame } from './kineticImageFramePureLeaf';
import { compressWebImageUri } from './externalLinks';
import {
  isLocalOrEphemeralMediaUri,
  isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl,
  readMediaUriAsUint8Array,
  validateSafeStorageImagePayload,
} from './kineticBinaryMediaHelper';

export const EVENT_MEDIA_CDN_BUCKET = 'imagenes';
export const EVENT_MEDIA_CACHE_CONTROL = '604800';

const sanitizeStorageSegment = (raw?: string | null): string =>
  String(raw || 'kinetic')
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, '_')
    .replace(/_+/g, '_')
    .replace(/^_|_$/g, '')
    .slice(0, 36) || 'kinetic';

export const buildEventMediaStoragePath = (
  folderKey?: string | null,
  slotTag = 'gallery',
  ext = 'jpg'
): string => {
  const safeFolder = sanitizeStorageSegment(folderKey || 'eventos');
  const safeSlot = sanitizeStorageSegment(slotTag);
  const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7);
  return `eventos/${safeFolder}/${safeSlot}_${Date.now()}_${rand}.${ext}`;
};

export const uploadEventMediaToCdn = async (
  rawUri?: string | null,
  folderKey = 'kinetic_evento',
  slotTag = 'media'
): Promise<string> => {
  const clean = String(rawUri || '').trim();
  if (!clean) return '';
  if (isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(clean)) return clean;

  const bytes = await readMediaUriAsUint8Array(clean);
  const check = validateSafeStorageImagePayload({ bytes, uriOrName: clean });
  if (!check.ok || !bytes) {
    throw new Error(
      check.reason === 'file_too_large'
        ? 'La imagen supera el peso máximo permitido (8 MB).'
        : 'Formato de imagen inválido o inseguro. Usa JPG, PNG, WEBP o HEIC.'
    );
  }

  const contentType = check.contentType || 'image/jpeg';
  const ext = check.ext || 'jpg';
  const path = buildEventMediaStoragePath(folderKey, slotTag, ext);

  const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
    .from(EVENT_MEDIA_CDN_BUCKET)
    .upload(path, bytes, {
      contentType,
      cacheControl: EVENT_MEDIA_CACHE_CONTROL,
      upsert: true,
    });

  if (uploadError) {
    throw new Error(uploadError.message || 'Error subiendo imagen al CDN.');
  }

  const { data } = supabase.storage.from(EVENT_MEDIA_CDN_BUCKET).getPublicUrl(path);
  const publicUrl = String(data?.publicUrl || '').trim();
  if (!isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(publicUrl)) {
    throw new Error('URL pública de CDN inválida.');
  }
  return publicUrl;
};

export const compressEventImageSafe = async (uri?: string | null): Promise<string> => {
  const clean = String(uri || '').trim();
  if (!clean) return '';
  if (isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(clean)) return clean;
  if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
    return compressWebImageUri(clean, 1080, 0.8);
  }
  return clean;
};

export interface IPickedEventImagesResult {
  previewUris: string[];
  uploadPromise: Promise<string[]>;
}

export const pickEventImagesInstantWithCdn = async (
  selectionLimit: number,
  folderKey = 'kinetic_evento',
  slotTag = 'gallery'
): Promise<IPickedEventImagesResult> => {
  const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
    mediaTypes: ['images'],
    allowsEditing: false,
    allowsMultipleSelection: selectionLimit > 1,
    selectionLimit: Math.max(1, selectionLimit),
    quality: 0.78,
    base64: false,
  });

  if (res.canceled || !Array.isArray(res.assets) || res.assets.length === 0) {
    return { previewUris: [], uploadPromise: Promise.resolve([]) };
  }

  const rawPicked = res.assets
    .map((asset) => String(asset?.uri || '').trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, Math.max(1, selectionLimit));

  let previewUris = rawPicked;
  if (selectionLimit === 1 && rawPicked[0]) {
    const framed = await openKineticImageFrame({
      uri: rawPicked[0],
      aspect: '16:9',
      lockAspect: false,
      titleEs: 'Portada de Evento',
      titleEn: 'Event Cover',
    });
    if (!framed) return { previewUris: [], uploadPromise: Promise.resolve([]) };
    previewUris = [framed];
  }

  const uploadPromise = Promise.all(
    previewUris.map(async (previewUri, idx) => {
      try {
        const prepared =
          Platform.OS === 'web' && !previewUri.startsWith('data:')
            ? await compressWebImageUri(previewUri, 1280, 0.82)
            : previewUri;
        return await uploadEventMediaToCdn(prepared || previewUri, folderKey, `${slotTag}_${idx}`);
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        void err;
        return '';
      }
    })
  ).then((urls) => urls.filter((u) => isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(u)));

  return { previewUris, uploadPromise };
};

export const pickCompressedEventImages = async (
  selectionLimit: number,
  folderKey = 'kinetic_evento'
): Promise<string[]> => {
  const { previewUris, uploadPromise } = await pickEventImagesInstantWithCdn(
    selectionLimit,
    folderKey,
    'gallery'
  );
  if (previewUris.length === 0) return [];
  const uploaded = await uploadPromise;
  return uploaded;
};

export const ensureHttpsMediaGallery = async (
  uris: string[],
  folderKey = 'kinetic_evento',
  slotPrefix = 'gallery'
): Promise<string[]> => {
  const cleanList = (uris || []).map((u) => String(u || '').trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const resolved = await Promise.all(
    cleanList.map(async (uri, idx) => {
      if (isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(uri)) return uri;
      if (isLocalOrEphemeralMediaUri(uri) || uri.startsWith('data:image/')) {
        try {
          const prepared =
            Platform.OS === 'web' && !uri.startsWith('data:')
              ? await compressWebImageUri(uri, 1280, 0.82)
              : uri;
          return await uploadEventMediaToCdn(prepared || uri, folderKey, `${slotPrefix}_${idx}`);
        } catch (err: unknown) {
          void err;
          return '';
        }
      }
      return '';
    })
  );
  return resolved.filter((u) => isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(u));
};

export const buildCompressedFullGallery = async (
  coverUri: string,
  galleryUris: string[],
  standardCover?: string,
  folderKey = 'kinetic_evento'
): Promise<{ coverHttpsUrl: string; fullGallery: string[]; cleanExtras: string[] }> => {
  const rawCover = String(coverUri || '').trim();
  const isCustomCover = Boolean(rawCover && rawCover !== standardCover);

  const [uploadedCoverArr, uploadedExtras] = await Promise.all([
    isCustomCover ? ensureHttpsMediaGallery([rawCover], folderKey, 'cover') : Promise.resolve([]),
    ensureHttpsMediaGallery(galleryUris || [], folderKey, 'extra'),
  ]);

  const coverHttpsUrl = uploadedCoverArr[0] || (isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(rawCover) ? rawCover : '');
  const cleanExtras = uploadedExtras.filter((u) => u !== standardCover).slice(0, 10);
  const fullGallery = Array.from(
    new Set([coverHttpsUrl, ...cleanExtras].filter((u) => isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(u)))
  ).slice(0, 11);

  return { coverHttpsUrl, fullGallery, cleanExtras };
};

export interface BestEventCoverInput {
  portada_url?: string | null;
  imagen_url?: string | null;
  galeria?: string[] | null;
  atributos?: { galeria?: string[] | null } | null;
}

export const resolveBestEventCoverUrl = (ev: BestEventCoverInput | null | undefined, fallbackUrl: string): string => {
  const candidates = [
    ev?.portada_url,
    ev?.imagen_url,
    ...(Array.isArray(ev?.galeria) ? ev.galeria : []),
    ...(Array.isArray(ev?.atributos?.galeria) ? ev.atributos.galeria : []),
  ]
    .map((u) => String(u || '').trim())
    .filter((u) => isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(u));
  return candidates[0] || fallbackUrl;
};

import type React from 'react';

interface EventMutableRecord extends Record<string, unknown> {
  id?: string | number | null;
  ubicacion?: string | null;
  latitud?: unknown;

export function notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed() {
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  emitKineticRootRefresh();
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.dispatchEvent === 'function') {
    try { window.dispatchEvent(new Event('kinetic:boveda-updated')); } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
  }
}

export async function verifyReservaSavedInDb(
  userId: string, serviceId?: string | null, servicioNombre?: string | null,
  exactLocalDate?: string | null, spotNumber?: number | null, minCreatedAtIso?: string | null
): Promise<Record<string, unknown> | null> {
  if (!userId) return null;
  try {
    let q = supabase.from('reservas').select('id, usuario_id, servicio_id, servicio, nombre_servicio, titulo_clase, negocio_nombre, estado, monto_escrow, monto_puerta, precio_kc, costo_total, fecha_clase, hora_inicio, spot_number, created_at')
      .eq('usuario_id', userId).in('estado', ['activa', 'pendiente', 'confirmada']);
    if (minCreatedAtIso) q = q.gte('created_at', minCreatedAtIso);
    const { data } = await q.order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(6);
    const normTitle = String(servicioNombre || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const minMs = minCreatedAtIso ? Date.parse(minCreatedAtIso) : Date.now() - 12000;
    const match = (data || []).find((r: Record<string, unknown>) => {
      const rCreated = Date.parse(String(r?.created_at || ''));
      if (Number.isFinite(minMs) && (!Number.isFinite(rCreated) || rCreated < minMs)) return false;
      const sameSrv = (serviceId && String(r?.servicio_id || '') === String(serviceId)) ||
        (normTitle && String(r?.servicio || r?.nombre_servicio || r?.titulo_clase || '').trim().toLowerCase() === normTitle);
      if (!sameSrv) return false;
      if (exactLocalDate && normalizeLocalIsoDate(String(r?.fecha_clase || '')) !== exactLocalDate) return false;
      if (spotNumber != null && Number(r?.spot_number) !== Number(spotNumber)) return false;
      return true;
    });
    if (match) notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed();
    return match || null;
  } catch { return null; }
}

export async function insertZeroKcReservaDirect(p: {
  userId: string; serviceId?: string | null; negocioNombre: string; servicioNombre: string;
  exactLocalDate: string; horaInicio?: string | null; spotNumber?: number | null; cupoMaximo?: number; lang?: string;
}) {
  const now = new Date();
  const todayIso = `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  let safeDate = normalizeLocalIsoDate(p.exactLocalDate);
  if (safeDate < todayIso) {
    const hm = String(p.horaInicio || '').match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
    const endedToday = hm ? now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes() > parseInt(hm[1], 10) * 60 + parseInt(hm[2], 10) + 60 : false;
    const tmr = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + (endedToday ? 1 : 0));
    safeDate = `${tmr.getFullYear()}-${String(tmr.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(tmr.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }
  const existing = await verifyReservaSavedInDb(p.userId, p.serviceId, p.servicioNombre, safeDate);
  if (existing) return existing;

  const validUuid = p.serviceId && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(String(p.serviceId).trim())
    ? String(p.serviceId).trim() : null;

  if (!validUuid) {
    throw new Error(
      p.lang === 'en'
        ? 'This session requires a valid catalog service ID to book.'
        : 'Esta sesión requiere un identificador válido de catálogo para reservar.'
    );
  }

  const { data: rpcData, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('procesar_reserva_escrow', {
    p_usuario_id: p.userId,
    p_negocio_nombre: p.negocioNombre,
    p_servicio_nombre: p.servicioNombre,
    p_precio_kc: 0,
    p_servicio_id: validUuid,
    p_fecha_clase: safeDate,
    p_spot_number: p.spotNumber ?? null,
    p_split_count: 1,
    p_es_pase_maestro: false,
  });
  if (rpcErr || !rpcData) {
    throw new Error(String(rpcErr?.message || 'Error procesando reserva 0 KC en servidor.'));
  }
  const base = (Array.isArray(rpcData) ? rpcData[0] : rpcData) as Record<string, unknown>;
  notifyGlobalBookingConfirmed();
  return {
    ...base,
    id: base?.reserva_id ?? base?.id,
    reserva_id: base?.reserva_id ?? base?.id,
    usuario_id: p.userId,
    servicio_id: validUuid,
    servicio: p.servicioNombre,
    negocio_nombre: p.negocioNombre,
    precio_kc: 0,
    costo_total: 0,
    monto_escrow: 0,
    monto_puerta: 0,
    estado: 'confirmada',
    fecha_clase: safeDate,
    spot_number: base?.spot_number ?? p.spotNumber ?? null,
  };
}

export async function shareEventExperienceAction(p: {
  eventId?: string; titleVal: string; discVal: string; clubName: string; locVal: string; detectedZone: string | null;
  readableDate: string; coachVal: string; totalKc: number; escrow40: number; language: string;
  td?: (v: string) => string; tDisc: (v: string) => string; tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
}) {
  const tr = p.td || ((v: string) => v);
  const slug = encodeURIComponent(String(p.eventId || p.titleVal || 'evento').trim());
  const directUrl = `https://kineticapp.com.mx/?evento=${slug}`;
  const cleanTitle = tr(p.titleVal || (p.language === 'en' ? 'Kinetic Event' : 'Evento Kinetic'));
  const cleanDisc = p.tDisc(p.discVal || 'Fuerza');
  const rawCoachVal = String(p.coachVal || '').trim();
  const cleanCoach =
    !rawCoachVal || rawCoachVal === 'Coach Oficial Kinetic'
      ? p.tx('Coach Oficial Kinetic', 'Official Kinetic Coach')
      : tr(rawCoachVal);
  const locDisplay = p.detectedZone || (p.locVal && !/^https?:\/\//i.test(p.locVal) ? p.locVal : 'Los Cabos, B.C.S.');
  const shareTitle = `Kinetic — ${cleanTitle}`;
  const bodyWithoutUrl =
    p.language === 'en'
      ? `⚡ Train with me on KINETIC!\n\n🏋️ Event: ${cleanTitle} (${cleanDisc})\n📍 Club: ${p.clubName} (${locDisplay})\n🗓️ Schedule: ${p.readableDate}\n👤 Coach: ${cleanCoach}\n💎 Investment: ${p.totalKc} KC (40% Protected Deposit: ${p.escrow40} KC)`
      : `⚡ ¡Entrena conmigo en KINETIC!\n\n🏋️ Evento: ${cleanTitle} (${cleanDisc})\n📍 Sede: ${p.clubName} (${locDisplay})\n🗓️ Fecha y Hora: ${p.readableDate}\n👤 Coach: ${cleanCoach}\n💎 Inversión: ${p.totalKc} KC (40% Anticipo Protegido: ${p.escrow40} KC)`;

  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof navigator !== 'undefined') {
    const nav = navigator as Navigator & {
      share?: (data: { title?: string; text?: string; url?: string }) => Promise<void>;
      clipboard?: { writeText?: (txt: string) => Promise<void> };
    };
    if (typeof nav.share === 'function') {
      try {
        await nav.share({ title: shareTitle, text: bodyWithoutUrl, url: directUrl });
        return;
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        if (typeof err === 'object' && err !== null && (err as { name?: string }).name === 'AbortError') return;
      }
    }
    try {
      if (nav.clipboard?.writeText) await nav.clipboard.writeText(`${bodyWithoutUrl}\n${directUrl}`);
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
    return;
  }

  const fullNativeMsg =
    p.language === 'en'
      ? `${bodyWithoutUrl}\n\n🔗 Open directly in Kinetic:\n${directUrl}`
      : `${bodyWithoutUrl}\n\n🔗 Abre este evento directo en Kinetic:\n${directUrl}`;
  try {
    await Share.share({ title: shareTitle, message: fullNativeMsg });
  } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
}

export async function persistEventExperienceAction(p: {
  cleanT: string; currentEv: EventMutableRecord | null | undefined; isCreateMode: boolean; isAdmin: boolean; canManage: boolean; clubName: string;
  coverUri: string; galleryUris: string[]; standardDisciplineImage: string; discVal: string; freqVal: string;
  dateVal: string; minTomorrowIso: string; timeVal: string; durVal: string; coachVal: string; locVal: string;
  mapsDeepUrl: string; locCoords: { lat: number | null; lng: number | null }; cupoVal: string; totalKc: number;
  ageVal: string; reqsVal: string[]; descVal: string; attrInt: string; attrEnf: string; attrImp: string;
  attrNiv: string; attrEnt: string; studioLayout: IStudioLayoutConfig | null; splitEscrowEnabled: boolean; splitMaxPersonas: number;
}) {
  const folderKey = String(p.currentEv?.id || p.clubName || p.cleanT);
  const [galleryPack, resolvedGps] = await Promise.all([
    buildCompressedFullGallery(p.coverUri, p.galleryUris, p.standardDisciplineImage, folderKey),
    resolveEventGpsCoordinates(
      `${p.mapsDeepUrl || ''} ${p.locVal || ''} ${p.currentEv?.ubicacion || ''}`.trim(),
      p.locCoords?.lat ?? p.currentEv?.latitud,
      p.locCoords?.lng ?? p.currentEv?.longitud,
      p.clubName || String(p.currentEv?.negocio_nombre || '')
    ),
  ]);
  const { coverHttpsUrl, fullGallery, cleanExtras } = galleryPack;
  const status = p.isAdmin || p.canManage ? 'aprobado' : 'pendiente';
  const safeAge = normalizeAgePolicy(p.ageVal);
  const { atributosObj, coreData, extData, effectiveLayout, finalCupo, durMinInt, reqsJoined, dbLoc, resolvedCoverUrl } =
    buildEventSavePayloads({
      cleanT: p.cleanT, discVal: p.discVal, freqVal: p.freqVal, dateVal: p.dateVal,
      minTomorrowIso: p.minTomorrowIso, timeVal: p.timeVal, durVal: p.durVal, coachVal: p.coachVal,
      locVal: p.locVal, mapsDeepUrl: p.mapsDeepUrl,
      locCoords: { lat: resolvedGps.latitud, lng: resolvedGps.longitud },
      cupoVal: p.cupoVal, totalKc: p.totalKc, ageVal: safeAge, reqsVal: p.reqsVal, descVal: p.descVal,
      attrInt: p.attrInt, attrEnf: p.attrEnf, attrImp: p.attrImp, attrNiv: p.attrNiv, attrEnt: p.attrEnt,
      studioLayout: p.studioLayout, coverHttpsUrl, fullGallery, status,
      splitEscrowEnabled: p.splitEscrowEnabled, splitMaxPersonas: p.splitMaxPersonas,
    });
  atributosObj.latitud = resolvedGps.latitud;
  atributosObj.longitud = resolvedGps.longitud;
  extData.latitud = resolvedGps.latitud;
  extData.longitud = resolvedGps.longitud;

  const evIdStr = p.currentEv?.id != null ? String(p.currentEv.id) : undefined;
  const isUpdate = Boolean(evIdStr && !p.isCreateMode);
  const savedRow = await writeServiceToSupabaseSafe({
    isUpdate, eventId: evIdStr, clubName: p.clubName, coreData, extData,
  });

  await saveEventAttrsDualCache([savedRow?.id, evIdStr, p.cleanT], atributosObj, effectiveLayout);

  if (p.currentEv) {
    Object.assign(p.currentEv, {
      nombre_servicio: p.cleanT, titulo: p.cleanT, categoria: p.discVal, fecha: coreData.fecha,
      fecha_proyectada: coreData.fecha, hora_inicio: p.timeVal, instructor_nombre: p.coachVal,
      descripcion: p.descVal, galeria: fullGallery,
      ...(resolvedCoverUrl ? { portada_url: resolvedCoverUrl, imagen_url: resolvedCoverUrl } : {}),
      frecuencia: p.freqVal, duracion_minutos: durMinInt,
      requisitos_traer: reqsJoined, edad_minima: safeAge, precio_kc: p.totalKc, ubicacion: dbLoc,
      latitud: resolvedGps.latitud, longitud: resolvedGps.longitud,
      atributos: atributosObj, studio_layout_json: effectiveLayout, cupo_maximo: finalCupo,
      split_escrow_enabled: p.splitEscrowEnabled, split_max_personas: p.splitMaxPersonas,
    });
  }
  emitKineticRadarUpdated();
  return { cleanExtras, safeAge, resolvedGps, coverHttpsUrl: resolvedCoverUrl, fullGallery };
}

export function resolveEventClubLogo(
  currentEv: EventMutableRecord | null | undefined,
  isOfficial: boolean,
  clubName: string,
  discVal: string
): string | null {
  const raw = currentEv?.avatar_club || currentEv?.avatar_url;
  if (raw && String(raw).trim().length > 5) return raw;
  if (isOfficial) return null;
  const found = ((KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || []) as AffiliateLogoEntry[]).find(
    (a) => String(a.nombre || '').trim().toLowerCase() === String(clubName).trim().toLowerCase()
  );
  return found?.avatar_url || found?.portada_url || resolveCategoryImage(discVal, clubName);
}

export function applyEventSheetOptionSelection(
  sheet: ActiveSheetType,
  val: string,
  ctx: {
    cupoVal: string;
    discVal: string;
    titleVal: string;
    setTimeVal: (v: string) => void;
    setDiscVal: (v: string) => void;
    setActivePhotoIdx: (v: number) => void;
    setStudioLayout: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<IStudioLayoutConfig | null>>;
    setFreqVal: (v: string) => void;
    setDurVal: (v: string) => void;
    setCupoVal: (v: string) => void;
    setPriceVal: (v: string) => void;
    setAgeVal: (v: string) => void;
    setAttrInt: (v: string) => void;
    setAttrEnf: (v: string) => void;
    setAttrImp: (v: string) => void;
    setAttrNiv: (v: string) => void;
    setAttrEnt: (v: string) => void;
  }
) {
  if (sheet === 'hora') ctx.setTimeVal(val);
  else if (sheet === 'disciplina') {
    ctx.setDiscVal(val);
    ctx.setActivePhotoIdx(0);
    ctx.setStudioLayout((prev) => syncLayoutOnDisciplineChange(prev, val, parseInt(ctx.cupoVal, 10) || 12, ctx.titleVal));
  } else if (sheet === 'frecuencia') ctx.setFreqVal(val);
  else if (sheet === 'duracion') ctx.setDurVal(val);
  else if (sheet === 'cupo') {
    const nextCap = Math.min(50, Math.max(1, parseInt(val, 10) || 12));
    ctx.setCupoVal(String(nextCap));
    ctx.setStudioLayout((prev) => buildGridForTotalSpots(nextCap, prev?.equipmentType || inferEquipmentByDiscipline(ctx.discVal, ctx.titleVal)));
  } else if (sheet === 'precio') ctx.setPriceVal(val);
  else if (sheet === 'edad') ctx.setAgeVal(normalizeAgePolicy(val));
  else if (sheet === 'intensidad') ctx.setAttrInt(val);
  else if (sheet === 'enfoque') ctx.setAttrEnf(val);
  else if (sheet === 'impacto') ctx.setAttrImp(val);
  else if (sheet === 'nivel') ctx.setAttrNiv(val);
  else if (sheet === 'entorno') ctx.setAttrEnt(normalizeEnvironmentLabel(val));
}

export function buildBookingSuccessAlertConfig(p: {
  realTitle: string;
  localBookingDateIso: string;
  timeVal: string;
  clubName?: string;
  discVal?: string;
  locVal?: string;
  coachVal?: string;
  durVal?: string;
  selectedSpot?: number | null;
  equipmentType?: string | null;
  totalKc?: number;
  escrowKc?: number;
  puertaKc?: number;
  isAdmin?: boolean;
  language?: string;
  fmtDate: (d: Date, opts?: Record<string, unknown>) => string;
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;
  onConfirm: () => void;
  secondaryActionText?: string;
  onSecondaryAction?: () => void;
}) {
  const isEn = p.language === 'en';
  const passVm = buildKineticDigitalPassViewModel(
    {
      negocio_nombre: p.clubName || 'Kinetic Oficial',
      servicio: p.realTitle,
      categoria: p.discVal || 'Fuerza',
      fecha_clase: p.localBookingDateIso,
      hora_inicio: p.timeVal || '08:00',
      duracion_minutos: Number(p.durVal) || 60,
      ubicacion: p.locVal || '',
      instructor_nombre: p.coachVal || '',
      spot_number: p.selectedSpot ?? null,
      es_pase_maestro: Boolean(p.isAdmin),
      precio_kc: p.isAdmin ? 0 : p.totalKc,
      monto_escrow: p.isAdmin ? 0 : p.escrowKc,
      monto_puerta: p.isAdmin ? 0 : p.puertaKc,
    },
    { isEn, equipmentTypeOverride: p.equipmentType, isAdminOverride: Boolean(p.isAdmin) }
  );
  const niceDate =
    p.fmtDate(parseIsoToLocalDate(p.localBookingDateIso), { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' }) ||
    passVm.fechaDisplayLabel;
  return {
    visible: true,
    type: 'success',
    title: passVm.headerTitle,
    message: formatDigitalPassAlertBody(passVm, niceDate),
    confirmText: p.tx('MI BÓVEDA', 'MY VAULT'),
    onConfirm: p.onConfirm,
    secondaryActionText: p.secondaryActionText,
    onSecondaryAction: p.onSecondaryAction,
  };
}

export function buildSpotCollisionAlertConfig(p: {
  tx: (es: string, en: string) => string;

}) {

      'Sorry! Another athlete took your spot while you were deciding. Hurry, spots are running out! Choose another spot.'
    ),
    confirmText: p.tx('ELEGIR OTRO LUGAR', 'CHOOSE ANOTHER SPOT'),

export function openEventMapsRouteAction(p: {
  mapsDeepUrl?: string;
  locVal?: string;
  rawUbicacion?: string;
  lat?: number | null;
  lng?: number | null;
  clubName: string;
}) {
  if (p.mapsDeepUrl) { openExternalLinkSafe(p.mapsDeepUrl); return; }
  const raw = String(p.locVal || p.rawUbicacion || '').trim();
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(raw)) { openExternalLinkSafe(raw); return; }
  const gps = extractSyncEventGpsCoordinates(raw, p.lat, p.lng, p.clubName);
  openExternalLinkSafe(buildGoogleMapsSearchUrl(raw ? `${p.clubName} ${raw}` : p.clubName, gps.latitud, gps.longitud));
}

export async function pickEventCoverWithCdnAction(
  entityKey: string,
  setCustomCoverUri: (u: string) => void,
  setActivePhotoIdx: (i: number) => void,
  onSuccessHaptic: () => void
) {
  const { previewUris, uploadPromise } = await pickEventImagesInstantWithCdn(1, entityKey, 'cover');
  if (!previewUris[0]) return;
  setCustomCoverUri(previewUris[0]); setActivePhotoIdx(0); onSuccessHaptic();
  const cdnUrls = await uploadPromise;
  if (cdnUrls[0] && isVerifiedHttpsMediaUrl(cdnUrls[0])) setCustomCoverUri(cdnUrls[0]);
}

export async function pickEventGalleryWithCdnAction(
  remaining: number,
  entityKey: string,
  stdImg: string,
  maxExtras: number,
  setGalleryUris: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string[]>>,
  onSuccessHaptic: () => void
) {
  const { previewUris, uploadPromise } = await pickEventImagesInstantWithCdn(remaining, entityKey, 'extra');
  if (previewUris.length === 0) return;
  setGalleryUris((prev) => Array.from(new Set([...prev, ...previewUris])).filter((u) => u && u !== stdImg).slice(0, maxExtras));
  onSuccessHaptic();
  const cdnUrls = await uploadPromise;
  if (cdnUrls.length > 0) {
    setGalleryUris((prev) => Array.from(new Set([...prev.filter((u) => !previewUris.includes(u)), ...cdnUrls])).filter((u) => u && u !== stdImg).slice(0, maxExtras));
  }
}

    FILE: src/utils/eventModalGpsResolverLeaf.ts

    // === src/utils/eventModalGpsResolverLeaf.ts === //
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES } from '../constants/exploreData';
import { supabase } from '../lib/supabase';

const CSL_DEFAULT_GPS = { latitud: 22.8905, longitud: -109.9167 };
const CORREDOR_GPS = { latitud: 22.9765, longitud: -109.7995 };
const SJD_GPS = { latitud: 23.0591, longitud: -109.6956 };

interface AffiliateCatalogEntry {
  nombre?: string | null;
  ubicacion?: string | null;
  latitud?: number | string | null;
  latitude?: number | string | null;
  longitud?: number | string | null;
  longitude?: number | string | null;
}

function parseExplicitGpsFromRaw(rawLocation?: string): { latitud: number; longitud: number } | null {
  if (!rawLocation) return null;
  let txt = String(rawLocation).trim();
  if (!txt) return null;
  try { txt = `${txt} ${decodeURIComponent(txt)}`; } catch {}
  const m =
    txt.match(/!3d(-?\d{1,2}\.\d+)!4d(-?\d{1,3}\.\d+)/i) ||
    txt.match(/@(-?\d{1,2}\.\d+)\s*,\s*(-?\d{1,3}\.\d+)/) ||
    txt.match(/[?&](?:q|ll|query|center|destination|daddr)=(-?\d{1,2}\.\d+)\s*,\s*(-?\d{1,3}\.\d+)/i) ||
    txt.match(/(?:^|[\s(])(-?\d{1,2}\.\d{3,})\s*,\s*(-?\d{1,3}\.\d{3,})(?:$|[\s)])/);
  if (!m) return null;
  const lat = parseFloat(m[1]);
  const lng = parseFloat(m[2]);
  if (!Number.isFinite(lat) || !Number.isFinite(lng) || Math.abs(lat) > 90 || Math.abs(lng) > 180 || (lat === 0 && lng === 0)) {
    return null;
  }
  return { latitud: Number(lat.toFixed(6)), longitud: Number(lng.toFixed(6)) };
}

function isValidLosCabosGps(fallbackLat?: unknown, fallbackLng?: unknown): { latitud: number; longitud: number } | null {
  const lat = typeof fallbackLat === 'number' ? fallbackLat : parseFloat(String(fallbackLat ?? ''));
  const lng = typeof fallbackLng === 'number' ? fallbackLng : parseFloat(String(fallbackLng ?? ''));
  if (Number.isFinite(lat) && Number.isFinite(lng) && lat >= 22.7 && lat <= 23.65 && lng >= -110.35 && lng <= -109.35) {
    return { latitud: Number(lat.toFixed(6)), longitud: Number(lng.toFixed(6)) };
  }
  return null;
}

function resolveLosCabosZoneFallback(rawLocation?: string): { latitud: number; longitud: number } {
  const low = String(rawLocation || '')
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase();
  if (/\b(san jose|sjd|palmilla|fonatur|zacatal|campestre|guaymitas|puerto los cabos)\b/.test(low)) return { ...SJD_GPS };
  if (/\b(corredor|corridor|tezal|el tule|chileno|santa maria|cabo bello|cabo del sol|twin dolphin|montage|esperanza)\b/.test(low)) {
    return { ...CORREDOR_GPS };
  }
  return { ...CSL_DEFAULT_GPS };
}

function findAffiliateCatalogCoords(clubName?: string, rawLocation?: string): { latitud: number; longitud: number } | null {
  const norm = (s?: string | null) =>
    String(s || '')
      .normalize('NFD')
      .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim();
  const cKey = norm(clubName);
  const lKey = norm(rawLocation);
  if (!cKey && !lKey) return null;
  const match = ((KINETIC_ALL_AFFILIATES || []) as AffiliateCatalogEntry[]).find((a) => {
    const aName = norm(a?.nombre);
    if (!aName) return false;
    return (cKey && (aName === cKey || aName.includes(cKey) || cKey.includes(aName))) ||
      (lKey && (lKey.includes(aName) || (a?.ubicacion && lKey.includes(norm(a.ubicacion)) && aName === cKey)));
  });
  if (!match) return null;
  return isValidLosCabosGps(match.latitud ?? match.latitude, match.longitud ?? match.longitude);
}

export function extractSyncEventGpsCoordinates(
  rawLocation?: string,
  fallbackLat?: unknown,
  fallbackLng?: unknown,
  clubName?: string
): { latitud: number; longitud: number } {
  return (
    parseExplicitGpsFromRaw(rawLocation) ||
    isValidLosCabosGps(fallbackLat, fallbackLng) ||
    findAffiliateCatalogCoords(clubName, rawLocation) ||
    resolveLosCabosZoneFallback(rawLocation)
  );
}

export async function resolveEventGpsCoordinates(
  rawLocation?: string,
  fallbackLat?: unknown,
  fallbackLng?: unknown,
  clubName?: string
): Promise<{ latitud: number; longitud: number }> {
  const direct = parseExplicitGpsFromRaw(rawLocation);
  if (direct) return direct;
  const shortMatch = String(rawLocation || '').match(/https?:\/\/(?:maps\.app\.goo\.gl|goo\.gl\/maps)\/[^\s"'<>]+/i);
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web' && shortMatch?.[0]) {
    const fetchExpanded = async (method: 'HEAD' | 'GET') => {
      const ctrl = typeof AbortController !== 'undefined' ? new AbortController() : null;
      const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl?.abort(), 3000);
      try {
        return await fetch(shortMatch[0], { method, redirect: 'follow', ...(ctrl ? { signal: ctrl.signal } : {}) });
      } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    };
    try {
      const headRes = await fetchExpanded('HEAD');
      const fromHead = parseExplicitGpsFromRaw(headRes?.url);
      if (fromHead) return fromHead;
    } catch {}
    try {
      const getRes = await fetchExpanded('GET');
      const fromGet = parseExplicitGpsFromRaw(getRes?.url) || parseExplicitGpsFromRaw(await getRes.text());
      if (fromGet) return fromGet;
    } catch {}
  }
  const explicitValid = isValidLosCabosGps(fallbackLat, fallbackLng);
  if (explicitValid) return explicitValid;

  const cleanClub = String(clubName || '').trim();
  if (cleanClub) {
    try {
      const { data: afRow } = await supabase
        .from('afiliados')
        .select('latitud, longitud, ubicacion')
        .ilike('nombre', cleanClub)
        .limit(1)
        .maybeSingle();
      const dbGps = isValidLosCabosGps(afRow?.latitud, afRow?.longitud) || parseExplicitGpsFromRaw(afRow?.ubicacion);
      if (dbGps) return dbGps;
    } catch {}
  }

  return findAffiliateCatalogCoords(clubName, rawLocation) || resolveLosCabosZoneFallback(rawLocation);
}

    FILE: src/utils/kineticFavoriteEventsMemoryLeaf.ts

    // === src/utils/kineticFavoriteEventsMemoryLeaf.ts === //
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { getExploreFavoritesSwrSync, setExploreFavoritesSwrSync } from '../services/kineticSwrMemoryHub';

export const FAV_EVENTS_STORAGE_KEY = '@kinetic_fav_events_v25';

let memoryFavEventsSet: Set<string> = new Set();
let hydratedFavUserKey: string | null = null;
let favoriteRadarPulseDepth = 0;
const favMemoryListeners = new Set<(ids: string[]) => void>();

export const isFavoriteOnlyRadarPulse = (): boolean => favoriteRadarPulseDepth > 0;

export const runWithFavoriteOnlyRadarPulse = (fn: () => void): void => {
  favoriteRadarPulseDepth += 1;
  try {
    fn();
  } finally {
    favoriteRadarPulseDepth = Math.max(0, favoriteRadarPulseDepth - 1);
  }
};

export const getCanonicalFixedEventKey = (ev: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined): string => {
  if (!ev) return '';
  const club = String(ev.negocio_nombre || ev.club || 'kinetic').trim().toLowerCase();
  const title = String(ev.titulo || ev.nombre_servicio || ev.servicio || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const disc = String(ev.categoria || ev.disciplina || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (title) return `${club}::${title}`;
  return String(ev.id || `${club}::${disc}`).trim().toLowerCase();
};

export const getFavoriteEventsIdsSync = (userId?: string | null): string[] => {
  if (memoryFavEventsSet.size === 0 && userId) {
    const swrIds = getExploreFavoritesSwrSync(userId);
    if (swrIds && swrIds.length > 0) {
      swrIds.forEach((id) => memoryFavEventsSet.add(id));
    }
  }
  return Array.from(memoryFavEventsSet);
};

export const subscribeFavoriteEventsMemory = (cb: (ids: string[]) => void): (() => void) => {
  favMemoryListeners.add(cb);
  return () => {
    favMemoryListeners.delete(cb);
  };
};

const notifyFavoriteEventsMemoryListeners = (ids: string[]): void => {
  favMemoryListeners.forEach((cb) => {
    try {
      cb(ids);
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
  });
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.dispatchEvent === 'function') {
    try {
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kinetic:fav-events-updated', { detail: { updated: ids } }));
    } catch (err: unknown) { void err; }
  }
};

export const setFavoriteEventsMemorySync = (
  nextIds: string[],
  userId?: string | null,
  emitRadar?: () => void
): string[] => {
  const clean = Array.from(new Set((nextIds || []).map(String).filter(Boolean)));
  memoryFavEventsSet = new Set(clean);
  if (userId) setExploreFavoritesSwrSync(userId, clean);
  notifyFavoriteEventsMemoryListeners(clean);
  if (emitRadar) runWithFavoriteOnlyRadarPulse(emitRadar);
  return clean;
};

export const hydrateFavoriteEvents = async (userId?: string | null): Promise<string[]> => {
  const storageKey = `${FAV_EVENTS_STORAGE_KEY}_${userId || 'guest'}`;
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    const parsed: unknown = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    const list = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.map(String).filter(Boolean) : [];
    if (list.length > 0 || hydratedFavUserKey !== storageKey) {
      memoryFavEventsSet = new Set(list);
      hydratedFavUserKey = storageKey;
      if (userId && list.length > 0) setExploreFavoritesSwrSync(userId, list);
    }
    return getFavoriteEventsIdsSync(userId);
  } catch {
    return getFavoriteEventsIdsSync(userId);
  }
};

export const isEventFavoriteSync = (ev: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined): boolean => {
  if (!ev) return false;
  const key = getCanonicalFixedEventKey(ev);
  const idStr = String(ev.id || '');
  return (Boolean(key) && memoryFavEventsSet.has(key)) || (Boolean(idStr) && memoryFavEventsSet.has(idStr));
};

export const getFavoriteEventsIds = async (userId?: string | null): Promise<string[]> => {
  if (memoryFavEventsSet.size > 0 && hydratedFavUserKey === `${FAV_EVENTS_STORAGE_KEY}_${userId || 'guest'}`) {
    return Array.from(memoryFavEventsSet);
  }
  try {
    const key = userId ? `${FAV_EVENTS_STORAGE_KEY}_${userId}` : FAV_EVENTS_STORAGE_KEY;
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(key);
    if (!raw) return getFavoriteEventsIdsSync(userId);
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    const clean = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.map((x) => String(x)).filter(Boolean) : [];
    clean.forEach((id) => memoryFavEventsSet.add(id));
    return Array.from(memoryFavEventsSet);
  } catch {
    return getFavoriteEventsIdsSync(userId);
  }
};

export const toggleFavoriteEventOptimistic = (params: {
  ev: Record<string, unknown>;
  userId?: string | null;
  emitRadar?: () => void;
  persistFn?: (nextIds: string[], userId?: string | null) => Promise<void>;
}): { nextFavorite: boolean; nextIds: string[]; persistencePromise: Promise<boolean> } => {
  const { ev, userId, emitRadar, persistFn } = params;
  const key = getCanonicalFixedEventKey(ev);
  const idStr = String(ev?.id || '');
  const storageKey = `${FAV_EVENTS_STORAGE_KEY}_${userId || 'guest'}`;
  const prevIds = Array.from(memoryFavEventsSet);
  const currentlyFav = isEventFavoriteSync(ev);

  if (currentlyFav) {
    if (key) memoryFavEventsSet.delete(key);
    if (idStr) memoryFavEventsSet.delete(idStr);
  } else {
    if (key) memoryFavEventsSet.add(key);
    if (idStr) memoryFavEventsSet.add(idStr);
  }

  const nextIds = Array.from(memoryFavEventsSet);
  hydratedFavUserKey = storageKey;
  if (userId) setExploreFavoritesSwrSync(userId, nextIds);
  notifyFavoriteEventsMemoryListeners(nextIds);
  if (emitRadar) runWithFavoriteOnlyRadarPulse(emitRadar);

  const persistTask = persistFn
    ? persistFn(nextIds, userId)
    : Promise.all([
        AsyncStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(nextIds)),
        AsyncStorage.setItem(FAV_EVENTS_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextIds)),
      ]).then(() => undefined);

  const persistencePromise = persistTask
    .then(() => !currentlyFav)
    .catch(() => {
      memoryFavEventsSet = new Set(prevIds);
      if (userId) setExploreFavoritesSwrSync(userId, prevIds);
      notifyFavoriteEventsMemoryListeners(prevIds);
      if (emitRadar) runWithFavoriteOnlyRadarPulse(emitRadar);
      return currentlyFav;
    });

  return { nextFavorite: !currentlyFav, nextIds, persistencePromise };
};
